### [1] [Divergent Multi-Version Execution (DME): Canonical Instruction-Trace Fault Detection via Structural Address-Space Decorrelation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12576)
*Petro Baran Yrievich*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出Divergent Multi-Version Execution (DME)，通过独立编译各副本来产生不同的代码和数据内存布局（保持语义一致），并比较规范指令轨迹来检测相关故障，从而解决传统冗余方法中因相关故障导致的数据静默损坏问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统冗余技术（lockstep、TMR）因所有副本执行相同二进制和内存布局，易受相关故障影响——例如程序计数器或数据指针的同一偏移错误会使所有副本沿相同错误路径执行，导致静默数据损坏。

Method: DME为每个副本独立编译，生成不同的代码和数据内存布局但保持相同语义；故障检测通过比较规范指令轨迹（包含操作码、寄存器标识符、加载/存储值及结果，但丢弃布局相关的地址信息）来实现。

Result: 该方法能够检测由故意篡改、软件漏洞、编译错误或物理干扰引起的相关故障，避免静默数据损坏。

Conclusion: DME通过多样化执行和语义一致性验证，有效克服了传统冗余方法面对相关故障时的脆弱性，提升了系统可靠性。

Abstract: Traditional redundancy (lockstep, TMR) executes identical binaries with identical memory layouts. A single correlated fault - for example, an arbitrary program counter value or a perturbation delta-PC in all replicas - redirects all replicas along the same incorrect path. The same applies to corruption of data pointers. Both types of faults, regardless of their origin (deliberate tampering, software bug, compilation bug, or physical disturbance), cause silent data corruption and erroneous program execution. This work presents Divergent Multi-Version Execution (DME), a runtime semantic consistency verifier for diversified executions. Each replica is compiled independently, producing different code and data memory layouts while preserving identical semantics. Faults are detected by comparing canonical instruction traces, which include opcodes, register identifiers, loaded/stored values, and results, while discarding layout-dependent addresses.

</details>


### [2] [Language-Based Agent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12863)
*Timothy Zhou,Loris D'Antoni,Nadia Polikarpova*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出语言型智能体控制（LBAC），一种将编程语言与语言安全技术应用于智能体控制的新编程模型。通过要求智能体生成在脚手架代码上下文中类型良好的程序，实现统一的策略执行（访问控制、信息流、数据溯源等），不安全的程序在执行前被类型检查器拒绝。同时保持充分表达性，通过三个案例（I/O沙箱、数据溯源、信息流控制）进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统编程中静态类型与运行时执行可保证用户指定的安全策略，但现有智能体应用缺乏类似保证。作者希望将编程语言领域的安全保障技术扩展到智能体应用，使策略能够统一应用于整个应用（包括智能体生成的行为和开发者编写的脚手架代码）。

Method: 提出LBAC编程模型，要求智能体生成在上下文脚手架代码中类型良好的程序，通过类型检查器在执行前拒绝不安全程序。智能体可执行任意无副作用的计算并递归调用子智能体，子智能体保留完整工具访问权限但受相同或更严格策略约束。

Result: 通过三个案例研究验证：文件系统能力进行I/O沙箱、数据溯源、信息流控制，展示了LBAC在不同策略场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: LBAC成功将编程语言的安全技术引入智能体控制，在保证安全策略统一执行的同时保持了充分的表达性，为构建安全的智能体应用提供了新的范式。

Abstract: This paper introduces language-based agent control (LBAC), a new programming model for agentic applications that brings techniques from programming languages and language-based security to the problem of agent control. In conventional programming, combinations of static typing and runtime enforcement have long been used to guarantee that well-typed programs satisfy user-specified policies, including policies for access control, information flow, data provenance, and more. The key idea behind LBAC is to extend these guarantees to agentic applications by requiring agents to generate programs that are themselves well typed in the context of the surrounding scaffolding code. Unsafe programs are rejected by the type-checker before execution, allowing policies to apply uniformly across the entire application, including both agent-generated behavior and developer-written scaffolding. At the same time, LBAC preserves substantial expressiveness: agents may perform arbitrary side-effect-free computation and recursively invoke subagents, which retain full tool access subject to the same -- or potentially more restrictive -- policies. We demonstrate LBAC with three case studies: I/O sandboxing via filesystem capabilities, data provenance, and information-flow control.

</details>


### [3] [LFPL: Revisited and Mechanized](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12893)
*Nathaniel Glover,Jan Hoffmann*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文对函数式编程语言LFPL及其元理论进行了现代化、自包含的阐述与机械化证明，包括使用新技术的可靠性证明（利用Aehlig & Schwichtenberg方法构造多项式边界）和简化了的完备性证明（通过新型栈式数据结构模拟多项式时间图灵机），并在证明助手Istari中完成了完整的形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LFPL在多项式时间可计算函数刻画、类型系统自动代价分析、线性依赖类型理论和函数式编程语言高效内存管理等领域具有重要影响，但长期以来缺乏自包含的完整阐述和完全机械化（形式化验证）的元理论。

Method: （1）可靠性证明：在扩展语言LFPL+上进行，采用Aehlig & Schwichtenberg（2002）的技术，针对大步代价语义显式构造多项式来界定表达式开销；（2）完备性证明：保持Hofmann（2002）原始证明结构，但引入一种由一阶函数和列表实现的新型栈式数据结构，大幅简化核心论证。（3）在证明助手Istari中完成全部形式化。

Result: 给出了LFPL及其元理论的现代化、自包含阐述；提供了新颖的可靠性证明和大幅简化的完备性证明；完成了Istari中元理论的机械化，是该证明助手的首批案例研究之一。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了LFPL缺乏完整自包含阐述和机械化验证的空白，其简化证明和形式化实现为线性类型系统、自动代价分析等领域提供了更坚实的基础设施。

Abstract: Hofmann (1999) introduced the functional programming language LFPL to characterize the functions computable in polynomial time using an affine type system. LFPL enables a natural programming style, including nested recursion, and has inspired the development of type systems for automatic cost analysis, linear dependent type theories, and efficient memory management in functional programming languages. Despite its prominence, there does not exist a self-contained presentation, let alone a full mechanization, of LFPL and its core metatheory. This article presents a modern account and mechanization of LFPL and its metatheory with the goal of being self-contained and accessible while streamlining the strongest-known soundness and completeness results. The soundness proof works with the language LFPL+, which extends LFPL with additional language features. The proof is novel, adapting a technique by Aehlig and Schwichtenberg (2002) to construct explicit polynomials that bound the cost of an LFPL+ expression with respect to a big-step cost semantics. The completeness proof shows that LFPL programs can simulate polynomial-time Turing machines while only relying on restricted forms of linear functions and lists. It has the same structure as the original proof by Hofmann (2002) but greatly simplifies the core argument with a novel stack-like data structure that is implemented with first-class functions and lists. The mechanization includes the full soundness and completeness proofs, and serves as one of the first case studies of mechanized metatheory in the recently developed proof assistant Istari.

</details>


### [4] [Liquid Tree Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13456)
*Ashish Mishra,Suresh Jagannathan*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Liquid Tree Automata（LTA）的组件化合成方法，通过精化类型驱动的状态合并机制，有效应对复杂逻辑查询下可行路径稀疏的挑战，显著提升合成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统组件化合成在处理精确约束时面临可行路径空间稀疏的难题，随着规约和查询的精确化，搜索有效执行路径变得异常困难。

Method: 引入Liquid Tree Automata（LTA），一种由精化类型规则驱动构建的新型树自动机变体。LTA利用子类型约束识别并在搜索过程中主动合并语义相似的状态，从而避免等价路径的冗余探索。

Result: 基于该方法实现的工具Hegel能够合成现有最先进工具无法处理的复杂查询的解。

Conclusion: 通过语义相似性驱动的状态合并策略，有效缓解了精确约束下路径稀疏问题，显著提升了组件化合成的可扩展性与求解能力。

Abstract: Component-based synthesis (CBS) generates loop-free programs from library components to satisfy logical queries. While expressive specifications and precise queries simplify the solution space, they make finding feasible execution paths significantly more difficult for traditional CBS procedures. As constraints become more exact, the search must navigate an increasingly sparse space of valid paths. We address this challenge by reasoning about \emph{logical similarities} between exploration paths. We consider library methods equipped with refinement-type specifications, which enrich base types with logical qualifiers to precisely constrain the value space. To efficiently explore this space, we introduce Liquid Tree Automata (LTA), a novel tree automata variant whose construction is driven by refinement typing rules. LTAs leverage subtyping constraints to identify and eagerly merge semantically similar states during search. This merging avoids redundant exploration of equivalent paths, significantly improving synthesis efficiency. We implement this approach in a tool called Hegel. Our evaluation demonstrates that Hegel synthesizes solutions to complex queries that are beyond the reach of existing state-of-the-art tools.

</details>


### [5] [On the Complexity of Checking Soundness of Natural Reductions (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13780)
*Constantin Enea,Azadeh Farzan,Dominik Klumpp*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类参数化并发程序中的语法归约——自然归约，通过引入原子块和全局同步点来简化正确性证明。研究了在无同步和有同步场景下判断自然归约是否关于给定可交换关系有效的问题，并给出了相应的算法复杂度和下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化并发程序的正确性验证中，归约（reductions）作为交错执行的代表性子集，能够显著简化证明过程。本文旨在提出一种更具表达力的归约形式——自然归约，并研究其有效性的判定问题。

Method: 通过在线程模板中引入原子块和全局同步点来定义自然归约。将该归约的有效性问题转化为关于给定（半）可交换关系的形式化判定问题，并分别在无同步和有同步（如加锁）两种场景下分析其计算复杂性。

Result: 无同步情况下，提出了一个完备的多项式时间判定算法；有同步情况下，证明了问题复杂度的一个一般性下界（参数化于同步机制），并指出即使对于加锁这类简单机制，问题已为coNP难。

Conclusion: 自然归约是一类表达力强的语法归约，其有效性判定在无同步时可高效求解，但在有同步时本质上更难（coNP难），为参数化并发程序的验证提供了理论上的复杂度界限。

Abstract: The verification of reductions, representative subsets of interleavings, simplifies correctness proofs of parameterized concurrent programs. We introduce an expressive class of syntactic reductions, which we call natural reductions. Natural reductions are specified by introducing atomic blocks and global rendezvous points in the parameterized program's thread template. We study the problem of deciding whether a given natural reduction is sound wrt. a given (semi-)commutativity relation. In the case that there is no synchronization between threads, we present a sound and complete polynomial-time algorithm. In the case where synchronization is considered, we provide a general lower bound for the problem (parametric in the synchronization mechanism), and show that the problem is coNP-hard already for a simple mechanism like locking.

</details>

### [6] [BackFlush: Knowledge-Free Backdoor Detection and Elimination with Watermark Preservation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12529)
*Jagadeesh Rachapudi,Ritali Vatsi,Pranav Singh,Praful Hambarde,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BackFlush是一个统一框架，通过旋转嵌入（RoPE遗忘技术）注入并遗忘辅助数据来检测和消除后门攻击，同时保留水印，在多种架构下实现约1%攻击成功率、约99%干净准确率并保持水印能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法需要预先知道触发器或负载信息、依赖干净参考模型、会牺牲模型效用或破坏水印完整性，无法在检测消除未知后门的同时保留水印。

Method: 提出BackFlush框架，基于两个新观察：后门冲刷现象（注入并遗忘辅助数据可消除后门）和后门敏感性放大（实现常数时间检测）。核心技术为基于旋转的参数编辑（RoPE）遗忘，通过旋转嵌入层来消除后门同时保留水印。

Result: 在多种触发器类型和架构下，BackFlush实现约1%攻击成功率、约99%干净准确率，水印能力得到保留，模型效用与干净基线相当。

Conclusion: BackFlush是首个能同时实现后门检测、后门消除和水印保留的统一框架，克服了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: In recent trends, one can observe Large Language Models (LLMs) are exposed to backdoor attacks where vicious triggers added during training or model editing to elicit harmful outputs on specific input patterns while maintaining clean performance on normal inputs. Legitimate watermarks used as ownership signatures share similar mechanisms to backdoors, creating a critical challenge: detecting and eliminating unknown backdoors without compromising watermark integrity. Existing defenses require prior knowledge of triggers or their payloads, depend on clean reference models, or sacrifice model utility without preserving the watermark. To address these limitations we introduce BackFlush and its variants, a unified framework for backdoor detection and elimination while preserving watermarks. We establish two novel observations: Backdoor Flushing Phenomenon, where injecting and unlearning auxiliary data eliminates pre established backdoors, and Backdoor Susceptibility Amplification, enabling constant time detection independent of vocabulary size. BackFlush employs Rotation based Parameter Editing (RoPE) Unlearning, a technique that preserves watermarks while eliminating backdoors by rotating the embeddings. Comprehensive evaluation across diverse trigger types over different architectures demonstrates BackFlush achieves approximately 1%Attack Success Rate (ASR), approximately 99% clean accuracy (CACC), and preserved watermarking capabilities in the realm where no existing method simultaneously provides these alongside maintaining model utility comparable to clean baselines. Codes are available at https://github.com/JagadeeshAI/BackFlush IJCNN.git.

</details>


### [7] [Ghost in the Context: Measuring Policy-Carriage Failures in Decision-Time Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12535)
*Igor Santos-Grueiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究LM智能体在决策时因状态组装（截断、压缩、重排序等）导致控制指令丢失或弱化，从而违反策略边界的问题。提出SafeContext控制层，在固定模型权重下固定控制状态，实验表明该风险系统存在且难以完全消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LM智能体并非直接作用于原始交互历史，而是通过截断、摘要、重排序和重写等操作构建一个有界的决策状态。若在此过程中携带指令的状态被丢弃、削弱或反弹，智能体可能在无需提示覆盖、模型更改或持久内存泄露的情况下跨越策略边界。现有研究尚未系统性地分析这一失效模式。

Method: 在Llama 3.1 8B、Qwen 2.5 7B和Mistral 7B上，通过判断精确约束遵循度和对组装状态可见性的直接审计来研究该失效模式。提出SafeContext控制层，其核心机制包括：固定控制状态（pins control state）、重用保留的控制前缀（reuses retained control prefixes），以及在压力下可选注入提醒（injects reminders under pressure），同时保持模型权重不变。

Result: 未缓解的风险是系统性的，但绝对的精确约束遵循度仍然较低。针对截断，SafeContext带来小幅提升；针对强结构化压缩策略，大部分聚合提升消失，残余收益主要体现在溢出驱逐和特定别名切片上。仅回放无法解释该效应。在Qwen 14B和Llama 70B上的扩展实验显示，更大模型下存在相同的失效对象，但符号和幅度仍取决于策略条件。

Conclusion: 决策时的上下文组装是控制路径中一个可测量的部分，可以部分硬化。但该风险难以完全消除，SafeContext只能带来有限的改进。

Abstract: LM agents do not act on raw interaction history; they act on a bounded decision state assembled by truncation, summarization, reordering, and rewriting. If directive-bearing state is dropped, weakened, or rebound during that step, an agent can cross a policy boundary without prompt override, model changes, or persistent-memory compromise. We study this failure mode over local Llama 3.1 8B, Qwen 2.5 7B, and Mistral 7B using judged exact constraint respect and direct audits of assembled-state visibility. We evaluate SafeContext, a control layer that pins control state, reuses retained control prefixes, and optionally injects reminders under pressure while keeping model weights fixed. Unmitigated risk is systematic, but absolute exact respect remains low. Against truncation, SafeContext yields small gains; against a strong structured-compaction policy, most aggregate lift disappears, leaving residual benefit mainly in overflow eviction and selected aliasing slices. Replay-only does not explain the effect. A larger-model extension on Qwen 14B and Llama 70B shows the same failure object under larger models, although sign and magnitude remain policy-conditional. Decision-time context assembly is therefore a measurable part of the control path that can be partially hardened.

</details>


### [8] [Persona-Conditioned Adversarial Prompting (PCAP): Multi-Identity Red-Teaming for Enhanced Adversarial Prompt Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12565)
*Cristian Morasso,Anisa Halimi,Muhammad Zaid Hameed,Douglas Leith*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 现有自动化红队测试管道常遗漏依赖攻击者身份、框架或多轮策略的攻击，导致低估真实风险。本文提出人物条件对抗提示（PCAP），通过将对抗搜索条件化为攻击者人物画像和策略卡，并运行并行人物条件波束搜索，发现多样且可迁移的越狱方法。PCAP与底层搜索算法正交，显著提升攻击成功率（如GPT-OSS~120B从~58%提升至~97%）和提示多样性，改善了攻击策略覆盖范围和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化红队测试方法无法有效捕获依赖攻击者身份、上下文框架或多轮交互策略的越狱攻击，导致对真实世界风险的评估不足。

Method: 提出Persona-Conditioned Adversarial Prompting (PCAP)，将对抗搜索条件化为攻击者人物画像和策略卡，通过并行的人物条件波束搜索来发现多样且可迁移的越狱方法。该方法与底层搜索算法正交。

Result: 攻击成功率显著提升（如GPT-OSS~120B从约58%提升至约97%），提示多样性和攻击策略覆盖率均有改善。

Conclusion: PCAP能有效发现被现有方法遗漏的依赖攻击者身份和策略的越狱攻击，更准确地评估大语言模型的安全风险。

Abstract: Existing automated red-teaming pipelines often miss attacks that depend on attacker identity, framing, or multi-turn tactics. This under-coverage underestimates real-world risk. We introduce Persona-Conditioned Adversarial Prompting (PCAP), which conditions adversarial search on attacker personas and strategy cards and runs parallel persona-conditioned beam searches to discover diverse, transferable jailbreaks. PCAP is orthogonal to the underlying search algorithm and substantially increases attack success rate (ASR) and prompt diversity (e.g., ASR on GPT-OSS~120B from $\approx58\% \rightarrow \approx97\%$), improving attack strategy coverage and diversity.

</details>


### [9] [Still Camouflage, Moving Illusion: View-Induced Trajectory Manipulation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12743)
*Shuo Ju,Qingzhao Zhang,Huashan Chen,Xuheng Wang,Haotang Li,Wanqian Zhang,Feng Liu,Kebin Peng,Sen He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型物理对抗攻击范式，将视角变化本身转化为攻击工具：通过在车辆上安装静态、被动的对抗性迷彩，其视点依赖的外观随相对运动自然演化，在连续帧间引发一致的特征漂移，使自动驾驶系统推断出物理上合理但错误的轨迹（如虚假切入），从而导致不必要的紧急制动。在nuScenes数据集上，端到端攻击成功率高达87.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理对抗攻击要么通过复杂物理补丁在进入视野时引起检测框漂移，要么通过动态变化补丁在不同时刻产生不同感知错误，均将视角变化视为挑战，需进行复杂的多视角优化。本文试图反转这一思路，利用视角变化本身作为攻击工具。

Method: 设计静态被动对抗迷彩，安装在目标车辆上，利用车辆相对运动导致的视角自然变化，使迷彩的外观在图像序列中随时间演化，从而在连续帧间诱导一致的特征漂移，使下游系统推断出虚假但物理可行的轨迹（如切入行为），进而触发不必要的紧急制动。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上实现端到端攻击成功率高达87.5%（以紧急制动事件为指标），且在不同场景背景、受害车辆速度和不同感知模型下均表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 相比以往需要多视角鲁棒性或主动干预的攻击方法，本攻击源于正常驾驶动力学，易于部署：一辆带有自然迷彩的停泊车辆即可导致经过的自动驾驶车辆紧急制动。

Abstract: Existing physical adversarial attacks on vision-based autonomous driving induce time-evolving perception errors, including biased object tracking or trajectory prediction, through (i) sophisticated physical patch inducing detection box drift when entering the view distance, or (ii) dynamically changing patches that cause different perception errors at different time. In both cases, viewing-angle variation is treated as a challenge, requiring adversarial patches to remain effective across frames under varying views, leading to complex multi-view optimization. In contrast, we show that viewing-angle variation itself can be turned into an attack tool. We design a new attack paradigm where a static, passive adversarial camouflage is mounted on a vehicle whose view-dependent appearance naturally evolves with relative motion, inducing consistent feature drift across frames. This causes the system to infer a physically plausible but incorrect trajectory, such as a false cut-in, which propagates to downstream decision-making and triggers unnecessary braking. Unlike prior approaches that require multi-view robustness or active intervention, our attack emerges from normal driving dynamics and is easy to deploy: a parked vehicle with a natural camouflage can induce hard braking in passing autonomous vehicles. We demonstrate the novel attack on nuScenes dataset, showing the effectiveness with an end-to-end success rate of up to 87.5%, measured by hard-braking events, and robustness across different scene backgrounds, victim vehicle speeds, and perception models.

</details>


### [10] [CoT-Guard: Small Models for Strong Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12746)
*Nirav Diwan,Han Wang,Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Ramin Moradi,Supriyo Chakraborty,Giri Iyengar,Sambit Sahu,Huan Zhang,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CoT-Guard，一个4B参数的小模型监控器，通过SFT+RL后训练流水线提升链式思维（CoT）推理中的隐藏目标检测能力，在代码生成任务中以低成本实现接近大模型的检测性能（G-mean²达75%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型（如GPT-5、Gemini-3-Flash）虽能有效监控CoT中的隐藏目标，但推理链长、API成本高；小模型（4B-8B）则难以准确区分隐藏目标和用户查询，亟需低成本且高效的小模型替代方案。

Method: 提出两阶段后训练流水线：先用SFT从强监控者（大模型）蒸馏检测行为，缩小域内任务差距；再用RL针对精心构造的硬样本进行训练，增强对域外监控任务的泛化能力。同时引入四种新的高难度任务以挑战现有强监控器。

Result: CoT-Guard（4B）在提示操控和代码操控两种攻击下，G-mean²达75%，优于GPT-5.4（56%）、GPT-5-mini（41%）、Qwen3-32B（54%），逼近Gemini-3-Flash（83%）。

Conclusion: CoT-Guard证明了小模型通过针对性后训练可以在隐藏目标检测任务上取得显著效果，提供了一种实用且经济的用户侧防御方案，大幅降低部署成本的同时保持良好的检测性能。

Abstract: Monitoring the chain-of-thought (CoT) of reasoning models is a promising approach for detecting covert misbehavior (i.e., hidden objectives) in code generation tasks. While large models (GPT-5, Gemini-3-Flash) can serve as effective CoT monitors, they are expensive to deploy due to the lengthy reasoning traces and high API cost, emphasizing the need for smaller, cheaper alternatives. Nevertheless, we find that current small models (4B--8B) struggle to detect hidden objectives despite access to the CoT, frequently misattributing them as part of the user query. To address this, we propose a post-training pipeline combining supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), where SFT narrows the gap for in-domain tasks by distilling detection behavior from stronger monitors, and RL on hard and subtly crafted hidden objectives helps the model generalize to out-of-domain monitoring tasks. To validate this generalization, we evaluate under a realistic threat model motivated by practical supply-chain attacks, where the adversary is a third-party LLM router injecting hidden objectives into code-generation requests through either prompt manipulation or code manipulation attacks. To push beyond objectives that large monitors already saturate, we also introduce four new challenging tasks even for strong monitors. Finally, we introduce CoT-Guard, a 4B-parameter monitor that demonstrates superior generalization performance under both prompt and code manipulation attacks, achieving a G-mean^2 (i.e., TNR x TPR) of 75% and outperforming GPT-5.4 (56%), GPT-5-mini (41%), and Qwen3-32B (54%), while closing the gap to Gemini-3-Flash (83%). These results demonstrate that CoT-Guard provides a practical and cost-effective user-side defense, substantially improving hidden-objective detection while avoiding the deployment cost of large monitors.

</details>


### [11] [GraphIP-Bench: How Hard Is It to Steal a Graph Neural Network, and Can We Stop It?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12827)
*Kaixiang Zhao,Bolin Shen,Yuyang Dai,Shayok Chakraborty,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphIP-Bench基准，统一评估GNN模型窃取攻击与防御，发现中等查询预算下窃取容易，多数防御效果有限，水印在提取后失效，异配图更难被窃取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作无法回答"窃取GNN有多难"和"能否阻止窃取"两个核心问题，因为实验使用不一致的数据集、威胁模型和评估指标，缺乏统一的比较框架。

Method: 构建GraphIP-Bench统一基准，整合12种窃取攻击、12种防御（水印、输出扰动、查询模式检测）、10个图数据集（同配/异配/大规模）、3种GNN骨干、3种图学习任务，在统一划分、查询和预算下评估保真度、任务效用、所有权验证和计算开销；并引入联合攻防赛道，评估防御在提取后的水印验证保留程度。

Result: 中等查询预算下窃取GNN容易，大多数防御无法显著改变这一情况；水印在受保护模型上验证良好，但在提取的替代模型上失去大部分验证信号；异配图系统性地更难窃取，跨架构不匹配会降低但不会阻止窃取。

Conclusion: 现有防御存在"单模型评估盲区"，水印在提取后失效暴露了防御评估方法的不足，提示未来需要更鲁棒的防御设计。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) deployed as cloud services can be \emph{stolen} through \emph{model-extraction attacks}, which train a surrogate from query responses to reproduce the target's behaviour, and a growing line of ownership defenses tries to prevent or trace such theft. The title of this paper asks two questions: \emph{how hard is it to steal a GNN?}, and \emph{can we stop it?} Prior work cannot answer either, because experiments use inconsistent datasets, threat models, and metrics. We introduce \emph{GraphIP-Bench}, a unified benchmark which evaluates both sides under a single black-box protocol. It integrates twelve extraction attacks, twelve defenses spanning watermarking, output-perturbation, and query-pattern-detection families, ten public graphs covering homophilic, heterophilic, and large-scale regimes, three GNN backbones, and three graph-learning tasks, and it reports fidelity, task utility, ownership verification, and computational cost on shared splits, queries, and budgets. We further add a joint attack-and-defense track which runs every attack on every defended target and measures watermark verification on the resulting surrogate, which exposes the protection that a defense retains after extraction. The empirical picture is short: stealing a GNN is easy at medium query budgets and most defenses do not change this; several watermarks verify reliably on the protected model but lose most of their verification signal on the extracted surrogate, which exposes a gap that single-model evaluations miss; and heterophilic graphs are systematically harder to steal, while a cross-architecture mismatch between target and surrogate reduces but does not prevent extraction. Code: \href{https://github.com/LabRAI/GraphIP-Bench}{LabRAI/GraphIP-Bench}.

</details>


### [12] [HE-PIM: Demystifying Homomorphic Operations on a Real-world Processing-in-Memory System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12841)
*Harshita Gupta,Mayank Kabra,Jaewoo Park,Priyam Mehta,Phillip Widdowson,Tathagata Barik,Nisa Bostancı,Konstantinos Kanellopoulos,Juan Gómez-Luna,Antonio J. Peña,Mohammad Sadrosadati,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在真实通用PIM系统（UPMEM）上全面实现了同态加密（HE）的内核操作，评估了性能与可扩展性，并与CPU/GPU对比。主要发现：HE操作存在计算密集和内存密集两种瓶颈；缺乏原生64位模整数乘法是主要计算瓶颈；有限的每bank内存容量是第二瓶颈；若配备原生模乘和高效PIM间数据移动，PIM可成为CPU/GPU的可行替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同态加密（HE）虽然能提供强隐私保障，但其高计算复杂度、大密文尺寸和大量数据移动限制了实际应用。传统处理器架构（CPU、GPU、ASIC）因密文大、数据局部性低、重线性化和自举等原语频繁访问大型辅助元数据而遇到根本性瓶颈。处理中内存（PIM）通过在内存附近或内部计算是一种有前景的缓解方案，但现有PIM方案要么不针对真实PIM系统，要么只覆盖狭窄的操作集。因此，本文旨在真实通用PIM系统上全面表征HE操作。

Method: 在UPMEM通用PIM系统上实现了新兴应用（数据库、机器学习）所需的完整HE内核集合，评估性能和可扩展性，并与CPU和GPU基线对比，讨论对未来PIM硬件的启示。

Result: 四大发现：（1）HE应用在不同执行阶段表现出不同瓶颈——部分内核因模算术而计算密集，部分因大密文和中间数据而内存密集，有限的每核计算能力和每bank容量加剧了这些瓶颈；（2）主要计算瓶颈是缺乏原生64位模整数乘法；（3）第二主要瓶颈是有限的每bank内存容量，HE密文和辅助元数据无法容纳，需要跨bank移动；（4）尽管有这些限制，配备原生模乘和高效PIM间数据移动后，PIM可成为CPU/GPU的可行替代方案。

Conclusion: PIM有潜力成为HE工作负载的可行计算平台，但需要硬件支持原生64位模乘和改进的PIM间数据移动机制。本文为未来PIM硬件设计提供了具体指导方向。

Abstract: Homomorphic encryption (HE) enables computation over encrypted data, offering strong privacy guarantees for untrusted computing environments. Practical adoption remains limited by high computational complexity, large ciphertext sizes, and substantial data movement. Processor-centric architectures (CPUs, GPUs, ASICs) hit fundamental bottlenecks on HE workloads because ciphertexts are large, data locality is low, and primitives such as relinearization and bootstrapping repeatedly access large auxiliary metadata. Processing-In-Memory (PIM) is a promising mitigation by computing near or inside memory. Prior PIM proposals for HE either do not target real-world PIM systems or cover only a narrow set of operations.
  We comprehensively characterize HE operations on a real-world, general-purpose PIM system. We implement a complete set of HE kernels used by emerging applications (databases, machine learning) on the UPMEM PIM system, evaluate performance and scalability, compare against CPU and GPU baselines, and discuss implications for future PIM hardware. Our results demonstrate four major findings. (1) HE-based applications expose distinct bottlenecks across execution stages: some kernels are compute-bound due to modular arithmetic, while others are memory-bound due to large ciphertexts and intermediate data. These bottlenecks are exacerbated by limited per-core compute and per-bank capacity, which force frequent data movement. (2) The dominant compute bottleneck is the lack of native 64-bit modular integer multiplication, a key HE primitive. (3) Limited per-bank memory capacity is the second major bottleneck, since HE ciphertexts and auxiliary metadata do not fit and require inter-bank movement. (4) Despite these limits, PIM can be a viable alternative to state-of-the-art CPU and GPU systems for HE when equipped with native modular multiplication and efficient inter-PIM data movement.

</details>


### [13] [Quantifying LLM Safety Degradation Under Repeated Attacks Using Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12869)
*Zvi Topol*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生存分析（Survival Analysis）的LLM越狱攻击评估框架，通过建模“越狱时间”来替代传统二分类指标，能够估计风险函数、生存曲线及风险因素，揭示不同模型在持续攻击下的动态脆弱性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱评估框架仅报告成功/失败的二元指标，无法捕捉攻击在持续对抗压力下成功的时间动态特性，缺乏对模型脆弱性时变特征的刻画。

Method: 将越狱成功视为生存结局（time-to-jailbreak），应用生存分析方法估计风险函数（hazard function）、生存曲线和风险因素；在HarmBench数据集的三个攻击类别上评估三个LLM。

Result: 不同模型呈现不同的脆弱性曲线：一个模型在迭代攻击下快速退化，另两个模型表现出稳定的中等脆弱性。

Conclusion: 该框架为模型和应用开发者提供了可操作的见解，将生存分析确立为LLM安全评估的严谨方法论。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in a wide range of applications, yet remain vulnerable to adversarial jailbreak attacks that circumvent their safety guardrails. Existing evaluation frameworks typically report binary success/failure metrics, failing to capture the temporal dynamics of how attacks succeed under persistent adversarial pressure. This preliminary work proposes a novel evaluation framework that applies survival analysis techniques to characterize LLM jailbreak vuln`erability. Our approach models the time-to-jailbreak as a survival outcome, enabling estimation of hazard functions, survival curves, and risk factors associated with successful attacks. We evaluate three LLMs against a subset of prompts from the HarmBench dataset spanning three attack categories. Our analysis reveals that models exhibit distinct vulnerability profiles: while one model demonstrates rapid degradation under iterative attacks, the two other models show consistent moderate vulnerability. Our framework provides actionable insights for model and LLM application developers and establishes survival analysis as a rigorous methodology for LLM safety evaluation.

</details>


### [14] [From Compression to Accountability: Harmless Copyright Protection for Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12942)
*Yan Liang,Ziyuan Yang,Mengyu Sun,Joey Tianyi Zhou,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SubPopMark，一种基于子种群驱动的无害保护框架，用于蒸馏数据集的知识产权保护，通过注入子种群偏差实现版权验证和用户溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒸馏数据集易于复制和分发，存在严重的版权侵权和数据泄露风险，现有保护方法主要针对原始数据集且依赖后门触发恶意行为，存在安全隐患。

Method: 两阶段优化：1) CVM优化阶段注入类别一致的子种群偏差，保留原始优化轨迹；2) USTM优化阶段引入用户可区分的扰动。构建参考行为库，通过比较模型输出行为签名实现黑盒验证与溯源。

Result: 该方法能够在不引入后门恶意行为的前提下，有效保护蒸馏数据集版权，并实现用户级别的可追溯性。

Conclusion: SubPopMark为蒸馏数据集提供了一种无害、可验证、可溯源的版权保护框架，解决了传统方法的安全隐患问题。

Abstract: Large-scale datasets have been a key driving force behind the rapid progress of deep learning, but their storage, computational, and energy costs have become increasingly prohibitive. Dataset distillation (DD) mitigates this problem by synthesizing compact yet informative datasets, thereby enabling efficient model training and storage. However, the ease of copying and distributing distilled datasets introduces serious risks of copyright infringement and data leakage. Existing protection methods are primarily designed for raw datasets rather than distilled datasets, and typically rely on backdoor-triggered malicious behaviors, which may raise security concerns. In this paper, we observe that deep neural networks tend to memorize subpopulation distributions during training, resulting in a systematic prediction bias, where models perform better on samples aligned with memorized subpopulations. Motivated by this observation, we propose SubPopMark, a harmless subpopulation-driven protection framework for distilled datasets. SubPopMark consists of two stages. First, the Copyright Verification Marker(CVM) optimization stage injects a class-consistent subpopulation bias while preserving the original optimization trajectory. Second, the User-Specific Tracing Marker (USTM) optimization stage further introduces user-distinguishable perturbations into the CVM-augmented data. To enable black-box verification and tracing, we construct a reference behavior bank by collecting model outputs over carefully designed test sets that cover both standard and subpopulation-shifted data distributions. The provenance of a suspicious model is then inferred by comparing its output behavior signature with the bank and identifying the most consistent reference behavior pattern.

</details>


### [15] [CLOUDBURST: Cloud-Layer Observations Using Beacons for Unified Real-time Surveillance and Threat Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12976)
*Abraham Itzhak Weinberg*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CLOUDBURST，首个云原生被动信标分类与测量框架，涵盖六大向量类及四个云提供商，并引入云归因分数（CAS）量化归因质量。实验表明，IAM Canary Roles和S3 Presigned URLs在检测抵抗性上表现优异，而临时基础设施流失会显著降低CAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蜜标和信标框架无法应对云原生环境中的新型数据窃取威胁（如S3预签名URL、容器镜像、Kubernetes密钥、Terraform状态模块、IAM角色令牌等），亟需针对云原生环境的被动信标分类与评估体系。

Method: 提出CLOUDBURST框架，定义六大向量类；引入云归因分数（CAS），包含临时基础设施惩罚（Ep）、IAM覆盖深度（Ic）和多云关联奖励（Mb）四个指标；部署21个信标、模拟205次回调和三种攻击者能力水平进行实验评估。

Result: IAM Canary Roles获得最高CAS（均值0.450）和检测抵抗性（DR=0.873）；S3 Presigned URLs最高检测抵抗性（DR=0.890）；临时基础设施流失导致CAS从约0.79降至0.18-0.22；Serverless Function Triggers检测抵抗性最差（DR=0.611）；云提供商间无显著CAS差异。

Conclusion: CLOUDBURST提供首个云原生被动信标分类与量化归因质量框架，IAM Canary Roles和S3 Presigned URLs是最具部署价值的向量，未来需设计隐蔽回调通道以改善Serverless Function Triggers的检测抵抗性。

Abstract: Modern cloud-native environments present a fundamentally different exfiltration threat surface than traditional file-based scenarios. Attackers targeting AWS, GCP, Azure, and OCI steal S3 presigned URLs, container images, Kubernetes secrets, Terraform state modules, and IAM role tokens -- artefacts that existing honeytoken and beacon frameworks do not address. We present \textbf{CLOUDBURST}, the first formal taxonomy and measurement framework for cloud-native passive beacons, comprising six vector classes across four major cloud providers.
  We introduce the \textit{Cloud Attribution Score} (CAS), a four-component metric that explicitly models ephemeral infrastructure penalty ($E_p$), IAM coverage depth ($I_c$), and multi-cloud correlation bonus ($M_b$) -- dimensions absent from all prior attribution quality metrics. Experiments across $21$ deployed beacons, $205$ simulated callbacks, and three attacker sophistication levels yield four principal findings. First, IAM Canary Roles achieve the highest CAS (mean $0.450$) and Detection Resistance (DR $= 0.873$), making them the most deployable vector. Second, S3 Presigned URLs achieve the highest detection resistance (DR $= 0.890$), surviving all three cloud-native scanner models (AWS Macie, Checkov/tfsec, Prisma Cloud/Wiz). Third, ephemeral infrastructure churn degrades CAS from $\approx 0.79$ at deployment to $\approx 0.18$--$0.22$ at $48$ hours for all vectors ($p < 0.001$), establishing the first quantitative model of attribution decay in containerised environments. Fourth, Serverless Function Triggers exhibit the worst detection resistance (DR $= 0.611$) due to their explicit outbound HTTP callback pattern, motivating covert callback channel design as future work. No significant CAS difference is observed across cloud providers ($H = 1.99$, $p = 0.57$), confirming that CLOUDBURST is provider-agnostic in its effectiveness.

</details>


### [16] [Insecure Despite Proven Updated: Extracting the Root VCEK Seed on EPYC Milan via a Software-Only Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12990)
*Muyan Shen,Yu Qin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭露AMD EPYC Milan处理器中SEV-SNP安全架构的严重漏洞，通过MilanLaunchy攻击实现安全处理器代码执行，再利用BadFuse攻击提取硬件根密钥，最终可伪造任意固件版本的证明报告，彻底破坏TCB回滚攻击防护机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMD声称SEV-SNP能够防止TCB回滚攻击，其安全保障依赖于硬件根密钥的安全性。作者旨在验证这一安全假设是否成立，揭示EPYC Milan处理器中的真实安全漏洞。

Method: 提出两步攻击链：(1) MilanLaunchy攻击，通过软件仅有的方式在AMD安全处理器上实现代码执行；(2) BadFuse攻击，利用熔丝控制器缺少写入限制的缺陷，提取硬件根密钥种子。

Result: 成功实现端到端攻击链，可提取硬件根密钥，进而为任意固件版本伪造有效的证明报告，彻底破坏SEV-SNP的安全模型。

Conclusion: AMD EPYC Milan中硬件根密钥的保护机制存在严重不足，SEV-SNP的TCB回滚防护承诺无法得到有效保障，需要在硬件层面加强熔丝控制器的访问控制。

Abstract: In the official whitepaper of Secure Encrypted Virtualization with Secure Nested Paging (SEV-SNP), AMD explicitly emphasizes the capability to prevent Trusted Computing Base (TCB) rollback attacks. Cryptographically, this is realized by signing attestation reports with the Versioned Chip Endorsement Key (VCEK), which is derived by incorporating the TCB version into the hardware root seed.
  In this architecture, safeguarding the hardware root seed is the ultimate line of defense. However, our research reveals that this protection is insufficient on EPYC Milan by presenting a software-only exploit. Specifically, we firstly introduce MilanLaunchy attack, an exploit that achieves code execution on the AMD secure processor. Building on this foundation, we develop the BadFuse attack, which extracts the hardware root seed by exploiting a lack of write restrictions in the fuse controller. This end-to-end attack chain enables an adversary to forge valid attestation reports for any firmware version, thereby effectively undermining the security model of SEV-SNP.

</details>


### [17] [Identifying AI Web Scrapers Using Canary Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13706)
*Steven Seiden,Triss Ren,Caroline Zhang,Taein Kim,Enze Liu,Emily Wenger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过金丝雀令牌（canary tokens）自动推断LLM相关爬虫的新技术，通过在动态网站上为每个爬虫分配唯一令牌，然后提示LLM生成包含这些令牌的内容，从而识别哪些爬虫为哪些LLM提供数据。实验在22个生产级LLM系统上进行，成功识别出多个未被公开披露的爬虫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有识别LLM相关爬虫的方法（如企业自愿披露、研究者单次实验、众包报告）既不可靠也不可扩展，网站所有者缺乏有效的技术手段来识别和限制这些爬虫对网站资源的抓取。

Method: 搭建动态网站，为每个访问的爬虫提供唯一的金丝雀令牌；然后向LLM查询关于这些网站的信息；如果LLM生成的输出中持续包含特定爬虫对应的令牌，则证明该爬虫的数据被该LLM使用。

Result: 在22个生产级LLM系统上的实验表明，该方法能够可靠地识别哪些爬虫为哪些LLM提供数据，包括多个未被公司公开披露的爬虫实例。

Conclusion: 该方法为无特权的第三方提供了一种有前景的手段来推断爬虫与LLM之间的数据供给关系，有望帮助网站所有者更好地控制不希望的爬虫抓取行为。

Abstract: From pre-training to query-time augmentation, web-scraped data helps to improve the quality and contextual relevancy of content generated by large language models (LLMs). However, large-scale web scraping to feed LLMs can affect site stability and raise legal, privacy, or ethics concerns. If website owners wish to limit LLM-related web scraping on their site, due to these or other concerns, they may turn to scraper access control mechanisms like the Robots Exclusion Protocol. To be most effective, such mechanisms require site owners to first identify the scrapers that they wish to restrict (e.g., via User-Agent strings). Existing mechanisms to identify LLM-related scrapers rely on voluntary disclosure by companies, one-off experiments by researchers, or crowd-sourced reports -- methods that are neither reliable nor scalable. This paper proposes a novel technique for accurately and automatically inferring LLM-related scrapers. We host dynamic websites that serve unique canary tokens to each visiting scraper, then prompt LLMs for information about our sites. If an LLM consistently generates outputs containing tokens unique to a scraper, it provides evidence of exposure to that scraper. Via experiments across 22 production LLM systems, we demonstrate that our approach can reliably identify which scrapers feed which LLM, including several that are not publicly known or disclosed by the companies. Our approach provides a promising avenue for unprivileged third parties to infer which scrapers serve data to which LLMs, potentially enabling better control over unwanted scraping.

</details>


### [18] [No Attack Required: Semantic Fuzzing for Specification Violations in Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13044)
*Ying Li,Hongbo Wen,Yanju Chen,Hanzhi Liu,Yuan Tian,Yu Feng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了规范违背（specification violation）问题：LLM智能体在执行用户正常请求时，因调用的技能违反自身规定的自然语言护栏（guardrails）而产生安全漏洞。作者开发了Sefz框架，一种目标导向的语义模糊测试工具，通过将护栏转化为可达性目标、利用LLM变异器生成良性输入、结合多臂老虎机算法引导搜索，自动发现技能中的规范违背。在402个真实技能中发现120个（29.9%）存在规范违背，包括26个先前未知的可利用漏洞，并归纳出六类反复出现的规范设计缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体技能中的自然语言护栏在自主执行时语义未定义或实现中静默忽略约束，导致良性输入也能触发违规行为（如删除文档、泄露凭证、转移资金），这些漏洞无法被静态分析、传统模糊测试或提示注入防御检测，破坏了用户对技能的安全信任。

Method: Sefz框架：1）将每条护栏翻译为带注释的执行轨迹上的可达性目标，将违规检测转化为确定性图查询；2）基于LLM的变异器生成良性输入，其轨迹逐步接近违规模式；3）采用多臂老虎机算法，以目标接近度作为奖励信号引导搜索方向。

Result: 在最大公开智能体技能市场的402个真实技能中，Sefz发现120个（29.9%）存在规范违背，其中26个为先前未知的可利用护栏漏洞。

Conclusion: 规范违背是LLM智能体安全中的系统性漏洞类别，六类反复出现的规范设计缺陷揭示了更安全的技能设计原则。

Abstract: LLM-powered agents can silently delete documents, leak credentials, or transfer funds on a routine user request, not because the agent was attacked, but because the skill it invoked broke its own declared safety rules. We call these specification violations: benign inputs cause a skill to breach the natural-language guardrails in its own specification, typically because the guardrail's semantics are undefined for autonomous execution, or because the implementation silently ignores the documented constraint. These violations are invisible to static analyzers, traditional fuzzers, and prompt-injection defenses alike, yet they undermine the very contract a user trusts when installing a skill.
  We present Sefz, a goal-directed semantic fuzzing framework that automatically discovers specification violations in agent skills. Sefz translates each guardrail into a reachability goal over an annotated execution trace, reducing violation checking to a deterministic graph query. An LLM-based mutator generates benign inputs whose traces progressively approach the violation patterns, guided by a multi-armed bandit that uses goal-proximity as its reward signal.
  On 402 real-world skills from the largest public agent-skill marketplace, Sefz finds specification violations in 120 (29.9%), including 26 previously unknown exploitable guardrail violations in deployed skills. Six recurring specification pitfalls explain the bulk of the failures, suggesting concrete principles for safer skill design.

</details>


### [19] [Watermarking Should Be Treated as a Monitoring Primitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13095)
*Toluwani Aremu,Nils Lukas,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出水印应被视为一种监控原语，并引入基于观察者的威胁模型，证明观察者可通过聚合输出来推断实体级信息，揭示了水印技术在归因与监控之间的双重用途张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印评估仅关注单样本层面的对抗鲁棒性，忽略了水印作为监控原语时，观察者可通过聚合信号推断实体级信息的风险。

Method: 引入基于观察者的威胁模型，形式化分析观察者如何通过跨输出聚合水印信号来推断实体级信息，并探讨外部监控随时间的涌现机制。

Result: 零比特水印在多密钥设置下也能实现归因；外部监控可从持久性密钥相关统计结构中涌现；分布保持或不可检测方案可缓解该问题。

Conclusion: 水印评估需超越单样本鲁棒性，考虑聚合和基于观察者的能力，以应对归因与监控之间的双重用途张力。

Abstract: Watermarking is widely proposed for provenance, attribution, and safety monitoring in generative models, yet is typically evaluated only under adversaries who attempt to evade detection or induce false positives at the level of individual samples. We argue that watermarking should be treated as a monitoring primitive, and that internal monitoring is unavoidable given per-entity attribution keys and messages, as well as detector access. We introduce an observer-based threat model in which observers can aggregate watermark signals across outputs to infer entity-level information, showing that even zero-bit watermarking enables attribution under multi-key settings. We further show that external monitoring can emerge over time from persistent, key-dependent statistical structure, although this depends on watermark design and may be mitigated by distribution-preserving or undetectable schemes. Our findings reveal a fundamental dual-use tension between attribution and monitoring, motivating evaluation of watermarking beyond per-sample robustness to account for aggregation and observer-based capabilities.

</details>


### [20] [Security Incentivization: An Empirical Study of how Micropayments Impact Code Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13100)
*Stefan Rass,Martin Pinzger,Rainer W. Alexandrowicz,Georg Sengstbratl,Johann Glock,Alexander Lercher,Fabian Oraze,Christoph Wedenig*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过控制实验（84名学生/14支团队）验证了将安全激励（奖金与静态扫描结果挂钩）与团队奖励挂钩机制的有效性：实验组的安全问题密度显著低于对照组（β=-0.396, p=0.0342），且前端/后端存在异质性效应，后端改进更明显。该机制通过半自动化管道（Bearer, Detekt, mobsfscan）实现可脚本化、可重复的规模化报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全开发因难以直接产生可见价值而常被开发者忽视，实践中投入不足。作者希望探索一种通过团队级激励与可量化的安全改进挂钩的干预措施，以提升代码安全性。

Method: 设计半自动化测量管道，聚合Bearer、Detekt、mobsfscan的静态分析结果，计算安全问题密度（security issue density），并根据跨冲刺的相对改进比率奖励团队。在课程实验中设置安全激励组（奖金与扫描结果挂钩）和对照组（评分机制相同但无安全激励），使用beta回归分析组间差异，并控制平台（前端/后端）因素。

Result: 激励组安全问题密度显著低于对照组（β=-0.396, p=0.0342）；后端在激励下问题更少、改进比率更高，存在前端/后端异质性；两组代码行数增长相似，排除代码膨胀假象；测量管道具备脚本化和自动化可行性。

Conclusion: 将奖励与自动化安全指标对齐可显著提升代码安全性，值得在专业环境及更长开发生命周期中进一步验证。

Abstract: Security often receives insufficient developer attention because it does not directly generate visible value, leading to underinvestment in practice. We evaluate a countermeasure by team-level incentives tied to measurable security improvements over time. Our semi-automated mechanism aggregates static analysis findings from Bearer, Detekt, and mobsfscan, computes security issue density, and rewards teams based on the relative improvement ratio across sprints, enabling repeatable, scriptable reporting at scale.
  In a controlled course experiment with 84 students across 14 teams, we compared a security-incentivized condition, in which bonus points were linked to security scanner results, against a control condition with an otherwise identical grading scheme. The treatment group achieved significantly lower security issue density overall (beta regression: $β= -0.396, p = 0.0342$), indicating improved measurable security under incentivization. After controlling for platform, we observed a marked front-end/back-end disparity, with back-ends showing fewer issues and higher improvement ratios under incentives, highlighting heterogeneous effects across stack layers. Notably, these gains were not the byproduct of inflated code volume, as lines of code increased similarly across groups over time. The measurement pipeline and toolchain proved feasible for scripting and automation, supporting scalable adoption in practice.
  Our results suggest that aligning rewards with automated security metrics can measurably improve code security and merit follow-up in professional contexts and longer development lifecycles.

</details>


### [21] [Extending Blockchain Untraceability with Plausible Deniability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13132)
*Eunchan Park,Kyonghwa Song,Won Hoi Kim,Wonho Song,Min Suk Kang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出可否认隐蔽资产转移（DCAT），通过将代币转移伪装成常见的DeFi损失事件（如三明治攻击、套利操作），实现传输事件的不可观测性。在以太坊和Arbitrum上验证了两种DCAT实例，证明其与普通MEV活动在语法上不可区分，并开发了基于多变量统计的法医分流方法以识别可疑案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链不可追踪方案（如混币器、隐私币）仅隐藏发送者-接收者关系，但传输事件本身仍可被观测。本文追求更强的目标：通过将传输融入常见的DeFi活动，使传输事件本身变得不可观测。

Method: 提出DCAT概念，设计两种实例化方案：基于三明治攻击的以太坊转移和基于套利操作的Arbitrum转移。通过实验验证其与MEV活动在语法上的不可区分性。对MEV损失进行大规模幂律分布分析，并开发多变量统计法医分流方法对经济足迹的联合稀有性进行排序。

Result: DCAT转移在两种链上经验上不可观测，与MEV活动语法相同，标准MEV检测工具将其分类为普通提取，代表性法医工具无法链接发送者和接收者。MEV损失的经济语义特征遵循幂律分布，极端损失和频繁被利用地址在真实世界中存在，固定阈值检测易产生误报。多变量统计方法成功缩小了搜索空间。

Conclusion: DCAT实现了比传统混币技术更强的不可观测性，通过伪装成MEV活动获得可否认性。由于语法不可区分且经济语义存在长尾分布，需要依赖多变量统计法医分流进行优先级排序，而非固定阈值检测。

Abstract: Traditional blockchain untraceability schemes, such as mixers and privacy coins, obscure the sender-receiver relationship by placing transfers within an anonymity set. This paper studies a stronger goal: whether the transfer event itself can be made unobservable by blending into common decentralized-finance (DeFi) activity. We introduce Deniable Covert Asset Transfer (DCAT), a class of transfers that stage common loss-producing events, such as sandwich and arbitrage operations, so that a sender appears to suffer an ordinary loss while the receiver appears to profit from it. We design and validate two DCAT instantiations: a sandwich-based transfer on Ethereum and an arbitrage-based transfer on Arbitrum. Our experiments show that, under the evaluated settings, DCAT transfers are empirically unobservable on both chains. They are syntactically identical to corresponding maximal extractable value (MEV) activities, classified as ordinary extractions by standard MEV detection tools, and leave the sender and receiver unlinked under representative forensic tools. Since syntactic inspection cannot distinguish DCAT from ordinary MEV activity, we examine whether economic semantics provide useful forensic signals. Through a large-scale study of MEV losses on Ethereum and Arbitrum, we show that key semantic features follow power laws. Extreme losses and repeatedly exploited addresses occur in the wild, and thus are not by themselves definitive evidence of collusion. This gives staged transfers plausible deniability and makes fixed-threshold detection prone to false positives. We therefore develop a multivariate statistical method for forensic triage that ranks incidents by the joint rarity of their economic footprint. Applied to real-world DeFi activity, our method narrows a large search space to suspicious cases for manual investigation; we present three such cases to illustrate this prioritization.

</details>


### [22] [LoREnc: Low-Rank Encryption for Securing Foundation Models and LoRA Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13163)
*Beomjin Ahn,Jungmin Kwon,Chanyong Jung,Jaewook Chung*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LoREnc是一个无需训练的安全框架，通过谱截断与补偿机制保护基础模型和低秩适配器，在<1%计算开销下有效防御模型恢复攻击，授权用户可恢复精确性能，未授权用户输出结构性崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法需要重训练或访问原始数据集，缺乏实用性。需要一种无需训练、即插即用的保护方案，防止设备端生成式AI中的知识产权泄露和模型恢复攻击。

Method: LoREnc采用三阶段保护：1) 对基础模型权重进行谱截断，抑制主导低秩分量；2) 在授权适配器中补偿丢失信息；3) 对保护后的适配器应用正交重参数化，掩盖结构指纹。

Result: 在<1%计算开销下提供强模型恢复保护，授权用户可精确恢复性能，未授权用户输出结构性崩溃。

Conclusion: LoREnc是一种训练免费的实用框架，可有效平衡设备端生成式AI的安全保护与性能恢复，为知识产权保护提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Foundation models and low-rank adapters enable efficient on-device generative AI but raise risks such as intellectual property leakage and model recovery attacks. Existing defenses are often impractical because they require retraining or access to the original dataset. We propose LoREnc, a training-free framework that secures both FMs and adapters via spectral truncation and compensation. LoREnc suppresses dominant low-rank components of FM weights, compensates for the missing information in authorized adapters, and further applies orthogonal reparameterization to obscure structural fingerprints of the protected adapter. Unauthorized users produce structurally collapsed outputs, while authorized users recover exact performance. Experiments demonstrate that LoREnc provides strong protection against model recovery with under 1% computational overhead.

</details>


### [23] [Automatic Detection of Reference Counting Bugs in Linux Kernel Drivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13246)
*Joe Hattori,Naoki Kobayashi,Ken Sakayori*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DrvHorn是一个自动化工具，通过将引用计数验证转化为断言检查问题，在Linux v6.6内核驱动中发现了545个引用计数bug（其中424个为未知），误报率仅29.9%，已有45个补丁被合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Linux内核驱动中的引用计数bug会导致严重的资源管理错误和安全漏洞，亟需自动化检测工具来解决这一问题。

Method: 将引用计数验证归约为断言检查问题，利用Linux驱动接口进行高效内核建模，结合激进程序切片技术实现自动化检测。

Result: 在v6.6 Linux内核所有平台驱动中发现545个bug，其中424个为之前未知；误报率29.9%，低于以往研究；45个补丁已被内核社区合并。

Conclusion: DrvHorn能够有效检测Linux内核驱动中的引用计数bug，具有低误报率和实际修复价值，对提升内核安全性有显著贡献。

Abstract: Reference counting bugs in Linux kernel drivers can lead to severe resource mismanagement and security vulnerabilities. We introduce DrvHorn, a novel automated tool to detect these bugs by reducing reference counting verification to an assertion checking problem leveraging the Linux driver interface. Through efficient modeling of the Linux kernel and aggressive program slicing, DrvHorn discovered 545 bugs, of which 424 were previously unknown, across all platform drivers in v6.6 Linux kernel, with a lower false positive rate of 29.9% compared to prior studies. To address the root causes of these newly discovered bugs, we submitted patches to the Linux kernel, and 45 of them were merged.

</details>


### [24] [Context-Aware Web Attack Detection in Open-Source SIEM Systems via MITRE ATT&CK-Enriched Behavioral Profiling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13337)
*Badr Alboushy,Assef Jafar,Mohamad Aljnidi,Mohamad Bashar Disoki,Aref Shaheed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 Smart-SIEM，一个集成于 Wazuh 开源 SIEM 平台的 AI 模块，通过构建基于源 IP 的行为上下文特征向量（包括 HTTP 响应状态分布、规则触发计数、MITRE ATT&CK 技术频率），并采用 LightGBM 与 XGBoost 两级混合级联模型（二分类+六分类），在多步 Web 应用攻击检测中显著优于传统规则引擎，宏 F1 提升至 0.967（二分类）和 0.914（六分类），且具备概念漂移自适应恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则的 SIEM 关联引擎在处理多步 Web 应用攻击时存在局限——每条事件独立检测，缺乏对源主机行为历史的参考，导致难以准确分类复杂攻击序列。

Method: (1) 构建每个源 IP 的行为上下文向量，编码最近 N 个事件的 HTTP 响应状态分布、规则峰值触发计数及 MITRE ATT&CK 技术频率；(2) 两阶段混合级联：第一阶段用 LightGBM 进行二分类攻击检测，第二阶段用 XGBoost 进行六分类攻击类型识别。

Result: 在 46,454 个 Wazuh 安全事件上，上下文特征使梯度提升算法的宏 F1 从约 0.705 提升至 0.947-0.967（第一阶段）和 0.876-0.914（第二阶段），平均提升 +0.254 和 +0.324。混合级联 F1 达 0.967（二分类）和 0.914（六分类）。传统规则引擎对暴力破解和认证破坏事件检出率为 0%，AI 模块分别达 100% 和 98.3%。自适应重训练机制可从概念漂移中恢复：F1 从 0.905 降至 0.465 后恢复至 0.814。

Conclusion: 基于行为上下文向量的混合级联 AI 模型可有效弥补传统 SIEM 规则引擎在多步 Web 攻击检测中的不足，并具备对新兴攻击模式的自适应能力。

Abstract: Security Information and Event Management (SIEM) systems aggregate log data from heterogeneous sources to detect coordinated attacks. Traditional rule-based correlation engines struggle to classify multi-step web application attacks because they examine each event without reference to the behavioural history of the originating host.
  We present Smart-SIEM, an AI module for the open-source Wazuh SIEM platform with two contributions: (1) a per-source-IP behavioural context vector encoding HTTP response-status distributions, peak rule activation counts, and MITRE ATT&CK technique frequencies from the N most recent prior events; (2) a two-stage hybrid cascade combining LightGBM for binary attack detection and XGBoost for six-class attack categorisation.
  Evaluated on 46,454 purpose-built Wazuh security events, context features improve all tested gradient boosting algorithms from ~0.705 macro F1 to 0.947-0.967 (Stage 1) and 0.876-0.914 (Stage 2), an average gain of +0.254 and +0.324 respectively. The hybrid cascade achieves F1 of 0.967 (binary) and 0.914 (six-class). Wazuh's native rule engine detects 0% of Brute Force and Broken Authentication events; the AI module detects 100% and 98.3% respectively. A self-adaptive retraining mechanism recovers from concept drift: F1 drops from 0.905 to 0.465 when unseen attack types emerge, recovering to 0.814 after retraining on the combined corpus.

</details>


### [25] [Model-Agnostic Lifelong LLM Safety via Externalized Attack-Defense Co-Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13411)
*Xiaozhe Zhang,Chaozhuo Li,Hui Liu,Shaocheng Yan,Bingyu Yan,Qiwei Ye,Haoliang Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EvoSafety提出了一种基于持久化、可检查、可重用的外部结构的LLM安全框架，通过对抗性技能库实现持续漏洞探测，并采用轻量级辅助防御模型配合记忆检索，实现高效、可迁移的模型无关安全改进，在Guard模式下达到99.61%的防御成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全范式将红队攻击与后训练耦合在封闭、以策略为中心的循环中，导致攻击发现快速饱和且难以暴露新故障模式，同时防御方法效率低、僵化且难以跨模型迁移。

Method: EvoSafety包含两个核心组件：(1) 红队攻击方面，为攻击策略配备对抗性技能库，通过简单扩展库实现持续漏洞探测；(2) 防御学习方面，用配备记忆检索的轻量级辅助防御模型替代模型特定的安全微调，支持Steer（激活受害模型内在防御）和Guard（直接过滤有害输入）两种模式。

Result: 在Guard模式下达到99.61%防御成功率，比Qwen3Guard-8B高出14.13%，参数量仅为其37.5%，同时在良性查询上保持推理性能。

Conclusion: EvoSafety通过外部化安全机制，实现了更高效、可迁移的模型无关安全改进，可通过单纯更新记忆来增强鲁棒性，为LLM安全提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Large language models remain vulnerable to adversarial prompts that elicit harmful outputs. Existing safety paradigms typically couple red-teaming and post-training in a closed, policy-centric loop, causing attack discovery to suffer from rapid saturation and limiting the exposure of novel failure modes, while leaving defenses inefficient, rigid, and difficult to transfer across victim models. To this end, we propose EvoSafety, an LLM safety framework built around persistent, inspectable, and reusable external structures. For red teaming, EvoSafety equips the attack policy with an adversarial skill library, enabling continued vulnerability probing through simple library expansion after saturation, while supporting the evolution of adversarial vectors. For defense learning, EvoSafety replaces model-specific safety fine-tuning with a lightweight auxiliary defense model augmented with memory retrieval. This enables efficient, transferable, and model-agnostic safety improvements, while allowing robustness to be enhanced solely through memory updates. With a single training procedure, the defense policy can operate in both Steer and Guard modes: the former activates the victim model's intrinsic defense mechanisms, while the latter directly filters harmful inputs. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of EvoSafety: in Guard mode, it achieves a 99.61% defense success rate, outperforming Qwen3Guard-8B by 14.13% with only 37.5% of its parameters, while preserving reasoning performance on benign queries. Warning: This paper contains potentially harmful text.

</details>

Method: 针对霍尔效应指尖触觉传感器，通过定向信号注入实施电磁干扰（EMI）攻击，评估对感知力大小和方向的影响，并测试其对基于学习的触觉分类模型的破坏效果。

Result: 干扰信号可使感知力放大约9倍、方向偏差达65°，足以瘫痪触觉分类模型，严重影响机器人运动控制。

Conclusion: 触觉传感器存在电磁干扰安全漏洞，攻击者可利用该漏洞操纵机器人行为（如压碎物体或掉落危险物品），亟需对触觉通道的安全性进行防护研究。

Abstract: Embodied intelligent robots rely on tactile sensors to interact with the physical world safely. While the security of visual perception systems has been studied (e.g., adversarial samples), the integrity of the tactile sensory channel remains unexplored. This work explores a vulnerability in Hall-effect fingertip sensors, showing their susceptibility to intentional Electromagnetic Interference (EMI). We demonstrate that a targeted signal injection can induce strong ``phantom forces'', amplifying perceived force magnitude by over \textbf{9$\times$} and deviating the inferred force direction by \textbf{65$^\circ$}. Such perturbations can paralyze learning-based tactile classification models, seriously affecting robot movement. An attacker could exploit this vulnerability to coerce a robot hand into crushing fragile objects or dropping dangerous payloads.

</details>


### [27] [EBCC: Enclave-Backed Confidential Containers via OCI-Compatible Runtime Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13676)
*Di Lu,Qingwen Zhang,Yujia Liu,Xuewen Dong,Yulong Shen,Zhiquan Liu,Jianfeng Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EBCC是一种兼容OCI标准的机密容器运行时架构，通过将REE侧锚点与TEE侧机密阶段封装为统一的容器化机密计算复合体，在Keystone等TEE后端上实现了标准OCI生命周期管理，实验表明其额外开销主要集中在宿主侧管理状态，且生命周期与阶段抽象可映射至多种TEE平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机密容器系统通常依赖VM部署栈或TEE特定执行基底，使得机密执行与传统OCI运行时生命周期分离，缺乏统一的、兼容OCI标准的机密容器运行时管理方案。

Method: EBCC提出了一种OCI兼容的运行时架构，将REE侧锚点与TEE侧机密阶段视为单一容器化机密计算复合体，保留标准OCI生命周期操作，将TEE特定执行封装在后端适配器中，并维护持久化每实例状态与每阶段工件以支持请求处理、响应生成、日志记录和证据绑定。

Result: 在Keystone后端上实现并评估了正确性、性能、内存占用及并发执行行为。EBCC相比原生Keystone执行引入了额外延迟，主要来源于生命周期中介、请求验证、EID分配、后端分发和工件持久化，但增加的占用主要集中在宿主侧管理状态。跨TEE案例研究表明SGX、TDX和OP-TEE均可映射到相同的生命周期与阶段抽象。

Conclusion: EBCC能够通过OCI风格的生命周期使TEE后端执行变得可管理，且不会显著扩大受保护侧的TCB。

Abstract: Container runtimes provide a stable operational interface for deploying, monitoring, and controlling modern workloads, while trusted execution environments (TEEs) provide hardware-enforced isolation for sensitive computation. Existing confidential-container systems often rely on VM-backed deployment stacks or TEE-specific execution substrates, which can separate confidential execution from the conventional OCI runtime lifecycle. This paper presents EBCC (Enclave-Backed Confidential Containers), an OCI-compatible runtime architecture for managing composite confidential-computing workloads. EBCC treats the REE-side anchor and TEE-side confidential stages as a single containerized confidential-computing composite, preserves standard OCI lifecycle operations, and keeps TEE-specific execution behind a backend adapter. It also maintains persistent per-instance state and per-stage artifacts for request handling, response generation, logging, and evidence binding.
  We implement EBCC on a Keystone backend and evaluate its correctness, performance, footprint, and concurrent execution behavior. The results show that EBCC introduces additional latency over native Keystone execution, mainly due to lifecycle mediation, request validation, EID allocation, backend dispatch, and artifact persistence, while keeping the added footprint concentrated on host-side management state. Cross-TEE case studies on SGX, TDX, and OP-TEE show that the same lifecycle and stage abstraction can be mapped to enclave-style, VM-style, and embedded-style TEEs. These results indicate that EBCC can make TEE-backed execution manageable through an OCI-style lifecycle without materially enlarging the protected-side TCB.

</details>


### [28] [DisAgg: Distributed Aggregators for Efficient Secure Aggregation in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13708)
*Haaris Mehmood,Giorgos Tatsis,Dimitrios Alexopoulos,Karthikeyan Saravanan,Jie Xu,Anastasios Drosou,Mete Ozay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DisAgg协议通过引入聚合者委员会执行聚合任务，客户端秘密共享更新向量给聚合者，聚合者本地计算部分和并返回聚合份额，服务器重组最终结果。相比OPA方案，处理100k个5G客户端的10万维更新向量时实现4.6倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习安全聚合方案存在通信轮次多、公钥操作开销大、难以处理客户端掉线等问题。单轮交互的OPA方案虽减少通信轮次，但给服务器和客户端带来巨大密码学和计算开销，亟需更高效的隐私保护聚合协议。

Method: DisAgg协议设计：1) 选取少量客户端作为聚合者委员会；2) 每个客户端对更新向量进行秘密共享并分发给聚合者；3) 聚合者在本地计算部分和；4) 聚合者仅返回聚合后的份额给服务器；5) 服务器重组得到最终聚合结果。避免了本地掩码和昂贵的同态加密操作。

Result: 在100k个5G客户端、100k维更新向量的场景下，DisAgg相比此前最优的OPA方案实现了4.6倍的加速，同时保持了针对好奇服务器和有限比例合谋客户端的隐私保护。

Conclusion: DisAgg通过引入聚合者委员会和秘密共享机制，在通信与计算成本之间实现最优权衡，显著降低了端点计算开销，为联邦学习安全聚合提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training across distributed clients, yet vanilla FL exposes client updates to the central server. Secure-aggregation schemes protect privacy against an honest-but-curious server, but existing approaches often suffer from many communication rounds, heavy public-key operations, or difficulty handling client dropouts. Recent methods like One-Shot Private Aggregation (OPA) cut rounds to a single server interaction per FL iteration, yet they impose substantial cryptographic and computational overhead on both server and clients. We propose a new protocol called DisAgg that leverages a small committee of clients called Aggregators to perform the aggregation itself: each client secret-shares its update vector to Aggregators, which locally compute partial sums and return only aggregated shares for server-side reconstruction. This design eliminates local masking and expensive homomorphic encryption, reducing endpoint computation while preserving privacy against a curious server and a limited fraction of colluding clients. By leveraging optimal trade-offs between communication and computation costs, DisAgg processes 100k-dimensional update vectors from 100k 5G clients with a 4.6x speedup compared to OPA, the previous best protocol.

</details>


### [29] [VectorSmuggle: Steganographic Exfiltration in Embedding Stores and a Cryptographic Provenance Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13764)
*Jascha Wanger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种针对RAG系统的隐写式数据窃取攻击：攻击者可在嵌入向量中隐藏数据而不影响检索行为。简单旋转扰动即可绕过大多数异常检测器。作者提出了VectorPin协议，通过Ed25519签名将嵌入向量与其源内容和模型绑定，从而阻断此类攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG系统将敏感内容转为高维嵌入向量存储在向量数据库中，但主流向量数据库产品缺乏嵌入完整性保护、分发时异常检测和密码学来源验证的原生控制，导致存在隐写式数据窃取攻击的安全漏洞。

Method: 1) 提出六种嵌入后扰动方法（噪声注入、旋转、缩放、偏移、碎片化及组合）隐藏payload数据；2) 在text-embedding-3-large、四个本地开源嵌入模型、BEIR NFCorpus和Quora子集（26,000+块）、七个向量数据库配置上评估攻击效果；3) 提出VectorPin协议：通过Ed25519签名将每个嵌入向量与其源内容和生成模型绑定，任何嵌入后修改都会破坏签名验证。

Result: 分布偏移型扰动易被简单异常检测器捕获；小角度正交旋转在所有（模型、语料库）组合上都能绕过分布检测。disjoint-Givens旋转编码器的理论容量上限为floor(d/2)*b比特，但实际嵌入流形存在容量-可检测性权衡，保持检索性能的工作点远低于该上限。

Conclusion: 嵌入级别完整性是一种可部署、可标准化的控制手段，可以有效阻断此类隐写攻击。VectorPin协议为RAG系统提供了可行的安全解决方案。

Abstract: Modern retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems convert sensitive content into high-dimensional embeddings and store them in vector databases that treat the resulting numerical artifacts as opaque. Major vector-store products do not provide native controls for embedding integrity, ingestion-time distributional anomaly detection, or cryptographic provenance attestation. We show this opens a class of steganographic exfiltration attacks: an attacker with write access to the ingestion pipeline can hide payload data inside embeddings using simple post-embedding perturbations (noise injection, rotation, scaling, offset, fragmentation, and combinations thereof) while preserving the surface-level retrieval behavior the RAG system exposes to legitimate users.
  We evaluate these techniques across a synthetic-PII corpus on text-embedding-3-large, four locally hosted open embedding models, a cross-corpus replication on BEIR NFCorpus and a Quora subset (over 26,000 chunks combined), seven vector-store configurations, an adaptive-attacker variant of the detector evaluation, and a paraphrased-query retrieval benchmark. Distribution-shifting perturbations are often caught by simple anomaly detectors; small-angle orthogonal rotation defeats distribution-based detection across every (model, corpus) pair tested. A disjoint-Givens rotation encoder gives a closed-form per-vector capacity ceiling of floor(d/2) * b bits, but real embedding manifolds impose a capacity-detectability trade-off, and the retrieval-preserving operating point sits well below it.
  We propose VectorPin, a cryptographic provenance protocol that pins each embedding to its source content and producing model via an Ed25519 signature over a canonical byte representation. Any post-embedding modification breaks signature verification. Embedding-level integrity is a deployable, standardizable control that closes this attack class.

</details>

### [30] [The Geno-Synthetic Algorithm: Type-Factored Coevolutionary Optimization for Heterogeneous Genotypes and Assembled Phenotypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13365)
*Alex Bogdan*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出基因-合成算法（GSA），一种类型分解的协同进化框架，将异构参数按表征类型分区、并行进化、组装为表型进行联合适应度评估。实验表明GSA是唯一能处理复数描述符和嵌入向量的方法，在BBOB-MixInt基准上与扁平化DE无统计差异，消融实验证实类型原生算子、精英信用和主动组装的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界优化问题通常包含异构参数（整数、实数、布尔值、类别、复数描述符、嵌入向量等），标准进化算法将其扁平化为单染色体并应用通用算子（含舍入和修复），牺牲了表征保真度。

Method: GSA将基因家族按表征类型分区，利用类型原生算子并行进化，通过显式组装算子将各部分组装为可执行表型进行联合适应度评估；形式化为类型乘积空间搜索过程。

Result: GSA是唯一能处理复数描述符和嵌入向量的方法。在平滑合成多家族问题上，扁平化差分进化（DE）仍是最强基线；在BBOB-MixInt上，100,000评估时GSA_DIRECT与FLATTENED_DE无统计差异，FLATTENED_EA从第二降至第五（渐近交叉）。消融实验：类型原生算子必不可少，精英信用优于集成信用，主动组装优于被动拼接。

Conclusion: GSA框架自然扩展到大型语言模型系统的提示和嵌入优化。架构性发现：类型分解的协同进化范式在处理异构参数优化问题时具有独特优势，特别是在传统方法无法处理的复杂表征类型场景。

Abstract: Many real-world optimization problems are not naturally homogeneous vectors but composite design objects with heterogeneous parameters: integers, real values, Booleans, categoricals, complex-valued descriptors, and embedding vectors. Standard evolutionary algorithms flatten these into a single chromosome and apply generic operators with rounding and repair, sacrificing representational fidelity. We introduce the Geno-Synthetic Algorithm (GSA), a type-factored coevolutionary framework in which gene families are partitioned by representational type, evolved in parallel with type-native operators, and assembled into executable phenotypes for joint fitness evaluation. GSA is formalized as a typed product-space search procedure with an explicit assembly operator. An open-source reference implementation (gsa-experiments, MIT-licensed) is released. A focused empirical study compares eight GSA variants against five baselines across seven benchmark problems (six synthetic plus the external COCO BBOB-MixInt suite) at budgets from 5,000 to 100,000 evaluations. The headline finding is architectural: GSA is the only method that operates when gene families include complex-valued descriptors or embedding vectors. On smooth synthetic multi-family problems, well-tuned flattened differential evolution remains the strongest baseline; on BBOB-MixInt at 100,000 evaluations, GSA_DIRECT becomes statistically indistinguishable from FLATTENED_DE while FLATTENED_EA drops from second to fifth rank, an asymptotic crossover. Ablations confirm that type-native operators are essential, elite credit dominates ensemble credit, and active assembly outperforms passive concatenation on gated benchmarks. The framework extends naturally to prompt and embedding optimization for large language model systems.

</details>

*Mirela-Magdalena Catrina,Ioana Cristina Plajer,Alexandra Băicoianu*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本研究通过引入新的训练方法，使神经细胞自动机（NCA）能够实现纹理的自我修复（healing）和嫁接（grafting），即在受损区域自主再生纹理，并在推理阶段无需重新训练即可无缝组合不同纹理，展示了NCA在自主系统中强大的动态纹理合成与自修复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NCA在多纹理合成方面存在局限，缺乏自修复机制和灵活的纹理组合能力，难以应用于动态、自适应的自主系统。

Method: 提出一种新的训练方法使NCA具备内在的纹理自再生（healing）机制；同时提出嫁接（grafting）技术，通过在推理阶段精确初始化NCA的基因组通道，无需重新训练即可实现不同纹理的无缝组合。

Result: 生成高质量、复杂的纹理，实现了平滑的纹理过渡，并验证了NCA在受损区域的自修复能力。

Conclusion: 该方法为动态纹理组合和自修复提供了高效且强大的范式，突显了NCA在自主系统中的自组织特性，超越了传统计算机图形学的应用范畴。

Abstract: This study significantly advances multi-texture synthesis using Neural Cellular Automata (NCAs) by introducing a novel training methodology that enables robust self-regeneration of textures in damaged regions. This inherent healing mechanism, essential for dynamic and adaptive systems, extends beyond traditional computer graphics applications, highlighting the fundamental self-organizing properties of NCAs. Furthermore, we present a versatile grafting technique, enabling the seamless combination of distinct textures. This is achieved efficiently during the inference phase, without requiring specialized retraining, through precise initialization of the NCA's genome channels. Our findings demonstrate the generation of high-quality, complex textures with fluid transitions, showcasing a powerful and efficient paradigm for dynamic texture composition and self-repair in autonomous systems.

</details>

### [32] [Fine-Tuning Models for Automated Code Review Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12610)
*Smitha S Kumar,Michael A Lones,Manuel Maarek,Hind Zantout*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究通过参数高效微调（PEFT）和提示工程两种方法，将大型专有模型的知识蒸馏到开源LLM Code Llama中，以提升其生成Java代码反馈的质量。实验表明PEFT显著优于提示工程，在学生评估中甚至与专有模型ChatGPT效果相当，为开发可自由部署的编程教育反馈工具提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有LLM在编程教育反馈生成中展示了潜力，但存在成本高、计算需求大以及学生代码共享的伦理问题。开源LLM虽可规避这些问题，但能力不及专有模型。因此需要探索如何有效增强开源LLM的反馈生成质量。

Method: 从大型高性能模型中导出的数据集上，分别应用参数高效微调（PEFT）和提示工程（prompt engineering）两种知识蒸馏方法，对开源LLM Code Llama进行适配和增强。反馈质量评估采用学生评价、人工标注以及BLEU、ROUGE、BERTScore等自动指标三种方式。

Result: PEFT在反馈质量上显著优于提示工程；学生评价认为PEFT模型的反馈与专有ChatGPT模型同样有效；学生建议PEFT模型的反馈可增加技术术语的额外解释以进一步提升效果。

Conclusion: 微调后的开源模型能够有效支持批判性思维培养，并为可扩展的教学系统设计提供指导。PEFT方法为开发可自由部署的编程教育反馈工具提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models have introduced new possibilities for programming education through personalized support, content creation, and automated feedback. While recent studies have demonstrated the potential for feedback generation, many techniques rely on proprietary models, raising concerns about cost, computational demands, and the ethical implications of sharing student code. Open LLMs provide an alternative approach, but they do not currently have the capabilities of proprietary models. To address this problem, we investigate whether parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) and prompt engineering, both of which distil knowledge from a dataset derived from a large, more capable model, can be used to adapt and enhance the quality of feedback generated by the open LLM Code Llama. Feedback quality on buggy Java code was assessed using a combination of student evaluation, manual annotation and the automated metrics BLEU, ROUGE, and BERTScore. Our findings indicate that PEFT leads to notable improvements in feedback quality and significantly outperforms prompt engineering, providing an avenue for developing freely deployable feedback tools that can be effectively used to guide student learning. Student evaluation indicates that learners value the PEFT model's feedback and see it as being equally effective as the proprietary ChatGPT model. Participants suggested that incorporating additional explanation for technical terms in the PEFT model's feedback could be more beneficial. This study demonstrates that fine-tuned models can effectively support critical thinking and guide the design of scalable pedagogical systems.

</details>


### [33] [User Reviews as a Source for Usability Requirements: A Precursor Study on Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12657)
*Cedric Wellhausen,Laura Reinhardt,Kurt Schneider*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文探索利用LLM的自然语言理解能力（而非传统ML/DL分类方法）结合大规模用户评论提取，以低成本快速分析用户需求。实验表明LLM能识别用户评论中的可用性非功能需求，但性能高度依赖提示词设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ML/DL方法需要大量人工标注数据进行训练，成本高且效率低；而LLM预训练于海量用户生成文本，具备天然的用户中心优势，因此探索利用LLM替代人工评估用户评论的可行性。

Method: 收集300条来自三类不同应用的包含可用性相关方面的用户评论，由两名人类评估者和LLM分别标注；基于10项Nielsen可用性启发式设计编码指南，经过两轮提示工程迭代构建初始提示词；对比LLM与人类评估者的F-score表现。

Result: LLM在识别用户评论中的可用性非功能需求方面，F-score表现与人类评估者可比，但性能与可靠性高度依赖于提示词的设计质量。

Conclusion: LLM能够支持快速、低成本的需求工程流程，但提示词工程是关键瓶颈，需进一步优化提示策略以提高稳定性。

Abstract: It is known that user-centered approaches to requirements engineering in general lead to a better suited product for the end-users. LLM4RE provides promising approaches to support the requirements elicitation process (e.g. classification of requirements). Previous approaches focus on Machine-Learning (ML) or Deep-Learning (DL) aspects, which require intensive training with a large amount of manually labeled data. LLMs, on the other hand, are pre-trained on large amounts of user-generated text data, enabling a user-centric workflow to analyze requirements. In this paper, we explore the possibility of exploiting the improved natural language understanding of LLMs, rather than strict ML classification, together with the mass extraction of user reviews to analyze if the performance of LLMs in understanding user reviews is comparable to the performance of human raters. This enables a quick and cheap workflow for development teams to gather and process their userś requirements. This paper provides three major contributions: (1) We provide a completely coded dataset of 300 user reviews containing usability-relevant aspects from three different types of apps, that were labeled by two human raters and by an LLM. (2) We build an initial prompt, based on two prompt engineering iterations and specifically developed coding guidelines derived from the 10 Nielsen Usability Heuristics, for LLMs to filter usability relevant user reviews. (3) We determine that LLMs are generally able to recognize usability as a non-functional requirement in user reviews, in terms of their F-score, but the performance and reliability is strongly dependent on the prompt.

</details>


### [34] [Finding a Crab in the C: Assured Translation via Comparative Symbolic Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12731)
*Caleb Helbling,Graham Leach-Krouse,Michael Crystal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出cozy，一种比较二进制分析工具，用于验证从非内存安全语言（如C）翻译到内存安全语言（如SPARK/Rust）的代码的语义等价性，并通过人工审核标记差异来保证“等价模开发者批准的变更”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高可信软件系统开发倾向于使用内存安全语言，但遗留系统中大量非内存安全代码（如C）重写成本过高或风险过大。开发者开始使用机器学习/AI自动翻译，但翻译后的代码与原代码在语义上并非完全相同，存在风险。

Method: 开发了cozy工具，采用比较二进制分析方法：同时分析从“不安全”源代码编译的二进制和从翻译到内存安全语言的代码编译的二进制，逐一呈现行为差异并请开发者判断差异是预期的（好的）还是错误的。未标记差异的部分通过形式化验证保证等价。

Result: cozy能够引导开发者审查两个二进制之间的行为差异，并保证除开发者批准的变更外，其余部分形式化等价。该工具可应用于自动翻译、缺陷纠正、代码审查、操作授权和自动翻译等多个场景。

Conclusion: cozy提供了一种“等价模开发者批准的变更”的审查流程，有效降低了自动代码翻译带来的风险，在安全关键系统中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Modern high-assurance software systems development favors memory safe languages such as SPARK (ADA) or Rust. However, developers often encounter non-memory safe code (e.g., C) in legacy systems and libraries which would be prohibitively expensive or risky to re-write. In response, developers have begun turning to machine learning/AI systems and other automated code translators. Automated translation comes with its own risks, however. The original and ported code are not precisely the same, semantically - otherwise there would be no point in performing the translation. To reduce these risks, we have developed cozy, a comparative binary analysis tool that simultaneously analyzes a binary compiled from "unsafe" source code and a binary compiled from a translation of the source code to a memory safe language. cozy walks the developer through differences in the behavior of the two binaries, presenting each difference and asking the user to assess whether the difference is intentional (good) or erroneous. Outside of the flagged differences, the binaries are formally verified to be equivalent. Consequently, the review process guarantees equivalence modulo changes approved by the developer. cozy has applications to automated translation, bug correction, code reviews, operation authorization, and automatic translation.

</details>

*Sanjiv Das,Andrii Ieroshenko,Piyush Jain,David Qiu,Michael Chin,Brian Granger*

TL;DR: 本文运用产品生命周期方法，通过微分方程系统建模开源项目中开发者参与度与增长水平的动态交互，并验证了该模型在多个项目中的适用性，最终实现了对开源项目生命周期生产价值的估算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将产品生命周期分析方法和内生增长理论引入开源软件工程，以理解开发者参与度随项目生命周期的演化规律，并量化开源项目的生命周期生产价值。
