Result: 模型能够较好地拟合多个开源项目的数据，并生成了开发者终身参与度和项目终身增长水平的估计值。

Conclusion: 该模型可用于估算开源项目的终身生产价值，为开源项目的经济价值评估提供了理论支撑和定量工具。

Abstract: Using methods previously applied to product life cycles, this paper models developer engagement through the project life cycle for open-source projects, and detects similar dynamics in a cross section of projects. Endogenous growth theory is used to model growth dynamics in open-source software engineering, while incorporating the interactions between growth levels and developer activity over time using systems of differential equations. The solution to this model calibrates well to many open-source projects. The model generates an estimate of the lifetime developer engagement and growth, which supports estimating a lifetime production value of open-source projects.

</details>


### [36] [AgentLens: Revealing The Lucky Pass Problem in SWE-Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12925)
*Priyam Sahoo,Gaurav Mittal,Xiaomin Li,Shengjie Ma,Benjamin Steenhoek,Pingping Lin,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示SWE智能体的二值化评估（通过/不通过）存在缺陷。通过对2,614条OpenHands轨迹分析，发现10.7%的通过轨迹实际上是“幸运通过”——包含混乱的试错过程。作者提出AgentLens框架进行过程级评估，构建了1,815条标注轨迹的数据集，并引入质量评分将通过轨迹分为幸运、扎实和理想三个等级，发现不同模型按质量排序与按通过率排序存在显著差异（排名最多变动5位）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SWE智能体评估仅以最终补丁是否通过测试作为二值信号，这等价地将有原则的解决方案与混乱的试错过程视为相同结果，但经验证据表明这种等价是错误的，需要更细粒度的过程级评估方法。

Method: 对8个模型后端的2,614条OpenHands轨迹在60个SWE-bench任务上进行评估，筛选出47个有足够通过轨迹的任务构建1,815条轨迹子集；提出AgentLens框架，利用Prefix Tree Acceptor (PTA)合并多个通过解决方案作为任务级过程参考，使用上下文敏感意图标注器将动作分类为探索、实现、验证或编排四个类别；定义质量评分和Lucky Pass的五种机制。

Result: 在通过轨迹中10.7%表现为Lucky Pass（包含回归循环、盲目重试、缺失验证、探索/实现/验证时序混乱等问题）；不同模型后端的Lucky率从0.5%到23.2%不等；按质量评分排序与按通过率排序相比，某些模型排名变动多达5位。

Conclusion: SWE智能体的二值化通过率评估具有误导性，过程级评估（如AgentLens）能揭示模型真实行为差异，应成为更全面的评估标准。

Abstract: Evaluation of software engineering (SWE) agents is dominated by a binary signal: whether the final patch passes the tests. This outcome-only view treats a principled solution and a chaotic trial-and-error process as equivalent. We show that this equivalence is empirically false. We evaluate 2,614 OpenHands trajectories from eight model backends on 60 SWE-bench Verified tasks. Of these, 47 have enough passing trajectories to construct task-level process references, yielding a 1,815-trajectory evaluation subset. Among passing trajectories in this subset, 10.7% exhibit behavior we call a Lucky Pass: regression cycles, blind retries, missing verification, or temporally disordered exploration, implementation, and verification.
  We introduce AgentLens, a framework for process-level assessment of SWE-agent trajectories, and release AgentLens-Bench, a dataset of 1,815 trajectories annotated with quality scores, waste signals, divergence points, and 47 task-level Prefix Tree Acceptor (PTA) references. AgentLens builds PTA references by merging multiple passing solutions for the same task, and uses a context-sensitive intent labeler to assign actions to Exploration, Implementation, Verification, or Orchestration based on trajectory history rather than tool identity alone.
  On AgentLens-Bench, the quality score separates passing trajectories into Lucky, Solid, and Ideal tiers and further decomposes Lucky Passes into five recurring mechanisms. Across the eight model backends, Lucky rates range from 0.5% to 23.2%, and some models move by as many as five rank positions when ranked by quality score instead of pass rate. We release the anonymized project repository, including the AgentLens-Bench dataset and AgentLens SDK, at https://github.com/microsoft/code-agent-state-trajectories/.

</details>


### [37] [Protocol-Driven Development: Governing Generated Software Through Invariants and Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12981)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出协议驱动开发（PDD），将机器可执行的协议（结构、行为、操作不变量）作为软件制品核心，实现基于协议满足和证据链的可信治理，替代对代码实现或生成器的信任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动程序合成降低了实现成本，却引入了更难治理的问题：如何判定生成的制品是否可被接纳。自然语言规范存在语义歧义，示例测试仅覆盖部分行为空间，单独使用均无法为自动化软件构造提供充分控制边界。

Method: 定义协议P=(S,B,O)，包含结构不变量、行为不变量和操作不变量，三者合取定义组件的可接纳实现空间。实现被视为可替换的实体，通过约束搜索发现，须满足协议并生成可验证的证据链（Evidence Chain）方可被接纳。

Result: 提出PDD治理层，结合形式化方法、基于属性的测试、策略即代码和软件溯源等思想，建立以协议满足和记录证据为基础的接纳机制。

Conclusion: 代码是暂时的，协议是至上的（code is transient; protocol is sovereign）。PDD为自动化软件工程提供了一种可治理的组织原则。

Abstract: Automated program synthesis has reduced the cost of producing candidate implementations, but it introduces a harder governance problem: determining which generated artifacts are admissible in a software system. Natural-language specifications remain semantically ambiguous, and example-based tests sample only part of the behavioral space. Used alone, neither provides a sufficient control boundary for automated software construction. We introduce Protocol-Driven Development (PDD), a development model in which the primary software artifact is a machine-enforceable protocol rather than implementation code. We define a protocol as the triplet P = (S, B, O), where S specifies structural invariants, B specifies behavioral invariants, and O specifies operational invariants. Their conjunction defines the admissible implementation space of a software component. Under PDD, implementations are treated as replaceable realizations discovered through constrained search. An implementation is admitted if and only if it satisfies the governing protocol and produces a verifiable Evidence Chain of compliance. Admission is therefore grounded not in trust in the generator, but in protocol satisfaction and recorded evidence. By combining ideas from formal methods, property-based testing, policy-as-code, and software provenance, PDD defines a governance layer for automated software engineering. Its organizing principle is simple: code is transient; protocol is sovereign.

</details>


### [38] [Code-Centric Detection of Vulnerability-Fixing Commits: A Unified Benchmark and Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13138)
*Nils Loose,Joseph Bienhüls,Kristoffer Hempel,Felix Mächtle,Thomas Eisenbarth*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过统一框架对基于代码语言模型的漏洞修复提交（VFC）检测进行了全面评估。在超过180次实验（125M-14B参数模型）中，发现模型无法仅从代码变更中习得可迁移的安全理解；提交消息主导模型注意力；移除消息后，额外语义上下文无法将注意力转向代码变更；分组分层评估显示性能下降约17%；在0.5%假阳性率下，所有纯代码微调模型遗漏超93%漏洞。发布统一框架和评估套件支持未来研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 漏洞修复提交的自动检测对于安全补丁的及时部署至关重要，因为咨询数据库相比补丁发布平均延迟25天，且许多修复从未发布安全通告。现有研究存在数据集碎片化、评估不够系统等问题，亟需统一的全面评估。

Method: 构建统一框架整合20多个碎片化数据集（覆盖18万+提交），使用125M到14B参数的不同规模代码语言模型进行超过180次微调实验，进行分组分层评估、注意力分析、归因分析等多种系统评估。

Result: 模型无法从代码变更中习得可转移的安全理解；提交消息主导注意力；移除消息后额外语义上下文无法引导注意力到代码变更；分组评估相比随机划分性能下降约17%；在0.5%假阳性率下所有纯代码模型遗漏超93%漏洞；更大规模数据和生成式方法仅有初步改进但未解决根本局限。

Conclusion: 当前基于代码语言模型的VFC检测方法存在根本性局限，无法仅从代码变更有效检测漏洞修复。研究发布统一框架和评估套件以支持未来在该方向上的研究。

Abstract: Automated detection of vulnerability-fixing commits (VFCs) is critical for timely security patch deployment, as advisory databases lag patch releases by a median of 25 days and many fixes never receive advisories. We present a comprehensive evaluation of code language model based VFC detection through a unified framework consolidating over 20 fragmented datasets spanning more than 180000 commits. Across over 180 experiments with fine-tuned models from 125 M to 14 B parameters, we find no evidence that models acquire transferable security-relevant code understanding from code changes alone. When commit messages are available, they dominate model attention, and when removed, an attribution analysis shows that enriching diffs with additional intra-procedural semantic context does not shift model attention toward the code changes. Group-stratified evaluation exposes approximately 17% performance drops compared to random splits, while temporal splits on aggregated datasets prove unreliable due to compositional shift in the underlying project distributions. At a false positive rate of 0.5% all fine-tuned code-only models miss over 93% of vulnerabilities. Larger and more diverse training data or generative approaches show preliminary improvements but do not resolve the underlying limitations. To support future research on code-centric VFC detection, we release our unified framework and evaluation suite.

</details>


### [39] [(How) Do Large Language Models Understand High-Level Message Sequence Charts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13773)
*Mohammad Reza Mousavi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了三种大型语言模型（Gemini-3、GPT-5.4、Qwen-3.6）对高层消息序列图（HMSC）形式语义的理解能力，通过129项语义任务测试发现，LLMs整体准确率仅约52%，在基本语义概念上表现较好（约88%），但在抽象组合（约36%）和迹/LTS推理（约42%）方面表现不佳，且完全无法理解和运用核心语义概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs被广泛用于自动化软件开发任务，但它们在处理构件语义方面的一致性尚不清楚，特别是在架构设计规格领域缺乏研究。本文旨在探究LLMs是否真正理解HMSC（UML序列图的基础）的形式语义。

Method: 选取三种LLMs（Gemini-3、GPT-5.4、Qwen-3.6），设计129项语义任务，涵盖从基本语义构造查询（事件及其排序）到语义保持抽象与组合、迹集计算及迹等价标签转移系统计算等多个层次，系统评估LLMs对HMSC形式语义的理解能力。

Result: LLMs整体准确率约52%，基本语义概念理解较好（约88%），但抽象与组合任务仅约36%，迹与LTS任务约42%。特别是所有LLMs均无法理解核心区域和显式因果依赖等关键概念，在语义保持变换中从未使用过这些概念。

Conclusion: 当前LLMs对HMSC形式语义仅有中等程度的理解，在高级语义推理任务上存在显著缺陷，尤其是无法掌握核心区域和显式因果依赖等关键语义概念，表明LLMs在形式语义理解方面仍有很大提升空间。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are being employed widely to automate tasks across the software development life-cycle. It is, however, unclear whether these tasks are performed consistently with respect to the semantics of the artefacts being handled. This question is particularly under-researched concerning architectural design specification. In this paper, we address this question for High-Level Message Sequence Charts (HMSCs). These are visual models with a rigorous formal semantics that have been used for various purposes, including as a foundation for Sequence Diagrams in the Unified Modelling Language (UML). We examine whether LLMs "understand" the semantics of HMSCs by examining three LLMs (Gemini-3, GPT-5.4, and Qwen-3.6) on how they perform 129 semantic tasks ranging from querying basic semantic constructs in HMSCs (i.e., events and their ordering) to semantic-preserving abstractions and compositions, and calculating the set of traces and trace-equivalent labelled transition systems. The results show that LLMs only have a modest understanding of the formal semantics of HMSCs (ca. 52% overall accuracy), with great variability across different semantic concepts: while LLMs seem to understand the basic semantic concepts of MSCs (ca. 88% accuracy), they struggle with semantic reasoning in tasks involving abstraction and composition (ca. 36% accuracy) and traces and LTSs (ca. 42% accuracy). In particular, all three LLMs struggle with the notions of co-region and explicit causal dependencies and never employed them in semantic-preserving transformations.

</details>


### [40] [UIBenchKit: A unified toolkit for design-to-code model evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13141)
*Chinh T. Le,Trevor Ong Yee Siang,Jingyu Xiao,Yuxuan Wan,Yintong Huo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: UIBenchKit 是一个开源、集成的标准化评估工具包，用于统一网页截图到HTML/CSS代码生成任务的评估，提供即插即用架构和多指标分析接口，并基于该工具进行了现有工具的基准测试研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前设计到代码的自动化生成方法众多，但缺乏标准化评估平台，导致方法间难以公平比较，限制了实际应用和系统研究进展。

Method: 提出了UIBenchKit开源工具包，通过抽象环境搭建、模型推理和代码渲染的复杂性，采用即插即用架构，并提供多指标对比分析接口，实现统一评估。

Result: 基于UIBenchKit对现有工具进行了基准测试研究，得出若干指导未来改进方向的发现。

Conclusion: UIBenchKit通过提供简化的实验和评估环境，旨在加速网页工程领域的未来基准测试和创新。

Abstract: Recent years have seen substantial progress in automated design-to-code generation, with many methods proposed for generating HTML and CSS from webpage screenshots. However, the absence of a standardized evaluation platform makes it difficult to compare these methods fairly, limiting both practical adoption and systematic research progress. To bridge this gap, we introduce UIBenchKit, an open-source, integrated toolkit designed to unify the evaluation of design-to-code tasks. UIBenchKit abstracts the complexities of environment setup, model inference, and code rendering, offering researchers a plug-and-play architecture to compare various methods under consistent settings. In addition, it offers an analytical interface for comparison across multiple metrics. Using UIBenchKit, we conduct a benchmarking study of existing tools and derive several findings that highlight directions for future improvement. By providing a streamlined environment for both experimentation and evaluation, UIBenchKit aims to accelerate future benchmarking and innovations in web engineering. The evaluation platform and toolkit are available at the project page https://www.uibenchkit.com/.

</details>


### [41] [Robust Mutation Analysis of Quantum Programs Under Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13279)
*Sophie Fortz,Eñaut Mendiluze Usandizaga,Shaukat Ali,Paolo Arcaini,Mohammad Reza Mousavi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子硬件噪声对变异分析的影响，通过41个量子程序在无噪声和三种噪声模拟器上的实验，发现噪声显著改变了程序与变异体之间的行为距离，增加了等价变异体的误判率。密度矩阵度量达到最佳判别效果（最高误判率16.77%），输出分布度量可达到73.03%准确率和74.89% F1分数。噪声感知阈值优于无噪声阈值，噪声影响与算法和电路特征的相关性高于变异类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子变异分析研究假设理想无噪声执行环境，忽略了量子硬件噪声的实际影响，导致对量子软件测试技术的鲁棒性评估不准确。

Method: 使用41个量子程序，在无噪声模拟器和模拟三种IBM设备噪声分布的模拟器上执行，比较多种距离度量和阈值策略，评估噪声环境下变异体检测效果。

Result: 噪声显著改变程序与变异体行为距离，密度矩阵度量误判率最高16.77%；输出分布度量可达73.03%准确率和74.89% F1分数；噪声感知阈值优于无噪声阈值；噪声影响与算法和电路特征相关性高于变异类型。

Conclusion: 量子变异分析需要根据目标量子设备的噪声特征进行调整，噪声感知方法对提升变异分析在真实量子硬件上的有效性至关重要。

Abstract: Mutation analysis has long been used in classical software testing and has recently been adopted for assessing the robustness of quantum software testing techniques. However, existing studies assume ideal, noiseless execution, overlooking the impact of quantum hardware noise. In this paper, we present an empirical study of noise-aware mutation analysis for quantum programs. We analyze how noise affects mutant detection using 41 quantum programs, executed on noiseless and noisy simulators emulating three IBM devices with different noise profiles. We compare several distance metrics and thresholding strategies to evaluate mutant detection under realistic noise. Our results show that noise significantly alters the behavioral distance between programs and mutants, making equivalent mutants harder to distinguish from real faults. Density-matrix metrics achieve the best discrimination, with misclassification rates up to 16.77%, but are not accessible on real hardware. Among practical alternatives, output-distribution metrics reach up to 73.03% accuracy and 74.89% F1-score. Noise-specific thresholds further improve detection compared to noiseless thresholds. We also find that noise effects correlate more with algorithm and circuit characteristics than with mutation types. Overall, our results highlight the need to adapt mutation analysis, and more generally quantum program comparison, to the noise profiles of target quantum devices.

</details>


### [42] [SkillOps: Managing LLM Agent Skill Libraries as Self-Maintaining Software Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13716)
*Hongji Pu,Xinyuan Song,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SkillOps提出了一种方法无关的插件框架，通过技能契约、层次化技能生态系统图和四维健康诊断来维护LLM智能体的技能库，消除技术债务，在ALFWorld上作为独立智能体达到79.5%任务成功率，比最强基线高8.8个百分点，且几乎不消耗额外LLM调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于技能的LLM智能体主要关注任务时的检索、规划和修复，而忽略了库层面的维护。随着技能不断被添加、复用、修补和链接到变化的依赖关系，技能库会积累持久缺陷（即技能技术债务），损害后续的检索、组合和执行性能。因此需要一种库维护机制来解决这一未被充分探索的问题。

Method: SkillOps是一个方法无关的插件式框架，核心包括：(1) 将每个技能表示为类型化的技能契约 (P, O, A, V, F)；(2) 用层次化技能生态系统图组织技能；(3) 从效用、兼容性、风险和验证四个维度诊断技能库健康状态。给定原始技能库，SkillOps输出维护后的技能库，可直接被现有检索或规划智能体使用，无需修改其内部代码。

Result: 在ALFWorld上，SkillOps作为独立智能体达到79.5%任务成功率，超出最强基线8.8个百分点，且不增加任务时LLM调用次数；作为插件层，使基于检索的基线提升0.68至2.90个百分点；基于规则的维护实现几乎不使用库维护时的LLM调用或token，表明技能库维护可以低开销的架构层形式加入。

Conclusion: 技能库维护可以作为低开销的架构层有效提升LLM智能体性能，填补了现有工作在库维护方面的空白；SkillOps的方法无关性和零额外任务时LLM调用特性使其易于集成到现有系统。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly rely on skill libraries for multi-step tasks, yet these libraries can accumulate persistent defects as skills are added, reused, patched, and linked to changing dependencies. We call this failure mode skill technical debt: library-level defects that may not break a single skill locally but can harm future retrieval, composition, and execution. Existing skill-based agents mainly focus on task-time retrieval, planning, and repair, while library-time maintenance remains underexplored. We propose SkillOps, a method-agnostic plug-in framework for maintaining skill libraries. SkillOps represents each skill as a typed Skill Contract (P, O, A, V, F), organizes skills with a Hierarchical Skill Ecosystem Graph, and diagnoses library health across utility, compatibility, risk, and validation dimensions. Given a raw skill library, SkillOps produces a maintained library that can be used by existing retrieval or planning agents without changing their internal code. On ALFWorld, SkillOps achieves 79.5 percent task success as a standalone agent, outperforming the strongest baseline by 8.8 percentage points with no additional task-time large language model calls. As a plug-in layer, it improves retrieval-heavy baselines by 0.68 to 2.90 percentage points. The current rule-based maintenance implementation uses nearly zero library-time large language model calls or tokens, showing that skill-library maintenance can be added as a low-overhead architectural layer.

</details>


### [43] [The Readability Spectrum: Patterns, Issues, and Prompt Effects in LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13280)
*Hengzhi Ye,Fengyuan Ran,Weiwei Xu,Minghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了LLM生成代码的可读性，发现其与人类编写代码整体相当，但存在独特的可读性问题模式；提示工程对可读性影响有限，函数签名、约束和风格描述是最关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成代码的功能质量已被广泛研究，但其可读性（关键非功能属性）尚未得到系统探讨。由于LLM生成代码仍需人工审查，理解其可读性以及提示设计的影响至关重要。

Method: 建立融合文本、结构、程序和视觉特征的综合可读性模型，基于WoC和LeetCode等大型代码库的5,869个场景，评估主流LLM生成代码的可读性，并分析不同提示维度的影响。

Result: LLM生成代码的整体可读性与人类编写代码相当，但存在独特的可读性问题模式；函数签名、约束和风格描述是最有影响力的提示因素，但提示设计的总体影响有限。

Conclusion: LLM生成代码在可读性方面至少与人类代码相当，验证了其从非功能角度融入软件工作流的潜力；但独特的可读性问题和提示工程的有限效果揭示了潜在的技术债务，需进一步研究以提升长期可维护性。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are transforming software development, the functional quality of generated code has become a central focus, leaving readability, one of critical non-functional attributes, understudied. Given that LLM-generated code still needs human review before adoption, it is important to understand its readability especially compared with human-written code and the role of prompt design in shaping it. We therefore set out to conduct a systematic investigation into the code readability of LLM-generated code. To systematically quantify code readability, We establish a comprehensive readability model that synthesizes textual, structural, program, and visual features of code. Based on the model, we evaluate the readability of code generated by the mainstream LLMs under 5,869 scenarios extracted from large code base including World of Code (WoC) and LeetCode. We find that current LLMs produce code with overall readability comparable to human-written code, but displaying distinct readability issue patterns. We further examine how different prompt dimensions affect the readability of LLM-generated code, and find that function signatures, constraints and style descriptions emerge as the most influential factors, while the overall impact of prompt design remains limited. Our findings indicate that, on one hand, LLM-generated code is at least comparable to human-written code in readability, validating its potential for systematic integration into software workflows from a non-functional perspective; on the other hand, distinct readability issue patterns and limited effectiveness of prompt engineering reveal a latent technical debt, highlighting the need for future research to improve the readability of LLM-generated code and thus ensure long-term maintainability.

</details>


### [44] [SieveFL: Hierarchical Runtime-Aware Pruning for Scalable LLM-Based Fault Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13491)
*Mahdi Farzandway,Fatemeh Ghassemi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SieveFL提出了一种五阶段层次化框架，通过LLM前的激进过滤（向量检索+JaCoCo运行时痕迹）将故障定位范围缩小至少量候选方法，再交由本地部署的中型MoE模型进行逐方法筛查与重排序。在Defects4J v1.2.0的395个bug上，Top-1准确率41.8%，MRR 0.469，优于AgentFL 2.1个百分点，同时减少79%的候选方法和49%的输入token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统计方法在故障定位中精度有限，而LLM方法面临全项目规模的token成本过高和信号稀释问题，亟需一种能在前置过滤阶段大幅缩小搜索空间、同时保持甚至提升定位精度的方案。

Method: 五阶段层次化框架：(1) 将失败测试转化为自然语言失败描述；(2) 使用稠密向量检索缩小可疑文件集；(3) 通过JaCoCo运行时痕迹排除未在失败测试中执行的方法；(4) 将幸存候选逐方法送入LLM筛查；(5) 对确认的嫌疑方法进行单次比较重排序。

Result: 在Defects4J v1.2.0的395个bug上，Top-1准确率41.8%，MRR 0.469，优于AgentFL 2.1个百分点；运行时剪枝移除79%候选方法，减少49%输入token消耗，Top-1保持不变，Top-3至Top-10提升最多2.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过合理的前置过滤架构，无需专有前沿模型即可实现高效的故障定位，证明了在消费级硬件上部署本地开源模型足以胜任此任务。

Abstract: Automated fault localization requires connecting an observed test failure to the responsible method across thousands of candidates--a task that purely statistical approaches handle with limited precision and that LLMs cannot yet handle at full project scale due to prohibitive token cost and signal dilution. We present SieveFL, a five-stage hierarchical framework that resolves this tension through aggressive pre-LLM filtering. SieveFL converts a failing test into a natural-language failure description, uses dense vector retrieval to narrow the search to a small set of suspicious files, and then eliminates any method not executed during the failing test via JaCoCo runtime traces. Only the surviving candidates are passed to the LLM, which screens each method individually and re-ranks the confirmed suspects in a single comparative pass. We evaluate SieveFL on 395 bugs from Defects4J v1.2.0 using a mid-sized, openly available MoE model deployed on a commodity workstation (32 GB RAM, 8 GB GPU) via Ollama--no frontier APIs or datacenter hardware required. Treating 12 incomplete runs as failures, SieveFL achieves Top-1 accuracy of 41.8% (165/395 bugs) and an MRR of 0.469, outperforming the strongest prior agent-based baseline (AgentFL) by 2.1 pp in Top-1. Runtime pruning removes 79% of candidate methods and reduces input token consumption by 49%, while simultaneously improving ranking quality: Top-1 is preserved exactly and Top-3 through Top-10 improve by up to 2.4 pp. These results demonstrate that, with the right filtering architecture, capable fault localization does not require proprietary frontier models.

</details>


### [45] [Scalable Deductive Verification of Data-Level Parallel Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13616)
*Lars B. van den Haak,Anton Wijs,Marieke Huisman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出多项技术提升面向数组和矩阵的数据级程序演绎验证的可扩展性，包括：带（嵌套）量词表达式的重写技术（已在定理证明器中验证正确性）、简化潜在重叠数组推理的规约构造（指示非别名或不可变性以建模为数学序列）。所有技术在VerCors验证器中实现。实验表明，在典型GPU内核上平均验证时间降低9倍，极端情况达150倍；应用于射电望远镜管道等案例，实现了先前不可行的验证并显著缩短时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有演绎验证技术在处理数组和矩阵等数据级程序时面临可扩展性瓶颈，主要障碍是带嵌套量词的表达式难以生成合适触发器，以及数组间潜在重叠导致推理复杂。

Method: 1. 提出表达式重写技术处理（嵌套）量词，在定理证明器中验证其正确性，使得能够为这些表达式生成合适的触发器；2. 提供规约构造用于指示和验证数组非别名或不可变性，从而可建模为数学序列；3. 所有技术在VerCors验证器中实现。

Result: 在典型GPU内核测试集上，验证时间平均降低9倍，极端情况达150倍；应用于先前实验和射电望远镜管道案例，实现了先前无法获得的验证结果，并显著缩短验证时间。

Conclusion: 提出的量词重写技术和数组非别名/不可变性规约构造能有效提升数据级程序演绎验证的可扩展性，在VerCors验证器中的实现取得了显著的性能提升。

Abstract: This paper introduces several techniques that improve the scalability of the deductive verification of data-level programs working on arrays and matrices. First of all, we introduce a technique to rewrite expressions with (nested) quantifiers, so suitable triggers can be generated for these expressions. We have proven this rewrite technique correct in a theorem prover. Second, we make reasoning about potentially overlapping arrays easier, by providing specification constructs to indicate and verify that two arrays are not aliases, or that they are immutable, so they can be modelled as mathematical sequences. All our techniques are implemented in the VerCors program verifier. We illustrate how our techniques improve scalability through a large number of experiments. Using our techniques on a set of typical GPU kernels, we achieve a reduction of verification time by, on average, a factor of 9, with outliers being up to 150 times faster. Additionally, applying these techniques to earlier experiments and an earlier case study of a radio telescope pipeline permitted the verification of results which were previously unobtainable and significantly reduced the verification time.

</details>


### [46] [EGSS: Entropy-guided Stepwise Scaling for Reliable Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05242)
*Chenhui Mao,Yuanting Lei,Zhixiang Wei,Ming Liang,Zhixiang Wang,Jingxuan Xu,Dajun Chen,Wei Jiang,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EGSS框架，通过熵引导自适应搜索和测试套件增强，在SWE-Bench-Verified上以28%更少的推理token实现5-10%的性能提升，将Kimi-K2-Intruct的解决率从63.2%提升至72.2%，GLM-4.6从65.8%提升至74.6%，并达到开源LLM新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agentic TTS方法存在两大瓶颈：(1)部署过大的模型集成导致高昂计算开销；(2)缺乏可靠的候选方案选择机制，限制了性能增益。这阻碍了TTS在实际中的采用。

Method: 提出熵引导逐步缩放(EGSS)框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)熵引导自适应搜索——动态平衡效率与效果；(2)鲁棒的测试套件增强机制，用于更可靠地选择最优候选解。

Result: 在SWE-Bench-Verified上，EGSS在所有评估模型上持续提升5-10%性能：Kimi-K2-Intruct解决率从63.2%→72.2%，GLM-4.6从65.8%→74.6%。GLM-4.6搭配EGSS达到开源LLM新SOTA。同时推理token使用量减少28%以上。

Conclusion: EGSS同时实现了有效性提升和计算效率改善，验证了通过熵引导自适应搜索可以解决TTS的高计算成本与候选选择可靠性问题，为Agentic TTS的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Agentic Test-Time Scaling (TTS) has delivered state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on complex software engineering tasks such as code generation and bug fixing. However, its practical adoption remains limited due to significant computational overhead, primarily driven by two key challenges: (1) the high cost associated with deploying excessively large ensembles, and (2) the lack of a reliable mechanism for selecting the optimal candidate solution, ultimately constraining the performance gains that can be realized. To address these challenges, we propose Entropy-Guided Stepwise Scaling (EGSS), a novel TTS framework that dynamically balances efficiency and effectiveness through entropy-guided adaptive search and robust test-suite augmentation. Extensive experiments on SWE-Bench-Verified demonstrate that EGSS consistently boosts performance by 5-10% across all evaluated models. Specifically, it increases the resolved ratio of Kimi-K2-Intruct from 63.2% to 72.2%, and GLM-4.6 from 65.8% to 74.6%. Furthermore, when paired with GLM-4.6, EGSS achieves a new state-of-the-art among open-source large language models. In addition to these accuracy improvements, EGSS reduces inference-time token usage by over 28% compared to existing TTS methods, achieving simultaneous gains in both effectiveness and computational efficiency.

</details>

### [47] [DelAC: A Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning of Team-Symmetric Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12555)
*Duan-Shin Lee,Yu-Hsiu Hung*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有m≥2个团队的团队对称博弈，证明了团队对称纳什均衡的存在性，提出了线性互补问题求解方法，并设计了一种基于演员-评论家的多智能体强化学习算法，仿真表明该算法显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团队对称博弈中，同一团队内的玩家具有对称身份和共同收益函数，需要研究此类博弈的纳什均衡存在性及其求解方法。

Method: 首先从理论上证明了团队对称博弈存在团队对称纳什均衡；然后建立并求解了团队对称纳什均衡的线性互补问题；最后提出了一种基于演员-评论家框架的多智能体强化学习算法。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的多智能体强化学习算法在性能上显著优于多种现有算法。

Conclusion: 团队对称博弈具有理论上的均衡保证，且提出的基于演员-评论家的强化学习方法在实践中表现优异。

Abstract: In this paper we study team-symmetric games with $m\ge 2$ teams. Players within a team have symmetric identity and have a common payoff function. We show that team-symmetric games always have a team-symmetric Nash equilibrium. We develop and solve a linear complementarity problem of team-symmetric Nash equilibria. We propose an actor-critic based multi-agent reinforcement learning algorithm for team-symmetric games. Through simulations, we show that this multi-agent reinforcement learning algorithm performs much better than many existing algorithms.

</details>


### [48] [Synthesizing the Expert: A Validated Multimodal Dataset for Trustworthy AI-Assisted Swimming Coaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12799)
*Ahmad Al-Kabbany,Esraa Kassem*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对游泳领域结构化RAG系统合成问题，提出一种利用多模态知识库和多智能体LLM架构生成1,864个高质量"问题-上下文-答案"三元组的生成框架，为可信AI在水上运动中的应用建立基础基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 游泳领域AI应用日益多样（实时技术教练、人才选拔、性能分析、训练周期个性化），RAG系统虽能基于权威领域知识确保生成输出的可信度，但使用真实水上数据构建RAG系统面临伦理约束（运动员生物特征）和人工专家标注成本高昂等重大挑战。

Method: 提出一种新颖的生成框架，利用跨四个维度的多模态知识库（生理数据、生理文献、运动学传感器数据、非结构化领域专业知识），采用多智能体LLM架构，从1,914个草稿中合成经过1,864个验证的"问题-上下文-答案"三元组，并依据12条生理合理性规则进行评估。

Result: 成功合成了1,864个经过验证的高保真三元组（来自1,914个草稿，通过12条生理合理性规则评估），建立了结构化合成基准数据。

Conclusion: 本工作为水上运动中的可信AI建立了基础基准，有望提升自动化教练的可靠性，并为"元智能体"开发和运动表现分析开辟未来方向，弥合原始数据工程与实用运动科学应用之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: This research is primarily concerned with the critical problem of synthesizing a structured Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system for advanced AI applications in the domain of swimming. As the integration of Artificial Intelligence in sports science matures, its applications in swimming have become increasingly diverse, spanning from real-time technical coaching and talent scouting to comprehensive performance profiling and the dynamic personalization of training periodization. Within this landscape, RAG-based systems represent a pivotal advancement in Large Language Model (LLM) enhanced swimming analysis, as they allow for the grounding of generative outputs in authoritative domain knowledge, thereby ensuring the credibility of AI-generated advice, contextually and technically. Despite this potential, building robust RAG systems using only real-world aquatic data presents significant challenges, including ethical constraints regarding athlete biometrics, and the high cost of manual expert labeling. To address these barriers, we propose a novel generative framework that leverages a multimodal knowledge base gathered across four dimensions: physiological data, physiological literature, kinematic sensor data, and unstructured domain expertise. Our proposed framework utilizes a multi-agent LLM architecture to synthesize a high-fidelity dataset of 1,864 validated "Question-Context-Answer" triplets-drawn from 1,914 drafts evaluated against 12 physiological soundness rules. By providing a structured, synthetic ground truth, this work establishes a foundational benchmark for trustworthy AI in aquatics. The outcomes of this research promise to enhance the reliability of automated coaching and open a plethora of future directions in "Meta-Agent" development and athletic profiling, ultimately bridging the gap between raw data engineering and practical sports science application.

</details>


### [49] [Mechanism Plausibility in Generative Agent-Based Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12824)
*Patrick Zhao,David Huu Pham,Nicholas Vincent*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种机制合理性量表（Mechanism Plausibility Scale），将LLM-ABM的评估分为生成充分性（能否复现现象）和机制合理性（现象如何产生）两个维度，并以四个层次进行可操作化定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在基于智能体的模型中展现出强大能力，但能力、预测与解释是不同的概念。研究者难以区分实验是否代表了能力的进步或解释的进步，需要一种跨领域的框架来评估模型在解释机制方面的贡献。

Method: 整合LLM-ABM前沿研究与当代科学哲学文献，将"合理性"（plausibility）操作化为四层量表，分离生成充分性与机制合理性的评估，并明确预测模型与解释模型的不同角色。

Result: 提出了一个四层机制合理性量表，使建模者能够基于机制合理性的层次来评估仿真实验的进步程度。

Conclusion: 该量表为LLM-ABM研究提供了区分能力展示与机制解释的理论框架，有助于研究者更清晰地定位其工作的贡献类型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate high-level diverse phenomena without explicitly programmed rules. This capability has led to their adoption within different agent-based models (ABMs) and social simulations. Recently, research has aim to test whether they are capable of generating different phenomena of interest, for example, human behavior on social media platforms or performance in game-theoretic scenarios.
  However, capability, prediction, and explanation are different -- drawing from the philosophy of science and mechanisms literature, \textit{explanation} requires showing, to some degree, how a phenomenon is produced by related organized entities and activities. For modelers, describing the characteristics of an experiment or whether a simulation provides progress in capability (or explanation), can be difficult without being grounded in potentially distant research areas.
  We integrate recent work on LLM-ABMs with contemporary philosophy of science literature and use it to operationalize a definition of `plausibility' in a four-level scale. Our scale separates the evaluation of a model's generative sufficiency (ability to reproduce a phenomenon) from its mechanistic plausibility (how the phenomenon could be produced), and clarifies the distinct roles of different models, such as predictive and explanatory ones. We introduce this as the Mechanism Plausibility Scale.

</details>

*Zhongkai Yu,Yichen Lin,Chenyang Zhou,Yuwei Zhang,Kun Zhou,Junxia Cui,Haotian Ye,Zhengding Hu,Zaifeng Pan,Ruiyi Wang,Yujie Zhao,Hejia Zhang,Jingbo Shang,Jishen Zhao,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: ChipMATE是首个自训练多智能体RTL生成框架，通过Verilog智能体与Python参考模型智能体相互验证，在无黄金测试集条件下实现75.0%和80.1%的pass@1准确率，超越包括1600B参数DeepSeek V4在内的现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于API的RTL代码生成系统存在三大问题：假设生成时已有黄金测试集、依赖闭源API不符合芯片厂商气隙安全要求、无法利用厂商自有RTL代码库进行训练。自训练模型虽解决了部署约束，但仍是单轮生成器，忽略了工业流程中验证的关键作用。

Method: 提出ChipMATE多智能体框架：(1) 配对一个Verilog智能体与一个Python参考模型智能体，两者无需黄金oracle即可相互验证输出；(2) 基于回溯的推理工作流，防止错误跨轮传播；(3) 两阶段训练流程——先分别训练各智能体饱和其代码生成能力，再联合训练团队协作；(4) 混合数据生成框架产出64.4K高质量参考模型训练样本。

Result: 在VerilogEval V2上，ChipMATE以4B和9B基础模型分别达到75.0%和80.1%的pass@1，超越所有现有自训练模型，甚至超过1600B参数的DeepSeek V4。

Conclusion: 通过智能体间相互验证代替黄金oracle，结合回溯推理和两阶段训练，ChipMATE有效弥合了RTL代码生成研究与工业实践之间的差距，证明了小模型通过巧妙的多智能体协作设计可以超越超大模型。

Abstract: Existing API-based agentic systems for RTL code generation are fundamentally misaligned with industrial practice: they assume a golden testbench is available at generation time, rely on closed-source APIs incompatible with chip vendors' air-gapped security requirements, and cannot be trained on vendors' proprietary RTL codebases, leaving valuable internal data unused. Recent self-trained models address the deployment constraint but remain single-turn generators that overlook the critical role of verification in real industrial flows.
  To bridge these gaps, we present ChipMATE, the first self-trained multi-agent framework for RTL generation. Inspired by industrial practice where correctness emerges from cross-comparison between independently written RTL modules and reference models, ChipMATE pairs a Verilog agent with a Python reference-model agent that mutually verify each other's outputs without any golden oracle. We design a backtrack-based inference workflow to prevent error propagation across turns, and a two-stage training pipeline that first trains each agent individually to saturate its code-generation capability, then trains the team jointly to collaborate effectively. To support the training, we further build a hybrid data-generation framework that produces 64.4K high-quality reference model training samples. ChipMATE achieves 75.0\% and 80.1\% pass@1 on VerilogEval V2 with 4B and 9B base models, outperforming all existing self-trained models and even DeepSeek V4 with 1600B parameters. Our code and model weights are publicly available in https://github.com/zhongkaiyu/ChipMATE.

</details>


### [51] [SHM-Agents: A Generalist-Specialist Integrated Agent System for Structural Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12916)
*Yuequan Bao,Xing Li,Huabin Sun,Dawei Liu,Yuxuan Tian,Haiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出SHM-Agents，一种融合大语言模型与专用算法的大语言模型智能体系统，用于结构健康监测(SHM)，通过自然语言实现端到端的多任务执行与灵活扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SHM专用算法虽有效，但存在实现门槛高、互操作性差、训练流程复杂等问题，亟需一种更易用、可扩展的通用解决方案。

Method: 提出SHM-Agents通用-专用智能体系统，将大语言模型的推理规划能力与专用算法相结合，支持自然语言交互、深度学习预训练简化部署，并采用模块化设计实现灵活扩展。

Result: 在超大跨斜拉桥上的实验表明，SHM-Agents能够准确高效地执行数据异常诊断与恢复、信号处理、统计分析、模态识别、损伤识别、有限元模型更新、车辆荷载建模、响应计算、可靠性评估、疲劳估计及桥梁知识问答等多样化SHM任务。

Conclusion: SHM-Agents通过自然语言实现了SHM任务的端到端执行，降低了使用门槛，具有良好的准确性和效率，为SHM领域提供了一种通用、可扩展的智能解决方案。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is increasingly used to simplify complex tasks. In engineering applications of structural health monitoring (SHM), existing specialized algorithms, while effective, often face high implementation barriers, limited interoperability and complex training procedures. To overcome these challenges, this paper proposes SHM-Agents, a generalist-specialist agent system that integrates the reasoning and planning abilities of large language models with the problem-solving strengths of specialized algorithms. SHM-Agents enables end-to-end execution of single and combined SHM tasks via natural language, supports deep learning pre-training to simplify deployment and allows flexible expansion through a modular design. Experiments on a long-span cable-stayed bridge show that SHM-Agents can accurately and efficiently perform diverse SHM tasks, including data anomaly diagnosis and recovery, signal processing, statistical analysis, modal identification, damage identification, finite element model updating, vehicle load modeling, response calculation, reliability assessment, fatigue estimation and bridge knowledge Q\&A.

</details>


### [52] [Embodied Multi-Agent Coordination by Aligning World Models Through Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12920)
*Vardhan Dongre,Dilek Hakkani-Tür*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于LLM的具身智能体在部分可观察环境下通过自然语言对话进行协作时，是否真正实现了世界模型对齐。实验发现对话能大幅减少行动冲突（40-83个百分点），但反而降低了任务成功率，表明当前模型存在表面协调与真正世界模型对齐之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体在部分可观察环境中的协作面临根本性困难，通信理论上可以弥合这一差距。然而，当前基于LLM的具身智能体是否真正具备通过通信对齐世界模型的能力尚不清楚，需要系统性的评估框架和实证研究。

Method: 在PARTNR协作家务机器人基准上扩展自然语言对话通道，使两个部分可观察的智能体在任务执行中通信。提出三个世界模型对齐指标：观测收敛性（私有世界模型是否随时间对齐）、信息新颖性（消息是否传递伙伴缺失的信息）、信念敏感通信（智能体是否建模伙伴的知识状态）。在三种LLM上进行实验评估。

Result: 对话将行动冲突减少40-83个百分点，但任务成功率反而低于静默协调。通过所提出的指标，刻画了表面协调与真正世界模型对齐之间的差距，并定位了当前模型在此谱系中的位置。

Conclusion: 当前基于LLM的具身智能体虽能通过对话减少冲突，但尚未实现真正的世界模型对齐，这解释了为何通信反而导致任务表现下降。所提出的评估框架为未来研究提供了诊断工具。

Abstract: Effective collaboration between embodied agents requires more than acting in a shared environment; it demands communication grounded in each agent's evolving understanding of the world. When agents can only partially observe their surroundings, coordination without communication is provably hard, but communication can, in principle, bridge this gap by allowing agents to share observations and align their world models. In this work, we examine whether LLM-based embodied agents actually realize the ability to communicate. We extend PARTNR, a benchmark for collaborative household robotics, with a natural-language dialogue channel that enables two agents with partial observability to communicate during task execution. To evaluate whether dialogue leads to genuine world-model alignment rather than superficial coordination, we propose a framework for measuring world-model alignment defined over per-agent world graphs: observation convergence (do private world models align over time?), information novelty (do messages convey what the partner lacks?), and belief-sensitive messaging (do agents model what their partner knows?). Our experiments across three LLMs reveal that dialogue reduces action conflicts 40 to 83 percentage points but degrades task success relative to silent coordination. Using our metrics, we characterize the gap between superficial coordination and genuine world-model alignment, and identify where current models fall on this spectrum.

</details>


### [53] [Counterfactual Reasoning for Causal Responsibility Attribution in Probabilistic Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13077)
*Chunyan Mu,Muhammad Najib*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反事实责任和Shapley值的责任分配框架，用于并发随机多智能体系统，支持责任感知系统中的验证与策略推理，并展示了如何在纳什均衡下计算稳定策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统中责任分配是设计与分析中的基本挑战，现有方法缺乏对策略结果下智能体责任的形式化量化。

Method: 将系统建模为并发随机多玩家博弈，引入回顾性反事实责任概念，利用Shapley值分配责任并证明其满足公平性与一致性等性质，基于此构建支持验证和策略推理的形式化框架，以纳什均衡为解概念计算稳定策略。

Result: 证明了Shapley值方法满足公平性与一致性等理想性质；通过纳什均衡实现了责任与期望奖励之间的权衡策略计算。

Conclusion: 该框架为多智能体系统的责任分配提供了形式化的理论基础，同时支持责任感知下的验证与策略推理。

Abstract: Responsibility allocation -- determining the extent to which agents are accountable for outcomes -- is a fundamental challenge in the design and analysis of multi-agent systems. In this work, we model such systems as concurrent stochastic multi-player games and introduce a notion of retrospective (backward) counterfactual responsibility, which quantifies an agent's accountability for outcomes resulting from a given strategy profile. To allocate responsibility among agents, we utilise the Shapley value and formally show that this method satisfies key desirable properties, including fairness and consistency. Building on this foundation, we propose a formal framework that supports both verification and strategic reasoning in responsibility-aware multi-agent systems. Furthermore, by adopting Nash equilibrium as the solution concept, we demonstrate how to compute stable strategy profiles in which agents trade off responsibility against expected reward.

</details>

*Ziqi Wang,Yuhao Yang,Zhiwei Ling,Wenzhuo Qian,Hailiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了分布式事件驱动调度基准(DESBench)，用于评估分层事件驱动调度中的智能体协调。通过工业调度中的共享离散事件驱动环境，系统研究了集中式、分层式、异分式和全息式四种协调范式，揭示了不同范式在效果、约束对齐、协调效率和鲁棒性方面的结构性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要在弱耦合环境中评估任务完成情况，缺乏对具有层次结构和耦合约束的共享动态演化系统中协调问题的研究，因此需要探索不同协调范式何时成功或失败。

Method: 构建基于工业调度共享离散事件驱动环境的DESBench基准，涵盖多时间尺度决策、部分可观测性和动态耦合约束；定义评估有效性、约束对齐、协调效率和鲁棒性的指标；对比四种协调范式：集中式、分层式、异分式和全息式。

Result: 集中式协调鲁棒且通信高效但随难度扩展性差；分层式协调通过分解提升效率但存在跨层级不匹配；异分式协调灵活但通信开销大；全息式协调约束满足好但失去全局鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 协调设计从根本上塑造了复杂环境中智能体系统的行为，揭示了仅凭结果指标无法捕获的结构性权衡，强调了未来多智能体系统研究需要更自适应、有原则且动态的协调机制。

Abstract: Recent advances in agent and multi-agent systems have shown strong performance on tool use, reasoning, and collaborative tasks. However, existing benchmarks mostly evaluate task completion in weakly coupled environments, and provide limited support for studying coordination in shared, dynamically evolving systems with hierarchy and coupled constraints. This leaves an important question underexplored: when do different coordination paradigms succeed or fail? We introduce Distributed Event-driven Scheduling Benchmark (DESBench), a benchmark for evaluating agent coordination in hierarchical event-driven scheduling. Built on a shared discrete-event driven environment in industrial scheduling, our benchmark captures multi-timescale decision making, partial observability, and dynamically coupled constraints. We define tasks and metrics that evaluate effectiveness, constraint alignment, coordination efficiency, and robustness, and focus on four representative coordination paradigms: centralized, hierarchical, heterarchical, and holonic. These paradigms correspond to distinct mechanisms of information flow, decision authority, and conflict resolution. Our controlled evaluations reveal clear coordination trade-offs: centralized coordination is robust and communication-efficient but scales poorly with difficulty; hierarchical coordination improves efficiency through decomposition but suffers from cross-level misalignment; heterarchical coordination is flexible but communication-heavy; and holonic coordination satisfies constraints well but loses global robustness. These findings demonstrate that coordination design fundamentally shapes agent system behavior in complex environments, revealing structural trade-offs that cannot be captured by outcome metrics alone and underscoring the imperative for more adaptive, principled, and dynamic coordination mechanisms in future MAS research.

</details>


### [55] [Constitutional Governance in Metric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13362)
*Ehud Shapiro,Nimrod Talmon*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在度量空间中实现宪政治理的端到端多项式时间流程，整合了聚合、审议、修正和共识等阶段，通过广义中位数规则和超级多数阈值实现平等自治，适用于数字社区和组织的民主治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算社会选择与算法决策理论研究将聚合、审议、修正和共识等阶段孤立处理，且关键度量空间聚合器是NP难的，缺乏一个完整的、多项式时间的平等自治流程。

Method: 提出宪政治理框架：每个可修正组件分配度量空间、聚合规则和超级多数阈值；成员提交理想元素（投票+个人提案）；任何成员可提交公共提案；宪法规则对提案与现状打分，采用正最大分的提案；同一规则以更高阈值修正宪法本身。以广义中位数作为工作规则。

Result: 建立框架级保证，证明无虚假报告弱占优真实投票，研究最佳峰值与无约束最优之间的妥协间隙——一维为零，一般有界，模拟中通过简单启发式缩小。在七个经典设定上实例化框架。

Conclusion: 通过统一度量空间聚合、现实感知社会选择、超级多数修正、宪政共识、审议联盟形成和AI调解，为数字社区和组织的宪政民主治理提供了全面解决方案。

Abstract: Computational social choice and algorithmic decision theory offer rich aggregation theory but no end-to-end, polynomial-time process for egalitarian self-governance: prior work treats aggregation, deliberation, amendment, and consensus in isolation, and key metric-space aggregators are NP-hard. We propose constitutional governance in metric spaces, integrating these stages into one polynomial-time process. The constitution assigns, per amendable component, a metric space, aggregation rule, and supermajority threshold. Each member submits an ideal element -- both vote and personal proposal. Any member may then submit a public proposal carrying supermajority public support under the revealed votes -- sourced from coalition deliberation, optimization, or AI mediation. The constitutional rule scores proposals against the status quo, adopting the supported proposal of positive maximal score (else retaining the status quo); the same rule, possibly with a higher threshold, amends the constitution itself. We develop the generalised median as the worked rule, establish framework-level guarantees, prove no misreport weakly dominates sincere voting, and study the compromise gap between best peak and unconstrained optimum -- zero in one dimension, bounded in general, narrowed in simulation by a simple heuristic. We instantiate the framework on seven canonical settings; the mean appears as a utilitarian alternative in the appendix. By unifying metric-space aggregation, reality-aware social choice, supermajority amendment, constitutional consensus, deliberative coalition formation, and AI mediation, this work delivers a comprehensive solution to the constitutional democratic governance of digital communities and organisations.

</details>


### [56] [EconAI: Dynamic Persona Evolution and Memory-Aware Agents in Evolving Economic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13762)
*Annie Liu,Zane Cao,Lang Chen,Zongxin Xu,Zigan Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出EconAI框架，通过引入经济情绪指数、记忆加权和动态决策机制，实现了首个统一模拟宏观/微观经济环境及交互的LLM驱动的经济仿真系统，在稳定性、就业-消费周期复现和决策鲁棒性上优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM经济仿真框架无法捕捉短期优化与长期战略规划之间的相互作用，依赖静态数据驱动预测，未能纳入受经济情绪、市场波动和个人目标影响的适应性行为。

Method: 提出EconAI框架，包含三个核心机制：经济情绪指数(ESI)量化经济信念、记忆加权调整历史数据影响、动态决策机制连接工作-消费行为，使智能体根据市场信号和长期目标自适应调整行动。

Result: EconAI在三个方面优于传统方法：提高经济响应稳定性、更好复现真实世界的就业-消费周期、增强整体决策鲁棒性。

Conclusion: EconAI是首个在统一框架下模拟宏观/微观经济环境和交互的LLM驱动仿真系统，标志着向更真实、自适应的经济智能体仿真迈出关键一步。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) in economic simulations has significantly enhanced agent-based modeling, yet existing frameworks struggle to capture the interplay between short-term optimization and long-term strategic planning. Conventional approaches rely on static data-driven predictions, failing to incorporate adaptive behaviors influenced by economic sentiment, market volatility, and individual goals. To address these limitations, we introduce a novel EconAI framework, incorporating economic sentiment indexing (ESI), memory weighting, and dynamic decision-making mechanisms. By quantifying economic belief, adjusting historical data influence, and linking work-consumption behaviors, EconAI achieves a more human-like decision process, where agents adapt their actions based on both market signals and long-term objectives. It is the first LLM-powered simulation system that can simulate the macro/microeconomic environment and interactions in a unified framework. Empirical evaluations show that EconAI improves stability in economic responses, better replicates real-world employment-consumption cycles, and enhances overall decision robustness. This advancement marks a crucial step towards more realistic, adaptive economic agent simulations.

</details>

### [57] [ToolMol: Evolutionary Agentic Framework for Multi-objective Drug Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12784)
*Andrew Y. Zhou,Sharvaree Vadgama,Sumanth Varambally,Peter Eckmann,Michael K. Gilson,Rose Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ToolMol 是一个将多目标遗传算法与基于LLM的智能体操作器相结合的进化框架，用于从头药物设计。它通过RDKit工具箱进行精确配体修饰，在多目标性质优化和结合亲和力预测上达到SOTA性能，相比现有方法提升超过10-35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的分子生成方法受限于模型对分子字符串的语法限制，导致产生大量无效和低质量的候选配体，亟需一种能够结合LLM化学先验知识与精确分子操作的新方法。

Method: 将多目标遗传算法与智能体LLM算子结合，构建包含RDKit支持函数的综合工具箱，使智能体算子能够持续进行精确的配体修饰，并通过迭代更新配体种群来优化分子。

Result: 在多目标性质优化任务上达到SOTA，所发现的类药可合成配体对三种蛋白靶点的预测结合亲和力比现有方法强>10%；在绝对结合自由能评分上超过现有方法>35%。

Conclusion: 工具调用使模型能够更忠实地执行其计划的修饰，有效利用LLM中强大的化学先验知识，通过思维链推理轨迹验证了这一机制的有效性。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) have recently opened new and promising avenues for small-molecule drug discovery. Yet existing LLM-based approaches for molecular generation often suffer from high rates of invalid and low-quality ligand candidates, a result of the syntactic limitations of current models with regard to molecular strings. In this paper, we introduce $\texttt{ToolMol}$, an evolutionary agentic framework for de novo drug design. $\texttt{ToolMol}$ combines a multi-objective genetic algorithm with an agentic LLM operator that iteratively updates the ligand population. We build a comprehensive toolbox of RDKit-backed functions that allows our agentic operator to consisently make precise ligand modifications. $\texttt{ToolMol}$ achieves state-of-the-art performance on multi-objective property optimization tasks, discovering drug-like and synthesizable ligands that have $>10\%$ stronger predicted binding affinity compared to existing methods, evaluated on three protein targets. $\texttt{ToolMol}$ ligands additionally achieve state-of-the-art results in gold-standard Absolute Binding Free Energy scores, gaining over existing methods by over $35\%$. By studying chain-of-thought reasoning traces, we observe that tool-calling enables the model to more faithfully execute its planned modifications, efficiently exploiting the strong chemical prior knowledge in LLMs.

</details>

*Jannis Lübsen,Annika Eichler*

TL;DR: 本论文将多任务贝叶斯优化在不确定相关矩阵下的安全保证从固有协同区域化模型扩展到线性协同区域化模型，后者通过组合多个特征实现更灵活的任务间相关建模。推导了线性协同区域化核的高斯过程向量值函数的均匀误差界，并在安全多任务贝叶斯优化基准上展示了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全多任务贝叶斯优化方法局限于固有协同区域化模型，无法灵活建模复杂的任务间相关结构，需要更通用的线性协同区域化模型来扩展安全保证。

Method: 将安全保证从固有协同区域化模型扩展到线性协同区域化模型，推导向量值高斯过程的均匀误差界，并通过数值实验比较性能。

Result: 在安全多任务贝叶斯优化基准上，使用线性协同区域化模型相比原有方法表现出性能提升。

Conclusion: 线性协同区域化模型能够更灵活地建模任务间相关性，同时保持安全保证，提升了多任务贝叶斯优化的安全性和性能。

Abstract: This paper extends safety guarantees for multi-task Bayesian optimization with uncertain correlation matrices from intrinsic co-reginalization models to linear models of co-reginalization. The latter allows for more flexible modeling of the inter-task correlations by composing multiple features. We derive uniform error bounds for vector-valued functions sampled from a Gaussian process with a linear model of co-reginalization kernel. Furthermore, we show the potential improvement of performance using linear models of co-reginalization in a numerical comparison on a safe multi-task Bayesian optimization benchmark.

</details>


### [59] [Finding the Weakest Link: Adversarial Attack against Multi-Agent Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13170)
*Maxwell Standen,Junae Kim,Claudia Szabo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多智能体强化学习系统中的单受害者通信扰动攻击，提出利用雅可比梯度信息识别最易受攻击的消息、智能体和时间步，并设计了两种对抗损失函数来平衡攻击成功率和影响，实验证明该方法在多种环境中有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统依赖通信进行信息共享和行动协调，但这种通信机制存在被攻击的漏洞，现有研究缺乏对单受害者通信扰动攻击的系统性分析。

Method: 利用雅可比矩阵的梯度信息识别最易受攻击的消息、智能体和时间步；提出两种对抗损失函数以在攻击成功率和攻击影响之间进行权衡。

Result: 提出的消息选择方法在几乎所有测试场景中达到与随机选择相似或更大的影响；受害者选择、消息选择、时间步和损失函数在30个测试场景中的一半提升了攻击效果。

Conclusion: 基于梯度引导的通信扰动攻击方法能有效识别关键通信链路并生成更具破坏性的扰动，为多智能体系统的安全防御提供了研究方向。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems rely on communication for information sharing and action coordination, which exposes a vulnerability to attacks. We investigate single-victim communication perturbation attacks against Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning-trained systems and propose methods that use gradient information from the Jacobian to identify which messages, agent, and timesteps are most susceptible to attack and have the greatest impact on the system. We enhance these methods with two proposed adversarial loss functions that trade-off attack success for attack impact which also create more effective perturbations. We empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of our methods against two different multi-agent communication methods in navigation, PredatorPrey, and TrafficJunction environments. Our results show that our novel message selection method achieves a similar or greater impact than random message selection across almost all tested scenarios. Our victim selection, message selection, tempo, and loss functions improve attack effectiveness in half of the thirty scenarios we tested.

</details>


### [60] [Learning to Decide with AI Assistance under Human-Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12646)
*Nina Corvelo Benz,Eleni Straitouri,Manuel Gomez-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究AI辅助决策中，人类与AI置信度对齐程度如何影响学习最优决策的复杂度。在二元预测与二元决策场景下，证明该问题等价于全反馈的双臂在线上下文学习问题，并给出了遗憾下界；同时证明在完美对齐条件下可达到更优的遗憾界，表明对齐能降低学习复杂度。真实实验验证了理论结果的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明AI辅助决策的效用与AI置信度和人类自身置信度之间的对齐程度正相关，但尚未阐明这种对齐程度如何影响通过重复交互学习最优决策的复杂度。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将问题形式化为二元预测与二元决策下的全反馈双臂在线上下文学习问题；建立遗憾下界；在完美对齐假设下推导改进的遗憾上界；利用Dvoretzky-Kiefer-Wolfowitz不等式的推广证明更紧的界；通过真实人类实验数据验证理论结果。

Result: 建立了Ω(√(|H|·|B|·T))的期望遗憾下界；在完美对齐下得到O(√(|H|·T log T))的期望遗憾界；当√(|H|)=O(log T)且B可数时，进一步改进为O(√(T log T))。

Conclusion: AI与人类置信度的对齐能够降低学习最优决策的复杂度，且理论结果在真实数据下对完美对齐的偏离具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: It is widely agreed that when AI models assist decision-makers in high-stakes domains by predicting an outcome of interest, they should communicate the confidence of their predictions. However, empirical evidence suggests that decision-makers often struggle to determine when to trust a prediction based solely on this communicated confidence. In this context, recent theoretical and empirical work suggests a positive correlation between the utility of AI-assisted decision-making and the degree of alignment between the AI confidence and the decision-makers' confidence in their own predictions. Crucially, these findings do not yet elucidate the extent to which this alignment influences the complexity of learning to make optimal decisions through repeated interactions. In this paper, we address this question in the canonical case of binary predictions and binary decisions. We first show that this problem is equivalent to a two-armed online contextual learning problem with full feedback, and establish a lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{|H| \cdot |B| \cdot T} )$ on the expected regret any learner can attain, where $H$ and $B$ denote the sets of human and AI confidence values. We then demonstrate that, under perfect alignment between AI and human confidence, a learner can attain an expected regret of $O(\sqrt{|H| \cdot T\log T})$ and, when $\sqrt{|H|} = O(\log T)$ and $B$ is countable, a non-trivial generalization of the Dvoretzky-Kiefer-Wolfowitz inequality improves the regret bound to $O(\sqrt{T\log T})$. Taken together, these results reveal that alignment can reduce the complexity of learning to make decisions with AI assistance. Experiments on real data from two different human-subject studies where participants solve simple decision-making tasks assisted by AI models show that our theoretical results are robust to violations of perfect alignment.

</details>


### [61] [Distinguishing performance gains from learning when using generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13731)
*Lixiang Yan,Samuel Greiff,Jason M. Lodge,Dragan Gašević*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 生成式AI虽可提升学习表现，但无法促进深度认知与元认知加工，不利于高质量学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨生成式AI在教育中的应用对学习质量的潜在影响，特别是其对深度认知和元认知过程的促进作用不足的问题。

Method: 基于对现有生成式AI教育应用的观察与分析，指出其在促进深层学习方面的局限性。

Result: 生成式AI的使用未能有效促进学习者所需的深度认知和元认知加工过程。

Conclusion: 当前生成式AI的教育应用方式不利于高质量学习，需要改进以支持更深层次的认知与元认知活动。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly being integrated into education, where it can boost learners' performance. However, these uses do not promote the deep cognitive and metacognitive processing that are required for high-quality learning.

</details>


### [62] [Learning When to Act: Communication-Efficient Reinforcement Learning via Run-Time Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12561)
*Adam Haroon,Erick J. Rodríguez-Seda,Cody Fleming,Tristan Schuler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在Lyapunov安全屏蔽下的自适应触发控制框架，通过学习策略自主决定控制执行时机，在保证稳定性的同时大幅降低通信/计算频率，并在倒立摆、车杆系统和平面四旋翼上验证了该方法相比固定采样控制器的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统安全强化学习关注智能体"该做什么"（控制输入），忽略了"何时需要执行"（触发时机）这一关键问题。本文旨在让单一策略同时学习控制输入和通信高效的时序决策，在Lyapunov安全屏蔽下实现安全性与稀疏通信的平衡。

Method: 采用CARE-based LQR备份和点态Lyapunov安全屏蔽的运行时保证（RTA）层，通过一步预测Lyapunov函数判断是否覆盖策略动作；引入CARE导出的Lyapunov奖励函数实现跨环境迁移；扩展为偏好条件化模型以覆盖完整权衡前沿。

Result: 在倒立摆、车杆系统和平面四旋翼上，学习策略的平均采样间隔（MSI）分别比Lyapunov触发基线高1.91倍、1.45倍和3.51倍；固定采样的LQR控制器在相同平均速率下不稳定；去除RTA屏蔽后MSI下降1.27-1.84倍且状态范数恶化；偏好条件化扩展仅用1/11训练算力即可恢复完整权衡前沿；在12维三维四旋翼上扩展有效，并对±30%质量变化和扰动具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 自适应触发时序而非降低平均采样率是安全稀疏控制的关键；点态Lyapunov安全屏蔽提供了比期望约束MDP方法更强的安全性保证；Lyapunov奖励函数可跨环境复用而无需重新设计；该方法可扩展至经典STC难以处理的高维系统，展示了优雅的性能退化特性。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) typically asks $\textit{what}$ an agent should do. We ask $\textit{when}$ it needs to act, and show that a single policy can jointly learn control inputs and communication-efficient timing decisions under a pointwise Lyapunov safety shield. We focus on stabilization around a known equilibrium, where CARE-based LQR backups, Lyapunov certificates, and classical Lyapunov-STC are well defined, enabling clean comparison against analytical baselines. A run-time assurance (RTA) layer overrides the policy via a one-step-ahead Lyapunov prediction and a precomputed LQR backup, providing a strictly stronger guarantee than constrained MDP methods that enforce safety only in expectation. On an inverted pendulum, cart--pole, and planar quadrotor, the learned policy achieves $1.91\times$, $1.45\times$, and $3.51\times$ higher mean inter-sample interval (MSI) than a Lyapunov-triggered baseline; a fixed LQR controller at the same average rate is unstable on all three plants, showing that adaptive timing, not a lower average rate, makes sparsity safe. A CARE-derived Lyapunov reward transfers across environments without redesign, with a single weight $w_c$ controlling the stability--communication tradeoff; ablations confirm the RTA shield is essential, with its removal reducing MSI by $1.27$--$1.84\times$ and degrading state norms. A preference-conditioned extension recovers the full tradeoff frontier from one model at $\tfrac{2}{11}$ of training compute, and SAC experiments show the results are algorithm-agnostic across discrete and continuous domains. A 12-state 3D quadrotor case study extends the framework to higher-dimensional systems where classical STC is intractable, and robustness to $\pm30\%$ mass variation and disturbances shows graceful degradation, with the RTA absorbing what the learned policy cannot.

</details>


### [63] [Towards Robust Federated Multimodal Graph Learning under Modality Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12584)
*Sirui Zhang,Haonan Wang,Xunkai Li,Zekai Chen,Shumeng Li,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedMPO框架，解决联邦多模态图学习中拓扑孤立局部补全和可靠性不平衡全局聚合两大挑战，通过拓扑感知跨模态生成、缺失感知专家路由和可靠性感知聚合，在6个数据集上实现最高4.10%和5.65%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实图中多模态数据常因数据共享限制而孤立且模态不完整，现有集中式MGL方法忽略联邦场景下的知识共享与泛化，而联邦MGL方法主要针对非图数据，因此亟需开发鲁棒的联邦多模态图学习方法。

Method: 提出FedMPO框架，包含：(1)拓扑感知跨模态生成，利用全局图上下文恢复缺失特征；(2)缺失感知专家路由，局部过滤噪声恢复信号；(3)可靠性感知聚合，降低不可靠更新的权重。

Result: 在3个任务6个数据集上，FedMPO优于基线方法，在高缺失和非IID设置下分别达到4.10%和5.65%的性能增益。

Conclusion: FedMPO通过拓扑感知生成、专家路由和可靠性聚合有效解决了联邦多模态图学习中的两大挑战，显著提升了鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recently, multimodal graph learning (MGL) has garnered significant attention for integrating diverse modality information and structured context to support various network applications. However, real-world graphs are often isolated due to data-sharing limitations across multiple parties, and their modalities are frequently incomplete. This highlights an urgent need to develop a robust federated approach. However, we find that existing methods remain insufficient. On the one hand, centralized MGL methods that handle missing modalities overlook the knowledge sharing and generalization in federated scenarios. On the other hand, while federated MGL methods have become increasingly mature, they primarily target non-graph data. Based on these technologies, we identify a two-stage pipeline wherein client-side completion reconstructs missing modalities, and server-side aggregation integrates the client-updated parameters of both the modality generator and the backbone models. Although this serves as a general solution, we identify two primary challenges in achieving greater robustness: (1) Topology-Isolated Local Completion: Client-side modality generation struggles to effectively leverage global semantics. (2) Reliability-Imbalanced Global Aggregation: Server-side multi-party collaboration is hindered by client updates with varying modality availability and recovery reliability. To address these challenges, we propose \textsc{FedMPO}, which utilizes topology-aware cross-modal generation to recover missing features using comprehensive graph context, missing-aware expert routing to locally filter out noisy recovered signals, and reliability-aware aggregation to appropriately down-weight unreliable updates. Extensive experiments on 3 tasks across 6 datasets demonstrate that FedMPO outperforms baselines, achieving performance gains of up to 4.10% and 5.65% in high-missing and non-IID settings.

</details>


### [64] [OceanCBM: A Concept Bottleneck Model for Mechanistic Interpretability in Ocean Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12639)
*Sanah Suri,Kieran Ringel,Maike Sonnewald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OceanCBM是一个用于海洋极端现象预测与物理解析的概念瓶颈模型（CBM），通过混合监督学习在保持预测性能的同时实现可解释、物理一致的中间表征，并显式刻画了可解释性-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有海洋极端现象（如海洋热浪）预测方法中，高精度机器学习模型缺乏可解释性，无法揭示物理驱动机制；而纯物理模型预测能力有限。亟需一种既能保持预测技能又能提供物理可解释性的方法。

Method: 提出OceanCBM，采用概念瓶颈结构，通过混合监督学习预测混合层热含量。模型引入基于地球物理流体动力学指定的先验概念层和一个"自由"概念，软性约束物理结构而不过度限制模型，自由概念同时正则化概念预测并捕获残差物理过程。

Result: 在多次集合初始化下，混合监督学习产生了一致的机制表征，而纯预测和纯指定基线的潜在结构高度可变但预测性能相似。OceanCBM在保持预测技能的同时实现了可解释、物理基础的表征，并显式刻画了可解释性-性能权衡。

Conclusion: OceanCBM证明了通过概念瓶颈架构和混合监督可以兼顾海洋动力系统预测的准确性与物理可解释性，为理解极端海洋现象背后的物理机制提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Extreme ocean phenomena are challenging not only to predict but to diagnose, as accurate forecasts alone do not reveal the underlying physical drivers. While recent machine learning approaches achieve strong predictive skill, they remain largely opaque and provide limited guarantees of fidelity to ground-truth physics. We introduce OceanCBM, the first concept bottleneck model (CBM) for spatiotemporal prediction and mechanistic interrogation of ocean dynamics. OceanCBM uses mixed supervision to predict mixed layer heat content, a key precursor of marine heatwaves, while routing information through an intermediate layer of prescribed concepts derived from geophysical fluid dynamics and a 'free' concept. This design imposes soft physical structure without over-constraining the model, and the free concept both regularizes concept predictions and captures residual physical processes. Across ensemble initializations, we show that mixed supervision yields consistent mechanistic representations, whereas prediction-only and prescription-only baselines learn highly variable latent structures despite similar predictive performance. OceanCBM achieves interpretable, physically grounded representations without sacrificing skill, explicitly characterizing the interpretability-performance trade-off.

</details>


### [65] [Runtime Monitoring of Perception-Based Autonomous Systems via Embedding Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12651)
*Parv Kapoor,Abigail Hammer,Ashish Kapoor,Karen Leung,Eunsuk Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出嵌入时序逻辑（ETL），一种直接在学习的嵌入空间中进行运行时监控的时序逻辑框架。ETL通过观测嵌入与参考嵌入间的距离定义谓词，利用时序算子组合表达高层感知概念，并结合保形校准实现可靠的谓词评估。在多个操作环境中的实验表明，ETL与真实语义具有强经验一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自主系统运行时监控依赖将连续传感器观测映射到低维状态变量上的离散逻辑命题，这种抽象在感知驱动的场景中失效——需要额外学习模块，这些模块通常计算开销大、脆弱且语义对齐差。

Method: 提出嵌入时序逻辑（ETL），在嵌入空间中定义谓词（通过观测嵌入与参考嵌入的距离），组合时序算子表达复杂时序行为，并设计ETL监控器结合保形校准过程实现可靠、面向安全的谓词评估。

Result: 在多个操作环境中，ETL与真实语义（ground-truth semantics）实现了强经验一致性，包括对时序组合行为的准确监控。

Conclusion: ETL有效解决了传统监控方法在感知驱动场景中的局限性，提供了一种直接在嵌入空间进行监控的新范式，实现了对高层感知概念和时序组合行为的可靠监控。

Abstract: Runtime monitoring of autonomous systems traditionally relies on mapping continuous sensor observations to discrete logical propositions defined over low-dimensional state variables. This abstraction breaks down in perception-driven settings, where such mappings require additional learned modules that are often computationally expensive, brittle, and semantically misaligned. In this work, we propose Embedding Temporal Logic (ETL), a temporal logic that performs monitoring directly in learned embedding spaces. ETL defines predicates through distances between observed embeddings and target embeddings derived from reference observations. This formulation allows specifications to capture high-level perceptual concepts, such as similarity to visual goals or avoidance of semantic regions, that are difficult or impossible to express using traditional predicates. By composing these predicates with temporal operators, ETL naturally expresses temporally extended and sequential perceptual behaviors. We introduce ETL monitors for evaluating specifications over bounded embedding traces, along with a conformal calibration procedure that provides reliable and safety-oriented predicate evaluation. We evaluate our approach across multiple manipulation environments to show that ETL achieves strong empirical agreement with ground-truth semantics, including accurate monitoring of temporally composed behaviors.

</details>


### [66] [Multi-Rollout On-Policy Distillation via Peer Successes and Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12652)
*Weichen Yu,Xiaomin Li,Yizhou Zhao,Xiaoze Liu,Ruowang Zhang,Haixin Wang,Yinyi Luo,Chen Henry Wu,Gaurav Mittal,Matt Fredrikson,Yu Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多 rollout 在策略蒸馏（MOPD）框架，通过利用学生模型对同一提示的多次采样结果（成功与失败轨迹）构建更丰富的教师信号，在编程、数学推理、科学问答和工具使用等基准上显著优于标准在策略蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在策略蒸馏（OPD）方法将每个 rollout 独立处理，忽略了同一提示下多次尝试之间的信息关联，导致稀疏验证者奖励提供的监督信号有限，无法有效指导模型在何处推理成功或失败。

Method: 提出多 rollout 在策略蒸馏（MOPD），利用学生模型的局部 rollout 组（包含成功和失败轨迹）来构建更有信息量的教师信号。具体包括两种 peer 上下文构建方式：正向 peer 模仿和对比式成功-失败条件化，前者利用成功轨迹提供正例，后者结合成败轨迹提供结构化对比信号。

Result: 在竞争性编程、数学推理、科学问答和工具使用多个基准上，MOPD 一致优于标准在策略基线。进一步分析表明，混合成功-失败上下文能够使教师分数与验证者奖励更好对齐，证明了性能提升来源于更忠实、实例自适应的监督。

Conclusion: 有效的在策略蒸馏应利用学生模型的多 rollout 试错行为（探索-利用），而非将每次 rollout 视为独立样本。MOPD 通过 peer 条件化蒸馏框架，成功将同一提示下的多次尝试信息整合为更密集、更结构化的教师信号。

Abstract: Large language models are often post-trained with sparse verifier rewards, which indicate whether a sampled trajectory succeeds but provide limited guidance about where reasoning succeeds or fails. On-policy distillation (OPD) offers denser token-level supervision by training on student-generated trajectories, yet existing methods typically distill each rollout independently and ignore the other attempts sampled for the same prompt. We introduce Multi-Rollout On-Policy Distillation (MOPD), a peer-conditioned distillation framework that uses the student's local rollout group to construct more informative teacher signals. MOPD conditions the teacher on both successful and failed peer rollouts: successes provide positive evidence for valid reasoning patterns, while failures provide structured negative evidence about plausible mistakes to avoid. We study two peer-context constructions: positive peer imitation and contrastive success-failure conditioning. Experiments on competitive programming, mathematical reasoning, scientific question answering, and tool-use benchmarks show that MOPD consistently improves over standard on-policy baselines. Further teacher-signal analysis shows that mixed success-failure contexts better align teacher scores with verifier rewards, indicating that the gains arise from more faithful, instance-adaptive supervision. These results indicate that effective on-policy distillation should exploit the student's multi-rollout trial-and-error behavior rather than treating rollouts as isolated samples.

</details>


### [67] [ERPPO: Entropy Regularization-based Proximal Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13131)
*Changha Lee,Gyusang Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MAPPO在多维环境中因非平稳观测无法提取最优策略的问题，本文提出基于熵正则化的近端策略优化(ERPPO)，通过分布时空模糊度(DSA)学习器估计不确定性，并采用自适应L1/L2正则化调整策略更新，在AirSim海事搜索实验中提升了检测精度和梯度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAPPO在多维环境中因观测非平稳性无法提取最优策略，导致目标检测失败和搜索策略欠佳。

Method: 提出ERPPO，包含(1)分布时空模糊度(DSA)学习器，用于估计非平稳约束下的目标检测不确定性；(2)熵正则化项，在高模糊度观测时施加强L1正则化以鼓励探索，低模糊度时施加弱L2正则化以稳定优化。

Result: 在AirSim海事搜索场景中，ERPPO比MAPPO具有更高的梯度，提高了精度性能，有效抑制了视觉不确定条件下的虚假检测。

Conclusion: ERPPO通过动态熵正则化有效解决了多维环境下的非平稳观测问题，在时间关键型操作中提升了目标定位成功率和检测可靠性。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO) is a variant of the Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm, specifically tailored for multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). MAPPO optimizes cooperative multi-agent settings by employing a centralized critic with decentralized actors. However, in case of multi-dimensional environment, MAPPO can not extract optimal policy due to non-stationary agent observation. To overcome this problem, we introduce a novel approach, Entropy Regularization-based Proximal Policy Optimization (ERPPO). For the policy optimization, we first define the object detection ambiguity under multi-dimensional observation environment. Distributional Spatiotemporal Ambiguity (DSA) learner is trained to estimate object detection uncertainty in non-stationary constraints. Then, we enhance PPO with a novel Entropy Regularization term. This regularization dynamically adjusts the policy update by applying a stronger (L1) regularization in high-ambiguity observation to encourage significant exploratory actions and a weaker (L2) regularization in low-ambiguity observation to stabilize the proximal policy optimization. This approach is designed to enhance the probability of successful object localization in time-critical operations by reducing detection failures and optimizing search policy. Experiments on a testbed with AirSim-based maritime searching scenarios show that the proposed ERPPO improves accuracy performance. Our proposed method improves higher gradient than MAPPO. Qualitative results confirm that ERPPO effectiveness in terms of suppressing false detection in visually uncertain conditions.

</details>


### [68] [Plan Before You Trade: Inference-Time Optimization for RL Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12653)
*Eun Go,Rohan Deb,Arindam Banerjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FPILOT，一种基于模型预测控制思想的推理时优化框架，通过在推理时利用价格预测轨迹优化投资组合管理策略，无需重新训练即可提升各类预训练策略的收益和风险调整指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习投资组合智能体在训练和部署时采用静态策略，无法在推理时利用价格预测信息，限制了策略的适应性和性能上限。

Method: 受模型预测控制启发，利用未来价格不依赖单智能体投资组合分配的关键洞察，使用预测模型生成多步价格轨迹，构建基于分配的可想象回报目标，在推理时优化策略后执行单步交易。该框架兼容任何预训练智能体，无需重新训练。

Result: 在TradeMaster DJ30基准上对五种策略学习算法进行评估，FPILOT在总回报和夏普比率、Sortino比率、Calmar比率等风险调整指标上均产生一致改进，其中随机策略比确定性策略获益更多。使用合成预测数据表明，性能提升随预测质量提高而持续改善。

Conclusion: FPILOT有效桥接了金融预测与强化学习策略之间的鸿沟，其性能随预测技术进步而持续提升，具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents for portfolio management are typically trained and deployed as static policies, with no mechanism for using price forecasts at inference time. We propose $\text{FPILOT}$ (**Fin**ancial **P**lugin **I**nference-time **L**earning for **O**ptimal **T**rading), a plugin inference-time optimization framework inspired by Model Predictive Control (MPC). Our key structural insight is that future prices mostly do not depend on one agent's portfolio allocation, so a suitable predictive model can produce a multi-step price trajectory without iterative action-conditioned rollouts as in typical reinforcement learning. At each decision step, we use the forecaster's predicted price trajectory to construct an allocation-based imagined return objective, and optimize the policy at inference-time before executing one step of the trade. Our framework is compatible with any pre-trained agent and adapts the policy to the forecaster's predictions without any retraining. Evaluated across five policy learning algorithms on the TradeMaster DJ30 benchmark, $\text{FPILOT}$ produces consistent improvements in total return and return-based risk-adjusted metrics (Sharpe, Sortino, Calmar), with stochastic policies benefiting more than deterministic ones. Further, using synthetic forecasts at calibrated quality levels, we show that gains consistently improve with forecaster quality, suggesting that our performance will improve based on advances in financial forecasting.

</details>


### [69] [Trajectory-Level Data Augmentation for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13401)
*Tobias Schmähling,Matthias Burkhardt,Tobias Windisch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向离线强化学习的数据增强方法，专门解决主动定位问题中从少量次优轨迹训练模型的需求。该方法利用任务结构、奖励与价值函数之间的几何关系以及日志策略的数学特性，通过轨迹级增强技术提升数据质量，并在不同维度和部分可观测条件下验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习通常面临数据量有限且轨迹质量次优的问题，导致模型训练效果不佳。现有方法未能充分利用任务结构信息和轨迹之间的几何关系来增强数据，因此需要一种能够从少量次优轨迹中高效学习的数据增强方法。

Method: 提出一种基于轨迹的数据增强技术，利用任务结构、奖励与价值函数之间的几何关系，以及日志策略的数学特性。该方法在数据收集过程中支持次优日志策略，通过几何变换生成新轨迹来提升数据质量。

Result: 在不同维度的定位任务以及部分可观测条件下，该方法在离线强化学习性能上取得了提升，验证了其有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的轨迹级数据增强方法能够有效利用任务几何结构和日志策略特性，从少量次优轨迹中提升离线强化学习性能，并具有理论保障和实证支撑。

Abstract: We propose a data augmentation method for offline reinforcement learning, motivated by active positioning problems. Particularly, our approach enables the training of off-policy models from a limited number of suboptimal trajectories. We introduce a trajectory-based augmentation technique that exploits task structure and the geometric relationship between rewards, value functions, and mathematical properties of logging policies. During data collection, our augmentation supports suboptimal logging policies, leading to higher data quality and improved offline reinforcement learning performance. We provide theoretical justification for these strategies and validate them empirically across positioning tasks of varying dimensionality and under partial observability.

</details>


### [70] [scShapeBench: Discovering geometry from high dimensional scRNAseq data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12662)
*Andrew J Steindl,João Felipe Rocha,Brian Tshilengi Di Bassinga,Zachary Warren,Matthew Scicluna,César Miguel Valdez Córdova,Shabarni Gupta,Leire Torices,Daniel Neumann,Timothy J. Mann,Ihuan Gunawan,Dhananjay Bhaskar,John G Lock,Christine L Chaffer,Guy Wolf,Smita Krishnaswamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出scShapeBench基准数据集和scReebTower方法，用于单细胞高维点云数据的自动形状检测（聚类、轨迹、多分支、原型四种形状），通过扩散几何提取Reeb图实现形状识别与下游流程选择的自动化，在合成和真实数据上优于PAGA和Mapper基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单细胞分析流程（如Seurat、Monocle、MIOFlow等）各自预设特定数据结构（聚类、树状、轨迹），研究人员需人工目测判断数据形状后再选择流程。随着AI科学家自动体的兴起，迫切需要自动化的形状检测方法来指导下游分析流程的选择。

Method: 1) 构建scShapeBench基准数据集，包含从真实骨架图采样的合成数据和经专家标注（分为聚类、单一轨迹、多分支、原型四类）的真实单细胞数据；2) 提出scReebTower基线方法，利用扩散几何提取Reeb图，将可视化与流程选择相连接；3) 提供拓扑感知的评估指标。

Result: 在合成和真实数据上，scReebTower在形状检测任务上的表现优于现有基线方法PAGA和Mapper。

Conclusion: 本文贡献包括基准数据集、评估指标和自动化形状检测基线方法，为单细胞数据分析中的流程自动化选择提供了基础。

Abstract: High-dimensional point cloud data arise across many scientific domains, especially single-cell biology. The shapes or topologies of these datasets determine the types of information that can be extracted. For example, clustered data supports cell-type identification, trajectory structures support transition analysis, and archetypal structures capture continua of cellular behaviors. Existing analysis pipelines often assume a specific shape. The standard Seurat pipeline combines UMAP visualization with Louvain clustering and therefore assumes clustered data, while tools such as Monocle and SPADE assume tree-like structures, and flow-based models such as MIOFlow and Conditional Flow Matching target trajectories. Choosing which pipeline to apply is therefore often left to bioinformaticians who visually inspect datasets before selecting an analysis strategy. With the rise of agentic AI scientists, automating shape detection is increasingly important for selecting downstream analysis pipelines. To address this problem, we introduce scShapeBench, a benchmark dataset for shape detection containing both synthetic and expert-annotated single-cell datasets. Synthetic datasets are sampled from ground-truth skeleton graphs with controlled variance. Real single-cell datasets are curated from diverse sources and annotated by experts into four categories: clusters, single trajectory, multi-branching, and archetypal. We additionally introduce scReebTower, a baseline method that uses diffusion geometry to extract Reeb graphs and connect visualization with pipeline selection. We provide topology-aware evaluation metrics and compare scReebTower against PAGA and Mapper on synthetic and real data. Our results indicate that scReebTower outperforms existing baselines. Overall, our contributions span benchmarks, evaluation metrics, and a baseline for automated shape detection in single-cell data.

</details>


### [71] [ODRPO: Ordinal Decompositions of Discrete Rewards for Robust Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12667)
*Nirmal Patel,Fei Wang,Inderjit Dhillon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ODRPO框架，通过将离散奖励分解为有序二元指标序列，结构性地隔离评估噪声，在RLAIF场景中实现了比GRPO和MaxRL更鲁棒的策略优化，在Qwen2.5-7B和Qwen3-4B模型上取得了最高14.8%的相对改进，且几乎不增加训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLAIF中LLM自动评分器存在固有不稳定性（prompt敏感性和采样随机性），这种随机性会传播并破坏标准优势估计器（如GRPO和MaxRL），导致全局学习信号退化。简单的多数投票降噪方法计算成本过高，亟需一种既能鲁棒处理噪声又保持计算效率的框架。

Method: ODRPO将离散的多级奖励（如1-10分）分解为有序的二元指示器序列，每个指示器对应一个递进的成功阈值。方法独立计算每个阈值下的优势并累积，从而防止异常评估污染全局更新，同时形成隐式的方差感知学习课程。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B和Qwen3-4B模型上，ODRPO相比基线方法在FACTS-grounding-v2上取得最高14.8%的相对提升，在Alpaca-Evals上取得7.5%的提升，且几乎不增加每步训练计算开销。

Conclusion: ODRPO提供了在RLAIF嘈杂的离散评估环境中可扩展且鲁棒的模型对齐框架，理论分析也证实了其优化稳定性。

Abstract: The alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) utilizes Reinforcement Learning from AI Feedback (RLAIF) for non-verifiable domains such as long-form question answering and open-ended instruction following. These domains often rely on LLM based auto-raters to provide granular, multi-tier discrete rewards (e.g., 1-10 rubrics) that are inherently stochastic due to prompt sensitivity and sampling randomness. We empirically verify the stochasticity of auto-raters that can propagate and corrupt standard advantage estimators like GRPO and MaxRL, as a noisy reward samples can skew normalization statistics and degrade the global learning signal. Empirically, sampling more rewards and taking majority voting may reduce the noise and improve performance, but this approach is computationally expensive. To address this bottleneck, we introduce $\textbf{O}$rdinal $\textbf{D}$ecomposition for $\textbf{R}$obust $\textbf{P}$olicy $\textbf{O}$ptimization ($\textbf{ODRPO}$), a framework that structurally isolates evaluation noise by decomposing discrete rewards into a sequence of ordinal binary indicators. By independently computing and accumulating advantages across these progressively challenging success thresholds, ODRPO prevents outlier evaluations from corrupting the global update while establishing an implicit, variance-aware learning curriculum. Empirically, ODRPO achieves robust performance on Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen3-4B models, outperforming baselines with relative improvements of upto 14.8% on FACTS-grounding-v2 and 7.5% on Alpaca-Evals. Critically, these gains are achieved with negligible training-time overhead, as ODRPO requires no additional compute per step compared to standard estimators.
