Method: 设计两层解耦求解器：基于缓存的任务/数据/模型信号进行物化，结合预热探测、已实现子集状态、局部配方编辑、高斯过程辅助排序和停滞触发的重新播种来搜索最优食谱。

Result: 在90K指令池上，AutoSelection在三个基座模型上均取得最强分布内推理平均性能，超过全量训练、随机配方搜索、随机top-k和单算子选择器；分布外图推理结果、稳定性分析和消融实验验证了配方结构的重要性。

Conclusion: SFT数据选择中，编排算子的配方结构比单个选择算子更重要；AutoSelection能有效搜索高质量固定池子集配方，且跨模型规模（1.5B→7B）可迁移。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data selection is commonly formulated as instance ranking: score each example and retain a top-$k$ subset. However, effective SFT training subsets are often produced through ordered curation recipes, where filtering, mixing, and deduplication operators jointly shape the final data distribution. We formulate this problem as fixed-pool data recipe search: given a raw instruction pool and a library of grounded operators, the goal is to discover an executable recipe that constructs a high-quality selected subset under a limited budget of full SFT evaluations, without generating, rewriting, or augmenting training samples. We introduce AutoSelection, a two-layer solver that decouples fixed-pool materialization based on cached task-, data-, and model-side signals from expensive full evaluation, using warmup probes, realized subset states, local recipe edits, Gaussian-process-assisted ranking, and stagnation-triggered reseeding. Experiments on a 90K instruction pool show that AutoSelection achieves the strongest in-distribution reasoning average across three base models, outperforming full-data training, random recipe search, random top-$k$, and single-operator selectors. Additional Out-of-distribution graph-reasoning results, search-stability analyses, structural ablations, and 1.5B-to-7B transfer checks further show that recipe structure matters beyond individual selection operators. Code is available at https://github.com/w253/AutoSelection.

</details>


### [108] [Separating Shortcut Transition from Cross-Family OOD Failure in a Minimal Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12945)
*Hongmin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过最小化二元分类模型，分离了捷径吸引、捷径规则转换和跨族群的OOD失败三个概念，证明训练相关性、学习到的捷径使用和测试失败并不必然一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究常用"捷径特征"解释分布外（OOD）失败，但训练相关性、模型学到的捷径使用与测试时的失败可能并不一致，亟需厘清三者关系。

Method: 构建一个含一个不变坐标和一个族群相关捷径坐标的最小二元模型，在确定性和噪声两种条件下分析逻辑回归+岭正则化的行为，并通过合成实验验证。

Result: 确定性条件下，平均捷径正相关性使ERM产生正捷径权重，但岭正则化可保持分类器由不变特征主导，避免OOD失败；当不变坐标含噪声时，训练捷径信号超过不变信号会导致模型切换到捷径规则，但切换是否造成OOD失败取决于保留族群：弱捷径相关性导致正超额风险，符号翻转族群导致高于随机水平的错误。

Conclusion: 训练侧的转换与测试侧的失败具有不同条件，同一训练转换对不同族群产生不同后果，需将捷径吸引、规则转换和跨族群OOD失败作为分离现象分析。

Abstract: Shortcut features are often invoked to explain out-of-distribution (OOD) failure, but training correlation, learned shortcut use, and test-time failure need not coincide. We study a minimal binary model with one invariant coordinate and one family-dependent shortcut coordinate. In the deterministic regime, positive average shortcut correlation pulls logistic ERM toward positive shortcut weight, but ridge regularization keeps the classifier invariant-dominated and prevents deterministic OOD failure. When the invariant coordinate is noisy, ridge-logistic ERM switches to the shortcut rule once the training shortcut signal exceeds the invariant signal. Whether that transition causes failure depends on the held-out family: weaker shortcut correlation yields positive excess risk, and sign-flipped families yield above-chance error. Synthetic checks match these analytic regimes and show that the same training-side transition can have different held-out consequences. The model separates shortcut attraction, shortcut-rule transition, and cross-family OOD failure.

</details>


### [109] [U-HNO: A U-shaped Hybrid Neural Operator with Sparse-Point Adaptive Routing for Non-stationary PDE Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12965)
*Yingzhe Ma,Xiao Yang,Yuxin Xie,Zihan Xiong,Jinliang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出U-HNO，一种U型混合神经算子，核心是稀疏点自适应路由（SPAR），通过逐像素硬掩码动态选择全局傅里叶分支与局部多尺度高斯分支的融合比例，在多种PDE基准上取得SOTA rollout精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子在处理PDE解中同时存在的全局光滑输运和局部尖锐特征（激波、界面、高频内容）时面临两难：傅里叶架构全局高效但欠解析局部非光滑特征，局部架构恢复细节但长程传播和滚动稳定性差。现有混合算子采用固定的空间均匀融合，在所有位置强制相同权衡，无法自适应地处理不同区域的需求。

Method: 提出U-HNO（U型混合神经算子），核心设计是SPAR（稀疏点自适应路由）：1）在每空间位置，通过逐像素硬掩码决定全局傅里叶分支与局部多尺度高斯分支的贡献；2）稀疏率是路由信号局部对比度的函数，光滑区和激波对齐区获得不同的全局-局部混合；3）嵌入分级编码器-瓶颈-解码器主干，带跳跃连接，双分支和门控在每个分辨率下运行；4）训练结合逐点监督、有限差分H^1梯度项和波段谱一致性正则化。

Result: 在1D Burgers、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky、KdV、2D advection、Allen-Cahn、Navier-Stokes、Darcy flow和3D跨声速可压缩Navier-Stokes（PDEBench）等基准上，U-HNO在多数任务中取得SOTA rollout精度（相对L^2和H^1指标），在尖锐局部特征主导的问题上增益最大；消融实验表明移除任何组件均会显著降低rollout误差。

Conclusion: U-HNO通过SPAR实现了全局与局部计算的自适应空间可变融合，有效解决了PDE求解中光滑背景与局部尖锐特征并存的挑战，在多种PDE问题上验证了其优越的长期预测能力。

Abstract: Solutions to many partial differential equations (PDEs) display coexisting smooth global transport and localized sharp features within a single trajectory: shock fronts, thin interfaces, and concentrated high-frequency content sit on top of slowly varying backgrounds. This poses a challenge for neural operators: Fourier-based architectures mix nonlocal interactions efficiently but tend to under-resolve localized non-smooth features, whereas spatially local architectures recover fine detail at the cost of long-range propagation and rollout stability. Existing hybrid operators paper over this tension with a fixed, spatially uniform fusion that forces the same trade-off everywhere.
  We propose U-HNO, a U-shaped hybrid neural operator whose central design is Sparse-Point Adaptive Routing (SPAR): at every spatial location, a per-pixel hard mask selects whether the global Fourier branch or the local multi-scale Gaussian branch should dominate, and the sparsity ratio is a function of the local contrast of the routing signal, so smooth and shock-aligned regions receive different mixtures of global and local computation. SPAR is embedded in a hierarchical encoder-bottleneck-decoder backbone with skip connections so that the dual branches and the gate operate at every resolution. Training combines pointwise supervision with a finite-difference H^1 gradient term and a band-wise spectral consistency regularizer.
  Across benchmarks spanning 1D Burgers, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky, KdV, 2D advection, Allen-Cahn, Navier-Stokes, Darcy flow, and 3D transonic compressible Navier-Stokes from PDEBench, U-HNO achieves state-of-the-art rollout accuracy on the majority of tasks in both relative L^2 and H^1 metrics, with the largest gains on problems dominated by sharp localized features. Ablations show that removing any single component substantially degrades rollout error.

</details>


### [110] [Controlling Logical Collapse in LLMs via Algebraic Ontology Projection over F2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12968)
*Hisashi Miyashita,Mgnite Inc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出代数本体投影（AOP），将LLM隐藏状态投影到伽罗瓦域F2中，仅用42个关系对作为代数密钥，在零样本设置下对未见概念对实现了最高93.33%的包含关系预测准确率。同时提出语义结晶化（SC）指标量化约束满足程度，发现系统提示作为代数边界条件，且指令微调可防止晚期层塌陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型内部是否以形式可验证的代数结构编码本体论关系，而非仅通过统计近似。

Method: 提出AOP方法将LLM隐藏状态投影到F2域，利用里氏替换原则（LSP）约束，仅用42个关系对作为代数密钥；引入SC指标量化F2约束满足度；通过系统提示作为代数边界条件进行分析。

Result: Gemma-2 Instruct在优化提示下零样本包含准确率最高达93.33%，多个模型家族一致达到86.67%；发现晚期层塌陷现象（10个条件中7个出现），指令微调可防止此退化。

Conclusion: 前向计算可被重新理解为代数组织的迭代过程，LLM的逻辑结构有望不仅是近似获得，而是形式上可访问的。

Abstract: Do large language models internally encode ontological relations in a formally verifiable algebraic structure? We introduce Algebraic Ontology Projection (AOP), which projects LLM hidden states into the Galois Field F2 under Liskov Substitution Principle constraints, using only 42 relational pairs as algebraic keys. AOP achieves up to 93.33% zero-shot inclusion accuracy on unseen concept pairs (Gemma-2 Instruct with optimized prompt), with consistent 86.67% accuracy observed across multiple model families -- with no model tuning, but through prompt alone.
  This algebraic structure is strongly layer-dependent. We introduce Semantic Crystallisation (SC), a metric that quantifies F2 constraint satisfaction relative to a random baseline and predicts zero-shot accuracy without held-out data. System prompts act as algebraic boundary conditions: only their combination with instruction tuning prevents Late-layer Collapse -- a systematic degradation of logical consistency in the final layers, observed in 7 of 10 conditions. These findings reframe forward computation as an iterative process of algebraic organisation, and open a path toward LLMs whose logical structure is not merely approximated, but formally accessible.

</details>


### [111] [Revisiting Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards from a Contrastive Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12969)
*Feng Zhang,Xinhong Ma,Ziqiang Dong,Xi Leng,Jianfei Zhao,Xin Sun,Yang Yang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出GRPO存在似然不对齐评分和分数不敏感信用分配两个结构性问题，并提出ConSPO框架，通过长度归一化的序列对数概率替代裁剪比率评分，结合组内InfoNCE对比目标和课程调度间隔，在数学推理任务上显著优于多个强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法（以GRPO为代表）存在两种结构性缺陷：1) 使用裁剪比率替代评分，优化的分数与生成似然不对齐；2) 组内正负样本间的信用分配不区分相对分数差距，导致优化效率低。亟需一种对齐似然且分数敏感的策略优化方法。

Method: 1) 将GRPO重新表述为加权正负分数差的判别形式，揭示其结构问题；2) 提出ConSPO框架：用长度归一化的序列对数概率替代裁剪比率评分；3) 采用组内InfoNCE对比目标，使正样本与同组负样本对比，信用分配依赖相对分数；4) 引入课程调度间隔，从粗粒度排序逐步过渡到强分离。

Result: 在不同骨干模型、参数规模和训练数据集上，ConSPO在具有挑战性的数学推理基准上一致优于多个强RLVR基线。

Conclusion: ConSPO通过对比序列级策略优化有效解决了GRPO的结构性缺陷，证明了评分-似然对齐和分数敏感信用分配对于RLVR的重要性。

Abstract: RLVR has become a widely adopted paradigm for improving LLMs' reasoning capabilities, and GRPO is one of its most representative algorithms. In this paper, we first show that GRPO admits an equivalent discriminative reformulation as a weighted positive-negative score difference. Under this view, GRPO increases sequence-level scores of verified positive rollouts and decreases those of negative rollouts, where the scores are averages of clipped token-level importance sampling ratios. This reformulation reveals two structural limitations of GRPO: likelihood-misaligned scoring, where clipped ratio-based surrogate scores are optimized instead of generation likelihoods, and score-insensitive credit assignment, where rollout-level credit is assigned without accounting for relative score gaps between positive and negative rollouts in the same group. To address these limitations, we propose ConSPO, a framework for Contrastive Sequence-level Policy Optimization in RLVR. ConSPO replaces GRPO's clipped ratio-based scores with length-normalized sequence log-probabilities, aligning the optimized rollout scores with the likelihoods used in autoregressive generation. It then optimizes a group-wise InfoNCE-style objective that contrasts each positive rollout against negative distractors from the same group, enabling credit assignment to depend on their relative scores. This contrastive formulation amplifies updates for poorly separated positives while concentrating suppressive updates on high-scoring negatives. Moreover, ConSPO introduces a curriculum-scheduled margin, guiding optimization from coarse positive-negative ordering in early training toward stronger separation in later stages. Extensive evaluations across diverse backbone models, parameter scales, and training datasets show that ConSPO consistently outperforms several strong RLVR baselines on challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [112] [CoRe-Gen: Robust Spectrum-to-Structure Generation under Imperfect Fingerprint Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12980)
*Tianbo Liu,Chixiang Lu,Jing Hao,Hengyu Zhang,Lifei Wang,Haibo Jiang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoRe-Gen通过频谱预训练、频率感知的指纹损坏和结构感知的自回归解码，解决了质谱到分子结构生成中训练与部署条件不匹配的问题，在NPLIB1上达到SOTA（Top-1 19.54%，Top-10 29.92%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谱图到结构的两阶段方法（先预测指纹再解码为结构）存在条件不匹配问题：训练时解码器使用真实（oracle）指纹，而部署时依赖预测指纹，导致结构误差尤其是长尾子结构的错误传播。

Method: 1) 通过合成频谱预训练编码器改善中间条件；2) 在解码器训练中引入频率感知的指纹损坏来匹配部署时的噪声；3) 使用组合SELFIES表示、辅助结构监督和轻量化学约束进行结构感知的自回归解码以缓解残差错误。

Result: 在NPLIB1上达到19.54% Top-1和29.92% Top-10精确匹配准确率，建立新SOTA；在更具挑战性的MassSpecGym基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: CoRe-Gen在保持自回归解码效率优势的同时，为实际条件下的鲁棒谱图到结构生成提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Molecular structure elucidation from tandem mass spectra (MS/MS) remains challenging, particularly for de novo generation beyond database coverage. A common approach decomposes the task into spectrum-to-fingerprint prediction followed by fingerprint-to-structure decoding, enabling the use of large-scale molecular corpora. However, at deployment, the decoder relies on predicted rather than oracle fingerprints, introducing structured errors that propagate into generation. This results in a fundamental condition mismatch, where models trained on clean inputs must operate under noisy, biased predictions, especially for long-tail substructures.
  We present CoRe-Gen that explicitly addresses this gap. CoRe-Gen improves the intermediate condition via synthetic-spectrum pretraining of the encoder, matches deployment-time noise through frequency-aware fingerprint corruption during decoder training, and mitigates residual errors using structure-aware autoregressive decoding with compositional SELFIES representations, auxiliary structural supervision, and lightweight chemical constraints. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that CoRe-Gen establishes a new state of the art on NPLIB1, achieving 19.54\% Top-1 and 29.92\% Top-10 exact-match accuracy, while remaining competitive on the more challenging MassSpecGym benchmark. Importantly, CoRe-Gen preserves the efficiency advantages of autoregressive decoding, providing a practical and scalable solution for robust spectrum-to-structure generation under realistic conditions.

</details>


### [113] [Decision Tree Learning on Product Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12983)
*Arshia Soltani Moakahr,Faraz Ghahremani,Kiarash Banihashem,MohammadTaghi Hajiaghayi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将贪心决策树算法的理论保证从均匀分布扩展到了更一般的任意乘积分布，并给出了无需先验知识的参数无关算法，显著拓展了理论覆盖范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贪心决策树算法在实践中极为成功，但理论分析长期缺乏严谨保证。Blanc等人首次给出了贪心算法的理论保证，但仅适用于均匀分布。本文旨在将其推广到更实际、更一般的任意乘积分布下。

Method: 理论分析上，证明了对任意能被最优决策树（大小s、最大深度D_opt、平均深度Δ_opt）计算的函数f，贪心启发式在任意乘积分布下构造的ε-近似树的大小至多为exp(Δ_opt D_opt log(e/ε))。同时设计了完全参数无关（无需知道最优树的大小或深度）的自顶向下贪心算法。

Result: 在最优树为满二叉树的情况下，本文的界优于Blanc等人的结果，且适用于更广的分布类。此外，参数无关算法无需任何先验知识，具有更强的实用性。

Conclusion: 本文将贪心决策树的理论分析从均匀分布扩展到任意乘积分布，并设计了更实用的参数无关算法，缩小了理论与实际应用之间的差距。

Abstract: Decision tree learning has long been a central topic in theoretical computer science, driven by its practical importance. A fundamental and widely used method for decision tree construction is the top-down greedy heuristic, which recursively splits on the most influential variable. Despite its empirical success, theoretical analysis of this heuristic has been limited. A recent breakthrough by Blanc et al. (ITCS, 2020) provided the first rigorous theoretical guarantees for the greedy approach, but only under the uniform distribution. We extend this analysis to the more general and practically relevant setting of arbitrary product distributions. Our main result shows that for any function $f$ computable by an optimal decision tree of size $s$, maximum depth $D_{\text{opt}}$, and average depth $Δ_{\text{opt}}$, the greedy heuristic constructs an $ε$-approximating tree whose size grows at most with $\exp\bigl(Δ_{\text{opt}} D_{\text{opt}} \log(e/ε)\bigr)$. In the special case where the optimal tree is a full binary tree, this bound improves upon the bound of Blanc et al. and holds under a strictly broader class of distributions. Moreover, we present an algorithm based on the top-down greedy heuristic that is entirely parameter-free -- it requires no prior knowledge of the optimal tree's size or depth -- offering a practical advantage over Blanc et al.'s method.

</details>


### [114] [Not Just RLHF: Why Alignment Alone Won't Fix Multi-Agent Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12991)
*Adarsh Kumarappan,Ananya Mujoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现，基于LLM的多智能体管道在模拟同行争论时，答案会从正确翻转为错误（称为"屈服率"），这一现象并非主要由RLHF导致的谄媚行为引起，而是源于预训练基座模型本身就存在的机制性缺陷。通过激活修补定位，问题集中在中间层注意力窗口，MLP贡献可忽略；该攻击面由通道框架和共识强度两个独立因素决定，多数共识下产生47.5个百分点的屈服率差距。一名正确论证的异议者可将屈服率降低54-73个百分点，而最强的提示级防御在攻击变体下失效。缓解措施应聚焦于管道层面的结构化异议机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示LLM多智能体管道在同行争论下从正确翻转为错误答案的机制，验证该现象是否由RLHF导致的谄媚行为引起，并探索有效的防御策略。

Method: 在四个模型家族上对比基座模型和指令微调变体；使用激活修补定位注意力窗口和MLP的因果权重；分解通道框架和共识强度两个独立因素；采用两种汇聚的激活空间干预方法；测试异议者和提示级防御效果。

Result: 基座模型比指令变体表现出更高的屈服率；注意力窗口承担主要因果权重，MLP贡献可忽略；修补窗口以上层可恢复96%的干净-受压正确率差距；通道框架和共识强度交互产生47.5个百分点的屈服率差距；压制干净推理特征而非激活谄媚回路；一名异议者将屈服率降低54-73个百分点；提示级防御在攻击变体外失效。

Conclusion: 缓解措施应针对机制本身，采用管道层面的结构化异议，而非提示级防御。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent pipelines flip from correct to incorrect answers under simulated peer disagreement at rates we term yield, a vulnerability widely attributed to RLHF-induced sycophancy. We test this attribution across four model families and find it largely wrong: pretrained base models exhibit the same substitution pattern as their Instruct variants, averaging higher yield than Instruct. Using activation patching, we localize the corruption to a narrow mid-layer window where attention carries the causal weight and MLP contribution is negligible; patching above this window restores 96% of the clean-to-pressured P(correct) gap. The attack surface decomposes into two independent factors (channel framing and consensus strength) whose interaction produces a 47.5 percentage-point yield gap at majority consensus, preserved across jury sizes $N \in \{4, 5, 6\}$. Two converging activation-space interventions show that pressure suppresses clean-reasoning features rather than activating a new sycophancy circuit. A single correctly-arguing dissenter reduces yield by 54-73 percentage points across all framings tested, whereas the strongest prompt-level defense fails on attack variants outside its design surface. Mitigations should target the mechanism, structured dissent at the pipeline level, rather than prompt-level defenses.

</details>


### [115] [DP-Muon: Differentially Private Optimization via Matrix-Orthogonalized Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12994)
*Jihwan Kim,Chenglin Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-Muon，一种将差分隐私与Muon优化器结合的算法，证明了其隐私保证与标准DP-SGD相同，并分析了噪声在Newton-Schulz正交化中引入的热平滑偏差，进而提出偏差修正版本DP-MuonBC，在E2E和DART任务上展示了更好的微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器在深度学习中被证明有效，但其与差分隐私训练的交互尚未被系统分析。现有DP优化方法主要针对SGD设计，而Muon涉及动量与非线性正交化等后处理步骤，是否引入额外隐私成本或偏差尚不明确。

Method: 提出DP-Muon：对每样本矩阵梯度进行裁剪，对裁剪后的批次平均添加高斯噪声，再依次应用动量和Newton-Schulz正交化作为后处理。理论上证明了隐私保证继承自同批次子采样高斯计数器的保证，无需额外隐私成本；建立了在矩阵裁剪下的有限时域和渐近驻点保证，并分离了优化误差、裁剪残差、隐私噪声和Newton-Schulz近似误差。分析了DP引入的偏差来源于Newton-Schulz非线性映射中的矩阵值热平滑偏差，并据此提出偏差修正版本DP-MuonBC。

Result: 在E2E和DART数据集上，Muon风格的矩阵更新改善了私有微调效果；DP-MuonBC进一步提升了实用性，且不增加隐私预算。

Conclusion: DP-Muon及其偏差修正版本DP-MuonBC为私有训练提供了一种新的有效优化器选择，证明了矩阵值优化器可以与差分隐私无缝结合，且偏差可被校正以获得更好性能。

Abstract: We study differentially private (DP) training with Muon, a matrix-valued optimizer that updates hidden-layer weights using momentum followed by Newton--Schulz orthogonalization. While DP-SGD is well understood, the interaction between per-example clipping, Gaussian noise, momentum, and nonlinear orthogonalization in Muon has not been systematically analyzed. We formulate DP-Muon, a private Muon procedure that clips per-example matrix gradients, adds Gaussian noise to the clipped lot average, and then applies momentum and Newton--Schulz orthogonalization as post-processing. We prove that DP-Muon inherits the privacy guarantee certified by the corresponding same-lot subsampled Gaussian accountant, with no additional privacy cost from Muon-specific post-processing. On the optimization side, we establish finite-horizon and vanishing stationarity guarantees under per-matrix clipping, with bounds that separate optimization error, clipping residual, privacy noise, and Newton--Schulz approximation error. We further show that the DP-induced bias in Muon arises not in the linear momentum buffer itself, but after the nonlinear Newton--Schulz map, where Gaussian noise induces a matrix-valued heat-smoothing bias. This motivates DP-MuonBC, a bias-corrected variant that removes the leading output-level bias term while preserving the same privacy guarantee. Experiments on E2E and DART show that Muon-style matrix updates improve private fine-tuning, and that DP-MuonBC further improves utility without increasing the privacy budget.

</details>


### [116] [F-GRPO: Factorized Group-Relative Policy Optimization for Unified Candidate Generation and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12995)
*Rohan Surana,Gagan Mundada,Junda Wu,Xintong Li,Yizhu Jiao,Bowen Jin,Sizhe Zhou,Tong Yu,Ritwik Sinha,Jiawei Han,Jingbo Shang,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出F-GRPO框架，将候选生成与排序统一在一个自回归序列中，通过分解式组相对策略优化实现端到端联合训练，解决了生成-排序耦合中的信用分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索流水线将候选生成与排序解耦，但排序受限于预定义候选集，与下游效用不一致。LLM虽可将两者统一为生成过程，但现有端到端优化存在信用分配缺口——模型无法区分糟糕结果是源于候选生成失败还是排序错误，导致优化不稳定且样本效率低。

Method: 提出F-GRPO框架：1）将策略分解为候选生成和排序两个阶段，共享单一LLM主干；2）设计排序不变的覆盖奖励（coverage reward）和位置感知的效用奖励（utility reward）；3）在两个阶段分别使用独立的组相对优势（group-relative advantages），实现两阶段序列级目标优化。

Result: 在序列推荐和多跳问答基准上，F-GRPO在top-ranked性能上优于GRPO和解耦基线，超过有监督方法，并与强零样本重排序器保持竞争力，且推理时无需架构改动。

Conclusion: 通过分解式策略优化统一候选生成与排序，能够有效解决信用分配问题，实现更稳定、样本高效的端到端学习，无需推理时架构变化即可提升排序性能。

Abstract: Traditional retrieval pipelines optimize utility through stages of candidate retrieval and reranking, where ranking operates over a predefined candidate set. Large Language Models (LLMs) broaden this into a generative process: given a candidate pool, an LLM can generate a subset and order it within a single autoregressive pass. However, this flexibility introduces a new optimization challenge: the model must search a combinatorial output space while receiving utility feedback only after the full ranked list is generated. Because this feedback is defined over the completed sequence, it cannot distinguish whether a poor result arises from failing to generate a relevant subset or from failing to rank that subset correctly. This credit assignment gap makes end-to-end optimization unstable and sample-inefficient. Existing systems often address this by separating candidate generation from ranking. However, such decoupling remains misaligned with downstream utility because ranking is limited by the candidate set it receives. To bridge this gap, we propose a unified framework that performs both within a single autoregressive rollout and optimizes them end-to-end via factorized group-relative policy optimization (F-GRPO). Our framework factorizes the policy into candidate generation and ranking while sharing a single LLM backbone, and jointly trains them with an order-invariant coverage reward and a position-aware utility reward. To address the resulting phase-specific credit assignment problem, we use separate group-relative advantages for generation and ranking within a two-phase sequence-level objective. Across sequential recommendation and multi-hop question answering benchmarks, F-GRPO improves top-ranked performance over GRPO and decoupled baselines, outperforms supervised alternatives, and remains competitive with strong zero-shot rerankers, with no architectural changes at inference time.

</details>


### [117] [\emph{DRIFT}: A Benchmark for Task-Free Continual Graph Learning with Continuous Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12998)
*Guiquan Sun,Xikun Zhang,Jingchao Ni,Dongjin Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出从无任务（task-free）视角重新审视持续图学习，构建了DRIFT基准，发现现有方法在无任务边界设定下性能显著下降，揭示了它们对任务边界信息的隐性依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续图学习（CGL）方法大多基于任务式（task-based）设定，假设数据流被划分为具有预定义边界的离散任务。然而，真实环境中数据分布往往连续演化且任务身份不可知，现有方法难以适应这种非平稳环境。

Method: 提出统一的无任务持续图学习形式化框架，将数据流建模为随时间变化的潜在任务分布混合体；基于高斯参数化构建DRIFT基准，覆盖从硬任务切换（hard task switches）到平滑分布漂移（smooth distributional drift）的多种过渡动态。

Result: 在无任务设定下，代表性的持续学习方法相比传统任务式协议性能显著下降；许多现有方法隐式依赖任务边界信息，在真实无任务图流中表现不佳。

Conclusion: 强调了在真实非平稳条件下研究持续图学习的重要性，并提供了DRIFT基准以推动该方向的未来研究。

Abstract: Continual graph learning (CGL) aims to learn from dynamically evolving graphs while mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Existing CGL approaches typically adopt a task-based formulation, where the data stream is partitioned into a sequence of discrete tasks with pre-defined boundaries. However, such assumptions rarely hold in real-world environments, where data distributions evolve continuously and task identity is often unavailable. To better reflect realistic non-stationary environments, we revisit continual graph learning from a task-free perspective. We propose a unified formulation that models the data stream as a time-varying mixture of latent task distributions, enabling continuous modeling of distribution drift. Based on this formulation, we construct DRIFT, a benchmark that spans a spectrum of transition dynamics ranging from hard task switches to smooth distributional drift through a Gaussian parameterization. We evaluate representative continual learning methods under this task-free setting and observe substantial performance degradation compared to traditional task-based protocols. Our findings indicate that many existing approaches implicitly rely on task boundary information and struggle under realistic task-free graph streams. This work highlights the importance of studying continual graph learning under realistic non-stationary conditions and provides a benchmark for future research in this direction. Our code is available at https://github.com/gqBond/DRIFT.

</details>


### [118] [JEDI: Joint Embedding Diffusion World Model for Online Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13013)
*Jing Yu Lim,Rushi Shah,Zarif Ikram,Samson Yu,Haozhe Ma,Tze-Yun Leong,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JEDI是首个在线端到端潜空间扩散世界模型，通过JEPA框架结合扩散去噪损失学习潜空间，在保持竞争性能的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散世界模型存在两难：像素扩散效果好但计算昂贵，潜扩散效率高但性能欠佳且依赖预训练潜编码器。作者希望结合JEPA预测表征学习和扩散迭代精炼的优势，实现首个在线端到端潜扩散世界模型。

Method: 提出JEDI框架，利用JEPA范式直接从扩散去噪损失中学习潜空间，通过去噪过程学习和预测未来潜表示（而非重建或依赖预训练模型）。理论上证明了传统JEPA目标诱导预测信息瓶颈，而条件扩散去噪具有类似的预测-压缩分解结构。

Result: 在Atari100k上具有竞争力，优于使用分离训练潜编码器的基线。相比像素扩散基线，JEDI显存降低43%，世界模型采样速度提升3倍以上，训练速度提升2.5倍。

Conclusion: 端到端预测潜表示不仅带来计算效率提升，还改变了任务级性能分布，表明潜空间学习方式对模型行为有本质影响。

Abstract: Diffusion world models have recently become competitive for online model-based reinforcement learning, but current approaches expose a tension: pixel diffusion is effective but computationally expensive while the latest latent diffusion approach improves efficiency yet performs subpar. The latter also relies on separately trained latents rather than the end-to-end world-model objectives that have driven much of modern MBRL progress. In particular, JEPA-style predictive representation learning has emerged as an especially promising direction for world modeling and MBRL. Concurrently, diffusion-style objectives have gained traction across multiple domains, with iterative refinement as a promising approach for multimodal and stochastic targets. Taken together, these trends motivate Joint Embedding DIffusion (JEDI), the first online end-to-end latent diffusion world model. JEDI learns its latent space directly from the diffusion denoising loss with a JEPA framework, using denoising to learn and predict future latents rather than relying on reconstruction and pretrained models. We provide a theoretical motivation showing that conventional JEPA objectives induce a predictive information bottleneck, and that conditional diffusion denoising admits a closely related predictive-compression decomposition. Empirically, JEDI is competitive on Atari100k and outperforms the baseline with seperately trained latents where directly comparable. Relative to the pixel diffusion baseline, JEDI uses 43% less VRAM, over 3$\times$ faster world-model sampling, and 2.5$\times$ faster training. JEDI also exhibits a markedly different task-level performance profile from the pixel baseline, suggesting that end-to-end predictive latents change more than compute alone.

</details>


### [119] [Rethinking Efficient Graph Coarsening via a Non-Selfishness Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13021)
*Xu Bai,Bin Lu,Kun Zhang,Shengbo Chen,Xinbing Wang,Chenghu Zhou,Meng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于非自私原则的图粗化方法NOPE，通过优先考虑邻域集体干扰实现线性内存和近线性计算复杂度，其加速变体NOPE*进一步将计算瓶颈从O(δ·d)降至O(d)，实验表明该方法在保持图质量的同时实现1-3个数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图粗化方法依赖基于全局信息的成对相似性匹配，每个节点独立搜索最佳伙伴，这种自私匹配范式导致巨大的计算和内存开销。

Method: 提出NOPE方法，采用非自私原则优先考虑邻域集体干扰；基于局部各向同性假设推导NOPE*变体，将干扰评估从O(δ·d)降至O(d)。

Result: NOPE*比NOPE快1.8-10倍；超越几乎所有基线方法，加速1-3个数量级；粗化图上的学习性能与原图相当，在LLM图推理任务上甚至表现更优。

Conclusion: 非自私的邻域集体干扰范式能有效替代传统自私匹配，实现高效图粗化且保持图结构语义属性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Graph coarsening is a graph dimensionality reduction technique that aims to construct a smaller and more tractable graph while preserving the essential structural and semantic properties of the original graph. However, most existing methods rely on pair-wise similarity matching, where each node independently searches for its best partner based on global information. This selfishness matching paradigm incurs substantial computational and memory overhead. To address this problem, we shift to a non-selfishness principle that prioritizes the collective interference of neighborhood in coarsening, and propose an efficient method named NOPE, which achieves linear memory consumption and near-linear computational complexity in the number of nodes. Furthermore, we derive a faster variant NOPE*, which reduces O(δ\dot d) interference evaluation to O(d) based on the local isotropy assumption, and consequently alleviates the computational bottleneck for high-degree nodes. Experimental results show that NOPE* achieves 1.8-10\times speedup over NOPE and surpass almost all baselines with 1-3 orders of magnitude acceleration. Meanwhile, learning on coarsened graphs yields comparable performance to original graphs, and can even show superior performance over LLM-based graph reasoning owing to compact graph information. The code can be available at https://github.com/dazonglian/NOPE-main.

</details>


### [120] [Offline Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games with KL Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13025)
*Claire Chen,Yuheng Zhang,Xinyu Liu,Zixuan Xie,Shuze Daniel Liu,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了离线零和马尔可夫博弈中的纳什均衡学习问题，提出KL正则化足以稳定学习并保证收敛。提出了ROSE理论框架和SOS-MD实用算法，分别实现了$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/n)$的收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线博弈学习方法通常依赖显式悲观主义来处理分布偏移，本文试图证明仅使用KL正则化即可实现稳定学习和收敛，从而简化算法设计并提升理论效率。

Method: 提出了Regularized Offline Sequential Equilibrium (ROSE)理论框架，以及基于最小二乘值估计和迭代自博弈更新的Sequential Offline Self-play Mirror Descent (SOS-MD)实用算法。

Result: ROSE在单侧可集中性假设下实现了$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/n)$的快速收敛率，优于未正则化设置下的$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{n})$。SOS-MD的最后迭代达到了相同的$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/n)$统计率，并带有$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{T})$的可忽略优化误差。

Conclusion: KL正则化可以替代显式悲观主义成为离线马尔可夫博弈中稳定学习的有效手段，所提方法在理论和算法层面均优于现有方法。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning Nash equilibria in offline two-player zero-sum Markov games. While existing approaches often rely on explicit pessimism to address distribution shift, we show that KL regularization alone suffices to stabilize learning and guarantee convergence. We first introduce Regularized Offline Sequential Equilibrium (ROSE), a theoretical framework that achieves a fast $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/n)$ convergence rate under \textit{unilateral concentrability}, improving over the standard $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{n})$ rates in unregularized settings. We then propose Sequential Offline Self-play Mirror Descent (SOS-MD), a practical model-free algorithm based on least-squares value estimation and iterative self-play updates. We prove that the last iterate of SOS-MD attains the same $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/n)$ statistical rate up to a vanishing optimization error of order $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{T})$ in the number of self-play iterations $T$.

</details>


### [121] [Understanding and Accelerating the Training of Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13026)
*Chunsan Hong,Sanghyun Lee,Chieh-Hsin Lai,Satoshi Hayakawa,Yuhta Takida,Yuki Mitsufuji,Seungryong Kim,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了掩码扩散模型（MDM）训练缓慢的原因，发现语言的位置偏移偏差（预测信息集中在附近位置）是主要因素，并提出钟形时间采样策略来加速训练，使MDM在LM1B基准上达到相同验证NLL的速度提升约4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散模型（MDM）作为自回归模型（ARM）的有前途替代方案，但训练速度显著慢于ARM，这在大规模模型扩展时成为问题。因此，研究如何加速标准MDM训练同时保持最终性能。

Method: 首先分析了MDM训练缓慢的原因，发现语言的位置偏移偏差（预测信息集中在附近位置）是主要因素。基于此发现，提出钟形时间采样（bell-shaped time sampling）作为训练策略。

Result: 在LM1B基准上，采用钟形时间采样训练的MDM达到相同验证NLL的速度比标准训练快约4倍；同时在生成困惑度、零样本困惑度和下游任务性能上也展现出更快的改进。

Conclusion: 钟形时间采样策略能有效加速MDM训练，解决其相比ARM训练缓慢的核心问题，且不影响最终性能。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive models (ARMs) for language modeling. However, MDMs are known to learn substantially more slowly than ARMs, which may become problematic when scaling MDMs to larger models. Therefore, we ask the following question: how can we accelerate standard MDM training while maintaining its final performance? To this end, we first provide a detailed analysis of why MDM training is slow. We find that the main factor is the locality bias of language: the predictive information for a token is concentrated in nearby positions. We further investigate how this bias slows learning and suggest a simple yet effective remedy: bell-shaped time sampling as a training strategy. Notably, MDMs trained with our training recipe reach the same validation negative log-likelihood (NLL) up to $\sim4\times$ faster than standard training on One Billion Word Benchmark (LM1B). We also show faster improvements in generative perplexity, zero-shot perplexity, and downstream task performance on various benchmarks.

</details>


### [122] [FeatCal: Feature Calibration for Post-Merging Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13030)
*Yanggan Gu,Shuo Cai,Zihao Wang,Wenjun Wang,Yuanyi Wang,Pengkai Wang,Sirui Huang,Su Lu,Jianmin Wu,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FeatCal方法，通过前向逐层校准合并模型权重以减少特征漂移（feature drift），在不使用梯度下降或迭代优化的情况下，以少量校准样本和高效闭式解提升模型合并性能，在CLIP和GLUE基准上显著优于现有后合并校准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并虽然避免了联合训练或部署多个专家模型，但合并后的模型性能仍落后于任务专家。作者研究这一性能差距，将其归因于特征漂移（feature drift），即合并模型与专家模型在相同输入上产生的特征差异，并希望从理论上分析该漂移的传播机制，提出缓解方法。

Method: FeatCal方法：利用少量校准集，按前向顺序逐层校准合并模型权重。每层通过闭式解更新权重，目标是减少该层的特征漂移，同时保持与原始合并权重的接近，从而保留模型合并的优势。无需梯度下降、迭代优化或额外模块。

Result: 在CLIP-ViT-B/32 Task Arithmetic (TA)上，FeatCal达85.5%，优于Surgery (77.0%)和ProbSurgery (78.8%)；在FLAN-T5-base GLUE上达85.2%，优于83.7%和82.2%。8样本/任务可达82.9%，256样本/任务仅需53秒，速度约为基线方法的4倍。

Conclusion: FeatCal通过前向逐层校准有效减少特征漂移，以更低的校准成本和更高的样本效率显著提升了模型合并的性能，是一种高效实用的后合并校准方法。

Abstract: Model merging combines task experts into one model and avoids joint training, retraining, or deploying many expert models, but the merged model often still underperforms task experts. We study this performance gap through feature drift, the difference between features produced by the merged model and by the expert on the same input. Our theory decomposes this drift into upstream propagation and local mismatch, tracks how it propagates and combines through later layers in forward order, and links final feature drift to output drift. This view motivates FeatCal, which uses a small calibration set to calibrate the merged model weights layer by layer in forward order, reducing feature drift while staying close to merged weights and preserving the benefits of model merging. FeatCal uses an efficient closed-form solution to update model weights, with no gradient descent, iterative optimization, or extra modules. On the main CLIP and GLUE benchmarks, FeatCal beats Surgery and ProbSurgery, the closest post-merging calibration baselines: 85.5% vs. 77.0%/78.8% on CLIP-ViT-B/32 Task Arithmetic (TA) and 85.2% vs. 83.7%/82.2% on FLAN-T5-base GLUE. On CLIP-ViT-B/32, 8 examples per task reach 82.9%, and 256 examples per task take 53 seconds, about 4x faster than both baselines, showing better sample efficiency and lower calibration cost.

</details>


### [123] [Bridging Domain Gaps with Target-Aligned Generation for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13054)
*Minung Kim,Jeongmo Kim,Gwanwoo Choi,Seungyul Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TCE是一个跨域离线强化学习框架，通过双评分生成模型扩展目标对齐的状态覆盖，有效利用源域数据解决目标域数据稀缺时的分布不匹配问题，性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域离线强化学习中，当目标域数据集极其有限时，如何有效利用源域数据同时减少分布不匹配是一个关键挑战。

Method: 提出目标对齐的覆盖扩展（TCE）框架，基于理论分析决定源数据的使用方式（直接纳入目标邻近转换或通过目标对齐生成扩展状态覆盖），构建双评分生成模型合成目标一致的转换。

Result: 在多种跨域环境下，TCE consistently优于最先进的跨域离线强化学习基线方法。

Conclusion: TCE通过理论引导的数据使用策略和双评分生成模型，有效解决了目标域数据稀缺时的跨域离线强化学习问题。

Abstract: Cross-domain offline reinforcement learning aims to adapt a policy from a source domain to a target domain using only pre-collected datasets, where environment dynamics may differ. A key challenge is to leverage source data while reducing distributional mismatch, particularly when the target dataset is extremely limited. To address this, we propose Target-aligned Coverage Expansion (TCE), a framework that decides how source data should be used, either by directly incorporating target-near transitions or by expanding state coverage through target-aligned generation, guided by theoretical analysis. TCE builds on a dual score-based generative model to synthesize target-consistent transitions over an expanded state region. Extensive experiments across diverse cross-domain environments show that TCE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art cross-domain offline RL baselines.

</details>


### [124] [Ergodic Trajectory Design by Learned Pushforward Maps: Provable Coverage via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13063)
*Ehsan Aghazadeh,Masoud Malekzadeh,Ahmad Ghasemi,Hossein Pishro-Nik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种"epushforward"框架，通过最优传输条件流匹配将均匀遍历的潜在轨迹映射到目标密度分布，实现解耦的遍历覆盖，并提供了加速-能量界、O(1/√K)遍历收敛率和近似误差界等理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机遍历覆盖方法要么在线重新优化轨迹（成本随规划时域增长，且需为每个目标、智能体和实现重新运行），要么依赖针对特定约束集定制的解析构造（每遇到新约束需重新推导），缺乏一种能同时兼顾覆盖保真度、能量预算、禁飞区和加速度限制的高效通用方法。

Method: 提出epushforward框架：1）设计一个在简单环形域上提供严格均匀遍历性的解析潜在轨迹；2）通过离线学习的条件流匹配（最优传输框架）学习一个映射，将潜在轨迹的占用转移到目标密度分布；3）组合后的轨迹对学习的推前分布渐近遍历，与目标的偏差由流匹配训练损失控制；4）可微操作约束（禁飞区、加速度上限、公平性惩罚）作为可加软惩罚加入训练损失。

Result: 证明了三个理论结果：加速-能量界、O(1/√K)遍历收敛率（K为轨迹周期数）、近似误差界，三者组合成一个端到端覆盖界，可通过CFM训练诊断（在v_θ的架构Lipschitz界下）估计。

Conclusion: 一旦为目标密度和约束集训练完成，该映射可服务无限数量的轨迹和多智能体舰队而无需逐个重新训练，为无人机遍历覆盖问题提供了一种高效、可扩展且可认证的解决方案。

Abstract: Designing continuous trajectories whose time-averaged occupancy provably matches a prescribed spatial density (the \emph{ergodic coverage} problem) is central to UAV-assisted data collection and sensing, robotic exploration, and mobile monitoring. For flying agents in particular, this challenge is acute: trajectories must balance coverage fidelity against tight energy budgets, no-fly zones, and acceleration limits. Existing methods either re-optimize each trajectory online (with cost growing in the horizon and re-running for every target, agent, and realization) or rely on bespoke analytical constructions that must be re-derived for each new constraint. We propose a \emph{epushforward} framework that decouples ergodicity from density matching: an analytic latent trajectory provides exact uniform ergodicity on a simple annular domain, and a single map, learned offline by optimal-transport conditional flow matching, transports this latent occupancy onto the prescribed target density. The composed trajectory is then asymptotically ergodic with respect to the learned pushforward distribution, with deviation from the target controlled by the flow-matching training loss. Once trained for a given target density and constraint set, the map serves an unbounded number of trajectories and a multi-agent fleet without per-agent retraining, and many differentiable operational constraints (no-fly zones, acceleration ceilings, or fairness penalties) enter as additive soft penalties in the training loss without re-deriving the design. We prove three results (an acceleration-energy bound, an $O(1/\sqrt{K})$ ergodic convergence rate in the number of trajectory cycles $K$, and an approximation-error bound) that combine into an end-to-end coverage bound estimable from CFM training diagnostics (certified given an architectural Lipschitz bound on $v_θ$).

</details>


### [125] [Spectral Flattening Is All Muon Needs: How Orthogonalization Controls Learning Rate and Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13079)
*Tien-Phat Nguyen,Truong Nguyen,Minh-Phuc Truong,Tuc Nguyen,James Bailey,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Muon优化器通过对动量缓冲进行正交化（利用Newton-Schulz迭代替换奇异值），实现了谱平坦化效应。理论证明其最大稳定步长由梯度平均奇异值而非最大奇异值决定，且在Kronecker分解曲率模型下改善了有效收敛因子。实验表明Muon在使SGD发散的学习率下仍保持稳定，并在相同步长下提前达到相同精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Muon优化器为何能容忍更大学习率且收敛更快，揭示其背后的几何机制。

Method: 1) 理论分析：证明Muon最大稳定步长与梯度平均奇异值相关（而非最大奇异值）；2) 将Muon重写为预条件梯度方法，在Kronecker分解曲率模型下分析收敛因子改进；3) 实验验证学习率鲁棒性和收敛加速效果。

Result: Muon在SGD发散的学习率下依然稳定；相同步长下提前数个epoch达到相同精度。

Conclusion: Muon的成功源于谱平坦化效应——通过正交化动量缓冲，将步长瓶颈从最大奇异值转移到平均奇异值，从而允许更大步长并加速收敛。

Abstract: Muon orthogonalizes the momentum buffer before each update, replacing its singular values with ones via Newton-Schulz iterations. This simple change lets Muon tolerate far larger learning rates and converge faster than other optimizers, but why? We show that the mechanism is spectral flattening, and develop two results around it. First, we prove that Muon's maximal stable step size scales with the average singular value of the gradient rather than the largest, which bottlenecks standard gradient descent. Second, we recast Muon as a preconditioned gradient method and show, under a Kronecker-factored curvature model, that it improves the effective convergence factor, with the improvement controlled by the spectrum of the gradient covariance. Extensive experiments validate both results: Muon remains stable at learning rates that cause SGD to diverge within the first few iterations, and reaches accuracy milestones several epochs earlier even at identical step sizes. Taken together, our results offer a principled, geometric explanation for Muon's empirical success.

</details>


### [126] [Bayesian Nonparametric Mixed-Effect ODEs with Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13088)
*Julien Martinelli,Maksim Sinelnikov,Harri Lähdesmäki,Quentin Clairon,Mélanie Prague*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MEGPODE，一种贝叶斯非参数混合效应ODE模型，通过高斯过程对群体和个体向量场分别建模，结合状态空间GP轨迹先验与虚拟配点观测实现高效推断，在异质性ODE基准上优于强基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典非线性混合效应ODE模型依赖参数化向量场，易受结构误设定和未建模机制影响；现有非参数ODE方法缺乏显式的混合效应层次结构，无法处理个体间异质性动态。

Method: 将每个个体的向量场分解为共享群体成分和个体特异性偏差，两者均赋予GP先验；使用状态空间GP轨迹先验结合虚拟配点观测，通过卡尔曼平滑更新轨迹并实现向量场的闭式回归。

Result: 在涵盖振荡和生物医学系统的异质性ODE基准上，MEGPODE在群体场恢复和个体轨迹预测方面均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: MEGPODE有效结合了非参数灵活性与混合效应层次结构，为异质动态系统建模提供了具有原则性不确定性量化的新框架。

Abstract: Dynamical modelling is central to many scientific domains, including pharmacometrics, systems biology, physiology, and epidemiology. In these settings, heterogeneity is often intrinsic: different subjects or units follow related but distinct continuous-time dynamics. Classical nonlinear mixed-effects Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) models address this by combining population-level structure with subject-specific effects, but they rely on a parametric vector field and are therefore vulnerable to structural misspecification and unmodelled mechanisms. This motivates nonparametric approaches that can retain principled uncertainty quantification, yet existing nonparametric ODE methods typically assume a single shared dynamical system rather than an explicit mixed-effect hierarchy over subject-specific dynamics. We propose MEGPODE, a Bayesian nonparametric mixed-effect ODE model in which each subject's vector field is decomposed into a shared population component and a subject-specific deviation, both endowed with Gaussian process (GP) priors. To avoid repeated ODE solves per subject during training, we combine state-space GP trajectory priors with virtual collocation observations, yielding Kalman-smoothing trajectory updates and closed-form regressions for the vector fields. Across controlled heterogeneous ODE benchmarks spanning oscillatory, biomedical systems, MEGPODE improves population-field recovery and subject-level trajectory prediction relative to strong baselines.

</details>


### [127] [Margin-calibrated Classifier Guidance for Property-driven Synthesis Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13101)
*Najwa Laabid,Vikas Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出序列补全排序（SCR）方法，通过对比学习和基于边界的损失函数校准分类器，解决了传统交叉熵分类器在引导单步逆合成模型时无法有效覆盖稀疏反应数据中令牌级分布的问题，显著提升了多步合成规划的成功率（反应类型引导78.4%，Tanimoto引导95.3%），并填补了模板自由与模板方法之间的多样性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分类器引导（交叉熵损失）在稀疏单断开反应数据集上无法有效覆盖无条件令牌级分布，导致无法正确引导自回归生成器满足特定化学约束或化学家偏好，且重新训练生成器成本高昂。

Method: 提出序列补全排序（SCR）方法：采用对比学习和基于边界的损失函数（margin-based loss）校准分类器，使其在解码过程中能够有意义地区分不同延续序列；从理论上证明了边界校准分类器可扩展受引导束搜索下满足属性序列的可达集。

Result: 在USPTO-190数据集上，SCR在化学家指定引导目标下，多步求解率从无引导的16.8%提升至反应类型引导78.4%、Tanimoto引导95.3%；为33个（17.4%）基线方法无法求解的目标找到了有效路线；有效填补了模板自由与模板方法之间的多样性差距。

Conclusion: SCR方法通过对比学习和边界损失校准分类器，克服了传统交叉熵分类器在稀疏反应数据上引导不足的问题，显著提升了多步逆合成规划的成功率和多样性，为化学家偏好约束下的合成规划提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Synthesis planning seeks an efficient sequence of chemical reactions that produce a target molecule. Typically, a pretrained single-step (autoregressive) retrosynthesis model is repeatedly invoked to generate such a sequence. Classifier guidance can, in principle, help steer the output of single-step model toward reactions that satisfy specific constraints or accommodate chemist's preferences during inference without having to retrain the autoregressive generator. We expose the insufficiency of auxiliary classifiers trained with cross-entropy loss to override the unconditional token-level distributions learned from typical sparse single-disconnection reaction datasets. We overcome this issue with a novel method called Sequence Completion Ranking (SCR), which employs contrastive argumentation and a margin-based loss to calibrate the classifier so that it can meaningfully discriminate between continuations during decoding. We formally establish that margin-calibrated classifiers can expand the set of property-satisfying sequences reachable under guided beam search. Empirically, on USPTO-190, given chemist-specified guidance targets, SCR substantially improves multi-step solve rates from $16.8\%$ (unguided generator) to $78.4\%$ with reaction-type guidance and $95.3\%$ with Tanimoto guidance, unlocking valid routes for 33 targets ($17.4\%$) previously unsolvable with baselines. Our method also effectively closes the long-standing diversity gap between template-free and template-based methods.

</details>


### [128] [MLGIB: Multi-Label Graph Information Bottleneck for Expressive and Robust Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13126)
*Chaokai Wu,Haofu Shi,Ningxuan Ma,Jianghong Ma,Xiaofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多标签图中GNN的过压缩问题，提出MLGIB框架，通过信息瓶颈理论构建标签感知的消息传递机制，在保留预测信号的同时抑制无关噪声，实验证明其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN在深度消息传递中面临过压缩问题，该问题在多标签图中尤为严重：邻居节点共享有限标签却在大量无关标签上存在差异，导致预测信号被噪声标签稀释。现有方法未能有效处理这一失效模式。

Method: 提出多标签图信息瓶颈（MLGIB）框架，将多标签消息传递建模为受约束的信息传输过程。构建马尔可夫依赖空间并推导可计算变分界：下界最大化与目标标签的互信息，上界约束冗余源信息。基于此设计端到端的标签感知消息传递架构。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，MLGIB相较于现有方法取得了一致性改进，验证了该框架的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: MLGIB通过信息瓶颈理论有效解决了多标签图中GNN的过压缩问题，为标签感知的图表示学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) suffer from over-squashing in deep message passing, where information from exponentially growing neighborhoods is compressed into fixed-dimensional representations. We show that this issue becomes a distinct failure mode in multi-label graphs: neighboring nodes often share only limited labels while differing across many irrelevant ones, causing predictive signals to be diluted by noisy label information. To address this challenge, we propose the Multi-Label Graph Information Bottleneck (MLGIB), which formulates multi-label message passing as constrained information transmission under irrelevant label noise. MLGIB balances expressiveness and robustness by preserving predictive label signals while suppressing irrelevant noise. Specifically, it constructs a Markovian dependence space and derives tractable variational bounds, where the lower bound maximizes mutual information with target labels and the upper bound constrains redundant source information. These bounds lead to an end-to-end label-aware message-passing architecture. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over existing methods, validating the effectiveness and generality of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [129] [KAST-BAR: Knowledge-Anchored Semantically-Dynamic Topology Brain Autoregressive Modeling for Universal Neural Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13133)
*Haoning Wang,Wenchao Yang,Shuai Shen,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KAST-BAR模型，通过知识锚定的语义动态拓扑脑自回归模型，解决了EEG基础模型在复杂时空拓扑建模和生理信号与文本语义之间的模态差距问题，在21个数据集上预训练后，在6个下游任务中取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型受限于两方面挑战：一是对复杂时空拓扑结构的建模不充分，二是低层生理信号与高层文本语义之间存在固有的模态差距。

Method: 提出KAST-BAR模型，包含三个核心组件：1) 双流层次注意力(DSHA)编码器，通过局部时间动态与全局空间上下文建模脑内非欧几里得拓扑；2) 知识锚定语义剖析器(KASP)，生成物理 grounded 的实例级文本描述；3) 语义文本感知精炼器(STAR)，利用潜在专家查询动态重建EEG表示。

Result: 在21个多样化的数据集上进行大规模预训练构建基础模型，在6个下游任务中一致取得优越性能。

Conclusion: KAST-BAR有效将专家级医学知识整合到EEG信号表示中，为神经解码提供了新的范式。

Abstract: While EEG foundation models have shown significant potential in universal neural decoding across tasks, their advancement remains constrained by the inadequacy modeling of complex spatiotemporal topology, as well as the inherent modality gap between low-level physiological signals and high-level textual semantics. To address these challenges, we propose a Knowledge-Anchored Semantically-Dynamic Topology Brain Autoregressive Model (KAST-BAR), which dynamically aligns physiological representations derived from multi-level brain topology with an expert-level semantic space. Specifically, we design a Dual-Stream Hierarchical Attention (DSHA) encoder that accurately captures the brain's intrinsic non-Euclidean topology by modeling local temporal dynamics with global spatial contexts. On this basis, a Knowledge-Anchored Semantic Profiler (KASP) is proposed to synthesize physically-grounded and instance-level textual profiles, which subsequently drive a Semantic Text-Aware Refiner (STAR) to dynamically reconstruct EEG representations using Latent Expert Queries. By conducting large-scale pre-training on 21 diverse datasets to build a foundation model, KAST-BAR effectively integrates expert-level medical knowledge into EEG signal representations, consistently achieving superior performance across six downstream tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/KAST-BAR/KAST-BAR

</details>


### [130] [Collaborating in Multi-Armed Bandits with Strategic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13145)
*Idan Barnea,Ofir Schlisselberg,Yishay Mansour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAOS机制，通过信息共享在持久性多智能体贝叶斯强盗问题中维持协作纳什均衡，并实现强遗憾保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多假设短命智能体（单次决策），忽略了持久性智能体在多次决策中的协作与搭便车问题；本研究旨在探讨纯信息共享能否在无货币转移下维持长期协作探索。

Method: 设计CAOS机制，通过信息共享策略激励持久性智能体协作，将协作探索建模为纳什均衡。

Result: CAOS机制下协作探索可作为纳什均衡得以维持，性能接近完全协作系统，仅通过信息共享即可实现强遗憾保证。

Conclusion: 纯信息共享足以维持多智能体贝叶斯强盗中的长期协作，无需货币转移，且性能损失有限。

Abstract: We study collaborative learning in multi-agent Bayesian bandit problems, where strategic agents collectively solve the same bandit instance. While multiple agents can accelerate learning by sharing information, strategic agents might prefer to free-ride and avoid exploration. We consider a setting with persistent agents that participate in multiple time periods. This is in contrast to most previous works on incentives in multi-agent MAB, which assume short-lived agents, namely each agent has a single decision to make and optimizes their expected reward in that single decision. As in the multi-agent MAB model with incentives, our model does not have monetary transfers, and the only incentives are through information sharing.
  We propose \texttt{CAOS}, a mechanism that sustains collaboration as a Nash equilibrium while achieving strong regret guarantees. Our results demonstrate that collaborative exploration can be sustained purely through information sharing, achieving performance close to that of fully cooperative systems despite strategic behavior.

</details>


### [131] [Continual Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models via Program Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13162)
*Hung Le,Svetha Venkatesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProCL通过受神经科学互补学习系统启发的程序记忆框架，将LoRA适配器组织为结构化记忆槽，实现动态检索与组合，在持续学习场景中平衡快速适应与知识保留，且不增加推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续LoRA方法将低秩空间视为同质更新区域，缺乏短期更新随时间整合的调节机制，难以平衡快速适应与知识保留。

Method: 提出ProCL框架，受互补学习系统启发，将LoRA适配器组织为程序记忆槽，通过输入条件注意力动态检索；底层适配器保持分布式表示，逐步积累跨任务知识。

Result: 在多种基准测试中，相比其他持续LoRA策略，ProCL在保留能力和减少灾难性遗忘方面表现更优。

Conclusion: ProCL在LoRA参数化内运作，无额外推理成本，有效平衡了持续学习中的可塑性与稳定性。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT), particularly Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), has become a standard approach for adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) under limited compute. However, in continual settings where models are updated sequentially with small datasets, conventional LoRA updates struggle to balance rapid adaptation and knowledge retention. Existing methods typically treat the low-rank space as a homogeneous update region, lacking mechanisms to regulate how short-term updates are consolidated over time. We propose a continual LoRA framework with \textbf{Pro}gram memory, inspired by \textbf{C}omplementary \textbf{L}earning Systems in neuroscience. Our approach, dubbed \textbf{ProCL}, organizes LoRA adapters into structured program memory slots that are dynamically retrieved through input-conditioned attention. This enables rapid and localized adaptation, encouraging similar inputs to reuse shared adapter regions while reserving unused capacity for future data. The slots are then combined with the underlying adapter, which maintains a distributed representation that gradually accumulates knowledge across tasks to balance plasticity and stability. Our method operates entirely within the LoRA parameterization and incurs no additional inference cost. Experiments on diverse benchmarks demonstrate improved retention and reduced catastrophic forgetting over other continual LoRA strategies.

</details>


### [132] [Do Heavy Tails Help Diffusion? On the Subtle Trade-off Between Initialization and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13175)
*Hamza Cherkaoui,Hélène Halconruy,Antonio Ocello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论和实验研究，对比了重尾(HT)噪声和轻尾(LT)高斯噪声在扩散和流生成模型中的应用，发现HT噪声虽然直觉上更适合重尾数据，但实际上会使统计估计问题更难，导致更差的采样误差界限，质疑了近期生成模型中越来越多使用HT噪声的设计趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究提出将重尾(HT)噪声引入扩散和流生成模型，旨在更好地恢复目标分布的尾部特征并提高生成多样性。这一动机直觉上合理：如果数据是重尾的，HT噪声可能比轻尾高斯噪声更匹配。然而，用HT噪声替代高斯噪声也改变了底层的估计问题，需要重新审视这一范式。

Method: 通过结合理论和实验研究，建立了两种代表性扩散模型（分别由HT和LT噪声驱动）的采样误差界限，并在合成和真实数据集上通过实验验证了预测的误差权衡关系。

Result: 理论分析表明HT噪声使统计估计问题变得更困难，导致更不利的采样误差界限；实验在合成和真实数据集上复现了预测的误差权衡，支持了理论发现。

Conclusion: 研究结果质疑了生成建模中日益增长的使用HT噪声的设计趋势，挑战了用HT噪声改善罕见区域探索的观点。

Abstract: Recent works have proposed incorporating heavy-tailed (HT) noise into diffusion- and flow-based generative models, with the goals of better recovering the tails of target distributions and improving generative diversity. This motivation is intuitive: if the data are heavy-tailed, HT noise may appear better matched than light-tailed (LT) Gaussian noise. However, replacing Gaussian noise by HT noise also changes the underlying estimation problem. In this paper, we revisit this paradigm through a combined theoretical and empirical study, establishing sampling-error bounds for two representative diffusion models driven by HT and LT noise. We show that HT noise makes the statistical estimation problem harder, leading to less favorable sampling-error bounds. We support these findings with experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets, empirically recovering the predicted error trade-off. Our results call into question a growing design trend in generative modeling and challenge the use of HT noise to improve rare-region exploration.

</details>


### [133] [Stable Attention Response for Reliable Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13181)
*Penghui Wen,Zexin Hu,Sen Zhang,Patrick Filippi,Xiaogang Zhu,Allen Benter,Thomas Bishop,Zhiyong Wang,Kun Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HARECast框架，通过头级注意力响应能量正则化来稳定跨样本注意力波动，从而提升降水短临预报的可靠性，在SEVIR和MeteoNet上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力机制方法主要关注表示学习和预测能力，忽视了注意力响应在跨样本间的不稳定性。作者发现注意力响应能量的跨样本方差是导致预报不可靠的重要且被低估的因素。

Method: 提出HARECast框架：显式建模头级注意力响应能量，通过分组正则化目标减少跨样本波动。该框架可泛化应用于单模态和多模态架构，实例化为含重建分支和扩散预测器的标准流水线。

Result: 在SEVIR和MeteoNet基准上达到SOTA性能。实证表明不准确预报与注意力响应能量在头间和层间的高方差相关。

Conclusion: 跨样本注意力响应能量方差增大预测误差下界，正则化该能量可显著提升预报稳定性与准确性，该框架具有良好泛化性。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting remains challenging due to the highly localized, rapidly evolving, and heterogeneous nature of atmospheric dynamics. Although recent methods increasingly adopt attention-based architectures in both unimodal and multimodal settings, they mainly emphasize stronger representation learning and prediction capacity, while paying less attention to the stability of attention responses across samples. In this work, we show that cross-sample instability of attention-response energy is an important and previously underexplored source of forecasting unreliability. Empirically, inaccurate forecasts are associated with larger attention-response energy variance across heads and layers. Theoretically, we show that cross-sample variability can propagate through self-attention, and enlarge a lower bound on prediction error. Based on this insight, we propose HARECast, a Head-wise Attention Response Energy-regulated framework for precipitation nowcasting. HARECast explicitly models head-wise attention-response energy and stabilizes it through a group-wise regularization objective that reduces cross-sample fluctuations. The proposed formulation is generic and applicable to both unimodal and multimodal nowcasting architectures. We instantiate HARECast in a standard forecasting pipeline with reconstruction branches and a diffusion-based predictor, and evaluate it on commonly used benchmarks--SEVIR and MeteoNet. Experimental results demonstrate that HARECast achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [134] [N-vium: Mixture-of-Exits Transformer for Accelerated Exact Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13190)
*Aleksander Lorenc,Frédéric Berdoz,Joël Mathys,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: N-vium是一种混合出口Transformer，通过在深度方向部分并行化计算，在标准硬件上提高每秒有效FLOPs，从而在不降低模型质量的情况下实现推理加速，1.5B参数模型在无困惑度损失下达到57.9%的墙钟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归Transformer的推理效率提升通常以减少每个token的FLOPs为目标，但这往往需要通过近似方法牺牲模型质量。作者希望在不降低模型质量的前提下提升推理速度。

Method: 提出N-vium，一种混合出口Transformer：在多个深度附加预测头，将下一个token分布定义为这些出口的加权混合（可学习混合权重），支持token自适应的路由选择。该公式严格泛化了标准Transformer（当路由将零质量分配给所有中间头时恢复为标准形式）。采样过程是精确的，通过延迟上层计算并与后续token批量处理来恢复完整的KV缓存。

Result: 在高达1.5B参数规模上预训练N-vium，最大模型在无困惑度损失的情况下，相比参数和数据匹配的标准Transformer实现了57.9%的墙钟加速。

Conclusion: N-vium通过并行化深度方向计算而非减少每token计算量，有效提升了推理效率，且不牺牲模型质量，为自回归Transformer的高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Improving the inference efficiency of autoregressive transformers typically means reducing FLOPs per token, usually through approximations that degrade model quality. We introduce N-vium, a mixture-of-exits transformer that partially parallelizes computation across depth on standard hardware, increasing effective FLOPs per second rather than minimizing compute per token. N-vium attaches prediction heads at multiple depths and defines the next-token distribution as a learned mixture over these exits, with token-adaptive routing. This formulation strictly generalizes the standard transformer, which is recovered exactly when routing assigns zero mass to all intermediate heads. Sampling from the mixture is exact, and complete KV caches are recovered by deferring the upper-layer computation and batching it with later tokens. We pretrain N-vium at scales up to 1.5B parameters. Our largest model reaches 57.9% wall-clock speedup over a parameter- and data-matched standard transformer at no perplexity cost.

</details>


### [135] [ECG-NAT: A Self-supervised Neighborhood Attention Transformer for Multi-lead Electrocardiogram Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13194)
*Mahsa Gazeran,Sayvan Soleymanbaigi,Fatemeh Daneshfar,Amjad Seyedi,Fardin Akhlaghian Tab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ECG-NAT，一种面向多导联心电图心律失常分类的自监督学习方法，采用生成式预训练（掩码自编码器）和判别式微调（对比学习+交叉熵损失）两阶段框架，通过层次化注意力机制高效捕获多尺度时间特征，在仅使用1%标注数据时达到88.1%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督方法多聚焦于全局上下文或局部形态特征，缺乏层次化多尺度特征提取；且ECG信号同时需要精细的拍级形态和更宽的节律级依赖关系，同时保持计算效率。

Method: 两阶段自监督学习框架：1) 生成式预训练阶段，使用掩码自编码器在多个数据集上重构部分掩码的ECG信号，学习鲁棒的域不变表示；2) 判别式微调阶段，结合监督对比损失和交叉熵损失的双损失函数；3) 层次化邻域注意力机制，高效捕获从局部拍形态到全局节律模式的多尺度时序特征。

Result: 在基准数据集上仅使用1%标注数据达到88.1%准确率，在低资源场景下表现出强有效性，同时兼具分类性能和计算效率。

Conclusion: ECG-NAT结合了自监督学习与层次化注意力机制，实现了高效准确的心律失常分类，适用于实时ECG诊断场景。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) arrhythmia classification remains challenging due to signal variability, noise, limited labeled data, and the difficulty in achieving both accuracy and efficiency in models. While self-supervised learning reduces label dependency, most methods target either global contextual features or local morphological patterns, but rarely implement hierarchical multi-scale feature extraction. ECG signals require architectures that simultaneously capture fine-grained beat-level morphology and broader rhythm-level dependencies with computational efficiency. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes the Electrocardiogram Neighborhood Attention Transformer (ECG-NAT), a novel self-supervised learning approach tailored for multi-lead ECG classification. Our two-stage approach begins with generative pretraining, using a masked autoencoder to reconstruct partially masked ECG signals across multiple diverse datasets, enabling the model to learn robust, domain-invariant representations from unlabeled data. This is followed by discriminative fine-tuning with a dual-loss function that combines supervised contrastive and cross-entropy losses, aligning representation learning with label prediction. The hierarchical attention mechanism efficiently captures multi-scale temporal features from localized beat morphology to broader rhythm patterns at low computational cost. ECG-NAT achieves robust performance on benchmark datasets, with 88.1\% accuracy using only 1\% labeled data, demonstrating strong efficacy in low-resource settings. The framework combines superior classification performance with computational efficiency, making it practical for real-time ECG diagnosis. The code will be made available upon acceptance at: https://github.com/Mahsagazeran/ECG-NAT.

</details>


### [136] [McCast: Memory-Guided Latent Drift Correction for Long-Horizon Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13197)
*Penghui Wen,Yu Luo,Lintao Wang,Mengwei He,Patrick Filippi,Thomas Francis Bishop,Zhiyong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: McCast提出了一种记忆引导的潜空间漂移校正方法，通过时间组织的记忆显式校正自回归预测中的轨迹漂移，在SEVIR和MeteoNet两个基准上实现SOTA性能，尤其擅长长时程降水预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有降水临近预报方法采用自回归方式逐帧预测，导致误差随 rollout 累积而偏离物理演化轨迹；已有工作多聚焦于提升单步预测精度，忽视了气象系统的全局时间演化，也缺乏主动校正漂移的机制。

Method: 提出McCast，核心是漂移校正记忆库（DCBank），分两阶段：1) 校正潜提取器从当前预测与参考潜状态中预测初始校正量；2) 校正感知记忆检索模块利用时间组织的历史记忆精炼该校正量，从而主动校正自回归潜演化轨迹。

Result: 在SEVIR和MeteoNet两个基准数据集上，McCast在长时程预测场景下显著优于现有方法，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: McCast通过显式校正潜空间轨迹漂移而非仅提升单步精度，生成了时间上更一致、更可靠的长期预报，验证了主动漂移校正策略的有效性。

Abstract: Existing precipitation nowcasting methods typically adopt an autoregressive formulation, where future states are predicted from previous outputs. However, such an approach accumulates errors over long rollouts, causing forecasts to drift away from physically plausible evolution trajectories. Although various studies have attempted to alleviate this problem by improving step-wise prediction accuracy, they largely neglect the global temporal evolution of meteorological systems and lack mechanisms to actively correct drift during rollouts. To address this issue, we propose McCast, a memory-guided latent drift correction method for precipitation nowcasting. Rather than treating memory as an unordered dictionary of latent states for passive conditioning, McCast leverages temporally organized memory to actively correct autoregressive latent evolution. Specifically, McCast introduces a Drift-Corrective Memory Bank (DCBank) that explicitly estimates the temporally consistent drift corrections to calibrate the divergent trajectory. DCBank performs drift correction in two stages: a Corrective Latent Extractor first predicts an initial correction from the current prediction and a reference latent state, and a Correction-Aware Memory Retrieval module then refines the initial correction using temporally organized historical memory. By explicitly correcting latent evolution, instead of improving step-wise prediction accuracy only, McCast produces more temporally coherent and reliable long-horizon forecasts. Experiments on two widely used benchmarks, SEVIR and MeteoNet, show that McCast achieves state-of-the-art performance, particularly in challenging long-horizon forecasting scenarios.

</details>


### [137] [Switching Successor Measures for Hierarchical Zero-shot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13207)
*Stefan Stojanovic,Alexandre Proutiere*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出切换后继度量（Switching Successor Measures）方法，将经典后继度量扩展至零样本分层强化学习，无需额外监督、固定时间抽象或人工设计子目标。基于此提出FB π-Switch算法，从前向-后向表示中同时提取高层子目标选择策略与底层控制策略，在目标条件任务和通用奖励任务上均优于非分层基线，匹配甚至超越最先进的分层方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层强化学习方法通常依赖固定时间抽象或目标条件目标函数等限制性设计，这将其局限于目标到达任务，限制了在通用奖励函数上的应用。作者希望突破这些限制，实现无需额外监督、固定时间跨度或人工设计子目标的通用分层零样本强化学习。

Method: (1) 提出切换后继度量（Switching Successor Measures），作为经典后继度量的扩展，保留其底层结构；(2) 基于此设计FB π-Switch算法，从单一的前向-后向（FB）表示中同时学习高层子目标选择策略和底层控制策略，使分层行为从学习到的表示中自然涌现。

Result: 在目标条件任务和通用奖励任务上，FB π-Switch均优于非分层基线方法；在目标条件设定下，该方法匹配了最先进的分层方法性能。

Conclusion: 结构化后继表示为超越目标到达任务的分层零样本强化学习提供了灵活基础，证明了无需严格设计约束即可实现有效的分层控制。

Abstract: Hierarchical reinforcement learning can improve generalization by decomposing long-horizon decision-making into simpler subproblems. However, existing approaches often rely on restrictive design choices, such as fixed temporal abstractions or goal-conditioned objectives, which largely confine them to goal-reaching tasks and limit their applicability to general reward functions. In this paper, we introduce switching successor measures, an extension of successor measures that enables hierarchical control in zero-shot reinforcement learning without additional supervision, fixed horizons, or manually designed subgoals. We show that switching successor measures arise naturally from classical successor measures while preserving their underlying structure. Building on this result, we propose FB $π$-Switch, an algorithm that extracts both a high-level subgoal-selection policy and a low-level control policy directly from forward-backward (FB) representations, allowing hierarchical behavior to emerge from a single learned representation. Experiments on both goal-conditioned and general reward-based tasks show that FB $π$-Switch improves over non-hierarchical baselines and matches state-of-the-art hierarchical methods in goal-conditioned settings. These results demonstrate that structured successor representations provide a flexible foundation for hierarchical zero-shot reinforcement learning beyond goal-reaching tasks. Our project website is available at: https://stestokth.github.io/switching-successors/.

</details>


### [138] [Machine Learning-Driven Multimodal Spectroscopic Liquid Biopsy for Early Multicancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13218)
*Alejandro Leonardo García Navarro,Javier Cachón Ortiz,Javier González Colsa,Samuel García Díaz,Carlos Viadero Valderrama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于傅里叶变换红外光谱、拉曼光谱和激发-发射矩阵荧光光谱三种光谱模态结合机器学习（XGBoost）的多模态光谱液体活检框架，用于多癌种检测。在乳腺癌、结直肠癌患者和健康对照的血清样本上，通过低层级数据融合整合三种光谱信息，实现了极高的分类性能（乳腺癌ROC-AUC 0.997，结直肠癌ROC-AUC 0.994），且多模态融合方案优于单一模态和双模态组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症是全球主要死因之一，亟需快速、微创、无标记且可扩展的诊断策略。光谱液体活检作为一种有前景的替代方案，能够全面表征生物流体的生化变化，但单一光谱模态的信息有限，因此本文旨在通过多模态光谱融合进一步提升癌症检测性能。

Method: 采集乳腺癌患者、结直肠癌患者和健康对照的血清样本，分别进行FTIR光谱、拉曼光谱和EEM荧光光谱测量。对每种模态进行特定预处理后，采用低层级数据融合（LLDF）整合三种光谱的互补生化信息，使用XGBoost模型进行分类。评估了三种单一模态、三种两两组合以及全模态融合共七种实验配置。

Result: 多模态融合方案取得了最佳且最均衡的结果：乳腺癌ROC-AUC为0.997，结直肠癌ROC-AUC为0.994，同时具有高度平衡的灵敏度和特异性。虽然部分单一模态也取得了较高的判别性能，但多模态融合在整体表现上最为优越。

Conclusion: 多模态光谱液体活检框架（FTIR+拉曼+EEM荧光）结合机器学习，能够实现高精度、高平衡性的多癌种检测，为癌症诊断提供了有效的无创策略。

Abstract: Cancer is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, making the development of rapid, minimally invasive, label-free and scalable diagnostic strategies a major challenge in modern oncology. In this context, spectroscopic liquid biopsy has emerged as a promising alternative, as it enables the holistic characterization of biochemical alterations in biological fluids. In this work, we propose a multimodal spectroscopic liquid biopsy framework for multicancer detection based on the combination of Fourier Transform Infrared (FTIR) spectroscopy, Raman spectroscopy, and Excitation-Emission Matrix (EEM) fluorescence spectroscopy together with Machine Learning (ML) methodologies. Serum samples from breast cancer patients, colorectal cancer patients, and healthy controls were analyzed through the three spectroscopic modalities. After modality-specific preprocessing, low-level data fusion (LLDF) was employed to integrate the complementary biochemical information encoded within the different spectroscopic measurements, and classification was performed using XGBoost models. Seven experimental configurations were evaluated, including the three unimodal approaches, all pairwise bimodal configurations, and the full multimodal approach of FTIR, Raman, and EEM fluorescence. The results show that although several individual modalities achieved high discrimination performance, the multimodal fusion provided the most balanced overall results, reaching a ROC-AUC of 0.997 for breast cancer and 0.994 for colorectal cancer, together with highly balanced sensitivity and specificity values.

</details>


### [139] [Teacher-Guided Policy Optimization for LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13230)
*Xinyu Liu,Kechen Jiao,Chunyang Xiao,Runsong Zhao,Junhao Ruan,Bei Li,Jiahao Liu,Qifan Wang,Xin Chen,Jingang Wang,Tong Xiao,JingBo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Teacher-Guided Policy Optimization (TGPO)算法，通过在学生轨迹上引入教师密集方向性指导，解决反向KL散度在师生分布差异大时失效的问题，在复杂推理任务上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Reverse KL (RKL)在师生分布差异显著时，负反馈信息不足，导致蒸馏效果不佳。

Method: 提出TGPO，一种on-policy算法，利用教师模型在学生rollout上的条件预测提供密集的方向性引导，无需额外数据标注，可与现有RLVR框架无缝集成。

Result: 在复杂推理基准上，TGPO显著优于标准基线方法，且对不同的教师模型具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TGPO通过密集方向性指导有效解决了RKL在分布差异大时的失效问题，为on-policy LLM蒸馏提供了更稳健的方案。

Abstract: The convergence of reinforcement learning and imitation learning has positioned Reverse KL (RKL) as a promising paradigm for on-policy LLM distillation, aiming to unify exploration with teacher supervision. However, we identify a critical limitation: when the student and teacher distributions diverge significantly, standard RKL often fails to yield meaningful improvement due to uninformative negative feedback. To address this inefficiency, we propose Teacher-Guided Policy Optimization (TGPO), an on-policy algorithm that incorporates dense directional guidance by leveraging teacher predictions conditioned on the student's rollout. Because TGPO remains on-policy, the algorithm integrates seamlessly with existing RLVR frameworks without requiring additional data annotation. Experiments on complex reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that TGPO significantly outperforms standard baselines and is robust to different teachers.

</details>


### [140] [EMO: Frustratingly Easy Progressive Training of Extendable MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13247)
*Linghao Jin,Chufan Shi,Huijuan Wang,Nuan Wen,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric Xing,Xuezhe Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出渐进式训练框架EMO，通过逐步扩展专家池解决了MoE训练中内存与通信开销大的效率悖论，在大规模实验中匹配固定专家设置性能的同时提升了墙钟效率并降低了GPU成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MoE模型存在效率悖论：增加专家数量可提升模型容量但不增加计算FLOPs，却导致内存和通信成本激增，实际训练效率低下。作者认为问题根源在于训练初期就分配过多专家，而早期数据可能不需要如此大的容量。

Method: 提出EMO渐进式训练框架，将MoE容量视为可扩展的"内存"，随训练过程逐步扩展专家池。该方法显式建模稀疏性缩放定律，推导出每个阶段计算最优的token预算，以指导渐进式扩展。

Result: 在大规模实验中，EMO匹配了固定专家设置（大专家池）的性能，同时显著改善了墙钟效率，减少了训练时间和GPU成本。

Conclusion: EMO为可扩展的MoE训练提供了一条简单而有效的路径，在保留大专家池优势的同时降低了训练开销。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models offer a powerful way to scale model size without increasing compute, as per-token FLOPs depend only on k active experts rather than the total pool of E experts. Yet, this asymmetry creates an MoE efficiency paradox in practice: adding more experts balloons memory and communication costs, making actual training inefficient. We argue that this bottleneck arises in part because current MoE training allocates too many experts from the beginning, even though early-stage data may not fully utilize such capacity. Motivated by this, we propose EMO, a simple progressive training framework that treats MoE capacity as expandable memory and grows the expert pool over the course of training. EMO explicitly models sparsity in scaling law to derive stage-wise compute-optimal token budgets for progressive expansion. Empirical results show that EMO matches the performance of a fixed-expert setup in large-scale experiments while improving wall-clock efficiency. It offers a surprisingly simple yet effective path to scalable MoE training, preserving the benefits of large expert pools while reducing both training time and GPU cost.

</details>


### [141] [LightSplit: Practical Privacy-Preserving Split Learning via Orthogonal Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13265)
*Mert Cihangiroglu,Alessandro Pegoraro,Phillip Rieger,Antonino Nocera,Ahmad-Reza Sadeghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LightSplit通过在分割学习中的切割层应用轻量级固定正交随机投影，将高维激活压缩为低维表示，在保持95%以上基线精度的同时实现最高32倍的传输维度缩减，并作为信息瓶颈限制实例级信息暴露，抵御重构攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分割学习中切割层的高维激活既导致巨大的通信开销，又容易受到重构攻击的威胁，现有方法通常只能单独解决效率或隐私问题，缺乏兼顾两者的轻量级方案。

Method: 在切割层引入固定的正交随机投影矩阵，将原始高维激活映射为低维表示；该投影基于香农信息论充当信息瓶颈，限制实例特定信息并抑制可被利用的每样本信号；投影不可逆，部分原始信息在客户端被不可逆丢弃；无需额外可训练组件，保持端到端可微分性。

Result: 在IID和非IID设置下，跨不同投影维度和客户端规模，LightSplit在保持稳定训练动态的同时，以最高32倍传输维度缩减保留了超过95%的基线准确率。

Conclusion: LightSplit是一种轻量级、与现有SL架构兼容的方法，同时有效解决了通信效率和隐私保护问题，特别适用于资源受限的边缘设备场景。

Abstract: Split learning (SL) enables collaborative training by partitioning a neural network across clients and a central server, but the cut-layer interface introduces a key challenge: high-dimensional activations incur substantial communication overhead while exposing representations vulnerable to reconstruction attacks. Existing approaches typically address efficiency or privacy in isolation, relying on additional mechanisms such as sparsification, quantization, or noise injection. We propose LightSplit, which limits information exposure and reduces communication overhead by applying a lightweight fixed orthogonal random projection at the cut layer. Based on Shannon's information theory, this projection acts as an information bottleneck that restricts instance-specific information and suppresses exploitable per-sample signals. By transmitting low-dimensional projections instead of raw activations, the server operates on lifted representations without requiring architectural modifications, ensuring compatibility with existing SL architectures. By avoiding additional trainable components on the client, the method remains lightweight and suitable for edge devices while preserving end-to-end differentiability via exact gradient propagation. As the projection is non-invertible, part of the original representation is irreversibly discarded at the client, LightSplit reduces the information available for reconstruction and limits information exposure. We extensively evaluate LightSplit on state-of-the-art benchmarks in both IID and non-IID settings across varying projection dimensions and client scales. Our results show that the method retains more than 95% of the baseline accuracy at up to 32x reduction in transmitted dimensionality while maintaining stable training dynamics.

</details>


### [142] [Byzantine-Robust Distributed Sparse Learning Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13283)
*Yuxuan Wang,Lixin Zhang,Kangqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了高维稀疏线性模型的拜占庭鲁棒分布式估计问题。通过结合局部ℓ1正则化鲁棒估计与服务器端的鲁棒聚合，该框架适用于伪Huber回归、分位数回归和稀疏SVM。研究表明，所提出的估计量在温和条件下具有非渐近保证、达到近乎最优的统计速率，并保持通信高效。模拟实验验证了该方法在各种拜占庭攻击下在估计、支持恢复和分类准确性方面的强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式估计方法在面对拜占庭故障（即节点可能任意偏离协议）时缺乏鲁棒性保障，特别是在高维稀疏线性模型场景下。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出一种兼具统计最优性和通信效率的鲁棒分布式估计框架。

Method: 提出一个两阶段框架：1) 各工作节点使用ℓ1正则化的鲁棒估计方法（伪Huber回归、分位数回归或稀疏SVM）进行局部估计；2) 服务器端采用鲁棒聚合方法整合局部估计结果，抵御拜占庭攻击。该框架兼顾了局部估计的鲁棒性与全局聚合的容错性。

Result: 理论结果表明所提估计量具有非渐近保证，在温和条件下达到近乎最优的统计率，且通信成本低。仿真实验验证了该方法在多种拜占庭攻击场景下在参数估计、支持恢复和分类精度方面均表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本文为高维稀疏线性模型提供了一种通信高效且具有理论保障的拜占庭鲁棒分布式估计框架，适用于多种损失函数，在理论和实证层面均展现了优越的鲁棒性能。

Abstract: We revisit Byzantine robust distributed estimation for high-dimensional sparse linear models. By combining local $\ell_1$-regularized robust estimation with robust aggregation at the server, the framework applies to pseudo-Huber regression, quantile regression, and sparse SVM. We show that the resulting estimators yield non-asymptotic guarantees and attain near-optimal statistical rates under mild conditions, while remaining communication-efficient. Simulations confirm strong robustness in estimation, support recovery and classification accuracy under various Byzantine attacks.

</details>


### [143] [Delightful Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13287)
*Ian Osband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Delight-gated exploration (DE)方法，将探索动作的分配视为一种定价机制——只有当动作的"愉悦度"（预期改进×惊讶度）超过门控价格时才执行探索。该方法恢复了Pandora's box问题中的保留价值规则，并且超参数可在不同任务间迁移无须重新调整，在未解决的不确定性状态下表现优于Thompson Sampling和ε-greedy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当动作空间过大、无法在预算内完全消除不确定性时，现有的探索算法（如Thompson Sampling、ε-greedy）要么搜索过广消耗预算，要么盲目覆盖导致效率低下。作者希望设计一种实用启发式方法，在有限探索预算下权衡探索收益与成本。

Method: 提出DE（Delight-gated exploration）算法，定义"愉悦度"为期望改进量乘以惊讶度（surprisal），只有当愉悦度超过设定的门控价格（gate price）时才执行探索动作。已解决的臂退出门控，新臂在超过先验阈值时关闭，线性bandit的覆盖选择消耗有限的信息预算。

Result: 在Bernoulli bandits、linear bandits和tabular MDPs上，DE使用相同的超参数无需重新调整。在测试的未解决不确定性状态下，DE的遗憾增长远弱于Thompson Sampling和ε-greedy。

Conclusion: 愉悦度改进决策的原因与改进学习的原理相同：通过收益与惊讶度的乘积来定价稀缺资源。DE提供了一种将有限探索预算合理分配给高潜力动作的实用框架。

Abstract: Most exploration algorithms search broadly until uncertainty is resolved. When the action space is too large to resolve within budget, practitioners default to $\varepsilon$-greedy, which bounds disruption but spends its override blindly. We introduce \textit{Delight-gated exploration} (DE), a host--override rule that spends exploratory actions only when their prospective delight (expected improvement times surprisal) exceeds a gate price. This practical heuristic recovers a classical result: Pandora's reservation-value rule for costly search, with surprisal setting the effective inspection cost. Resolved arms exit the gate, fresh arms shut off above a prior-determined threshold, and selected linear-bandit overrides consume finite information budget. Across Bernoulli bandits, linear bandits, and tabular MDPs, the same hyperparameters transfer without retuning, and DE shows much weaker regret growth than Thompson Sampling and $\varepsilon$-greedy in the tested unresolved regimes.
