Delight improves acting for the same reason it improves learning: it prices scarce resources by the product of upside and surprisal.

</details>


### [144] [PaMM: Periodic Motif Memory for Atomistic Models with an Explicit Local-Structure Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13297)
*Ryan Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PaMM通过在UMA ESCN-MD边缘编码器中引入显式的成对和三体基序记忆查找表，在固定训练预算下提升了能量和力的预测精度，证明了结构化基序记忆作为周期性原子建模中有用归纳偏置的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性晶体中频繁出现的局部配位模式在现有的等变原子模型中通常仅被隐式编码在密集的边缘特征中，本文旨在验证是否可以通过显式的基序记忆机制来增强模型对周期性结构的建模能力。

Method: 提出PaMM（Periodic Motif Memory），在UMA ESCN-MD边缘编码器中增加显式的成对基序（按(Z_j, Z_i, b_r)键控）和三体基序（按(Z_j, Z_i, Z_k, b_θ)键控）查找表，通过哈希映射到固定大小的表中，并使用轻量级门控和仿射融合变体与基线边缘表示结合。

Result: 在10k步检查点，门控变体在能量MAE上最优，仿射变体在力MAE上最优；20k步检查点保持同样趋势。消融实验显示，仅用成对、仅用三体、随机桶和参数匹配MLP替代方案的增益减弱，表明收益来自结构化成对/三体组织而非泛化的容量增加。OMAT24源族评估也显示了一致的小幅提升。

Conclusion: 在所研究的UMA-S + OMAT设定下，显式的成对/三体基序记忆对周期性原子建模是一种有用的归纳偏置。本文不做跨数据集泛化、唯一最优融合变体或强科学可解释性的广泛声明。

Abstract: Periodic crystals repeatedly instantiate similar local coordination motifs across translated cells and chemically related structures, but current equivariant atomistic models usually encode these patterns only implicitly in dense edge features. We introduce PaMM, a periodic motif memory that augments the UMA eSCN-MD edge encoder with explicit pair and triplet lookup features. Pair motifs are keyed by $(Z_j, Z_i, b_r)$ and triplet motifs by $(Z_j, Z_i, Z_k, b_θ)$, hashed into fixed-size tables and fused with the baseline edge representation through lightweight gate-only and affine-equipped variants. We evaluate PaMM in a matched UMA-S OMAT setting and focus on a narrow question: whether explicit motif memory helps at a fixed intermediate training budget. At the 10k-step checkpoint, both PaMM variants improve over the plain baseline; gate-only gives the best energy MAE, while the affine-equipped variant gives the best force MAE. A matched 20k follow-up keeps the same operating-point picture. Aligned controls show that the gain weakens for pair-only, triplet-only, random-bucket, and parameter-matched MLP alternatives, suggesting that the benefit is tied to structured pair/triplet organization rather than generic added capacity. A within-OMAT24 source-family evaluation also shows small but consistent gains across held-out generation families. We therefore make a focused claim: in the studied UMA-S + OMAT regime, explicit pair/ triplet motif memory is a useful inductive bias for periodic atomistic modeling. We do not claim broad cross-dataset transfer, a uniquely preferred fusion variant, or strong scientific interpretability beyond a more inspectable local-structure interface.

</details>


### [145] [MPINeuralODE: Multiple-Initial-Condition Physics-Informed Neural ODEs for Globally Consistent Dynamical System Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13305)
*Lake Yang,Antonio Malpica-Morales,Frank Ioannis Papadakis Wood,Serafim Kalliadasis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MPINeuralODE，结合软物理信息残差与多初始点多射击课程，解决Neural ODE在未见初始条件和长时域上的泛化问题，在Lotka-Volterra上实现最低的样本外误差和长时域MSE，相比基线Neural ODE降低26%，在哈密顿漂移上基本匹配PINN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Neural ODE在拟合训练轨迹时表现良好，但对未见初始条件和长时域泛化能力差，需要提升其学习到的向量场对真实动力学的恢复能力。

Method: 提出MPINeuralODE，融合两个结构性互补的组件：(1) 软物理信息残差（physics-informed residual），在支撑集上锚定向量场幅度；(2) 多初始点（MIC）多射击课程，扩大支撑集覆盖范围。

Result: 在Lotka-Volterra系统上，MPINeuralODE在样本外误差和长时域MSE上均达到数据驱动方法中的最优，相比基线Neural ODE降低26%的MSE；在哈密顿漂移指标上与PINN消融实验基本持平。

Conclusion: MPINeuralODE通过物理信息约束和多初始点课程的协同作用，有效改善了Neural ODE对未知初始条件和长时域的泛化能力，同时更好地恢复了底层向量场。

Abstract: Neural ordinary differential equations (Neural ODEs) often fit training trajectories while generalizing poorly to unseen initial conditions and long horizons. We propose MPINeuralODE, which combines a soft physics-informed residual with a Multiple-Initial-Condition (MIC) multiple-shooting curriculum whose ingredients are structurally complementary: the physics term anchors the vector-field magnitude on the support that MIC enlarges. We evaluate along three axes: out-of-sample error, long-horizon stability, and Hamiltonian drift, which together expose whether the learned dynamics recover the underlying vector field. On Lotka-Volterra, MPINeuralODE achieves the lowest out-of-sample and long-horizon MSE among data-driven methods, with a 26% reduction over the baseline Neural ODE, while essentially matching the PINN ablation on Hamiltonian drift.

</details>


### [146] [Shortcut Mitigation via Spurious-Positive Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13340)
*Phuong Quynh Le,Jörg Schlötterer,Sari Sadiya,Gemma Roig,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需额外标注或平衡数据的捷径缓解方法，通过识别模型依赖虚假属性的关键神经元并正则化其影响，迫使模型学习更具信息量的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有捷径缓解策略通常依赖训练数据标注、组平衡保留数据或训练数据中存在所有组（即虚假属性与类的所有组合），这些条件在实际中很少满足。

Method: 1) 进行目标模型分析以识别模型依赖虚假属性的小样本集；2) 基于“该特征不应用于预测”的推理，识别中间层中高度相关的神经元；3) 正则化这些神经元的影响，迫使模型学习信息量特征而非虚假关联。

Result: 模型学习依赖信息量特征而非因错误原因正确，提升鲁棒性，无需额外平衡保留数据或标注。

Conclusion: 该方法在不满足传统捷径缓解前提条件的实际场景中，仍能有效改善模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: Shortcut mitigation strategies commonly rely on training data annotations, group-balanced held-out data or the presence of all groups, i.e., all combinations of (spurious) attributes and classes, in the training data. However, these requirements are rarely met in practice. We instead propose a method for targeted model analysis to identify a small set of instances in which the model relies on spurious attributes. Using that set and following ``this feature should not be used for prediction'' reasoning, we identify highly relevant neurons in an intermediate layer and regularize their impact. This ensures that models learn to depend on informative features rather than being right for the wrong reasons, thereby improving robustness without requiring additional balanced held-out data or annotations.

</details>


### [147] [Contextual Bandits for Resource-Constrained Devices using Probabilistic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13346)
*Marco Angioli,Kevin Johansson,Antonello Rosato,Amy Loutfi,Denis Kleyko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概率化HD-CB，一种低精度上下文强盗算法，用概率更新规则替代确定性累加，在保持内存效率的同时避免溢出，并使用低至3比特的组件精度接近全精度HD-CB的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性CB算法在资源受限设备上计算和内存成本过高；HD-CB虽具优势但其累加式学习规则导致向量值持续增长，需要高精度存储；周期性二值化虽可防止溢出但会丢弃幅度信息，降低决策质量。

Method: 提出概率化HD-CB，用概率更新规则替代确定性累加：每一步仅随机更新向量组件的子集，更新概率随时间衰减，组件值约束在预定义范围[-k,+k]内。这允许低精度组件、无需周期性二值化即可防止溢出，并将期望更新成本降低至与更新组件比例成正比。

Result: 在Open Bandit Pipeline的标准合成CB基准上的离线策略评估表明：概率化HD-CB在相同精度下始终优于二值化HD-CB，且使用低至3比特/组件即可接近全精度HD-CB的性能。

Conclusion: 概率化HD-CB为资源受限设备上的上下文强盗问题提供了一种高效低精度解决方案，在内存、计算和决策质量之间取得更优平衡。

Abstract: Contextual bandits (CB) are online sequential decision-making problems under partial feedback that underpin many adaptive services. There is a growing demand to deploy CB agents directly on-device, under strict constraints on memory, compute, and energy. However, standard linear CB algorithms are often impractical for resource-constrained devices with their unfavorable scaling in computational and memory costs. Recently, HD-CB, a CB approach based on hyperdimensional computing principles, has been proposed to model and solve CB problems by moving into high-dimensional spaces. HD-CB offers faster convergence, favorable scalability, and improves memory efficiency compared to linear CB algorithms. However, its learning rule is accumulation-based: the values of action vectors grow over time, requiring high precision. While periodic binarization can prevent overflow in low-precision components, it may discard important information about magnitudes and degrade decision quality. This paper introduces probabilistic HD-CB, a low-precision variant that replaces deterministic accumulation with a probabilistic update rule. At each step, only a random subset of vector components is updated, with a time-decaying update probability, and component values are constrained to a predefined range [-k,+k]. This approach enables low-precision components, prevents overflow without periodic binarization, and reduces the expected update cost in proportion to the fraction of updated components. Off-policy evaluation on standardized synthetic CB benchmarks using the Open Bandit Pipeline shows that probabilistic HD-CB consistently outperforms binarized HD-CB at equal precision, while approaching the performance of HD-CB with as few as 3 bits per component.

</details>


### [148] [GeoFlowVLM: Geometry-Aware Joint Uncertainty for Frozen Vision-Language Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13352)
*Mayank Nautiyal,Li Ju,Andreas Hellander,Ekta Vats,Prashant Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoFlowVLM 是一种后验适配器，通过黎曼流匹配在双编码器 VLM 的乘积超球面上学习联合分布，从而同时量化 aleatoric 不确定性（跨模态歧义）和 epistemic 不确定性（训练分布外支持缺失），并导出条件检索熵和边际典型性评分两种指标，分别在检索和分类任务上实现校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的双编码器视觉-语言模型通过 ℓ2 归一化将图像和文本映射到单位超球面上的确定性点，无法表达 aleatoric 不确定性（跨模态歧义）和 epistemic 不确定性（训练分布外支持缺失）。现有后验方法要么只能恢复其中一种不确定性成分，要么忽略了这些模型嵌入的超球面几何结构。

Method: 提出 GeoFlowVLM，一种后验适配器。它在乘积超球面 S^{d-1} × S^{d-1} 上通过带单一掩码速度场的黎曼流匹配来学习配对 ℓ2 归一化双编码器 VLM 嵌入的联合分布。从该单一模型中导出两种量：条件检索熵（量化 aleatoric 歧义，通过 Fano 型界限给出决策论解释）和边际典型性 epistemic 评分（通过联合 NLL 的精确链式分解证明合理性）。

Result: 在三个检索基准上，熵在双向检索中几乎达到 Recall@1 的理想单调校准；在四个零样本分类基准上，边际典型性求和实现了持续校准的选择性准确率。经验表明，跨模态点互信息项在结构上具有判别性而非 epistemic 性，且是唯一始终无信息价值的独立成分。

Conclusion: GeoFlowVLM 能同时捕捉两种不确定性，并在检索和分类任务上展现出良好的校准性能，为后验不确定性量化提供了兼顾超球面几何的有效方案。

Abstract: Standard dual-encoder vision-language models that map images and text to deterministic points on a shared unit hypersphere through $\ell_2$ normalization typically expose neither \emph{aleatoric} uncertainty (cross-modal ambiguity) nor \emph{epistemic} uncertainty (lack of training-distribution support). Existing post-hoc methods either recover at most one of the two uncertainty components, or ignore the hyperspherical geometry of these models' embeddings. We propose \textbf{GeoFlowVLM} as a post-hoc adapter that learns the joint distribution of paired $\ell_2$-normalised dual-encoder VLM embeddings on the product hypersphere $\mathbb{S}^{d-1} \times \mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ via Riemannian flow matching with a single masked velocity field. A consistency result shows that, in the population limit, the trained network exposes the joint flow and both cross-modal conditional flows as valid Riemannian flow-matching velocity fields on their respective domains. We derive two quantities from this single model: a conditional retrieval entropy that quantifies aleatoric ambiguity with a decision-theoretic interpretation via a Fano-type bound, and a marginal-typicality epistemic score justified by an exact chain-rule decomposition of the joint NLL. This decomposition isolates a cross-modal pointwise-mutual-information term that is structurally discriminative rather than epistemic, and is empirically the only consistently uninformative standalone component. Empirically, the entropy tracks Recall@1 with near-ideal monotonic calibration across three retrieval benchmarks in both directions, and the marginal-typicality sum yields consistently calibrated selective accuracy across four zero-shot classification benchmarks.

</details>


### [149] [Building Interactive Real-Time Agents with Asynchronous I/O and Speculative Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13360)
*Coleman Hooper,Minwoo Kang,Suhong Moon,Nicholas Lee,Eric Wen,John Wawrzynek,Michael W. Mahoney,Yakun Sophia Shao,Amir Gholami,Kurt Keutzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出异步I/O和推测性工具调用方法，通过解耦智能体的推理-执行线程与等待外部信息的过程，实现复杂多轮工具调用场景下的实时低延迟交互。针对云端模型可无改造应用，提供1.3-1.7倍加速；针对边缘模型引入基于时钟的训练方法和合成数据策略，在Qwen2.5-3B和Llama-3.2-3B上实现1.6-2.2倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在执行复杂多轮工具调用时，需要数秒甚至更长的延迟，无法满足语音控制等实时交互场景对亚秒级延迟的要求。因此亟需一种能够在不显著损失准确率的前提下大幅降低延迟的方法，使智能体在等待用户输入或环境反馈的同时能够并行处理任务。

Method: 提出两种核心方法：1) 异步I/O——将智能体的推理-执行线程与等待外部信息（用户输入或环境响应）解耦，允许在等待期间重叠处理智能体任务；2) 推测性工具调用——在智能体不确定是否已获取完整信息时，提前执行可能的工具调用以管理任务时序。针对边缘小模型，还提出基于时钟的训练方法使模型适应流式输入和异步响应，并设计了合成数据生成策略用于SFT微调。

Result: 针对强云端模型（现有实时API），无改造应用即可获得1.3-1.7倍加速，准确率损失极小。针对边缘小模型（Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct和Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct），在多个工具调用基准上取得1.6-2.2倍加速。

Conclusion: 异步I/O和推测性工具调用能够有效降低复杂工具调用场景下的延迟，使实时交互成为可能。该方法对云端模型可即插即用，对边缘模型结合专门的训练策略也能显著提升速度，为实现实时语音交互的智能体系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: There is a growing demand for agentic AI technologies for a range of downstream applications like customer service and personal assistants. For applications where the agent needs to interact with a person, real-time low-latency responsiveness is required; for example, with voice-controlled applications, under 1 second of latency is typically required for the interaction to feel seamless. However, if we want the LLM to reason and execute an agentic workflow with tool calling, this can add can add several seconds or more of latency, which is prohibitive for real-time latency-sensitive applications. In our work, we aim to enable real-time interaction even for agents with complex multi-turn tool calling. We propose Asynchronous I/O, which decouples the core agent reason-and-act thread from waiting for additional information from either the user or environment, thereby allowing for overlapping agentic processing while waiting on external delays. We also propose Speculative Tool Calling as a method to manage task execution when the agent is still unsure if it has received the full information or if additional user information may later be provided. For strong cloud models, our method can be applied out-of-the-box to existing real-time cloud APIs, providing 1.3-1.7$\times$ speedups with minor accuracy loss. To enable real-time interaction with small edge-scale models, we also present a clock-based training methodology that adapts the model to handle streaming inputs and asynchronous responses, and demonstrate a synthetic data generation strategy for SFT. Altogether, this approach provides 1.6-2.2$\times$ speedups with the Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct models across multiple tool calling benchmarks.

</details>


### [150] [Phasor Memory Networks: Stable Backpropagation Through Time for Scalable Explicit Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13370)
*Sungwoo Goo,Hwi-yeol Yun,Sangkeun Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Phasor Memory Network (PMNet) 通过酉相量动力学和分层可学习锚点解决了显式记忆架构在语言建模中因梯度爆炸/消失导致训练不稳定的固有问题，在字节级合成任务中实现近乎完美的长距离检索，并以119M参数匹配三倍规模Mamba模型的零样本长上下文鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式记忆架构（如神经图灵机）在语言建模中因反向传播过程中的灾难性梯度不稳定性而长期无法实用，现有方法依赖暴力缩放而非结构性的解决方案。

Method: 提出PMNet，核心创新包括：(1) 酉相量动力学——将循环状态更新约束在复单位圆上的相位旋转，从而保持梯度范数、本质防止梯度发散；(2) 分层可学习锚点——构建85槽位的层次化记忆树结构，实现远超滑动窗口注意力感受野的长程精确检索。

Result: 在合成Copy-Paste任务中实现近乎100%的精确检索；以119M参数/18.8B tokens训练的模型在零样本长上下文鲁棒性上匹配三倍规模的Mamba模型；消融实验和梯度分析验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式记忆的历史失败本质上是结构对齐问题，PMNet通过理论驱动的架构设计有效克服了这一障碍，为可扩展的序列建模提供了理论坚实的基础。

Abstract: For over a decade, explicit memory architectures like the Neural Turing Machine have remained theoretically appealing yet practically intractable for language modeling due to catastrophic gradient instability during Backpropagation Through Time. In this work, we break this stalemate with \textit{Phasor Memory Network} (PMNet), a novel architecture that structurally resolves memory volatility through \textit{Unitary Phasor Dynamics} and \textit{Hierarchical Learnable Anchors}. Rather than relying on brute-force scaling, we present a mechanistic proof-of-concept in a controlled byte-level setting. By constraining recurrent state updates to phase rotations on a complex unit circle, PMNet preserves gradient norms and inherently prevents divergence without the need for specialized initialization. We empirically demonstrate the active actuation of the memory module through a synthetic Copy-Paste task, where PMNet utilizes an expansive \textit{85-slot hierarchical memory tree} ($=\sum^{4}_{h=1}4^{h-1}$) to achieve near 100\% exact retrieval across temporal distances that completely exceed the local sliding window attention's receptive field. Furthermore, despite being a compact 119M parameter model trained on 18.8B tokens, PMNet matches the zero-shot long-context robustness of a Mamba model that is three times larger. Our ablation studies and gradient analyses confirm that the historical failure of explicit memory was a structural alignment problem, which PMNet effectively overcomes, providing a theoretically grounded foundation for scalable sequence modeling.

</details>


### [151] [Beyond Oversquashing: Understanding Signal Propagation in GNNs Via Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13383)
*Eden Nagar,Ya-Wei Eileen Lin,Ron Levie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出受量子力学启发的Schrödinger GNN，通过可观测量概念建模信号传播，解决GNN中的过平滑和过挤压问题，证明标准谱GNN信号传播能力差，而新模型能更优地路由图上信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GNN中的信号传播存在信息损失，表现为过平滑（oversmoothing）和过挤压（oversquashing），信号在图上扩散而非被有意路由到感兴趣区域。

Method: 受量子力学启发，使用可观测量（observables）概念建模信号在图上的位置、集中程度以及向目标位置的传播量。在此基础上提出Schrödinger GNN，一种新型谱GNN架构。

Result: 证明标准谱GNN信号传播能力较差；提出的Schrödinger GNN在路由信号跨图传播方面具有更优性能。

Conclusion: 量子力学可观测量框架为GNN信号传播提供了新视角，Schrödinger GNN能有效改善信号路由，缓解过平滑和过挤压问题。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) perform computations on graphs by routing the signal between graph regions using a graph shift operator or a message passing scheme. Often, the propagation of the signal leads to a loss of information, where the signal tends to diffuse across the graph instead of being deliberately routed between regions of interest. Two notions that depict this phenomenon are oversmoothing and oversquashing. In this paper, we propose an alternative approach for modeling signal propagation, inspired by quantum mechanics, using the notion of observables. Specifically, we model the place in the graph where the signal lies, how much the signal is concentrated there, and how much of the signal is propagated towards a location of interest when applying a GNN. Using these new concepts, we prove that standard spectral GNNs have poor signal propagation capabilities. We then propose a new type of spectral GNN, termed Schrödinger GNN, which we show has a superior capacity to route the signal across the graph.

</details>


### [152] [Teaching and Learning under Deductive Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13384)
*Jan Arne Telle,Brigt Håvardstun,Jose Hernandez-Orallo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的教学与学习框架，将学习者在演绎推理中可能出现的错误（如人类和大型语言模型在少样本学习中的随机性失败）纳入考虑。在修订的PAC（可能近似正确）设置下，研究了教师如何找到高概率使学习者猜测近似正确假设的教学集，分析了六个相关问题的计算复杂度，并给出了基于教学集大小的参数化算法（XP算法）及紧时间界。最后通过实验研究了最能代表LLM教学行为的学习协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器教学与学习模型假设学习者在内部演绎推理中不会出错，但人类和LLM在少样本学习中经常出现随机性错误或不一致，现有框架无法刻画这一现实。

Method: 提出考虑演绎误差的教学与学习框架，在修改的PAC设置下研究教师如何找到高概率使学习者学习到近似正确假设的教学集，分析六个相关问题的计算复杂度，给出基于教学集大小的XP算法及紧时间界，并通过实验验证。

Result: 给出了六个计算最优PAC教学集问题的计算复杂度分析，提供了参数化的XP算法并证明了在ETH等标准复杂性假设下的紧时间界；实验部分揭示了不同教学协议对LLM教学行为的表征能力。

Conclusion: 将演绎误差纳入教学框架是必要的，所提出的PAC教学框架在理论上有明确的计算复杂度刻画，且能更好地建模LLM等现实学习者的行为。

Abstract: Most models of machine teaching and learning assume the learner makes no errors in its internal deductive inference. However, humans and large language models in few-shot learning regimes are two important examples of learners where this does not hold. They fail on some consistency checks, and they can fail stochastically. In this paper we introduce a teaching and learning framework that takes these deductive errors into account. We specifically study the case of machine teaching, as different characterizations of the teacher can account for both machine teaching and learning. In an overhauled Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) setting, we study theoretically that, for some estimated error level, the teacher must find a PAC teaching set that with high probability will lead the learner to guess a hypothesis that is approximately correct. We study the computational complexity of six different problems related to computing optimal PAC teaching sets. We give XP algorithms parametrized by size of teaching set, with tight runtime bounds under standard complexity assumptions like ETH. These results are complemented with a small experimental study of which teaching and learning protocols can best represent the observed behavior in some LLM teaching sessions.

</details>


### [153] [Support-Conditioned Flow Matching Is Kernel Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13386)
*Daniel Matsui Smola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在最优传输高斯路径下，基于有限支持集的条件生成模型的速度场等价于Nadaraya-Watson核平滑器，其带宽随流时间递减。单头高斯核注意力可精确实现该场，从而建立交叉注意力条件生成与经典核理论之间的联系。理论预测了高维核最近邻坍缩、各向同性核与数据几何不匹配、支持不足三个失效模式，实验验证了这些发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从理论上理解条件生成模型中交叉注意力机制的工作原理，将其与经典核平滑方法建立联系，并揭示其潜在失效模式。

Method: 通过分析高斯最优传输路径下的速度场，证明其等价于Nadaraya-Watson核平滑器，并证明单头高斯核注意力可精确计算该速度场，从而建立理论框架并预测三种失效模式。

Result: 理论预测了三种失效模式（高维核最近邻坍缩、各向同性核与数据几何不匹配、支持不足），并在高斯混合、球壳及DINOv2 ImageNet特征上验证了这些预测，确认IP-Adapter的交叉注意力在实践中近似实现NW平滑。

Conclusion: 条件生成模型中的交叉注意力本质上是一种随时间变化的核平滑过程，其理论框架有助于理解模型行为并指导改进设计。

Abstract: Generative models are often conditioned on a small set of examples via cross-attention. Under the Gaussian optimal-transport path, we show that the exact velocity field induced by a finite support set is a Nadaraya--Watson kernel smoother whose bandwidth decreases with flow time, from broad averaging at early steps to nearest-neighbor at late steps. A single Gaussian-kernel attention head exactly computes this field, connecting cross-attention conditioning to classical kernel theory. The theory predicts three failure regimes: nearest-neighbor collapse of the kernel at high dimension, mismatch between the isotropic kernel and the data geometry, and insufficient support for nonparametric estimation. Experiments on Gaussian mixtures, spherical shells, and DINOv2 ImageNet features confirm that learned conditioning improves in precisely these regimes, and that IP-Adapter's cross-attention implements approximate NW smoothing in practice.

</details>


### [154] [Taming the Long Tail: Rebalancing Adversarial Training via Adaptive Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13395)
*Lilin Zhang,Yimo Guo,Yue Li,Jiancheng Shi,Xianggen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RobustLT框架，通过自适应调整对抗训练中的扰动，同时解决长尾数据下的对抗脆弱性和类别不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗训练研究主要针对平衡数据集，忽略了现实世界中长尾数据带来的挑战。对抗样本的扰动会改变训练分布，这一特性尚未被充分研究以应对长尾数据问题。

Method: 首先理论分析长尾数据对抗训练的两个局限性：类别不平衡导致的训练目标倾斜和对抗分布的不稳定演化。基于扰动能同时应对对抗脆弱性和类别不平衡的洞察，提出RobustLT即插即用框架，自适应调整对抗训练过程中的扰动。

Result: 大量实验表明，RobustLT在长尾数据集上一致提升了对抗鲁棒性和类别平衡性。

Conclusion: 对抗训练中的扰动可以同时解决对抗脆弱性和类别不平衡问题，RobustLT框架有效验证了这一理论。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are highly vulnerable to adversarial examples, i.e.,small perturbations that can significantly degrade model performance. While adversarial training has become the primary defense strategy, most studies focus on balanced datasets, overlooking the challenges posed by real-world long-tail data. Motivated by the fact that perturbations in adversarial examples inherently alter the training distribution, we theoretically investigate their impact. We first revisit adversarial training for long-tail data and identify two key limitations: (i) a skewed training objective caused by class imbalance, and (ii) unstable evolution of adversarial distributions. Furthermore, we show that perturbations can simultaneously address both adversarial vulnerability and class imbalance. Based on these insights, we propose RobustLT, a plug-and-play framework that adaptively adjusts perturbations during adversarial training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RobustLT consistently enhances adversarial robustness and class-balance on long-tailed datasets. The code is available at \href{https://github.com/zhang-lilin/RobustLT}{https://github.com/zhang-lilin/RobustLT}.

</details>


### [155] [The Diffusion Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13399)
*Akhil Premkumar,Sarah Lucioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用扩散模型表达能力的新型编码器，通过受期望最大化算法启发的交替训练方案，解决了传统变分自编码器中编码器与解码器之间的同步问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VAE中，重参数化技巧将编码器限制在简单的分布族中，限制了其表达能力。作者希望用扩散模型替代编码器，以提升编码器的表达能力，同时解决编码器与解码器更新方向冲突的问题。

Method: 提出一种交替训练方案，受期望最大化（EM）算法启发。在训练过程中，编码器（扩散模型）和解码器交替更新，解决了二者倾向于沿相反方向更新潜在表示估计的问题，从而实现了可靠的同步。

Result: 该方法实现了编码器与解码器之间更可靠的同步，同时保留了标准扩散模型的简单高效训练目标。

Conclusion: 通过扩散模型构建新型编码器是可行的，EM启发的交替训练有效解决了传统VAE中编码器表达能力受限的问题，且不增加训练复杂性。

Abstract: We construct a new kind of encoder, leveraging the expressive power of diffusion models. In a traditional variational autoencoder, the encoder and decoder jointly negotiate a latent representation of the input. This is made possible by the reparameterization trick, which simplifies training at the cost of restricting the encoder to a simple family of distributions. Replacing this encoder with a diffusion model requires rethinking how the decoder pressure can be transmitted back to the encoder, given that they tend to update their internal estimates of the latent in opposing directions. We solve this problem with an alternating training scheme, inspired by the expectation-maximization algorithm. Our method enables more reliable synchronization between encoder and decoder, while preserving the simple and efficient training objective of standard diffusion models.

</details>


### [156] [When is Warmstarting Effective for Scaling Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13405)
*Neeratyoy Mallik,Maciej Janowski,Johannes Hog,Herilalaina Rakotoarison,Josif Grabocka,Frank Hutter,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了从检查点扩展模型（模型生长）的实用边界，发现：保持初始性能并非必要，简单架构无关策略优于复杂算子；存在生长因子g的上限，超过该上限从头训练更高效；2×生长因子在多数场景下最可靠，尤其在低数据预算（<20 tokens/参数）下收益显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型生长（从给定检查点扩展大模型）有望节省训练资源，但在大规模训练中实际应用有限。作者认为原因在于：（1）过度强调保持小模型初始性能，限制了新架构的算子设计；（2）对生长与超参数、缩放行为的交互分析不足，且文献中生长因子不一致。

Method: 在密集MLP和密集语言模型上进行消融实验，系统比较不同生长策略、生长因子g对收敛速度的影响；拟合缩放定律为实践提供预测指导。

Result: （1）保持基模型初始生长后性能对最终性能并非必要；（2）架构无关的简单生长策略优于复杂算子；（3）存在生长因子g的上限，超出后从头训练更高效，且该上限在先前的已发表结果中存在但未被报告；（4）2×生长因子在收敛加速上最可靠，收益在<20 tokens/参数预算下最显著，随预算增加递减。

Conclusion: 为模型生长提供了实用指南和经验边界：2×是最可靠的生长因子，生长策略可简化，并给出了何时生长、生长多少的预测性建议。

Abstract: Model growth from a given checkpoint aims to accelerate training of a larger model, offering potential resource savings. Despite recent interest, warmstarting has seen limited practical adoption in large-scale training. We attribute this to two underexplored factors: (1) an overemphasis on preserving the smaller model's performance at initialization, which constrains operator design for new architectures, and (2) insufficient analysis of how growth interacts with hyperparameters and scaling behavior, compounded by inconsistent growth factors across the literature.
  We show that preserving the base model's initial post-growth performance is not necessary for strong final performance, and that simple, architecture-agnostic growth strategies can outperform more complex warmstarting operators. Crucially, we empirically identify an upper bound on the growth factor $g$ beyond which training from scratch is more efficient. We observe this across multiple ablation setups. Notably, this limit is also present, but unreported, in prior published results. Across our experiments on dense MLPs and dense language models, we find that a $2\times$ growth factor is the most reliable in yielding convergence speedups, with gains most pronounced under 20 tokens/parameter budgets and diminishing as budget increases. We fit scaling laws over these observations to provide predictive guidance for practitioners deciding when and how much to grow. Together, our analysis provides practical guidelines and empirical limits for model growth.

</details>


### [157] [LIFT: Last-Mile Fine-Tuning for Table Explicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13424)
*Divij Khaitan,Ashish Tiwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种"最后一英里微调"(Lift)流水线：用预训练大模型从非结构化剪贴板文本提取初始表格，再用微调小模型(1B-24B参数)修复表格错误。在2596个表格基准上，该方法在TEDS指标上匹配或超越端到端小模型微调，仅需1000个训练样本即可超过端到端微调最多0.144 TEDS点，且对输入格式变化更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在从非结构化文本提取表格的任务中，端到端微调小语言模型需要大量训练数据且对输入格式变化敏感，本文旨在探索一种数据高效且鲁棒的替代方案。

Method: 提出Lift流水线：首先用预训练大语言模型从非结构化剪贴板文本中提取初始表格，然后微调一个小语言模型(1B-24B参数)专门修复初始表格中的错误。这种"最后一英里"的纠错范式将任务分解为粗提取和精修复两个阶段。

Result: 在三个数据集的2596个表格上，Lift在TEDS指标上匹配或超越端到端SLM微调；仅需1000个训练样本即可超过端到端微调最多0.144 TEDS点；且对输入格式变化更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 最后一英里微调在训练数据有限或需要输入鲁棒性时，是一种有吸引力的替代方案，能在不牺牲准确性的前提下提供更好的数据效率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: We propose last-mile fine-tuning, or Lift, a pipeline in which a pre-trained large language model extracts an initial table from unstructured clipboard text, and a fine-tuned small language model (1B-24B parameters SLM) repairs errors in the extracted table. On a benchmark of 2,596 tables from three datasets, Lift matches or exceeds end-to-end SLM fine-tuning on tree-edit-distance-based similarity (TEDS) metric while requiring as little as 1,000 training examples - where it outperforms end-to-end fine-tuning by up to 0.144 TEDS points. We term this approach last-mile fine-tuning and show it also more robust to input format variability. Comparisons with self-debug and end-to-end fine-tuning approaches show that last-mile fine-tuning provides an attractive option when training data is limited or when robustness to input variation is sought without compromising on accuracy.

</details>


### [158] [Strategic PAC Learnability via Geometric Definability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13426)
*Yuval Filmus,Shay Moran,Elizaveta Nesterova,Nir Rosenfeld,Alexander Shlimovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究战略分类中个体为影响分类器决策而修改特征的学习问题。发现即使VC维为1的简单假设类，在基本区间邻域下也可能因策略行为导致诱导类VC维无限，使可学问题变为不可学。为此引入基于实数指数域的一阶可定义性几何假设，证明在此假设下可学习性得以保持，样本复杂度由定义公式的复杂度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究战略分类中诱导假设类的样本复杂度是否总能被底层假设类和成本结构的复杂度所控制，以及当一般性保证失效时，如何通过额外结构恢复可学习性。

Method: 首先通过反例证明一般情形下策略行为可导致不可学习性；然后引入基于实数指数域（ℝ_exp）的一阶可定义性几何假设，要求假设类和成本诱导的邻域关系均可由包含算术运算、指数、对数和比较的一阶公式定义；最后在此假设下证明可学习性保持定理。

Result: 存在VC维为1的假设类在简单区间邻域下诱导出VC维无限的策略假设类；但在一阶可定义性假设下，可学习性得以保持，样本复杂度由定义公式复杂度控制。

Conclusion: 战略行为在一般情况下会严重破坏学习问题的可学习性，但通过施加适当的几何可定义性条件（涵盖ℓ_p距离、Wasserstein距离、信息论散度等广泛情形），可以恢复可学习性保证。

Abstract: Strategic classification studies learning settings in which individuals can modify their features, at a cost, in order to influence the classifier's decision. A central question is how the sample complexity of the induced (strategic) hypothesis class depends on the complexities of the underlying hypothesis class and the cost structure governing feasible manipulations. Prior work has shown that in several natural settings, such as linear classifiers with norm costs, the induced complexity can be controlled. We begin by showing that such guarantees fail in general - even in simple cases: there exist hypothesis classes of VC dimension $1$ on the real line such that, even under the simplest interval neighborhoods, the induced class has infinite VC dimension. Thus, strategic behavior can turn an easy learning problem into a non-learnable one. To overcome this, we introduce structure via a geometric definability assumption: both the hypothesis class and the cost-induced neighborhood relation can be defined by first-order formulas over $\mathbb{R}_{\mathtt{exp}}$. Intuitively, this means that hypotheses and costs can be described using arithmetic operations, exponentiation, logarithms, and comparisons. This captures a broad range of natural classes and cost functions, including $\ell_p$ distances, Wasserstein distance, and information-theoretic divergences. Under this assumption, we prove that learnability is preserved, with sample complexity controlled by the complexity of the defining formulas.

</details>


### [159] [Rescaled Asynchronous SGD: Optimal Distributed Optimization under Data and System Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13434)
*Ammar Mahran,Artavazd Maranjyan,Peter Richtárik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rescaled ASGD方法，通过按计算时间重新缩放各工作节点的步长，在保持标准异步SGD框架的同时，纠正了因数据异质性导致的收敛偏差，并证明了其在非凸设置下收敛到正确全局目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准异步SGD在本地数据分布异质时存在偏差：较快的worker贡献更多更新，导致模型收敛到频率加权的局部目标平均值而非全局目标。现有解决方案引入了额外的同步阶段、缓冲或内存，破坏了ASGD的简洁性。

Method: 提出Rescaled ASGD方法，保持标准ASGD机制不变，仅根据各worker的计算时间重新缩放其步长，使得每个worker在一个周期内贡献相同的累积学习率。

Result: 在非凸光滑且 bounded heterogeneity 假设下，证明了Rescaled ASGD收敛到正确全局目标的平稳点；时间复杂度在主导项上匹配已知下界，延迟和数据异质性仅影响低阶项；实验验证收敛到正确目标且与baselines竞争。

Conclusion: 无需改变ASGD的基本框架，仅通过步长重缩放即可消除数据异质性带来的偏差，方法简洁且理论完备。

Abstract: Asynchronous stochastic gradient descent (ASGD) is a standard way to exploit heterogeneous compute resources in distributed learning: instead of forcing fast workers to wait for slow ones, the server updates the model whenever a gradient arrives. Vanilla ASGD applies each arriving gradient with the same weight. When local data distributions are heterogeneous, this becomes problematic: faster workers contribute more updates, and we show theoretically that the method is biased toward a frequency-weighted average of the local objectives rather than the desired global objective. Existing remedies typically move away from the simple ASGD template by introducing gathering phases, buffering, or extra memory. We show that this is unnecessary. Keeping the standard ASGD mechanism, we recover the correct objective by rescaling worker-specific stepsizes in proportion to their computation times, so that each worker contributes the same aggregate learning rate over a cycle. In the non-convex setting, under smoothness and bounded heterogeneity assumptions, we prove that the resulting method, Rescaled ASGD, converges to stationary points of the correct global objective in the fixed-computation model. Its time complexity matches the known lower bound in the leading term, while the effects of staleness and data heterogeneity appear only in lower-order terms. Experiments confirm that the method converges to the correct objective and is competitive with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [160] [Q-Flow: Stable and Expressive Reinforcement Learning with Flow-Based Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13435)
*JaeHyeok Doo,Byeongguk Jeon,Seonghyeon Ye,Kimin Lee,Minjoon Seo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-Flow通过利用流模型的确定性动力学将终端轨迹价值显式传播到中间潜状态，实现了无需展开数值求解器的稳定策略优化，在离线强化学习OGBench基准上平均超越SOTA 10.6个百分点，同时支持稳定的在线适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于流的模型在强化学习中具有高表达能力，但基于梯度的优化需要反向传播通过数值求解器，导致不稳定。现有方法通过限制流策略的表达能力来缓解此问题，造成了优化稳定性与表示灵活性之间的权衡。

Method: 提出Q-Flow框架，利用流动力学的确定性特性，将终端轨迹价值显式传播到沿策略诱导流的中间潜状态，从而在不展开数值求解器的情况下使用中间价值梯度进行稳定的策略优化。

Result: 在离线学习设置的OGBench基准测试中，Q-Flow平均超越SOTA基线10.6个百分点，并在同一框架内支持稳定的在线适应。

Conclusion: Q-Flow有效弥合了基于流的策略在优化稳定性与表达能力之间的差距，为高表达流模型在强化学习中的价值最大化提供了一种稳定且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: There is growing interest in utilizing flow-based models as decision-making policies in reinforcement learning due to their high expressive capacity. However, effectively leveraging this expressivity for value maximization remains challenging, as naive gradient-based optimization requires backpropagating through numerical solvers and often leads to instability. Existing approaches typically address this issue by restricting the expressive capacity of flow-based policies, resulting in a trade-off between optimization stability and representational flexibility. To resolve this, we introduce Q-Flow, a framework that leverages the deterministic nature of flow dynamics to explicitly propagate terminal trajectory value to intermediate latent states along the policy-induced flow. This formulation enables stable policy optimization using intermediate value gradients without unrolling the numerical solver, effectively bridging the gap between stability and expressivity. We evaluate Q-Flow in the offline learning setting on the challenging OGBench suite, where it consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by an average of 10.6 percentage points, while also enabling stable online adaptation within the same framework.

</details>


### [161] [Efficient Sensor Fusion for Gesture Recognition on Resource-Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13462)
*Pietro Bartoli,Christian Veronesi,Tommaso Bondini,Andrea Giudici,Franco Zappa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于低分辨率ToF和红外热传感器融合的轻量级手势识别系统，在STM32微控制器上仅用6,343参数实现92.3%准确率，系统功耗仅50 mW。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉手势识别在智能眼镜等可穿戴设备上面临高功耗、高延迟和隐私泄露问题，亟需一种轻量级、保护隐私的方案。

Method: 使用8×8多区域ToF传感器(VL53L8CH)和8×8红外阵列(AMG8833)分别采集深度和热信号，通过紧凑型CNN（分组卷积架构）在MCU上进行多模态融合识别7种静态手势。

Result: 在7种静态手势数据集上达到92.3%准确率和0.93宏F1分数，在STM32F4/H7上推理延迟为毫秒级，系统总功耗50 mW。

Conclusion: 该融合方案显著优于单传感器基线，且资源需求极低，适合部署于智能眼镜等资源受限的可穿戴设备。

Abstract: Gesture recognition is a cornerstone of Human-Computer Interaction (HCI) for smart eyewear, enabling natural and device-free control in augmented reality environments. Traditional vision-based approaches face significant challenges regarding power consumption, computational latency, and user privacy. This paper proposes a lightweight, privacy-preserving gesture recognition system based on the fusion of low-resolution Time-of-Flight (ToF) and Infrared (IR) thermal sensors. We used an 8 times 8 multizone ToF sensor (VL53L8CH) and an 8 times 8 IR array (AMG8833) to capture complementary depth and thermal cues. A compact Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) with a specialized grouped-convolution architecture is designed to fuse these modalities efficiently on a microcontroller (MCU). Experimental results on a custom dataset of 7 static gestures, validated via k-fold cross-validation, demonstrate that the proposed fusion strategy significantly outperforms single-sensor baselines with an accuracy of 92.3% and a macro F1-score of 0.93. Finally, on-device benchmarks on STM32F4 and STM32H7 MCUs confirm the system's suitability for resource-constrained wearables, requiring only 6,343 parameters and achieving millisecond-level inference latency with a total system power of 50 mW.

</details>


### [162] [A Unified Three-Stage Machine Learning Framework for Diabetes Detection, Subtype Discrimination, and Cognitive-Metabolic Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13464)
*Vishal Pandey,Ruzina Haque Laskar,Rishav Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可复现的三阶段机器学习框架，用于糖尿病检测、亚型聚类及代谢-认知关联分析。在第一阶段，五种监督分类器与Stacking集成在NCSU糖尿病数据集上进行了基准测试，SVM-RBF和Logistic回归取得了最优ROC-AUC（0.825±0.026），SHAP可解释性识别出Glucose、BMI和Age为关键预测生物标志物。第二阶段对确诊糖尿病患者进行K-Means聚类（k=2，轮廓系数≈0.116），恢复了临床合理的亚型分区。第三阶段对俄亥俄纵向认知数据集（n=373）的统计分析揭示了血糖控制与认知功能之间的显著正相关（ρ_s=0.208，p=5.29×10^{-5}）。研究结果支持统计严谨且可解释的机器学习管道在糖尿病分析中的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习研究主要将糖尿病预测建模为二分类问题，而对亚型导向分析和血糖-认知关联的探索相对不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，提出一个涵盖检测、亚型聚类和关联分析的可复现框架。

Method: 三阶段框架：Stage 1采用五种监督分类器（含Stacking集成）进行糖尿病检测，基于分层五折交叉验证评估，使用SHAP进行模型解释；Stage 2对确诊患者进行轮廓系数验证的K-Means聚类（k=2），恢复亚型分区；Stage 3对俄亥俄纵向认知数据集进行Spearman相关分析，检验血糖控制与认知功能的关系。

Result: Stage 1: SVM-RBF和Logistic回归ROC-AUC最高（0.825±0.026），随机森林准确率最高（0.762±0.030）。SHAP识别出Glucose、BMI和Age为关键生物标志物。Stage 2: K-Means聚类轮廓系数约0.116，恢复临床合理的亚型分区。Stage 3: 血糖控制与认知功能显著正相关（ρ_s=0.208, p=5.29×10^{-5}），通过Holm校正。

Conclusion: 统计严谨且可解释的机器学习管道在可复现的糖尿病分析和亚型探索分析中具有实用价值。血糖控制与认知功能的显著关联经多重比较校正后仍然成立，为代谢-认知关联提供了统计证据。

Abstract: Diabetes mellitus affects over 537 million adults worldwide and remains a major challenge in preventive healthcare. Existing machine-learning studies primarily formulate diabetes prediction as a binary classification problem, while subtype-oriented analysis and glycaemic-cognitive associations remain comparatively underexplored. We present a reproducible three-stage machine learning framework for diabetes detection, subtype-oriented clustering, and metabolic-cognitive association analysis. In Stage 1, five supervised classifiers together with a stacking ensemble are benchmarked on the NCSU Diabetes Dataset using stratified five-fold cross-validation and evaluation metrics including ROC-AUC, balanced accuracy, recall, and F1-score. SVM-RBF and Logistic Regression achieve the highest ROC-AUC ($0.825 \pm 0.026$), while Random Forest achieves the highest accuracy ($0.762 \pm 0.030$). SHAP explainability identifies Glucose, BMI, and Age as the dominant predictive biomarkers. In Stage 2, silhouette-validated K-Means clustering ($k=2$, silhouette $\approx 0.116$) is applied to confirmed diabetic cases using Glucose, Insulin, and Age, recovering clinically plausible subtype-oriented partitions without requiring ground-truth subtype labels. In Stage 3, statistical analysis of the Ohio Longitudinal Cognitive Dataset ($n=373$) reveals a significant positive association between glycaemic control and cognitive function ($ρ_s = 0.208$, $p = 5.29 \times 10^{-5}$), which survives Holm correction. The findings support the utility of statistically grounded and interpretable ML pipelines for reproducible diabetes analytics and subtype-aware exploratory analysis.

</details>


### [163] [Twincher: Bijective Representation Learning for Robust Inversion of Continuous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13470)
*Arkady Gonoskov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Twincher架构，通过结构化微分同胚变换堆叠与对抗训练学习前向过程的双射表示，实现鲁棒且高效的逆问题求解，在合成系统上展现出比基线方法更好的数据效率和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI进展主要依赖大规模神经架构的函数逼近能力，而非针对资源高效感知与规划的归纳偏置或推理策略。本文探索通过双射表示学习实现连续前向过程的鲁棒逆变换，以提升逆问题求解的效率与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Twincher架构，基于结构化微分同胚变换堆叠，结合定制化对抗训练策略，学习对噪声和模型失配不敏感且与前向参数双射对齐的表示。

Result: 在合成系统上验证了架构高效学习双射表示的能力，实现了鲁棒且高效的迭代逆推理。相比基线逆建模方法，在数据效率和鲁棒性上均有提升。

Conclusion: 双射表示学习在机器人、视觉和物理AI领域具有潜力，为资源高效的逆问题求解提供了新路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI have been primarily driven by large-scale neural architectures that excel at function approximation, rather than by tailored inductive biases and inference or learning strategies that could be important for resource-efficient real-world perception and planning through the solution of inverse problems. In this work, we consider the possibility of enabling robust inversion of continuous forward processes $p \mapsto y$ by learning representations of $y$ that are bijectively aligned with $p$ while remaining insensitive to perturbations in $y$ caused by noise or model mismatch. We propose Twincher, a class of architectures based on stacks of structured diffeomorphic transformations and tailored adversarial training strategies that enable learning such bijective representations. We provide a public API for training and inference and empirically demonstrate the ability of the proposed architecture to efficiently learn bijective representations of synthetic systems, thereby enabling robust and efficient iterative inverse inference. Compared to a baseline inverse-modeling approach, the method exhibits improved data efficiency and robustness, providing initial evidence for the potential of bijective representation learning in robotics, vision, and physical AI.

</details>


### [164] [OSDN: Improving Delta Rule with Provable Online Preconditioning in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13473)
*Chenyu Zhou,Hongpei Li,Yuerou Liu,Jianghao Lin,Dongdong Ge,Yinyu Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Online Scaled DeltaNet (OSDN)，通过在线对角预处理器增强DeltaNet的标量门控，在不破坏硬件高效的分块并行流水线的前提下，显著提升了关联召回能力，并在340M和1.3B参数规模上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DeltaNet虽然通过单步在线梯度下降实现了恒等内存的关联召回，但其步长依赖单一标量门控，忽略了内部目标函数在特征维度的曲率信息，导致召回能力受限。

Method: OSDN引入在线超梯度反馈更新的对角预处理器，该右预处理等价于对写入侧键进行逐特征缩放，从而保留DeltaNet的分块并行流水线。同时提出自适应预处理器遗忘机制应对非平稳上下文。

Result: 340M参数下，JRT风格内上下文召回提升32%；1.3B参数下，召回残差比降低39%，通用下游任务（困惑度、LongBench）保持持平。

Conclusion: 在线预处理机制能够有效迁移并放大至十亿参数规模，在不牺牲通用任务性能的前提下显著提升关联召回能力。

Abstract: Linear attention and state-space models offer constant-memory alternatives to softmax attention, but often struggle with in-context associative recall. The Delta Rule mitigates this by writing each token via one step of online gradient descent. However, its step size relies on a single scalar gate that ignores the feature-wise curvature of the inner objective. We propose Online Scaled DeltaNet (OSDN), which augments the scalar gate with a diagonal preconditioner updated online via hypergradient feedback. Crucially, this right-preconditioning is algebraically equivalent to a per-feature scaling of the write-side key. This equivalence allows OSDN to strictly preserve the hardware-friendly chunkwise parallel pipeline of DeltaNet without incurring high-dimensional state overhead. Theoretically, by exploiting the exact-quadratic structure of the inner regression loss, we establish super-geometric convergence against a right-Newton comparator and prove an algorithm-aligned token-local residual contraction bound. To handle non-stationary contexts, we further introduce Adaptive Preconditioner Forgetting (APF) to dynamically refresh stale calibration. Empirically, OSDN demonstrates strong performance across scales. At the 340M-parameter scale, OSDN improves JRT-style in-context recall by 32% over DeltaNet. Scaling to 1.3B parameters, it achieves a 39% reduction in the recall residual ratio while maintaining parity on general downstream tasks (e.g., perplexity and LongBench) -- demonstrating that our online-preconditioning mechanism effectively transfers and amplifies at the billion-parameter scale.

</details>


### [165] [Discovery of Hidden Miscalibration Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13484)
*Katarzyna Kobalczyk,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种发现并校正模型局部校准失败的方法，通过学习校准感知的输入空间表示，利用核平滑估计有符号的局部误校准场，实验发现大语言模型存在普遍输入相关的校准异质性，且该方法能有效修正系统误校准区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统校准评估仅依赖置信度分数，将可靠性视为置信度的单一函数，这掩盖了模型在不同输入上可能表现出的系统性过置信或欠置信结构，导致全局可靠性指标无法发现局部的校准失败。

Method: 定义误校准场概念，提出诊断框架：首先学习校准感知的输入空间表示，然后在学习到的几何空间中使用核平滑估计有符号的局部误校准。

Result: 在四个真实世界LLM基准和十二个LLM上，发现输入相关的校准异质性普遍存在；所发现的场可用于局部置信度校正，在系统误校准区域比等温回归和温度缩放等基于置信度的方法更有效。

Conclusion: 输入依赖的校准异质性是LLM中普遍存在的现象，通过建模局部误校准场可以揭示隐藏的校准失败结构，并支持针对性的局部校正，弥补传统全局校准方法的不足。

Abstract: Calibration is commonly evaluated by comparing model confidence with its empirical correctness, implicitly treating reliability as a function of the confidence score alone. However, this view can hide substantial structure: models may be systematically overconfident on some kinds of inputs and underconfident on others, causing global reliability diagnostics to obscure localised calibration failures. To address this, we formulate the problem of discovering hidden miscalibration regimes without assuming access to predefined data slices. We define the corresponding miscalibration field and propose a diagnostic framework for estimating it. Our approach learns a calibration-aware representation of the input space and estimates signed local miscalibration by kernel smoothing in the learned geometry. Across four real-world LLM benchmarks and twelve LLMs, we find that input-dependent calibration heterogeneity is prevalent. We further show that the discovered fields are actionable: they support local confidence correction and reduce calibration error in systematically miscalibrated regions where confidence-based methods such as isotonic regression and temperature scaling are less effective.

</details>


### [166] [Effective Context in Transformers: An Analysis of Fragmentation and Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13485)
*Amirmehdi Jafari Fesharaki,Mohammadamin Rami,Aslan Tchamkerten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定上下文窗口下，序列表示方式（字节、字符、子词）如何影响Transformer的预测能力。针对马尔可夫源，发现：1）将表示单元细化（如字节级表示）会因“碎片化”现象导致预测损失增加，即使扩大窗口也无法弥补；2）贪心分词（如BPE）可让短token窗口等效于更长的源上下文窗口，其保证取决于token窗口覆盖源历史的能力与压缩率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相同数据可表示为不同粒度（字节、字符、子词），这些表示在给定固定上下文窗口下，暴露给模型的信息不同，从而影响预测能力。作者希望从信息论角度理解这种差异的本质原因。

Method: 在马尔可夫源上从两个方向研究：1）定义“碎片化”（fragmentation）概念——将源符号无损重编码为多个更小单元，证明碎片化会严格增加有限上下文的对数损失；2）分析贪心分词（BPE、WordPiece等）将源符号组合成更大单元的效果，给出损失保证条件，并设计诊断指标衡量真实分词器在固定窗口内包含的源上下文量。

Result: 1）碎片化导致有限上下文预测损失增加是表示本身的固有性质，非优化或容量问题，解释了ByT5、CANINE等字节/字符级模型相比子词模型的性能差距；2）贪心分词可使短token窗口等效于更长源上下文窗口，效果取决于token窗口可靠覆盖源历史的程度和分词器压缩率。

Conclusion: 建立了关于Transformer中表示选择的有限上下文信息论框架，从理论上解释了不同粒度表示（字节、字符、子词）对预测能力影响的本质原因，并为实际分词器提供了诊断工具。

Abstract: Transformers predict over a representation of a sequence. The same data can be written as bytes, characters, or subword tokens, and these representations may be lossless. Yet, under a fixed context window, they need not expose the same information to the model. This raises a basic question: how does the choice of representation change what a finite-context predictor can achieve?
  We study this question on Markov sources and uncover two complementary phenomena. First, we observe that moving to smaller representation units can hurt prediction even when the context window is enlarged to cover the relevant source history. To explain this, we introduce fragmentation: a lossless recoding that replaces each source symbol by several smaller units. We prove that fragmentation can strictly increase the optimal finite-context log-loss, showing that the gap is not merely an optimization or capacity issue, but can be intrinsic to the representation. This gives a theoretical account of the finite-context gap observed in byte- and character-level models such as ByT5 and CANINE relative to subword-tokenized models. Second, we study the opposite direction: greedy tokenization -- BPE, WordPiece, and related methods -- which groups source symbols into larger units. We show that tokenization can make a short token window behave like a longer source-context window, and we give a loss guarantee describing when this is achievable. The guarantee depends on how reliably token windows span the needed source history, together with the compression rate of the tokenizer. This also yields a simple diagnostic for real tokenizers: measuring how much source context a fixed token window reliably contains. Together, the two directions establish a finite-context information-theoretic framework for reasoning about representation choices in Transformers.

</details>


### [167] [Path-independent Flow Matching for Multi-parameter Generative Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13487)
*Francisco Téllez,AmirHossein Zamani,Philippe Martin,Shuang Ni,Guy Wolf,Eugene Belilovsky,Sina Sanjari,Yanlei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出路径无关流匹配（PiFM），将标准Flow Matching推广到多参数情形，确保变换路径无关性，并近似Wasserstein重心，在合成与真实数据上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Flow Matching仅支持单参数形式，无法处理需要路径无关性的多参数分布变换场景，而路径无关性对确保变换结果仅依赖初始与目标分布、而非具体路径至关重要。

Method: 提出PiFM框架，学习满足结构条件的向量场以实现路径无关传输；设计了一种可训练的、无仿真的目标函数，用于回归多参数条件概率路径。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据上，PiFM在路径无关轨迹插值和生成期望的分布外样本方面优于其他方法。

Conclusion: PiFM有效推广了Flow Matching到高维参数域，同时为分布插值提供了理论联系（近似Wasserstein重心），具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Flow Matching is a powerful framework for learning transport maps between probability distributions. Yet its standard single-parameter formulation is not designed to capture multi-parameter variations where the resulting transport should be path-independent. Path independence is crucial because it ensures that transformations depend only on the initial and target distributions, not on the specific path. In this work, we introduce Path-independent Flow Matching (PiFM), a method for learning vector fields whose induced flows yield path-independent transport between distributions. We show that PiFM generalizes Flow Matching to higher-dimensional parameter domains while enforcing structural conditions that ensure consistency of composed transformations. In addition, we show that, under suitable assumptions, PiFM approximates the Wasserstein barycenter, linking the framework to a notion of distributional interpolation. To enable practical training, we propose a tractable, simulation-free objective that regresses onto multi-parameter conditional probability paths. We showcase empirically that PiFM outperforms other approaches on both synthetic and real world data in interpolating path-independent trajectories and generating desired out of distribution samples.

</details>


### [168] [Temper and Tilt Lead to SLOP: Reward Hacking Mitigation with Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13537)
*Ye Wang,Jing Liu,Toshiaki Koike-Akino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过参考模型温度调整将推理时对齐技术推广到生成式奖励模型集成（SLOP），并设计了一种校准算法以缓解奖励破解问题，实验表明该方法在保持对齐性能的同时提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐技术已被理论证明为对最优倾斜分布采样的近似，但尚未充分探索如何将其推广到多奖励模型集成场景，且存在奖励破解风险。

Method: 引入参考模型温度调整，将推理时对齐扩展为基于锐化对数意见池（SLOP）的生成式奖励模型集成；提出一种SLOP权重参数的校准算法来缓解奖励破解。

Result: 实验表明所提出的校准算法在保持对齐性能的同时，显著提升了模型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 参考模型温度调整和SLOP集成策略有效扩展了推理时对齐技术的适用范围，而校准算法则为其实际部署提供了可靠性保障。

Abstract: Inference-time alignment techniques offer a lightweight alternative or complement to costly reinforcement learning, while enabling continual adaptation as alignment objectives and reward targets evolve. Existing theoretical analyses justify these methods as approximations to sampling from distributions optimally tilted toward a given reward model. We extend these techniques by introducing reference-model temperature adjustment, which leads to further generalization of inference-time alignment to ensembles of generative reward models combined as a sharpened logarithmic opinion pool (SLOP). To mitigate reward hacking, we propose an algorithm for calibrating SLOP weight parameters and experimentally demonstrate that it improves robustness while preserving alignment performance.

</details>


### [169] [Decoupled and Divergence-Conditioned Prompt for Multi-domain Dynamic Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13540)
*Haonan Yuan,Qingyun Sun,Junhua Shi,Xingcheng Fu,Jianxin Li,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DyGFM，一种基于解耦和分歧条件提示的多领域动态图基础模型，通过语义-时间解耦的双分支预训练、分歧感知专家选择的路由机制以及分歧条件提示生成器，解决了多领域动态图统一建模中的负迁移问题，在节点分类和链接预测任务上超越12个基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图在不同领域间的语义和时间模式存在固有差异，导致多领域预训练困难，"预训练-微调"范式常出现严重的负知识迁移，目前尚不存在多领域动态图基础模型。

Method: 提出DyGFM，包含三个核心组件：(1) 双分支预训练策略实现语义-时间解耦，分离可迁移语义与领域特定动态；(2) 跨域路由机制通过分歧感知专家选择缓解领域适应中的负迁移；(3) 分歧条件提示生成器注入轻量级可学习图提示，适配语义和时间特征。

Result: 在连续动态图基准上，DyGFM在节点分类和链接预测任务上一致超越12个最先进基线方法，展现出优越的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: DyGFM成功构建了首个多领域动态图基础模型，通过解耦和分歧条件提示有效克服了跨域负迁移问题，为动态图统一建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Dynamic graphs are ubiquitous in real-world systems, and building generalizable dynamic Graph Foundation Models has become a frontier in graph learning. However, dynamic graphs from different domains pose fundamental challenges to unified modeling, as their semantic and temporal patterns are inherently inconsistent, making the multi-domain pre-training difficult. Consequently, the widely used "pretrain-then-finetune" paradigm often suffers from severe negative knowledge transfer. To the best of our knowledge, there exists no multi-domain dynamic GFM. In this work, we propose DyGFM, a Dynamic Graph Foundation Model over multiple domains based on decoupled and divergence-conditioned prompting. To disentangle transferable semantics from the domain-specific dynamics, we introduce a dual-branch pre-training strategy with semantic-temporal decoupling. To alleviate negative transfer during domain adaptation, we further develop a cross-domain routing mechanism with divergence-aware expert selection. To enable efficient downstream fine-tuning, we design a divergence-conditioned prompt generator that injects lightweight, learnable graph prompts tailored to semantic and temporal traits. Extensive experiments on continuous dynamic graph benchmarks demonstrate that DyGFM consistently outperforms 12 state-of-the-art baselines on both node classification and link prediction tasks, achieving superior effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [170] [Self-Supervised On-Policy Reinforcement Learning via Contrastive Proximal Policy Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13554)
*Asim Osman,Sasha Abramowitz,Mark Bergh,Ulrich Armel Mbou Sob,Ruan John de Kock,Omayma Mahjoub,Oussama Hidaoui,Noah De Nicola,Arnol Manuel Fokam,Felix Chalumeau,Daniel Rajaonarivonivelomanantsoa,Siddarth Singh,Refiloe Shabe,Juan Claude Formanek,Simon Verster Du Toit,Arnu Pretorius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CPPO，一种基于策略的对比强化学习算法，通过对比Q值直接推导策略优势，使用PPO目标优化，无需奖励函数或经验回放。在连续/离散、单智能体/多智能体任务上，CPPO不仅在14/18个任务上显著优于现有CRL基线，还在12/18个任务上匹配或超越使用手工密集奖励的PPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比强化学习(CRL)算法均依赖离策略优化且局限于连续动作空间，与广泛使用且高效的在策略训练流程（如PPO，应用于单/多智能体、连续/离散环境）脱节。

Method: 提出CPPO(Contrastive Proximal Policy Optimisation)，一种在策略对比RL算法：从对比Q值中直接推导策略优势，并通过标准PPO目标进行优化，无需奖励函数或经验回放缓冲区。

Result: 在18个连续/离散、单智能体/多智能体任务上，CPPO在14个任务中显著优于先前CRL基线，在12个任务中匹配或超越使用手工密集奖励的PPO。

Conclusion: CPPO成功将CRL与在策略PPO训练流程连接起来，弥合了CRL与主流PPO框架之间的鸿沟，在无奖励设定下展现出强大竞争力。

Abstract: Contrastive reinforcement learning (CRL) learns goal-conditioned Q-values through a contrastive objective over state-action and goal representations, removing the need for hand-crafted reward functions. Despite impressive success in achieving viable self-supervised learning in RL, all existing CRL algorithms rely on off-policy optimisation and are mostly constrained to continuous action spaces, with little research invested in discrete environments. This leaves CRL disconnected from widely used and effective, modern on-policy training pipelines adopted across both single-agent and multi-agent RL in continuous and discrete environments. To establish a first connection, we introduce Contrastive Proximal Policy Optimisation (CPPO). CPPO is an on-policy contrastive RL algorithm that derives policy advantages directly from contrastive Q-values and optimises them via the standard PPO objective, without requiring a reward function or a replay buffer. We evaluate CPPO across continuous and discrete, single-agent and cooperative multi-agent tasks. Whilst the existence of an on-policy approach is inherently useful, we observe that \textbf{CPPO not only significantly outperforms the previous CRL baselines in 14 out of 18 tasks, but also matches or exceeds PPO's performance, which uses hand-crafted dense rewards, in 12 out of the 18 tasks tested.}

</details>


### [171] [Spatiotemporal downscaling and nowcasting of urban land surface temperatures with deep neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13566)
*Solomiia Kurchaba,Angela Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用U-Net模型将SEVIRI/MSG（3 km/15分钟）的LST数据降尺度至1 km/15分钟分辨率，并结合ConvLSTM实现15-75分钟的LST临近预报，在欧洲大城市测试中RMSE为1.92°C（降尺度）和0.57-1.15°C（预报）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卫星LST产品存在空间分辨率与时间分辨率之间的根本性权衡（高空间或高时间，但无法兼得），亟需一种方法提供同时具备高空间和高时间分辨率的LST数据。

Method: 两步法：首先使用U-Net模型，将SEVIRI/MSG（3 km/15 min）的LST映射到Terra/Aqua MODIS（1 km，每日4次过境）的LST，实现空间降尺度；然后基于ConvLSTM架构，对降尺度后的LST场进行15-75分钟提前量的临近预报。

Result: 降尺度模型在保留测试集上RMSE=1.92°C，MBE=0.01°C；临近预报模型RMSE为0.57-1.15°C，偏差-0.1至0.14°C，优于持久性和气候滚动中位数基准。

Conclusion: 该方法能够生成高时空分辨率（1 km/15 min）的LST预报，可直接应用于基于卫星的LST业务监测。

Abstract: Land Surface Temperature (LST) is a key variable for various applications, such as urban climate and ecology studies. Yet, existing satellite-derived LST products provide either high spatial or high temporal resolution, resulting in a fundamental trade-off between the two. To address this trade-off, we combine observations from a geostationary and a polar orbiting satellite and provide LST fields at high spatial and high temporal resolution (1 km at 15-min intervals). We demonstrate their application for intraday forecasting of LSTs. To estimate LST fields at high spatiotemporal resolution, a U-Net model is trained to map LST fields from SEVIRI/MSG (3 km and 15 min resolution) to LST fields from Terra/Aqua MODIS (1 km, 4 overpasses per day) that are collocated in space and time. The presented model has been trained on LSTs across large European cities with a population exceeding 1 million inhabitants, and achieves an RMSE = $1.92$°C and near-zero bias MBE = $0.01$°C on the hold-out test set. As a second step, we present an LST nowcasting model based on ConvLSTM architecture, trained across downscaled LST fields with forecast lead times of 15 to 75 minutes. The nowcasting model outperforms a persistence and a Climatological Rolling Median benchmarks, with RMSEs of $0.57$ to $1.15$°C for the considered lead times and biases ranging from $-0.1$ to $0.14$°C. An additional validation conducted against independent MODIS overpasses confirms robust performance. Our LST forecast model at high spatiotemporal resolution is directly applicable to operational satellite-based LST monitoring.

</details>


### [172] [Dynamical Predictive Modelling of Cardiovascular Disease Progression Post-Myocardial Infarction via ECG-Trained Artificial Intelligence Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13568)
*Riccardo Cavarra,Lupo Lovatelli,Shaheim Ogbomo-Harmitt,Shahid Aziz,Adelaide De Vecchi,Andrew King,Oleg Aslanidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合对比学习和监督多任务头的预训练AI模型，利用临床结构化心电图建模，在有限数据下显著提升心肌梗死后不良结局预测性能（AUC 0.794 vs 0.608）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心肌梗死是主要死因，需要预测其不良结局。现有基于ECG的预后模型表现不佳，因为深度学习需要大量标注数据而医学数据稀缺。基础模型可从无标注ECG中自监督学习，但医学相关的训练策略尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出一个预训练AI模型，通过对比学习结合患者特定时间信息，并搭配监督多任务头，然后在心肌梗死后结局预测任务上进行微调。

Result: 所提模型在有限数据场景下显著优于从头训练的模型（AUC 0.794 vs 0.608）。

Conclusion: 临床结构化心电图建模能在数据有限的情况下改善分类性能，验证了结合医学先验知识的自监督预训练策略的有效性。

Abstract: Myocardial infarction (MI) is a leading cause of death, and its adverse outcomes are urgent to predict. Yet ECG-based prognostic models underperform because deep learning requires large, labelled datasets, which are scarce in medicine. Foundation models can learn from unlabelled ECGs via selfsupervision, but medically relevant training strategies remain underexplored. We propose a pretrained artificial intelligence model that combines patient-specific temporal information using contrastive learning with supervised multitask heads, then fine-tunes on post-MI outcome prediction. The proposed model outperformed a model trained from scratch (0.794 vs 0.608 AUC) showing that clinically structured ECG modelling improves classification in limited data regimes.

</details>


### [173] [Achieving $ε^{-2}$ Sample Complexity for Single-Loop Actor-Critic under Minimal Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13639)
*Ishaq Hamza,Zaiwei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对离策略演员-评论家方法，在单循环、单时间尺度实现下，首次证明了在仅存在可诱导不可约马尔可夫链的策略这一最弱假设下，达到ε-最优策略的样本复杂度为Õ(ε^{-2})，并通过耦合李雅普诺夫漂移框架解决了单循环实现中的耦合更新和潜在无界迭代问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中，Õ(ε^{-2})的样本复杂度仅能通过嵌套循环更新或在策略的强假设（如均匀混合、均匀探索）下实现。本文旨在最小假设下建立离策略演员-评论家方法在单循环、单时间尺度实现下的最后迭代收敛率。

Method: 采用耦合李雅普诺夫漂移框架，分别建立演员的几何收敛率和评论家的Õ(1/T)收敛率，并通过交叉支配性质将两个李雅普诺夫漂移不等式结合，以处理单循环实现中的耦合更新方程和离策略学习导致的潜在无界迭代问题。

Result: 在仅存在可诱导不可约马尔可夫链的策略这一最弱假设下，首次证明了单循环、单时间尺度离策略演员-评论家方法达到ε-最优策略的样本复杂度为Õ(ε^{-2})。

Conclusion: 建立了离策略演员-评论家方法在最小假设下的最后迭代收敛率，所提出的耦合李雅普诺夫漂移框架具有独立价值，可推广至其他具有无界迭代的耦合迭代算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish last-iterate convergence rates for off-policy actor--critic methods in reinforcement learning. In particular, under a single-loop, single-timescale implementation and a broad class of policy updates, including approximate policy iteration and natural policy gradient methods, we prove the first $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity guarantee for finding an $ε$-optimal policy under minimal assumptions, namely, the existence of a policy that induces an irreducible Markov chain. This stands in stark contrast to the existing literature, where an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity is achieved only through nested-loop updates and/or under strong, algorithm-dependent assumptions on the policies, such as uniform mixing and uniform exploration. Technically, to address the challenges posed by the coupled update equations arising from the single-loop implementation, as well as the potentially unbounded iterates induced by off-policy learning, our analysis is based on a coupled Lyapunov drift framework. Specifically, we establish a geometric convergence rate for the actor and an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/T)$ convergence rate for the critic, and combine the two Lyapunov drift inequalities through a cross-domination property. We believe this analytical framework is of independent interest and may be applicable to other coupled iterative algorithms with unbounded

</details>


### [174] [Multi-Objective and Mixed-Reward Reinforcement Learning via Reward-Decorrelated Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13641)
*Yang Bai,Kaiyuan Liu,Ziyuan Zhuang,Jiahong Zhou,Rongxiang Weng,Xin Chen,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多任务和混合奖励强化学习环境中的奖励分布异质性和维度相关性问题，提出RDPO方法，通过幅度感知分位数归一化和马氏白化处理，显著提升长上下文物模型的指令遵循、写作质量和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂强化学习环境常采用多任务和混合奖励设置，其中异质化的奖励分布和相关联的奖励维度会破坏标量优势函数的构建稳定性，导致训练不稳定。

Method: 提出Reward-Decorrelated Policy Optimization (RDPO)，包含两个关键组件：1) 幅度感知分位数归一化，用于稳定跨二元、分数和连续奖励的提示级优势分配；2) 在每个活跃奖励子空间内应用马氏白化，在聚合前缓解相关性冗余。

Result: 在LongCat-Flash的后训练中应用RDPO，增强了指令遵循能力、写作质量和难提示鲁棒性，同时在推理和编码评估上保持广泛竞争力。

Conclusion: RDPO通过显式解耦奖励分布异质性和奖励维度相关性问题，提供了一种有效的奖励处理方法，能够在保持推理和编码能力的同时提升语言模型在指令遵循和写作方面的表现。

Abstract: Complex reinforcement learning environments frequently employ multi-task and mixed-reward formulations. In these settings, heterogeneous reward distributions and correlated reward dimensions often destabilize the construction of scalar advantages. To address these challenges, we propose Reward-Decorrelated Policy Optimization (RDPO), a reward-processing method designed to explicitly target both failure modes. RDPO first utilizes Magnitude-Aware Quantile normalization to stabilize prompt-level advantage allocation across binary, fractional, and continuous rewards. It then applies Mahalanobis whitening within each active reward subspace to mitigate correlation redundancy prior to aggregation. When applied during the post-training of LongCat-Flash, RDPO enhances instruction following, writing quality, and robustness to hard prompts while remaining broadly competitive on reasoning and coding evaluations.

</details>


### [175] [Beyond Perplexity: A Geometric and Spectral Study of Low-Rank Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13652)
*Namrata Shivagunde,Vijeta Deshpande,Sherin Muckatira,Anna Rumshisky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统地比较了五种低秩预训练方法（GaLore、Fira、CoLA、SLTrain、ReLoRA）与全秩训练在60M、130M、350M三个模型尺度上的表现，从损失景观、权重谱结构、激活相似性和下游性能等16个指标进行多维评估，发现低秩方法与全秩训练在几何和表征上存在根本性差异，且验证困惑度不能充分反映模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低秩预训练方法虽能缓解全秩训练的内存开销，但现有比较仅依赖单次运行的验证困惑度，而困惑度无法揭示模型是否收敛到不同的损失景观区域和内部表征。作者旨在填补这一评估空白，系统性地考察低秩方法是否与全秩训练达到本质上不同的解。

Method: 选取五种代表性低秩方法（GaLore、Fira、CoLA、SLTrain、ReLoRA），在60M、130M、350M三个尺度上与全秩训练对比，沿四个维度（1-D损失景观、检查点插值、权重谱结构、激活相似性）共16个指标进行系统评估。

Result: 低秩方法与全秩训练不等价，彼此也不等价；全秩训练沿随机方向收敛到更尖锐的盆地，而沿top-1 PCA方向则相反；每种方法收敛到几何上不同的盆地；低秩激活在后期层偏离全秩训练，GaLore最接近；验证困惑度不能可靠预测下游性能。

Conclusion: 验证困惑度不足以衡量模型质量，需引入几何和谱指标；低秩预训练方法的解空间与全秩训练存在本质差异，这为未来低秩方法的设计和评估提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Pre-training large language models is dominated by the memory cost of storing full-rank weights, gradients, and optimizer states. Low-rank pre-training has emerged to address this, and the space of methods has grown rapidly. A central question remains open: do low-rank methods produce models that generalize comparably to full-rank training, or does the rank constraint fundamentally alter the solutions reached? Existing comparisons rely almost entirely on validation perplexity from single-seed runs, often carried forward from prior literature. Yet perplexity is a poor proxy for solution quality; two methods can match on perplexity while converging to different loss landscape regions and internal representations. We close this gap by characterizing the solutions found by five low-rank pre-training methods, GaLore and Fira (memory-efficient optimizers), CoLA and SLTrain (architecture reparameterizations), and ReLoRA (adapter-style updates with periodic resets), against full-rank training at three model scales (60M, 130M, 350M). We evaluate each along 16 metrics across four dimensions: 1-D loss landscape along random/top-K PCA directions, 1-D interpolation between checkpoints, spectral structure of the weights and learned updates, and activation similarity to full-rank training. We show that low-rank methods are not equivalent to full-rank training, nor to one another, even when validation perplexity is close. Full-rank training settles into a sharper basin than low-rank methods along random directions, while the reverse holds for the top-1 PCA direction. Each method converges to a geometrically distinct basin. Low-rank activations diverge from full-rank in later layers as training progresses, with GaLore tracking full-rank most closely. Further, validation perplexity does not translate to downstream performance at every scale. Adding geometric and spectral metrics improves the prediction.

</details>


### [176] [Three-Stage Learning Unlocks Strong Performance in Simple Models for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13678)
*Zhenan Yu,Guangxin Jiang,Jin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出STAIR训练范式，通过分阶段（共享学习→个体微调→残差学习）逐步提升简单时间映射模型（如浅层MLP）的长期时序预测能力，在9个基准上匹配或超越复杂模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强基线仍依赖频域建模、显式分解等复杂结构先验，忽略了简单时间映射如何训练和组织的核心问题；旨在无需复杂架构即可激发简单模型潜力。

Method: 三阶段渐进式训练：1) 共享时间映射学习跨变量公共动态；2) 通道级微调适配每个变量的独有模式（Shared-to-Individual Fine-tuning）；3) 残差学习补充跨变量信息。另提出alpha-RevIN缓解标准RevIN的过强归一化先验。

Result: 在9个长期预测基准上，以浅层MLP为核心时序预测器，STAIR匹配或超越近期强基线方法。

Conclusion: 提供了一种简洁有效的长期时序预测建模视角：通过合理的训练策略而非复杂架构即可释放简单模型的能力。

Abstract: Recent studies on long-term time series forecasting have shown that simple linear models and MLP-based predictors can achieve strong performance without increasingly complex architectures. However, many competitive baselines still rely on structural priors such as frequency-domain modeling, explicit decomposition, multi-scale mixing, or sophisticated cross-variable interaction modules, while paying less attention to how simple temporal mappings should be trained and organized. In this paper, we propose STAIR, short for Stagewise Temporal Adaptation via Individualization and Residual Learning, a training paradigm for long-term time series forecasting that aims to unlock the capacity of simple temporal mapping models without introducing complex architectural modules. STAIR decomposes forecasting ability into three progressive stages: it first learns common temporal dynamics across variables through a shared temporal mapping, then adapts the shared model to each variable via channel-wise fine-tuning to capture variable-specific patterns, and finally complements the backbone with cross-variable information through residual learning. We further introduce Shared-to-Individual Fine-tuning and alpha-RevIN to mitigate the limitations of strict channel independence and the overly strong normalization prior induced by standard RevIN. This design gradually increases modeling flexibility while keeping the core temporal predictor as a shallow MLP in the main experiments, with linear variants analyzed separately. Experiments on nine long-term forecasting benchmarks show that STAIR matches or outperforms recent strong baselines while preserving a simple temporal backbone, providing a concise and effective modeling perspective for long-term time series forecasting.

</details>


### [177] [Sampling from Flow Language Models via Marginal-Conditioned Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13681)
*Iskander Azangulov,Leo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于后验预测的采样器用于流语言模型（FLMs），该采样器通过从因子化后验分布中采样干净的单热端点，然后以该端点为条件从奥恩斯坦-乌伦贝克桥中采样连续状态，实现了无需额外训练的解码控制，并改善了质量-多样性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准DDPM风格采样器将后验边际坍缩为单一条件均值端点，然后向这个单纯形值桥接，但该值通常不是有效的单热序列。因此需要一种更自然的采样方法，能够利用FLM去噪器的特殊结构。

Method: 提出后验预测采样器：每个逆向步骤中，先根据FLM token边际的因子化后验采样干净的单热端点，再以该端点为条件从解析的奥恩斯坦-乌伦贝克桥中采样下一个连续状态。该方法无需额外训练，且支持温度缩放和核截断等token级解码控制。

Result: 在精确后验边际下，端点近似误差恰好是token位置间的条件多信息。一步桥核保留了所有token-wise后验预测边际，仅损失了残差交叉位置依赖。Girsanov路径空间比较证明边际条件桥的去噪误差项不大于冻结条件均值桥，且在中间坐标桥观测提供清洁token额外信息时严格改进。实验表明该方法改善了质量-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 后验预测采样器是FLM更自然的采样方式，提供了理论保证和实际性能提升，且便于实现各种解码控制策略。

Abstract: Flow Language Models (FLMs) are a recently introduced class of language models which adapt continuous flow matching for one-hot encoded token sequences. Their denoisers have a special structure absent from generic continuous diffusion models: each block of the denoising mean is a posterior marginal distribution over the clean token at that position. Standard DDPM-style samplers collapse these marginals to a single conditional-mean endpoint and bridge toward this simplex-valued point, which is generally not a valid one-hot sequence. We argue that the natural sampler for an FLM is instead posterior-predictive. At each reverse step, we sample a clean one-hot endpoint from the factorized posterior defined by the FLM token marginals, and then sample the next continuous state from the analytic Ornstein--Uhlenbeck bridge conditioned on that endpoint. The method is training-free, uses the same model evaluations as standard sampling, and gives a principled interface for token-level decoding controls such as temperature scaling and nucleus truncation. We show that, under exact posterior marginals, the endpoint approximation error is exactly the conditional multi-information among token positions. The induced one-step bridge kernel preserves all token-wise posterior-predictive marginals and loses only the residual cross-position dependence. Finally, we prove a Girsanov path-space comparison showing that the marginal-conditioned bridge has a no-larger denoising-error term than the frozen conditional-mean bridge, with strict improvement whenever intermediate coordinate-wise bridge observations reveal additional information about the clean token. Experiments with FLMs show that the sampler improves the quality--diversity tradeoff. Code is available at: github.com/imbirik/mcb.

</details>


### [178] [Scale-Sensitive Shattering: Learnability and Evaluability at Optimal Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13684)
*Shashaank Aiyer,Yishay Mansour,Shay Moran,Han Shao,Tom Waknine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了实值函数类在尺度γ下的一致收敛、agnostic可学习性与fat-shattering维数有限性之间的等价关系，将原始PAC学习基本定理推广到尺度敏感版本。关键贡献包括：直接界经验ℓ∞覆盖数以避免通过填充数的标准绕行，从而消除先前工作中存在的乘性2倍间隙；获得关于fat-shattering尺度的渐近度量熵紧界（O(log² n)和O(log n)）；并应用于有界积分概率度量，证明每个IPM要么可估计，要么无法在乘性因子c<3下弱评估，而3-弱评估总是成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Anthony和Bartlett（1999）关于实值函数类学习性的精确尺度问题，反驳Phil Long关于乘性2倍间隙不可避免的猜想，改进Bartlett和Long（1998）的上界；同时解决Alon等（1997）以及Rudelson和Vershynin（2006）关于度量熵的开放问题；并回答Aiyer等（ICML 2026）关于有界积分概率度量的可评估性问题。

Method: 关键技术创新是直接界经验ℓ∞覆盖数，避免了传统通过填充数（packing numbers）的迂回途径。基于此，建立了尺度敏感的泛化界，并推导出关于fat-shattering尺度γ的渐近度量熵上界：在尺度γ/2时得到O(log² n)界，在尺度2γ时得到O(log n)界。

Result: 1) 建立了均匀收敛、agnostic可学习性与fat-shattering维数之间的尺度敏感等价关系（消除了乘性2倍间隙）；2) 获得了最优的渐近度量熵界（O(log² n)在某些情况下紧）；3) 对有界积分概率度量，证明了每个IPM要么可估计，要么无法在因子c<3下弱评估，而3-弱评估总是成立。

Conclusion: 该工作将PAC学习基本定理推广到尺度敏感版本，解决了多个长期开放的猜想和问题，并揭示了实值函数类学习中尺度间隙的精确本质。同时提出了若干关于定量样本复杂度和可评估性的开放问题。

Abstract: We study the optimal scale at which real-valued function classes exhibit uniform convergence and learnability. Our main result establishes a scale-sensitive generalization of the fundamental theorem of PAC learning: for every bounded real-valued class and every $γ>0$, uniform convergence at scale $γ$, agnostic learnability at scale $γ/2$, and finiteness of the fat-shattering dimension at every scale $γ'>γ$ are equivalent. This resolves a question by Anthony and Bartlett (Cambridge Univ. Press 1999) on the precise scales governing learnability, refuting a conjecture attributed there to Phil Long that a multiplicative 2-factor gap is unavoidable, and improves the upper bounds of Bartlett and Long (JCSS 1998), which incur such a loss.
  The key technical ingredient is a direct bound on empirical $\ell_\infty$ covering numbers, avoiding the standard detour through packing numbers. As a consequence, we obtain sharp asymptotic metric-entropy bounds in terms of the fat-shattering scale $γ$: an $O(\log^2 n)$ bound holds already at scale $γ/2$, while an $O(\log n)$ bound holds at scale $2γ$. We further show that the $O(\log^2 n)$ bound is sometimes tight. These results resolve open questions by Alon et al. (JACM 1997) and Rudelson and Vershynin (Ann. of Math. 2006).
  As an application, we establish a sharp dichotomy for bounded integral probability metrics: every such IPM is either estimable or cannot be weakly evaluated within any multiplicative factor $c<3$, while $3$-weak evaluability always holds, resolving an open question from Aiyer et al. (ICML 2026). We also highlight several open questions on quantitative sample complexity and evaluability.

</details>


### [179] [A Hierarchical Language Model with Predictable Scaling Laws and Provable Benefits of Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13687)
*Jason Gaitonde,Frederic Koehler,Elchanan Mossel,Joonhyung Shin,Allan Sly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一类具有层次结构的合成语言（基于树上广播过程），通过精确k-gram假设替代Transformer，证明有界上下文自回归模型采样长度n序列需要Ω(n)上下文长度，而仅需Θ(log n)工作记忆的自回归推理模型可实现指数级改进的精确采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作缺乏对自回归生成中上下文长度与推理能力间关系的精确理论分析，需要一种可解析研究的合成语言框架。

Method: 提出树上广播过程生成的层次结构合成语言族；引入精确k-gram假设替代k上下文长度的Transformer（经实证验证）；在Ising广播过程（软约束）和染色广播过程（硬约束）两种设定下进行理论分析。

Result: 1) Ising过程：生成和的方差与上下文深度呈对数线性关系，峰度收敛到高斯分布，任何次线性上下文均偏离真实语言；2) 染色过程（冻结区）：有界上下文自回归生成序列高概率与底层树的任何有效染色不一致；3) 推导出忠实采样长度n序列需Ω(n)上下文下界；4) 证明仅需Θ(log n)工作记忆的自回归推理模型可精确采样（指数级改进）；5) 实验结果定量验证理论预测。

Conclusion: 上下文长度与推理能力之间存在根本性权衡：纯自回归模型需要线性上下文才能忠实生成，而引入推理能力（工作记忆）可将需求降至对数级别，实现指数级改进。

Abstract: We introduce a family of synthetic languages with hierarchical structure -- generated by a broadcast process on trees -- for which the role of context length and reasoning in autoregressive generation can be analyzed precisely. At the heart of our analytic approach is an \emph{exact $k$-gram ansatz} in place of transformers with context length $k$, a substitution we then validate empirically. Using this ansatz we derive explicit asymptotic predictions for distributional statistics of the sequences produced by a trained model, instantiated in two settings. For the \emph{Ising broadcast process} (a soft-constrained language), we prove that the variance of the generated sum scales log-linearly in the context depth and its kurtosis converges to that of a Gaussian -- both deviating from the true language for any sublinear context. For the \emph{coloring broadcast process} (a hard-constrained language) in the freezing regime, bounded-context autoregression produces sequences that, with high probability, are inconsistent with \emph{any} valid coloring of the underlying tree. Together these results imply an $Ω(n)$ lower bound on the context length required to faithfully sample length-$n$ sequences. In contrast, we prove that an autoregressive \emph{reasoning} model with only $Θ(\log n)$ working memory can sample exactly from the true language -- an exponential improvement. We confirm both the lower-bound predictions and the reasoning-based upper bound empirically with transformers trained on the synthetic language; the trained models track our asymptotic predictions quantitatively across a wide range of context sizes.

</details>


### [180] [Polyhedral Instability Governs Regret in Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13692)
*Yuetai Li,Fengqing Jiang,Yichen Feng,Kaiyuan Zheng,Luyao Niu,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Linda Bushnell,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究组合动作在线决策问题的遗憾界，提出“多面体不稳定性”（区域切换次数）是核心量，并证明遗憾界在满信息反馈下为 Θ(√((1+RS_T) T log V_max))，在在线子模-凹博弈中简化为 Θ(√((1+SC_T) T log n))，实验验证了低不稳定性场景在实际中可自然出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合动作空间上的在线决策问题通常通过凸松弛转化为在线凸优化（OCO），但传统OCO遗憾界依赖维度，无法充分利用组合结构的特殊性。本文旨在揭示组合动作在线学习中遗憾的根本来源是多面体结构的不稳定性（即活动区域的切换次数），而非单纯的动作空间维度，从而在低切换场景下获得更紧的遗憾界。

Method: 理论方面：在满信息反馈和固定划分假设下，定义区域切换次数 RS_T 和每区域最大顶点数 V_max，推导出遗憾的上下界 Θ(√((1+RS_T) T log V_max))。针对在线子模-凹博弈，通过Lovász凸化将其转化为多面体OCO，将RS_T进一步简化为排列切换次数 SC_T，得到 Θ(√((1+SC_T) T log n))。实验方面：在最短路径和影响力最大化等组合问题上验证了理论预测的尺度律。

Result: 建立了遗憾与多面体不稳定性之间的精确量化关系：遗憾界为 Θ(√((1+RS_T) T log V_max))；在在线子模-凹博弈中，遗憾界简化为 Θ(√((1+SC_T) T log n))；实验表明实际组合问题中RS_T和SC_T可以远小于理论上限，低不稳定性场景确实存在。

Conclusion: 本文揭示组合在线决策的遗憾主要由多面体结构的不稳定性（区域切换次数）主导，而非动作空间的维度，提供了介于专家学习（对数依赖）与维度依赖OCO（线性依赖）之间的插值结果，为利用组合问题低不稳定性特性设计更高效在线算法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Many online decision problems over combinatorial actions are addressed via convex relaxations, leading to online convex optimization with piecewise linear objectives and induced polyhedral structure.
