We show that regret in such problems is governed by \emph{polyhedral instability}: the number of changes of the active region. Under full information feedback and fixed partition assumptions, if $\mathrm{RS}_T$ denotes the number of region switches and $V_{\max}$ the maximum number of vertices per region, we prove $\Regret_T= Θ(\sqrt{(1+\mathrm{RS}_T)\,T\,\log V_{\max}})$ interpolating between experts-like and dimension-dependent OCO rates. For online submodular--concave games under Lovász convexification, this reduces to the permutation-switch count $\mathrm{SC}_T$, yielding the matching rate $\Regret_T= Θ(\sqrt{(1+\mathrm{SC}_T)\,T\,\log n})$. Experiments on synthetic and real combinatorial problems (shortest path, influence maximization) validate the predicted scaling and indicate that low-instability regimes can arise in practice without explicit enumeration of actions.

</details>


### [181] [MILM: Large Language Models for Multimodal Irregular Time Series with Informative Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13711)
*Hsing-Huan Chung,Shijun Li,Yoav Wald,Xing Han,Suchi Saria,Joydeep Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MILM，将多模态不规则时间序列（MITS）表示为XML格式的时间有序三元组，通过两阶段微调策略训练LLM进行分类：第一阶段仅基于采样模式训练（数值掩蔽），第二阶段联合建模采样模式与观测值。该方法在多个EHR数据集上取得最优或次优性能，且能有效利用采样模式中的预测信号，在值缺失场景下表现尤为突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态不规则时间序列（MITS）包含异步、不规则采样的异构数值与文本数据（如EHR中的实验室测量和临床笔记），其不规则的时间与通道模式本身携带预测信号。现有方法难以统一处理这类异构数据，而LLM具备跨文本与数值领域的预训练知识，有潜力成为处理MITS的天然候选。

Method: 1) 将MITS表示为时间有序三元组的XML格式；2) 设计两阶段微调策略：第一阶段用值掩蔽的MITS训练LLM仅从采样模式中学习预测信号，第二阶段用完整MITS训练联合建模采样模式与观测值。同时对比了单阶段微调版本MILM-Direct。

Result: MILM-2S（两阶段）和MILM-Direct（单阶段）在多个EHR数据集上分别取得最佳和次佳平均性能。值掩蔽评估证实采样模式确实携带预测信号，且MILM-2S能有效利用它们。在提出的"值待定"评估场景（预测时部分值不可用）中，MILM-2S相比MILM-Direct的优势更大。

Conclusion: LLM结合XML格式的序列化表示与两阶段微调，能够有效处理MITS数据，不仅利用观测值本身，还能捕获不规则采样模式中的预测信号，尤其在值缺失场景下展现出鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal irregular time series (MITS) consist of asynchronous and irregularly sampled observations from heterogeneous numerical and textual channels. In healthcare, for example, patients' electronic health records (EHR) include irregular lab measurements and clinical notes. The irregular timing and channel patterns of observations carry predictive signal alongside the numerical values and textual content. LLMs are natural candidates for processing such heterogeneous data, given their extensive pretrained knowledge spanning textual and numerical domains. We introduce MILM (Multimodal Irregular time series Language Model), which represents MITS as time-ordered triplets in Extensible Markup Language (XML) format and fine-tunes an LLM through a two-stage strategy for MITS classification. The first stage trains on value-redacted MITS to predict from sampling patterns alone, and the second stage trains on full MITS to jointly model sampling patterns and observed values. Our two-stage model (MILM-2S) and its single-stage counterpart (MILM-Direct) achieve the best and second-best average performance on multiple EHR datasets. Further value redaction evaluations confirm that sampling patterns carry predictive signal and that MILM-2S learns to exploit them. In the value pending evaluation we introduce, where some values are unavailable at prediction time, MILM-2S outperforms MILM-Direct by a larger margin compared to standard evaluation. For MILM-2S, preserving the time and channel of value-pending observations as additional sampling information further improves in-hospital mortality prediction.

</details>


### [182] [Tight Sample Complexity Bounds for Entropic Best Policy Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13717)
*Amer Essakine,Claire Vernade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限时域风险敏感强化学习中的最优策略识别问题，提出了一种基于KL探索奖励的前向模型算法，利用指数效用函数的平滑性推导出更紧的集中界，并设计了新的停止规则，从而将样本复杂度从O(e^{2|β|H})降至与下界Ω(e^{|β|H})匹配，弥合了指数级差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作在熵风险度量下的最优策略识别中存在指数级差距：样本复杂度下界为Ω(e^{|β|H})，而上界仅为O(e^{2|β|H})，作者旨在弥合这一差距，实现与下界匹配的样本复杂度。

Method: 提出前向模型算法，基于KL散度的探索奖励，利用指数效用函数的平滑性导出更紧的集中界，并设计新的停止规则以充分利用这种紧性。

Result: 样本复杂度达到与下界Ω(e^{|β|H})匹配的水平，消除了额外的指数因子。

Conclusion: 通过更紧的集中界和新的停止规则，成功弥合了风险敏感强化学习中最优策略识别的指数级样本复杂度差距，证明了该问题的紧致上界。

Abstract: We study best-policy identification for finite-horizon risk-sensitive reinforcement learning under the entropic risk measure. Recent work established a constant gap in the exponential horizon dependence between lower and upper bounds on the number of samples required to identify an approximately optimal policy. Precisely, known lower bounds scale in $Ω(e^{|β| H})$ where $H$ is the horizon of the MDP, while the state-of-the-art upper bound achieves at best $O(e^{2|β| H})$ (arXiv:2506.00286v2) using a generative model. We show that this extra exponential factor can be traced to overly loose concentration control for exponential utilities. To close this open gap, we revisit the analysis of this problem through a forward-model based algorithm building on KL-based exploration bonuses that we adapt to the entropic criterion. The improvement we get is due to two main novel technical innovations. We leverage the smoothness properties of the exponential utility to derive sharper concentration bounds, and we propose a new stopping rule that exploits further this tightness to obtain a sample complexity that matches the lower bound.

</details>


### [183] [Robust and Explainable Bicuspid Aortic Valve Diagnosis Using Stacked Ensembles on Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13730)
*Christos Chrysanthos Nikolaidis,Vasileios Sachpekidis,Nikolas Moustakidis,Theofilos Moustakidis,Pavlos S. Efraimidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多骨干视频集成的可解释AI模型，利用常规经胸超声心动图胸骨旁长轴电影循环区分二叶式主动脉瓣与三叶式主动脉瓣，在90例患者研究中实现了0.907的F1分数，并通过Grad-CAM和SHAP提供了透明的病例级可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经胸超声心动图是诊断二叶式主动脉瓣的首选影像学方法，但其诊断性能受操作者经验和图像质量影响，存在诊断不一致的问题。开发可靠的自动化辅助诊断工具有助于减少对操作者经验的依赖。

Method: 采用多骨干视频集成模型，在90例患者研究（48例BAV，42例TAV）上使用泄漏感知的分层外部交叉验证协议进行训练和评估。利用帧级Grad-CAM定位主动脉根部和瓣叶平面的关键证据，并使用全局聚合的SHAP值量化每个视频骨干对集成预测的贡献。

Result: 校准后的集成模型在外部交叉验证中达到了0.907的F1分数和0.877的召回率。

Conclusion: 基于PLAX视频集成的方法能够从常规超声心动图电影循环中可靠地进行BAV/TAV分类，有助于在非专科或资源有限的临床环境中实现更早期的检测。

Abstract: Transthoracic echocardiography (TTE) is the first-line imaging modality for diagnosing bicuspid aortic valve (BAV), yet diagnostic performance varies with operator expertise and image quality. We developed an explainable AI model that distinguishes BAV from tricuspid aortic valves (TAV) using routinely acquired parasternal long-axis (PLAX) cine loops. A multi-backbone video ensemble was trained and evaluated using a leakage-aware, stratified outer cross-validation protocol on $N{=}90$ patient studies (48 BAV, 42 TAV). Across fixed outer splits and 10 random seeds, the calibrated stacked ensemble achieved an outer-CV F1-score of $0.907$ and recall of $0.877$. Frame-level Grad-CAM localized salient evidence to the aortic root and leaflet plane, while globally aggregated SHAP values quantified each video backbone's contribution to the stacked prediction, enabling transparent, case-level auditability. These findings indicate that PLAX-based video ensembles can support reliable BAV/TAV classification from routine echocardiographic cine loops and may facilitate earlier detection in non-specialist or resource-limited clinical settings.

</details>


### [184] [Learning POMDP World Models from Observations with Language-Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13740)
*Valentin Six,Frederik Panse,Mathis Fajeau,Lancelot Da Costa,Mridul Sharma,Alfonso Amayuelas,Tim Z. Xiao,David Hyland,Philipp Hennig,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Pinductor方法，利用LLM作为先验从少量观测-动作轨迹中归纳POMDP世界模型，通过迭代优化信念似然分数，在无需隐藏状态信息的情况下匹配甚至超越需要隐藏状态信息的LLM方法，显著优于表格型POMDP基线，展示了语言模型先验在部分可观测环境下进行样本高效世界模型学习的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测环境中，智能体学习有效的内部世界模型（POMDP）通常需要大量环境交互，样本效率低下。研究者希望利用语言模型蕴含的先验知识来减少昂贵的交互成本，实现从少量轨迹中高效学习世界模型。

Method: 提出Pinductor框架：LLM作为先验，从少量观测-动作轨迹中候选生成POMDP模型，然后通过迭代优化机制，基于信念似然分数（belief-based likelihood score）对候选模型进行筛选和精炼，逐步提升模型质量。

Result: 1) Pinductor在性能和样本效率上与需要隐藏状态特权信息的LLM-based POMDP学习方法持平；2) 显著优于表格型POMDP基线的样本效率；3) 性能随LLM能力提升而提高；4) 当环境语义信息被遮蔽时，性能下降但仍然保持一定鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 语言模型先验可以作为部分可观测环境下样本高效世界模型学习的实用工具，为构建能够在真实环境中运行的通用智能体迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Whether navigating a building, operating a robot, or playing a game, an agent that acts effectively in an environment must first learn an internal model of how that environment works. Partially-observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) provide a flexible modeling class for such internal world models, but learning them from observation-action trajectories alone is challenging and typically requires extensive environment interaction. We ask whether language-model priors can reduce costly interaction by leveraging prior knowledge, and introduce \emph{Pinductor} (POMDP-inductor): an LLM proposes candidate POMDP models from a few observation-action trajectories and iteratively refines them to optimize a belief-based likelihood score. Despite using strictly less information, \emph{Pinductor} matches the performance and sample efficiency of LLM-based POMDP learning methods that assume privileged access to the hidden state, while significantly surpassing the sample efficiency of tabular POMDP baselines. Further results show that performance scales with LLM capability and degrades gracefully as semantic information about the environment is withheld. Together, these results position language-model priors as a practical tool for sample-efficient world-model learning under partial observability, and a step toward generalist agents in real-world environments. Code is available at https://github.com/atomresearch/pinductor.

</details>


### [185] [GHGbench: A Unified Multi-Entity, Multi-Task Benchmark for Carbon Emission Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13743)
*Yifan Duan,Siyuan Zheng,Lihuan Li,Chao Xue,Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GHGbench 是一个面向公司和建筑层面温室气体排放预测的开放数据集与基准，涵盖32,000+公司年记录和491,591建筑年记录，定义了标准化的数据划分与评估协议，发现了建筑排放预测更难、分布内到分布外差距主导性能差异、多模态遥感嵌入在表格模型泛化失效处有效等核心发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实体级碳排放预测的开放数据集和基准在可访问性、规模、粒度以及评估标准上存在碎片化问题，缺乏统一、标准化的评估框架。

Method: 构建了两个track：公司track（32,000+公司年记录，含Scope 1+2和Scope 3披露及金融/行业信号）和建筑track（491,591建筑年记录，来自13个开放源，覆盖26个大都市区，含气候协变量和多模态遥感嵌入）。定义标准数据划分方案，以分布内和跨区域/城市迁移为主要任务，时间留出和短期预测为补充。基线包括梯度提升树、表格基础模型、MLP、FT-Transformer和多模态融合，辅以LLM面板，使用多种子配对Bootstrap检验评估。

Result: 三个基准级发现：(i) 建筑排放预测结构性更难；(ii) 分布内到分布外差距远大于任何模型间差距，表格基础模型首次在配对Bootstrap检验中显著超越调优树；(iii) 多模态遥感嵌入在表格泛化失效处发挥精准作用。还暴露了灾难性城市迁移和行业因子查找天花板等系统性失败模式。

Conclusion: GHGbench为碳排放预测提供了统一的开放基准，揭示了当前方法的局限性（如跨城市迁移失败），并指出了多模态融合等有前景的方向。代码和重建配方已公开。

Abstract: Open datasets and benchmarks for entity-level carbon-emission prediction remain fragmented across access, scale, granularity, and evaluation. We introduce GHGbench, an open dataset and benchmark for company- and building-level greenhouse-gas prediction. The company track contains 32,000+ company-year records from 12,000+ firms with Scope 1+2 and Scope 3 disclosures and financial/sectoral signals; the building track harmonises 491,591 building-year records from 13 open sources into a single schema across 26 metropolitan areas (10 U.S., 15 Australian, 1 Singaporean), with climate covariates and multimodal remote-sensing embeddings. GHGbench defines canonical splits with in-distribution and cross-region/city transfer as primary tasks and temporal hold-out plus short-horizon forecasting as supplementary appendix evidence; headline baselines span gradient-boosted trees, a tabular foundation model, MLP, FT-Transformer, and multimodal fusion, with an LLM panel as auxiliary, all evaluated under multi-seed paired-bootstrap tests. Three benchmark-level findings emerge: (i) building emissions are structurally harder than company emissions; (ii) the in-distribution to out-of-distribution gap dwarfs any within-model gap across both the company track and the building track, and a tabular foundation model is, to our knowledge, the first baseline to open a paired-bootstrap-significant gap over tuned trees on a multi-city building-emissions task; (iii) multimodal remote-sensing embeddings help precisely where tabular generalisation breaks. GHGbench also exposes catastrophic city transfer and the sector-factor lookup ceiling as systematic failure modes. Code and reconstruction recipes are available at GHGbench.

</details>


### [186] [Min Generalized Sliced Gromov Wasserstein: A Scalable Path to Gromov Wasserstein](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13753)
*Ashkan Shahbazi,Xinran Liu,Ping He,Soheil Kolouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出min-GSGW，一种基于可学习广义切片器的切片化Gromov-Wasserstein方法，通过学习兼容的非线性切片器将单调耦合从投影域提升到原始空间，实现高效且刚体运动不变的几何匹配，在动物网格匹配、马网格插值和ShapeNet部件迁移任务中以更低计算成本取得有意义的几何对应和GW目标值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Gromov-Wasserstein方法计算成本高，现有切片化GW方法使用线性切片器表达能力有限，且缺乏高效的传输计划构建方式。本文旨在通过引入可学习的广义非线性切片器，实现更高效、更具表达力的切片化GW求解。

Method: 1) 将广义切片器引入切片化GW框架；2) 构建基于切片的高效GW传输计划；3) 开发摊销变体，用学习到的切片器替代每个实例的优化，实现未见输入对的快速推理。

Result: 在动物网格匹配、马网格插值和ShapeNet部件迁移任务上，min-GSGW相比现有GW求解器以显著更低的计算代价产生了有意义的几何对应关系和GW目标值。

Conclusion: min-GSGW通过可学习的广义切片器实现了高效且刚体运动不变的GW求解，为几何匹配和形状分析提供了实用的计算框架，其摊销变体进一步提升了泛化能力。

Abstract: We propose min Generalized Sliced Gromov--Wasserstein (min-GSGW), a sliced formulation for the Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) problem using expressive generalized slicers. The key idea is to learn coupled nonlinear slicers that assign compatible push-forward values to both input measures, so that monotone coupling in the projected domain lifts to a transport plan evaluated against the GW objective in the original spaces. The resulting plan induces a GW objective value, and min-GSGW minimizes this cost directly in the original spaces. We further show that min-GSGW is rigid-motion invariant, a crucial property for geometric matching and shape analysis tasks. Our contributions are threefold: 1) we introduce generalized slicers into the sliced GW framework, 2) we construct a slicing-based efficient GW transport plan; and 3) we develop an amortized variant that replaces per-instance optimization with a learned slicer for unseen input pairs. We perform experiments on animal mesh matching, horse mesh interpolation, and ShapeNet part transfer. Results show that min-GSGW produces meaningful geometric correspondences and GW objective values at substantially lower computational cost than existing GW solvers.

</details>


### [187] [Fast and effective algorithms for fair clustering at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13759)
*Claudio Mantuano,Manuel Kammermann,Philipp Baumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对公平聚类问题提出一个通用框架及三种启发式算法，在聚类成本与公平性之间提供精确的权衡控制，并实现从高质量到高可扩展性的多层级优化，实验表明其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类在公平敏感领域（如客户、员工、学生分组）中应用日益广泛，但现有方法对聚类成本与公平性之间的权衡控制能力有限，且难以兼顾大规模数据下的可扩展性和解的质量。

Method: 提出一个通用公平聚类框架，并基于该框架设计三种启发式算法：第一种注重解质量和约束灵活性；第二种在保持高质量的同时提升可扩展性；第三种面向最大可扩展性，可在数秒内处理百万级对象实例。

Result: 在基准数据集上的综合数值实验表明，所提出的三种启发式算法在所有评估指标上均优于现有方法。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 本文提出的框架和启发式算法能够有效管理聚类成本与公平性的权衡，兼顾解质量与可扩展性，为大规模公平聚类问题提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Clustering is an unsupervised machine learning task that consists of identifying groups of similar objects. It has numerous applications and is increasingly used in fairness-sensitive domains where objects represent individuals, such as customers, employees, or students. We address a fair clustering problem in which objects belong to protected groups. The problem consists of partitioning the objects into a predefined number of clusters while attaining a user-defined target level of fairness, meaning that each protected group is sufficiently represented in each cluster. The objective is to minimize the clustering cost, defined as the sum of squared Euclidean distances between the objects and the centers of their clusters. Since clustering cost and fairness are generally in conflict, managing the trade-off between them is essential in practical applications. Existing methods provide limited control over this trade-off and either fail to scale to large datasets or, when they scale, produce low-quality solutions. We propose a general framework for fair clustering that provides precise control over the cost-fairness trade-off and introduce three heuristics based on it. The first heuristic focuses on solution quality and the flexibility to incorporate additional constraints, the second improves scalability while retaining high solution quality, and the third is designed for maximum scalability, producing solutions for instances with millions of objects in seconds. The proposed heuristics outperform existing approaches in comprehensive numerical experiments on benchmark datasets. The source code of our heuristics and instructions for reproducing the experiments are publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [188] [High-Rate Quantized Matrix Multiplication II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13768)
*Or Ordentlich,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了权重量化中的矩阵乘法问题，利用协方差矩阵进行水填充（waterfilling）优化，提出了WaterSIC方案，证明其在高速率下基无关且接近信息论极限，同时发现GPTQ结合随机旋转也接近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM的权重量化中，现有方案（如GPTQ）对所有坐标等分配量化比特，而经典率失真理论中的水填充解表明应非均匀分配。本文旨在利用协方差矩阵信息改进量化性能。

Method: 将权重量化建模为加权均方误差（WMSE）源编码问题，采用逆水填充（reverse waterfilling）分配比特率；分析仅使用标量INT量化器的WaterSIC方案的高速率性能，证明其基无关性并与信息论极限对比；通过Llama-3-8B的实际协方差矩阵评估GPTQ与随机旋转的性能。

Result: WaterSIC在高速率下：(a) 基无关，性能由Σ_X的行列式决定，不受随机旋转影响；(b) 与信息论失真极限相差乘法因子2πe/12（约0.25 bit/条目）。GPTQ在随机旋转后与WaterSIC相差约0.1 bit/条目，表明其也接近最优。

Conclusion: 利用协方差矩阵的水填充策略能有效改进权重量化；WaterSIC具有理论最优性保证；GPTQ配合随机旋转在实际中已接近理论极限，为LLM量化提供了理论支撑和实用建议。

Abstract: This is the second part of the work investigating quantized matrix multiplication (MatMul). In part I we considered the case of calibration-free quantization, whereas here we discuss the setting where covariance matrix $Σ_X$ of the columns of the second factor is available. This setting arises in the ubiquitous task of weight-only post-training quantization of LLMs.
  Weight-only quantization is related to the problem of weighted mean squared error (WMSE) source coding, whose classical (reverse) waterfilling solution dictates how one should distribute rate between coordinates of the vector. We show how waterfilling can be used to improve practical LLM quantization algorithms (GPTQ), which at present allocate rate equally. A recent scheme (known as ``WaterSIC'') that only uses scalar INT quantizers is analyzed and its high-rate performance is shown to be (a) basis free (i.e., characterized by the determinant of $Σ_X$ and, thus, unlike existing schemes, is immune to applying random rotations); and (b) within a multiplicative factor of $\frac{2πe}{12}$ (or 0.25 bit/entry) of the information-theoretic distortion limit. GPTQ's performance, in turn, is affected by the choice of basis, but for a random rotation and actual $Σ_X$ from Llama-3-8B we find it to be within 0.1 bit (depending on the layer type) of WaterSIC, suggesting that GPTQ with random rotation is also near optimal, at least in the high-rate regime.

</details>


### [189] [MinT: Managed Infrastructure for Training and Serving Millions of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13779)
*Mind Lab,:,Song Cao,Vic Cao,Andrew Chen,Kaijie Chen,Cleon Cheng,Steven Chiang,Kaixuan Fan,Hera Feng,Huan Feng,Arthur Fu,Jun Gao,Hongquan Gu,Aaron Guan,Nolan Ho,Mutian Hong,Hailee Hou,Peixuan Hua,Charles Huang,Miles Jiang,Nora Jiang,Yuyi Jiang,Qiuyu Jin,Fancy Kong,Andrew Lei,Kyrie Lei,Alexy Li,Lucian Li,Ray Li,Theo Li,Zhihui Li,Jiayi Lin,Kairus Liu,Kieran Liu,Logan Liu,Xiang Liu,Irvine Lu,Maeve Luo,Runze Lv,Pony Ma,Verity Niu,Anson Qiu,Vincent Wang,Rio Yang,Maxwell Yao,Carrie Ye,Regis Ye,Wenlin Ye,Josh Ying,Danney Zeng,Yuhan Zhan,Anya Zhang,Di Zhang,Ruijia Zhang,Sueky Zhang,Ya Zhang,Wei Zhao,Ada Zhou,Changhai Zhou,Yuhua Zhou,Xinyue Zhu,Murphy Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MinT（MindLab Toolkit），一个用于低秩适配（LoRA）后训练和在线服务的托管基础设施系统。MinT支持在少量昂贵的基模型部署上产生大量训练策略的场景，通过保持基模型常驻内存、仅移动导出的LoRA适配器版本来实现策略的滚动、更新、导出、评估、服务和回滚。该系统在三个维度上扩展：Scale Up（扩展到前沿规模，支持超过1T总参数）、Scale Down（适配器仅占基模型<1%大小，降低步骤测量时间18.3x/2.85x，缩短墙钟时间1.77x/1.45x）、Scale Out（支持10^6级可寻址策略目录和千级适配器活跃波，冷加载作为调度服务，MoE LoRA张量打包提升引擎加载速度8.5-8.7x）。MinT能够在共享的1T级基模型上管理和服务百万级LoRA策略目录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LoRA后训练和在线服务场景中，通常需要在少量昂贵的基模型上产生大量训练策略。传统方法将每个策略物化为完整检查点，成本高昂且效率低下。MinT旨在解决这一问题，通过保持基模型常驻、仅移动轻量级LoRA适配器的方式，实现高效的策略管理和服务。

Method: MinT设计了一个托管基础设施，将基模型常驻内存，通过导出LoRA适配器修订版本在rollout、update、export、evaluation、serving、rollback等阶段流转。系统在三个维度扩展：(1) Scale Up：支持前沿规模的密集和MoE架构（含MLA和DSA注意力路径），训练和服务已验证超过1T总参数；(2) Scale Down：仅移动导出的LoRA适配器（rank-1设置下<基模型1%），适配器移交降低步骤时间18.3x（4B密集模型）和2.85x（30B MoE），并发多策略GRPO缩短墙钟时间1.77x和1.45x且不增加峰值内存；(3) Scale Out：分离持久策略可寻址性与CPU/GPU工作集，张量并行部署支持10^6级可寻址目录，千级适配器活跃波，冷加载作为调度服务，MoE LoRA张量打包提升引擎加载速度8.5-8.7x。

Result: MinT在超过1T总参数的模型上验证了训练和服务能力。适配器移交在4B密集模型上降低步骤时间18.3x，在30B MoE上降低2.85x。并发多策略GRPO缩短墙钟时间1.77x（4B）和1.45x（30B）。支持10^6级可寻址策略目录，单个引擎扫描100K策略。MoE LoRA张量打包提升引擎加载速度8.5-8.7x。

Conclusion: MinT实现了在共享1T级基模型上管理和服务百万级LoRA策略目录的能力，通过适配器级别的精细调度和资源管理，大幅降低了LoRA后训练和在线服务的开销和复杂度。

Abstract: We present MindLab Toolkit (MinT), a managed infrastructure system for Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) post-training and online serving. MinT targets a setting where many trained policies are produced over a small number of expensive base-model deployments. Instead of materializing each policy as a merged full checkpoint, MinT keeps the base model resident and moves exported LoRA adapter revisions through rollout, update, export, evaluation, serving, and rollback, hiding distributed training, serving, scheduling, and data movement behind a service interface. MinT scales this path along three axes. Scale Up extends LoRA RL to frontier-scale dense and MoE architectures, including MLA and DSA attention paths, with training and serving validated beyond 1T total parameters. Scale Down moves only the exported LoRA adapter, which can be under 1% of base-model size in rank-1 settings; adapter-only handoff reduces the measured step by 18.3x on a 4B dense model and 2.85x on a 30B MoE, while concurrent multi-policy GRPO shortens wall time by 1.77x and 1.45x without raising peak memory. Scale Out separates durable policy addressability from CPU/GPU working sets: a tensor-parallel deployment supports 10^6-scale addressable catalogs (measured single-engine sweeps through 100K) and thousand-adapter active waves at cluster scale, with cold loading treated as scheduled service work and packed MoE LoRA tensors improving live engine loading by 8.5-8.7x. MinT thus manages million-scale LoRA policy catalogs while training and serving selected adapter revisions over shared 1T-class base models.

</details>


### [190] [Attention Once Is All You Need: Efficient Streaming Inference with Stateful Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13784)
*Victor Norgren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于数据驱动的流式推理引擎，通过持久化KV缓存和增量预填充将查询延迟降至O(|q|)，并利用空闲GPU周期预回答问题，在流式市场数据基准上实现最高5.9倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer推理引擎在流式工作负载中，每次查询都需支付O(n)的预填充代价，随着累积上下文增长，延迟成本变得不可接受。

Method: 1) 数据驱动的有状态会话模型：持久化KV缓存随新数据增量推进，预填充移出关键路径；2) Flash Queries机制：利用数据到达间的空闲GPU周期预评估已注册问题并缓存答案；3) 多租户连续批处理调度器：采用单元预算准入和前缀感知分组预填充，支持数十个有状态会话在单GPU上共存。

Result: 在流式市场数据基准测试中，相比vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM、llama.cpp等传统推理引擎，实现了最高5.9倍加速，且查询延迟不随累积上下文增长而增加。

Conclusion: 有状态会话模型能有效解决流式场景下传统无状态引擎的性能瓶颈，将推理从请求驱动转向数据驱动，为实时流式AI推理提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Conventional transformer inference engines are request-driven, paying an O(n) prefill cost on every query. In streaming workloads, where data arrives continuously and queries probe an ever-growing context, this cost is prohibitive. We introduce a data-driven computational model centred on stateful sessions: a persistent KV cache advanced incrementally as new data arrives, so prefill is moved off the critical path and query latency becomes O(|q|), independent of accumulated context size. Building on this, Flash Queries reclaim idle GPU cycles between data arrivals to pre-evaluate registered questions and return cached answers before the user asks, a pattern that is structurally impossible in stateless engines because they discard intermediate state between requests. A multi-tenant continuous-batching scheduler with cell-budget admission and prefix-aware grouped prefill lets dozens of stateful sessions coexist on a single GPU while preserving full quadratic self-attention. On streaming market-data benchmarks the reference implementation achieves up to 5.9x speedup over conventional inference engines (vLLM, SGLang, TensorRT-LLM, llama.cpp), holding query latency constant as accumulated context grows.

</details>


### [191] [Interpretable Machine Learning for Antepartum Prediction of Pregnancy-Associated Thrombotic Microangiopathy Using Routine Longitudinal Laboratory Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13786)
*Chuanchuan Sun,Zhen Yu,Qin Fan,Qingchao Chen,Feng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用机器学习方法分析纵向临床检验数据，成功构建了妊娠相关血栓性微血管病（P-TMA）的早期风险预测模型。梯度提升模型在独立测试集中实现了0.872的AUROC和0.883的AUPRC，其中胱抑素C在第6周的表现成为有潜力的早期监测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 妊娠相关血栓性微血管病（P-TMA）罕见但危及生命，其早期风险预测极具挑战性，因为相关实验室异常表现微妙、多维，且常被妊娠期血小板减少症和妊娠相关蛋白尿等常见生理变化所掩盖，与良性产科和肾脏病变高度重叠。传统单变量或基于规则的方法难以捕捉这种复杂性。

Method: 回顾性研究纳入300例妊娠（142例P-TMA病例，158例对照），保留146个纵向实验室预测变量。采用分层抽样按80%/20%划分训练集和测试集，评估了逻辑回归、支持向量机（RBF核）、随机森林、极端随机树和梯度提升五种算法，通过交叉验证平均AUROC选择最终模型。

Result: 梯度提升模型经交叉验证被选定。在独立测试集中，模型AUROC为0.872（95% CI: 0.769-0.952），AUPRC为0.883（95% CI: 0.780-0.959），灵敏度0.750，特异度0.812。

Conclusion: 常规临床实验室检验中包含可用于P-TMA风险预测的信息性且临床合理的信号，其中胱抑素C在第6周表现出作为早期监测指标的潜力。

Abstract: Background: Pregnancy-associated thrombotic microangiopathy (P-TMA) is rare but life-threatening. Early risk prediction before overt clinical presentation remains challenging, as the associated laboratory abnormalities are subtle, multidimensional, and frequently masked by common physiological changes such as gestational thrombocytopenia and pregnancy-related proteinuria, thus overlapping heavily with benign obstetric and renal conditions. This complexity is poorly captured by univariate or rule-based approaches; however, it is addressable by machine learning, which can extract latent, time-dependent risk signatures from longitudinal clinical tests. Methods: This retrospective study included 300 pregnancies comprising 142 P-TMA cases and 158 controls. After exclusion of identifiers and non-informative variables, 146 longitudinal laboratory predictors were retained. Participants were divided into a training cohort (80%) and a held-out test cohort (20%) using stratified sampling. Five algorithms were evaluated: logistic regression, support vector machine with radial basis function kernel, random forest, extra trees, and gradient boosting. The final model was selected by mean cross-validated AUROC, refitted on the full training cohort, and evaluated once in the held-out test cohort. Interpretability analyses examined global feature importance and distributional patterns of leading predictors. Results: Gradient boosting was prespecified by cross-validation in the training cohort. The model achieved an AUROC of 0.872 (95% CI: 0.769-0.952) and an AUPRC of 0.883 (95% CI: 0.780-0.959) in a held-out test cohort, with sensitivity of 0.750 and specificity of 0.812. Conclusions: Longitudinal clinical laboratory tests obtained during routine care contained informative and clinically plausible signals for P-TMA risk. Notably, cystatin C at week 6 showed promise as an early monitoring indicator.

</details>


### [192] [Force-Aware Neural Tangent Kernels for Scalable and Robust Active Learning of MLIPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13788)
*Eszter Varga-Umbrich,Zachary Weller-Davies,Paul Duckworth,Jules Tilly,Olivier Peltre,Shikha Surana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分块特征空间后验方差筛选的线性扩展采集框架，并将神经正切核（NTK）扩展到力感知场景（力NTK和联合能量-力NTK），实现了大规模、力感知且分布稳健的主动学习，可在单预训练MLIP模型基础上高效微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习原子间势的主动学习面临三大挑战：大规模候选池的扩展性、能量-力联合监督的利用、以及候选池分布偏移时的鲁棒性。现有方法未能同时解决这些问题。

Method: 1) 提出分块特征空间后验方差筛选框架，避免全核矩阵计算，线性扩展至~20万结构筛选；2) 将NTK扩展至力感知场景，通过混合参数-坐标导数推导出力NTK和联合能量-力NTK，为向量场预测提供自然相似度度量。

Result: 在OC20数据集上，联合能量-力NTK在所有指标和分布划分上取得最低能量和力MAE/RMSE；在T1x、PMechDB、RGD基准上，力NTK方法与现有方法竞争力相当且比基于委员会的方法显著高效；在T1x分布偏移案例中，基于预训练MLIP嵌入和NTK的采集保持稳健，而委员会方法方差更高。

Conclusion: 单个预训练MLIP模型即可实现可扩展、力感知且分布稳健的主动学习，为基础模型微调提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Active learning for machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) must address several challenges to be practical: scaling to large candidate pools, leveraging energy-force supervision, and maintaining robustness when candidate pools are biased relative to the target distribution. In this work, we jointly address these challenges. We first introduce a linearly scaling acquisition framework based on chunked feature-space posterior-variance shortlisting. By avoiding materialisation of the candidate and train set kernels, this approach enables screening of ~200k structures within hours and applies broadly to acquisition strategies that score candidates based on molecular similarity metrics. We then extend the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) to a force-aware setting via mixed parameter-coordinate derivatives, yielding a force NTK and a joint energy-force NTK that provide natural similarity metrics for vector-field prediction. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the joint energy-force NTK on the OC20 dataset, where force-aware acquisition is crucial: it achieves the lowest energy and force MAE and RMSE across all metrics and distribution splits. Across T1x, PMechDB, and RGD benchmarks, our force NTK methods remain competitive with established baselines while being significantly more efficient than committee-based approaches. Under a controlled candidate-pool shift case study on T1x, acquisition based on pretrained MLIP embeddings and NTKs remains robust, whereas committee-based methods exhibit higher variance. Overall, these results show that a single pretrained MLIP can enable scalable, force-aware, and distribution-robust active learning for foundation-model fine-tuning.

</details>


### [193] [ENSEMBITS: an alphabet of protein conformational ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13789)
*Kaiwen Shi,Carlos Oliver*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Ensembits是首个蛋白质构象系综分词器，通过残差VQ-VAE和帧蒸馏目标在大规模分子动力学语料上训练，能够将蛋白质动态信息离散化为token，在RMSF预测、运动幅度分析等动态任务上超越现有方法，同时在静态任务上匹敌甚至超越传统静态分词器，且支持从单张预测结构推断动态token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质结构分词器仅捕捉静态结构的局部分子几何信息，忽略了蛋白质系综所揭示的相关运动与替代构象状态，缺乏对蛋白质动态信息的离散化编码能力。

Method: 使用残差VQ-VAE结合帧蒸馏目标函数，在大规模分子动力学语料上训练；设计了跨构象的几何描述符、置换不变的变长系综编码策略，并解决了动力学数据的稀疏性问题。

Result: 在RMSF预测上优于所有相关方法；在基于token的ANOVA测试中，是每残基运动幅度预测最强的独立结构分词器；在EC、GO、结合位点/亲和力预测及零样本突变效应预测上匹配或超越静态分词器，且预训练数据量远少。

Conclusion: 帧蒸馏目标使Ensembits能单张预测结构推断动态token，缓解动力学数据稀疏问题；为蛋白质语言模型和设计引入动态信息的离散词汇表，推动领域从静态结构预测走向系综生成。

Abstract: Protein structure tokenizers (PSTs) are workhorses in protein language modeling, function prediction, and evolutionary analysis. However, existing PSTs only capture local geometry of static structures, and miss the correlated motions and alternative conformational states revealed by protein ensembles. Here we introduce Ensembits, the first tokenizer of protein conformational ensembles. Ensembits address challenges inherent to tokenizing dynamics: deriving informative geometric descriptors across conformations, permutation-invariance encoding of variable-size ensembles, and conquering sparsity in dynamics data. Trained with a Residual VQ-VAE using a frame distillation objective on a large molecular dynamics corpus, Ensembits outperforms all related methods on RMSF prediction, and is the strongest standalone structural tokenizer on an token-conditioned ANOVA test on per-residue motion amplitude. Ensembits further matches or exceeds static tokenizers on EC, GO, binding site/affinity prediction, and zero-shot mutation-effect prediction despite using far less pretraining data. Notably, the distillation objective enables Ensembits to predict dynamics token from one single predicted structure, which alleviates dynamics data sparsity. As the field moves from static structure prediction toward ensemble generation, Ensembits offer the discrete vocabulary needed to bring dynamics into protein language modeling and design.

</details>


### [194] [Di-BiLPS: Denoising induced Bidirectional Latent-PDE-Solver under Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13790)
*Zhonghao Li,Chaoyu Liu,Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Di-BiLPS是一种统一的神经框架，通过变分自编码器压缩高维输入、潜在扩散模块建模不确定性以及对比学习对齐表示，在极稀疏观测数据（低至3%）下高效求解正向和逆向PDE问题，并支持零样本超分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中PDE观测数据极其稀疏，传统数值求解器和现有神经方法在极稀疏条件下精度严重下降且推理效率受限，亟需一种能在极端稀疏观测下同时处理正向和逆向PDE问题的统一框架。

Method: 提出Di-BiLPS框架，包含三个核心组件：变分自编码器（VAE）将高维输入压缩到紧凑潜在空间、潜在扩散模块建模不确定性、对比学习对齐表示。所有操作在潜在空间进行，并引入基于方差保持扩散过程的PDE-informed去噪算法提升推理效率。

Result: 在多个PDE基准上，Di-BiLPS在极稀疏输入（低至3%）下持续达到SOTA性能，同时大幅降低计算成本，并实现了零样本超分辨率。

Conclusion: Di-BiLPS为极稀疏观测下的PDE问题提供了高效且准确的统一解决方案，潜在空间操作使其兼具推理效率与灵活输入输出映射能力，零样本超分辨率特性进一步拓展了其应用前景。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) are fundamental for modeling complex natural and physical phenomena. In many real-world applications, however, observational data are extremely sparse, which severely limits the applicability of both classical numerical solvers and existing neural approaches. While neural methods have shown promising results under moderately sparse observations, their inference efficiency at high resolutions is limited, and their accuracy degrades substantially in the extremely sparse regime. In this work, we propose the Di-BiLPS, a unified neural framework that effectively handle both forward and inverse PDE problems under extremely sparse observations. Di-BiLPS combines a variational autoencoder to compress high-dimensional inputs into a compact latent space, a latent diffusion module to model uncertainty, and contrastive learning to align representations. Operating entirely in this latent space, the framework achieves efficient inference while retaining flexible input-output mapping. In addition, we introduce a PDE-informed denoising algorithm based on a variance-preserving diffusion process, which further improves inference efficiency. Extensive experiments on multiple PDE benchmarks demonstrate that Di-BiLPS consistently achieves SOTA performance under extremely sparse inputs (as low as 3%), while substantially reducing computational cost. Moreover, Di-BiLPS enables zero-shot super-resolution, as it allows predictions over continuous spatial-temporal domains.

</details>


### [195] [Improving Reproducibility in Evaluation through Multi-Level Annotator Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13801)
*Deepak Pandita,Flip Korn,Chris Welty,Christopher M. Homan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对生成式AI模型（如大语言模型）面临的可重复性危机，提出了一种多层次自助法（multi-level bootstrapping）来模拟标注者行为。通过分析具有大量评分和持久标注者标识符的数据集，研究了在实现统计显著性时，项目数量（N）和每个项目的响应数量（K）之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI领域存在可重复性危机，表现为不可靠的评估和不可重复的实验结果。人类标注者会引入不同的偏见和主观意见，而标准评估实践通常只依赖少量标注（3-5个），且缺乏持久标注者标识符来建模个体差异，因此克服这种方差极具挑战。

Method: 提出一种多层次自助法（multi-level bootstrapping approach）来真实地建模标注者行为，利用具有大量评分和持久标注者标识符的数据集，分析N（项目数量）与K（每个项目响应数量）之间的权衡关系。

Result: 通过多层级自助法建模，揭示了在达到统计显著性时，项目数量和每项目响应数量之间存在明确的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 为了提升AI评估的可重复性和可信度，需要超越当前少量标注的实践，合理配置项目数量和每项目标注数量，以有效建模和降低标注者间的方差。

Abstract: As generative AI models such as large language models (LLMs) become more pervasive, ensuring the safety, robustness, and overall trustworthiness of these systems is paramount. However, AI is currently facing a reproducibility crisis driven by unreliable evaluations and unrepeatable experimental results. While human raters are often used to assess models for utility and safety, they introduce divergent biases and subjective opinions into their annotations. Overcoming this variance is exceptionally challenging because very little data exists to study how experimental repeatability actually improves as the annotator pool grows. Standard evaluation practices typically rely on a small number of annotations per item (often 3 to 5) and lack the persistent rater identifiers necessary to model individual variance across items. In this work, we introduce a multi-level bootstrapping approach to realistically model annotator behavior. Leveraging datasets with a large number of ratings and persistent rater identifiers, we analyze the tradeoffs between the number of items ($N$) and the number of responses per item ($K$) required to achieve statistical significance.

</details>


### [196] [Provable Quantization with Randomized Hadamard Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13810)
*Ying Feng,Piotr Indyk,Michael Kapralov,Dmitry Krachun,Boris Prokhorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种结合随机Hadamard变换与抖动标量量化的向量量化方法。作者证明，通过单一随机Hadamard变换（而非完整随机旋转）并在量化前减去随机标量偏移，该方案是无偏的，且其均方误差界渐近匹配完全随机旋转矩阵的理论性能。特别地，抖动版本的TurboQuant在b比特/坐标下达到(π√3/2 + o(1))·4^(-b)的均方误差，其中o(1)项对所有单位向量和所有维度一致地趋于零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密随机旋转虽有干净的理论保证，但需要Θ(d²)时间，计算成本过高。随机Hadamard变换可将成本降至O(d log d)，但其离散结构导致分析复杂且只能提供较弱或纯经验的压缩保证。本文旨在弥合效率与理论保证之间的差距，设计一种既高效又具有严格理论保证的量化方法。

Method: 该方法对输入向量应用随机Hadamard变换HD，然后减去一个随机标量偏移（抖动），再进行标量量化。这种抖动操作以可忽略的额外成本注入额外随机性。作者分析了该量化器的无偏性和均方误差界，并特别研究了抖动版本的TurboQuant。

Result: 证明该方案是无偏的，且提供渐近匹配完全随机旋转矩阵的均方误差界。抖动TurboQuant在b比特/坐标下达到(π√3/2 + o(1))·4^(-b)的均方误差，其中o(1)项在所有单位向量和所有维度上一致收敛。

Conclusion: 抖动随机Hadamard变换结合标量量化是一种高效且有严格理论保证的向量量化方法，在相似性搜索、联邦学习和KV缓存压缩等应用场景中具有重要价值。

Abstract: Vector quantization via random projection followed by scalar quantization is a fundamental primitive in machine learning, with applications ranging from similarity search to federated learning and KV cache compression. While dense random rotations yield clean theoretical guarantees, they require $Θ(d^2)$ time. The randomized Hadamard transform $HD$ reduces this cost to $O(d \log d)$, but its discrete structure complicates analysis and leads to weaker or purely empirical compression guarantees.
  In this work, we study a variant of this approach: dithered quantization with a single randomized Hadamard transform. Specifically, the quantizer applies $HD$ to the input vector and subtracts a random scalar offset before quantizing, injecting additional randomness at negligible cost. We prove that this approach is unbiased and provides mean squared error bounds that asymptotically match those achievable with truly random rotation matrices. In particular, we prove that a dithered version of TurboQuant achieves mean squared error $\bigl(π\sqrt{3}/2 + o(1)\bigr) \cdot 4^{-b}$ at $b$ bits per coordinate, where the $o(1)$ term vanishes uniformly over all unit vectors and all dimensions as the number of quantization levels grows.

</details>


### [197] [Uncertainty-Driven Anomaly Detection for Psychotic Relapse Using Smartwatches: Forecasting and Multi-Task Learning Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13816)
*Nikolaos Tsalkitzis,Panagiotis P. Filntisis,Petros Maragos,Niki Efthymiou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种基于智能手表的每日精神病复发检测框架：一种通过预测心脏动力学并检测预测与观测特征的偏差，另一种采用多任务学习融合睡眠、运动和心脏信号。两者均使用Transformer编码器及集成MLP估计的不确定性输出每日异常分数。通过后期融合策略将两种架构的异常信号结合，在e-Prevention挑战赛数据集上相比冠军基线提升8%，表明整合心脏、运动和睡眠等多模态数字表型对高保真复发检测至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用数字表型持续被动监测行为与生理信号，实现精神病复发的早期检测。

Method: 两个基于智能手表的框架：1) 预测心脏动力学并检测预测-观测偏差；2) 多任务学习融合睡眠、运动和心脏信号，学习时间感知嵌入并预测测量时序。均使用Transformer编码器，通过集成MLP估计预测不确定性输出每日异常分数，最后采用后期融合策略整合两种架构。

Result: 在2nd e-Prevention Grand Challenge数据集上，融合模型相比竞赛冠军基线提升8%相对性能。

Conclusion: 整合心脏、运动和睡眠等多模态数字表型是实现现实场景中高保真精神病复发检测的关键。

Abstract: Digital phenotyping enables continuous passive monitoring of behavior and physiology, offering a promising paradigm for early detection of psychotic relapse. In this work, we develop and systematically study two smartwatch-based frameworks for daily relapse detection. The first forecasts cardiac dynamics and flags deviations between predicted and observed features as indicators of abnormality. The second adopts a multi-task formulation that fuses sleep with motion and cardiac-derived signals, learning time-aware embeddings and predicting measurement timing. Both pipelines use Transformer encoders and output a daily anomaly score, derived from predictive uncertainty estimated via an ensemble of multilayer perceptrons to improve robustness to real-world wearable variability. While each framework independently demonstrates strong predictive power, we show that they capture complementary physiological signatures. Consequently, we propose a late-fusion strategy that synergistically combines the anomaly signals from both architectures into a unified decision score. We benchmark our methodology on the 2nd e-Prevention Grand Challenge dataset, where our fused model achieves a 8% relative improvement over the competition-winning baseline. Our results, supported by extensive ablation studies, suggest that the integration of diverse digital phenotypes, cardiac, motion, and sleep, is essential for the high-fidelity detection of psychotic relapse in real-world settings.

</details>


### [198] [Topology-Preserving Neural Operator Learning via Hodge Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13834)
*Dongzhe Zheng,Tao Zhong,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从函数空间视角研究几何网格上的物理场方程解算子，揭示Hodge正交性通过将不可学习的拓扑自由度与可学习的几何动力学分离来从根本上解决频谱干扰问题，并提出一种结合Hodge理论与算子分裂的混合欧拉-拉格朗日架构（Hodge Spectral Duality, HSD），在几何图上实现了更高的精度和效率，且更保真于物理不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决几何网格上物理场方程求解中频谱干扰问题，即不可学习的拓扑自由度与可学习的几何动力学之间的相互干扰，从而提升解算子的学习效果。

Method: 基于Hodge理论揭示Hodge正交性可分离拓扑自由度与几何动力学；利用算子分裂方法推导算子级分解；提出混合欧拉-拉格朗日架构（HSD），其中离散微分形式捕获拓扑主导成分，正交辅助环境空间表示复杂局部动力学。

Result: 在几何图上取得了更高的精度和效率，同时对物理不变量保持更强的保真度。

Conclusion: Hodge Spectral Duality框架通过代数层面的归纳偏置有效分离了拓扑与几何成分，为几何网格上的物理场方程求解提供了一种结构保持且高效的深度学习方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we study solution operators of physical field equations on geometric meshes from a function-space perspective. We reveal that Hodge orthogonality fundamentally resolves spectral interference by isolating unlearnable topological degrees of freedom from learnable geometric dynamics, enabling an additive approximation confined to structure-preserving subspaces. Building on Hodge theory and operator splitting, we derive a principled operator-level decomposition. The result is a Hybrid Eulerian-Lagrangian architecture with an algebraic-level inductive bias we call Hodge Spectral Duality (HSD). In our framework, we use discrete differential forms to capture topology-dominated components and an orthogonal auxiliary ambient space to represent complex local dynamics. Our method achieves superior accuracy and efficiency on geometric graphs with enhanced fidelity to physical invariants. Our code is available at https://github.com/ContinuumCoder/Hodge-Spectral-Duality

</details>

### [199] [OxyEcomBench: Benchmarking Multimodal Foundation Models across E-Commerce Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13173)
*Yong Liu,Ximan Liu,Guoqing Yang,Bing Bai,Xiaoqiang Xu,Zhen Chen,Ke Zhang,Yan Li*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出OxyEcomBench，一个包含约6300条中英双语真实电商数据的统一多模态基准，覆盖平台运营者、商家和消费者三方视角的6个能力维度、29项任务，采用P0-P3四级难度设计。评估20个主流LLM/MLLM发现，顶尖模型表现有限且性能差距缩小，表明电商领域知识缺失削弱了通用模型的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电商基准存在单一视角、任务狭窄、挑战孤立等问题，无法全面评估模型对电商全链路的理解能力；LLM/MLLM在电商场景中存在显著的性能差距。

Method: 构建包含约6300条高质量实例的双语（中英）多模态基准，覆盖平台、商家、消费者三方角色，6个能力维度（如实操知识、多模态推理等），29项任务，支持纯文本和混合模态输入（单图/多图、单轮/多轮）。所有数据来自真实电商平台并经领域专家验证，采用P0-P3四级难度标注。

Result: 在20个主流LLM/MLLM上评估，即使顶尖模型性能也较为有限，且模型间性能差距在OxyEcomBench上相比通用基准有所缩小。

Conclusion: 电商特定领域知识的不足削弱了先进通用模型在该领域的优势，亟需针对性知识注入以弥合开放域与电商性能之间的差距。

Abstract: LLMs and MLLMs have become indispensable tools across a wide range of applications. E-commerce, however, poses distinctive challenges -- including intricate domain knowledge, long-tail product evidence, heterogeneous visual data, and the interplay among multiple stakeholder roles -- that diverge substantially from the general world knowledge these models are primarily trained on, often causing a notable gap between their open-domain and e-commerce performance. To systematically quantify this gap, we introduce OxyEcomBench, a unified multimodal benchmark comprising approximately 6,300 high-quality instances for real-world bilingual Chinese--English e-commerce. Although several e-commerce benchmarks have been proposed, they typically adopt a single stakeholder perspective, target a narrow set of tasks, or address isolated challenges, making it difficult to holistically assess models' understanding of the full e-commerce pipeline. OxyEcomBench addresses these limitations by jointly covering platform operators, merchants, and customers across 6 capability aspects and 29 tasks, supporting text-only and mixed-modality inputs with single-image, multi-image, single-turn, and multi-turn configurations. All data is sourced from authentic e-commerce platforms and verified by domain experts. The benchmark further adopts a difficulty-aware design with a four-level P0--P3 rubric applied to all 29 tasks whose difficulty admits stable expert consensus, and rigorously prioritizes visually salient multimodal cases in which key evidence resides in images rather than text alone. Evaluations on 20 mainstream LLMs and MLLMs show that even the leading models attain modest performance and that performance gaps narrow on OxyEcomBench, suggesting that insufficient e-commerce-specific knowledge infusion mutes the advantages of advanced general-purpose models in this domain.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [Toward Communication-Efficient Space Data Centers: Bottlenecks, Architectures, and New Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12681)
*Minghao Sun,Zehui Chen,Jinbo Hou,Kezhi Wang,Xiaoli Chu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了太空数据中心（SDC）面临的通信瓶颈——地面数据中心PB级内部数据交换与星地链路Gb级容量之间的巨大差距，并提出语义通信作为关键使能范式，通过传输紧凑的任务相关语义表示来减轻上行链路压力。文章通过评估由中继卫星和轨道计算节点组成的多层异构SDC框架（在耦合能量和热约束下运行）展示了通信高效轨道AI基础设施的可行性，并概述了可扩展部署的开放研究挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面数据中心在高功率密度下面临能源供应、冷却和空间可扩展性等多重挑战，促使研究者探索太空数据中心（SDC）这一在轨托管高能耗计算基础设施的新范式。然而SDC面临的根本限制——通信能力——尚未得到系统分析，星地链路容量与地面数据中心内部数据交换量之间存在巨大鸿沟。

Method: 采用系统分析方法，首先识别并量化SDC架构中的通信约束瓶颈，然后引入语义通信范式作为核心技术方案。构建多层异构SDC框架（包含中继卫星和轨道计算节点），在耦合能量与热约束下进行评估验证。

Result: 语义通信可通过传输紧凑的、任务相关的语义表示而非原始数据，大幅降低上行链路压力。在耦合能量和热约束下评估的多层异构SDC框架证明了通信高效的轨道AI基础设施的可行性。

Conclusion: 太空数据中心面临的根本限制是通信能力而非电力或场地；语义通信是解决该瓶颈的关键使能技术；该领域仍存在众多开放研究挑战需要克服以实现可扩展部署。

Abstract: The rapid growth of foundation model training and large-scale AI services has driven ground data centers toward unprecedented power densities, intensifying challenges in energy supply, cooling, and spatial scalability. Space Data Centers (SDCs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for hosting energy-intensive computing infrastructures in orbit, leveraging continuous solar energy and radiative cooling advantages. However, unlike ground facilities primarily constrained by power and site availability, SDCs are fundamentally limited by communication capability. The gap between petabit-scale internal data exchange in ground data centers and the gigabit-scale capacity of ground-space links forms a critical bottleneck. This article systematically analyzes communication constraints in SDC architectures and explores semantic communication as a key enabling paradigm. By transmitting compact, task-relevant semantic representations instead of raw data, uplink pressure can be substantially reduced. The feasibility of communication-efficient orbital AI infrastructures is demonstrated through the evaluation of a multi-layer heterogeneous SDC framework consisting of relay satellites and orbital computing nodes operating under coupled energy and thermal constraints. The article further outlines open research challenges toward scalable deployment.

</details>


### [201] [NeuroRisk: Physics-Informed Neural Optimization for Risk-Aware Traffic Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12862)
*Yingming Mao,Ximeng Liu,Jingyi Cheng,Xiyuan Liu,Jiashuai Liu,Yike Liu,Zhen Yao,Yuzhou Zhou,Siyuan Feng,Qiaozhu Zhai,Shizhen Zhao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: NeuroRisk是一种物理启发的深度展开优化器，通过利用Sort-and-Select结构，在WAN流量工程中实现了风险感知优化，相比传统求解器获得10²-10⁵倍加速，且最优性差距小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产广域网中，相关故障主导了可用性损失，迫使运营商预留大量安全余量，导致容量利用率低下。在严格可用性目标下实现高利用率需要风险感知的流量工程，但现有方法在操作时间尺度上难以求解。

Method: 将现有风险感知公式统一为嵌入的Sort-and-Select结构，提出NeuroRisk——一种物理信息深度展开优化器。通过门控边局部预留强制执行可行性，利用排列不变、梯度对齐的线索表示场景集。

Result: 在生产级WAN评估中，NeuroRisk在风险目标上相比求解器获得10²-10⁵倍加速，最优性差距小，且在名义吞吐量上优于神经基线方法。

Conclusion: 证明了Sort-and-Select结构统一了风险感知TE公式，揭示了表达性与可解性之间的根本权衡；NeuroRisk通过利用该结构实现了高效的风险感知流量工程。

Abstract: In production Wide-Area Networks (WANs), correlated failures dominate availability losses, forcing operators to reserve large safety margins that leave substantial capacity underutilized. Achieving high utilization under strict availability targets therefore requires risk-aware Traffic Engineering (TE) over dozens to hundreds of probabilistic failure scenarios-yet solving this problem at operational timescales remains elusive. We demonstrate that existing risk-aware formulations can be unified under an embedded Sort-and-Select structure, exposing a fundamental trade-off between expressiveness and tractability: classical optimizers either restrict scenario selection for efficiency or incur prohibitive decomposition costs. While deep learning appears promising, prior Deep TE methods mainly target maximum link utilization and rely on scaling-based feasibility, which fundamentally breaks under explicit capacity constraints and scenario-dependent risk. We present NeuroRisk, a physics-informed deep unrolled optimizer that exploits the structure of Sort-and-Select. NeuroRisk enforces feasibility via gated edge-local reservations and represents scenario sets through permutation-invariant, gradient-aligned cues. Evaluations on production-style WANs show that NeuroRisk achieves small optimality gaps relative to the solver with orders of magnitude speedup $(10^2- 10^5 \times)$ on risk objectives, while outperforming neural baselines on nominal throughput.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Adaptive Smooth Tchebycheff Attention for Multi-Objective Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12771)
*Alejandro Murillo-Gonzalez,Mahmoud Ali,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应平滑Tchebycheff框架，通过冲突感知的动态曲率调节机制，在深度多目标强化学习中兼顾非凸Pareto前沿的可达性与优化稳定性，并在机器人视觉搜索任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性标量化无法恢复非凸Pareto前沿区域的解，而静态非线性标量化（如Tchebycheff）虽在理论上可行，但在深度强化学习中遭受严重的梯度方差和优化不稳定性。需要一种方法同时解决可达性与稳定性的矛盾。

Method: 提出自适应平滑Tchebycheff框架，核心是冲突驱动控制器：基于实时梯度干扰动态调节优化曲率——目标对齐时退火至精确的非凸标量化，梯度冲突出现时弹性恢复至稳定的平滑近似。

Result: 在机器人隐蔽视觉搜索任务中，该方法能够稳健发现线性基线无法触及、静态非线性方法不稳定的非凸帕累托最优策略，大量消融实验证实了冲突感知适应机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 冲突感知的动态曲率调节能够有效解决多目标深度强化学习中非凸Pareto前沿的可达性与训练稳定性之间的根本矛盾。

Abstract: Multi-objective reinforcement learning in robotic domains requires balancing complex, non-convex trade-offs between conflicting objectives. While linear scalarization methods provide stability, they are theoretically incapable of recovering solutions within non-convex regions of the Pareto front. Conversely, static non-linear scalarizations (e.g., Tchebycheff) can theoretically access these regions but often suffer from severe gradient variance and optimization instability in deep RL. In this work, we propose an Adaptive Smooth Tchebycheff framework that resolves this tension by dynamically modulating the curvature of the optimization landscape. We introduce a novel conflict-driven controller that regulates the optimization smoothness based on real-time gradient interference. This allows the agent to anneal toward precise, non-convex scalarization when objectives align, while elastically reverting to stable, smooth approximations when destructive gradient conflicts emerge. We validate our approach on a challenging robotic stealth visual search task -- a proxy for monitoring of protected/fragile ecosystems -- where an agent must balance search, exposure/interference minimization and exploration speed. Extensive ablations confirm that our conflict-aware adaptation enables the robust discovery of Pareto-optimal policies in non-convex regions inaccessible to linear baselines and unstable for static non-linear methods.
  Website: https://alejandromllo.github.io/research/pasta/

</details>


### [203] [Action Emergence from Streaming Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12622)
*Pengfei Jing,Victor Shea-Jay Huang,Hengtong Lu,Jifeng Dai,Xie Yan,Benjin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"动作涌现"作为端到端自动驾驶的目标能力，并设计Streaming Intent机制——通过连续思维链语义化流式表达驾驶意图，再以意图引导流匹配动作头生成轨迹。所提SI模型在Waymo基准上取得竞争力结果（RFS 7.96/7.74），并首次在全端到端VLA中实现意图忠实可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶范式无法实现"动作涌现"：自回归轨迹解码器将多模态未来坍缩为单一平均输出，扩散/流匹配生成器虽能表达多模态但不可被推理意图引导。需要一种机制使生成的动作既具多模态性又能被高层语义意图可控。

Method: 提出Streaming Intent：语义维度上通过四步连续思维链（chain-of-thought）从场景理解因果推导出驾驶意图，意图以离散token表达；时间维度上跨片段流式传递，保持意图一致性。意图token驱动分类器无引导（CFG）作用于流匹配动作头，仅需两步去噪即可生成轨迹。

Result: Waymo端到端基准验证集RFS 7.96，测试集RFS 7.74。首次在全端到端VLA中展示意图忠实可控性：固定场景下改变推理时的意图类别可产生定性不同且质量一致的高质量规划，无需预设轨迹库或后处理选择器。

Conclusion: Streaming Intent为端到端自动驾驶提供了一种可行的动作涌现实现路径，通过语义流式意图+轻量流匹配生成，在保持竞争性能的同时实现了意图可控的多模态动作生成，标志着VLA模型从"感知-映射"向"因果推理-生成"的范式转变。

Abstract: We formalize action emergence as a target capability for end-to-end autonomous driving: the ability to generate physically feasible, semantically appropriate, and safety-compliant actions in arbitrary, long-tail traffic scenes through scene-conditioned reasoning rather than retrieval or interpolation of learned scene-action mappings. We show that previous paradigms cannot deliver action emergence: autoregressive trajectory decoders collapse the inherently multimodal future into a single averaged output, while diffusion and flow-matching generators express multimodality but are not steerable by reasoned intent. We propose Streaming Intent as a concrete way to approach action emergence: a mechanism that makes driving intent (i) semantically streamed through a continuous chain-of-thought that causally derives the intent from scene understanding, and (ii) temporally streamed across clips so that intent commitments remain coherent along the driving horizon. We realize Streaming Intent in a VLA model we call SI (Streaming Intent). SI autoregressively decodes a four-step chain-of-thought and emits an intent token; the decoded intent then drives classifier-free guidance (CFG) on a flow-matching action head, requiring only two denoising steps to generate the final trajectory. On the Waymo End-to-End benchmark, SI achieves competitive aggregate performance, with an RFS score of 7.96 on the validation set and 7.74 on the test set. Beyond aggregate metrics, the model demonstrates -- to our knowledge for the first time in a fully end-to-end VLA -- intent-faithful controllability: for a fixed scene, varying the intent class at inference yields qualitatively distinct yet consistently high-quality plans, arising purely from data-driven learning without any pre-built trajectory bank or hand-coded post-hoc selector.

</details>


### [204] [Distributionally Robust Safety Under Arbitrary Uncertainties: A Safety Filtering Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12974)
*Daniel M. Cherenson,Haejoon Lee,Taekyung Kim,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对分布模糊下的非线性系统，提出了一种基于备份的安全过滤框架，利用Wasserstein模糊集进行分布鲁棒性安全认证，将高维轨迹优化问题简化为一维切换时间搜索，并通过采样方法提供有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全过滤方法通常假设不确定性服从已知分布，但实际中分布结构未知且存在模糊性，需要一种能够处理任意分布模糊性的安全保证方法。

Method: 基于备份的安全过滤框架，采用Wasserstein模糊集描述分布模糊性，利用备份策略的结构特性将安全认证简化为对名义策略与备份策略间切换时间的一维搜索，并设计基于采样的有限样本认证过程。

Result: 在Dubins车、高速赛车和战斗机三个系统上的仿真验证了方法的广泛适用性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够有效处理分布模糊下的非线性系统概率安全保证问题，兼具理论保证与计算可行性。

Abstract: In this work, we study how to ensure probabilistic safety for nonlinear systems under distributional ambiguity. Our approach builds on a backup-based safety filtering framework that switches between a high-performance nominal policy and a certified backup policy to ensure safety. To handle arbitrary uncertainties from ambiguous distributions, i.e., where the distribution is not of specific structure and the true distribution is unknown, we adopt a distributionally robust (DR) formulation using Wasserstein ambiguity sets. Rather than solving a high-dimensional DR trajectory optimization problem online, we exploit the structure of backup-based safety filtering to reduce safety certification to a one-dimensional search over the switching time between nominal and backup policies. We then develop a sampling-based certification procedure with finite-sample guarantees, where empirical failure probabilities are compared against a Wasserstein-inflated threshold. We validate our method through simulations across three systems, from a Dubins vehicle to a high-speed racing car and a fighter jet, demonstrating the broad applicability and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [205] [MindVLA-U1: VLA Beats VA with Unified Streaming Architecture for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12624)
*Yuzhou Huang,Benjin Zhu,Hengtong Lu,Victor Shea-Jay Huang,Haiming Zhang,Wei Chen,Jifeng Dai,Yan Xie,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MindVLA-U1 是首个统一流式 VLA 架构，通过单次前向传播联合生成语言 token 和连续动作轨迹，采用流式逐帧处理、学习记忆通道和 MoT 骨干，在 WOD-E2E 上首次超越人类驾驶员（8.20 RFS vs 8.13 GT RFS），达到 SOTA 规划性能并保持 16 FPS 吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型在规划质量上落后于 VA 模型，原因是 VLA 被构建为孤立的子任务改进而非连贯的驾驶能力组合。本文旨在弥合这一差距，设计真正统一的流式 VLA 架构。

Method: 1) 统一 VLM 骨干：单次前向传播同时生成自回归语言 token 和流匹配连续动作轨迹；2) 流式设计：逐帧处理驾驶视频而非固定视频-动作块；3) 学习记忆通道：跨帧传递时序上下文，避免冗余多帧 VLM 建模；4) 快/慢速 MoT 执行：通过灵活自注意力上下文管理实现稠密/稀疏 Mixture-of-Transformers 骨干；5) 语言到动作路由：语言预测的驾驶意图通过无分类器引导（CFG）控制动作扩散生成。

Result: 在 WOD-E2E 长尾基准上，MindVLA-U1 首次超越人类驾驶员（8.20 RFS vs 8.13 GT RFS），仅用 2 步扩散；规划 ADE 大幅超越此前 VA/VLA 方法；吞吐量达 16 FPS，接近 VA 类方法 RAP-DINO 的 18 FPS，同时保留自然语言接口。

Conclusion: 统一流式 VLA 架构能够有效弥合 VLA 与 VA 之间的规划质量差距，同时保持语言交互能力，证明了语义推理、时序上下文与连续控制的有机组合可超越人类驾驶水平。

Abstract: Autonomous driving has progressed from modular pipelines toward end-to-end unification, and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a natural extension of this journey beyond Vision-to-Action (VA). In practice, driving VLAs have often trailed VA on planning quality, suggesting that the difficulty is not simply model scale but the interface through which semantic reasoning, temporal context, and continuous control are combined. We argue that this gap reflects how VLA has been built -- as isolated subtask improvements that fail to compose into coherent driving capabilities -- rather than what VLA is. We present MindVLA-U1, the first unified streaming VLA architecture for autonomous driving. A unified VLM backbone produces autoregressive language tokens and flow-matching continuous action trajectories in a single forward pass over one shared representation, preserving the natural output form of each modality. A streaming design processes the driving video framewise rather than as fixed video-action chunks, while a learned memory channel carries temporal context across frames so planned trajectories evolve smoothly without redundant multi-frame VLM modeling. The unified architecture admits fast/slow execution on dense/sparse Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) backbones via flexible self-attention context management, and exposes a measurable language-to-action route: a language-predicted driving intent steers action diffusion through classifier-free guidance (CFG), turning language-side intent into a control signal for continuous trajectory generation. On the long-tail WOD-E2E benchmark, MindVLA-U1 surpasses experienced human drivers for the first time (8.20 RFS vs. 8.13 GT RFS) with 2 diffusion steps, achieves state-of-the-art planning ADEs over prior VA/VLA methods by large margins, and matches VA-class throughput (16 FPS vs. RAP-DINO's 18 FPS) while preserving natural-language interfaces.

</details>


### [206] [Local Conformal Calibration of Dynamics Uncertainty from Semantic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13028)
*Luís Marques,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OCULAR算法，一种基于共形预测的不确定性量化方法，利用感知信息为未见测试环境提供保证。该方法使用视觉相似环境数据校准任意保真度的线性高斯动力学模型，生成包含未来状态的概率保证区域，适用于概率安全规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共形预测方法无法区分不同状态-动作空间区域导致的不同模型失配程度，且需要环境特定数据，缺乏泛化能力。

Method: 提出OCULAR算法，利用视觉相似环境收集的数据对线性高斯动力学模型进行校准，生成共形预测区域，同时处理偶然不确定性和认知不确定性。

Result: 在双积分器系统上验证，支持简化传感器和模拟相机两种设置，在分布内和分布外场景中均能适当量化不确定性，且体积效率优于需要环境特定数据的基线方法。

Conclusion: OCULAR提供非渐近、无分布假设的保证，能区分不同输入导致的不同下一状态不确定性，对概率安全规划具有实际价值。

Abstract: We introduce Observation-aware Conformal Uncertainty Local-Calibration (OCULAR), a conformal prediction-based algorithm that uses perception information to provide uncertainty quantification guarantees for unseen test-time environments. While previous conformal approaches lack the ability to discriminate between state-action space regions leading to higher or lower model mismatch, and require environment-specific data, our method uses data collected from visually similar environments to provably calibrate a given linear Gaussian dynamics model of arbitrary fidelity. The prediction regions generated from OCULAR are guaranteed to contain the future system states with, at least, a user-set likelihood, despite both aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty -- i.e., uncertainty arising from both stochastic disturbances and lack of data. Our guarantees are non-asymptotic and distribution-free, not requiring strong assumptions about the unknown real system dynamics. Our calibration procedure enables distinguishing between observation-velocity-action inputs leading to higher and lower next-state-uncertainty, which is helpful for probabilistically-safe planning. We numerically validate our algorithm on a double-integrator system subject to random perturbations and significant model mismatch, using both a simplified sensor and a more realistic simulated camera. Our approach appropriately quantifies uncertainty both when in-distribution and out-of-distribution, being comparatively volume-efficient to baselines requiring environment-specific data.

</details>


### [207] [Driving Intents Amplify Planning-Oriented Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12625)
*Hengtong Lu,Victor Shea-Jay Huang,Chengmin Yang,Pengfei Jing,Jifeng Dai,Yan Xie,Benjin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了DIAL框架，通过意图条件流匹配（intent-CFG）和多意图GRPO偏好强化学习，解决连续动作驾驶策略从单轨迹演示训练时的模式坍缩问题，在WOD-E2E上首次超越人类驾驶演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续动作策略从单条演示轨迹训练时会产生模式坍缩，样本聚集在演示动作周围，无法表示语义上不同的替代方案；在偏好评估下，这会限制best-of-N性能上限，即使oracle选择也无法恢复采样分布中不存在的内容。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段使用离散意图标签条件化流匹配动作头，配合无分类器引导（CFG）扩展采样分布以打破模式坍缩；第二阶段通过多意图GRPO进行偏好强化学习，在每组偏好中覆盖所有意图类别，防止微调时重新坍缩到当前偏好模式。

Result: intent-CFG采样在best-of-128下RFS达到9.14，超越先前最优（RAP 8.5）和人类驾驶演示（8.13）；多意图GRPO将保留集RFS从7.681提升至8.211，而所有单意图基线均更低且训练结束时退化。

Conclusion: 偏好强化学习在基于演示训练的连续动作策略上的瓶颈不仅是如何更新策略，更是如何扩展并保持被优化的采样分布。

Abstract: Continuous-action policies trained on a single demonstrated trajectory per scene suffer from mode collapse: samples cluster around the demonstrated maneuver and the policy cannot represent semantically distinct alternatives. Under preference-based evaluation, this caps best-of-N performance -- even oracle selection cannot recover what the sampling distribution does not contain. We introduce DIAL, a two-stage Driving-Intent-Amplified reinforcement Learning framework for preference-aligned continuous-action driving policies. In the first stage, DIAL conditions the flow-matching action head on a discrete intent label with classifier-free guidance (CFG), which expands the sampling distribution along distinct maneuver modes and breaks single-demonstration mode collapse. In the second stage, DIAL carries this expanded distribution into preference RL through multi-intent GRPO, which spans all intent classes within every preference group and prevents fine-tuning from re-collapsing around the currently preferred mode. Instantiated for end-to-end driving with eight rule-derived intents and evaluated on WOD-E2E: competitive Vision-to-Action (VA) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) Supervised Finetuning (SFT) baselines plateau below the human-driven demonstration at best-of-128, with the strongest prior (RAP) capping at Rater Feedback Score (RFS) 8.5 even with best-of-64; intent-CFG sampling lifts this ceiling to RFS 9.14 at best-of-128, surpassing both the prior best (RAP 8.5) and the human-driven demonstration (8.13) for the first time; and multi-intent GRPO improves held-out RFS from 7.681 to 8.211, while every single-intent baseline peaks lower and degrades by training end. These results suggest that the bottleneck of preference RL on continuous-action policies trained from demonstrations is not only how to update the policy, but to expand and preserve the sampling distribution being optimized.

</details>


### [208] [Multistep Belief Space Dynamics Learning For Risk-Aware Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12628)
*Jason Gibson,Bogdan Vlahov,Patrick Spieler,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种学习分布动力学（distributional dynamics）的框架，用于实时模型预测控制（MPC），通过结构化学习降低保守性，并在真实越野驾驶场景中验证了其有效性，车辆能根据环境自然调节速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从简化研究到实际部署，自动驾驶系统与人类驾驶之间存在巨大差距，需要发展风险感知行为。传统方法难以预测动态不确定性的演化，且优化出的计划过于保守，难以满足真实世界需求。

Method: 提出一个学习分布动力学的框架，用于实时MPC优化。通过结构化学习（学习分布动力学）来预测不确定性演化，并进行严格的消融实验验证结构的重要性。最终将学习模型与规划栈部署到全尺寸实车上。

Result: 在大型真实越野驾驶数据集上的消融实验表明，偏离所提结构会显著影响性能。实车部署在数英里多样化地形上持续表现出智能行为，能够根据环境自然调节车速。

Conclusion: 提出的分布动力学学习框架结合MPC能够实现非保守的风险感知规划，在真实越野场景中有效调节速度与行为，弥合了自动驾驶从研究到实际应用的差距。

Abstract: As autonomous vehicles move from a simplified research setting to practical use, there exists a large gap between the dynamic behavior of a human driving and an autonomous system. Risk-aware behavior needs to naturally develop in order to scale to the demands of the real world. A major issue for risk-aware planning and control has been predicting how dynamical uncertainty evolves through time and optimizing plans that account for this without being overly conservative. Here, we present a learning framework to predict distributional dynamics that can be optimized in real time for Model Predictive Control (MPC). We explore the importance of structure when learning distributional dynamics for use in MPC. A rigorous ablation study is conducted on a large dataset of real world off-road driving that shows the impact of deviations from our proposed structure. Furthermore, we deploy our learned model and planning stack on a full sized vehicle in challenging off-road conditions. Our planning architecture is able to naturally regulate the speed of the vehicle based on the environment and consistently demonstrates intelligent behavior over miles of diverse terrain.

</details>


### [209] [TinySDP: Real Time Semidefinite Optimization for Certifiable and Agile Edge Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13748)
*Ishaan Mahajan,Jon Arrizabalaga,Andrea Grillo,Fausto Vega,James Anderson,Zachary Manchester,Brian Plancher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TinySDP是首个面向嵌入式系统的半定规划求解器，通过在ADMM求解器中集成正半定锥投影和缓存Riccati结构，实现了在微控制器上进行实时模型预测控制，解决非凸障碍约束的运动规划问题，在复杂场景下比基线方法路径缩短73%，并在Crazyflie四旋翼上验证了实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半定规划求解器计算开销过大，无法在资源受限的嵌入式系统上实现实时控制，而运动规划中的非凸几何约束（如障碍物避碰）又需要SDP提供的凸松弛框架来保证全局最优性，因此亟需一种能在嵌入式平台上实时运行的SDP求解器。

Method: 将正半定锥投影集成到基于缓存Riccati的ADMM求解器中，利用问题的计算结构实现嵌入式可解性；同时设计后验秩1证书，将松弛解在每个时间步转换为显式的几何保证。

Result: 在死胡同和动态避障等挑战性基准场景中，TinySDP实现了无碰撞导航，路径相比最先进的基线方法缩短高达73%；在Crazyflie四旋翼平台上验证了半定约束可以在实时速率下执行。

Conclusion: TinySDP首次证明了半定规划约束可以在资源受限的嵌入式系统上以实时速率实现，为敏捷嵌入式机器人运动规划提供了可行的SDP解决方案。

Abstract: Semidefinite programming (SDP) provides a principled framework for convex relaxations of nonconvex geometric constraints in motion planning, yet existing solvers are too computationally expensive for real-time control, particularly on resource-constrained embedded systems. To address this gap, we introduce TinySDP, the first semidefinite programming solver designed for embedded systems, enabling real-time model-predictive control (MPC) on microcontrollers for problems with nonconvex obstacle constraints. Our approach integrates positive-semidefinite cone projections into a cached-Riccati-based ADMM solver, leveraging computational structure for embedded tractability. We pair this solver with an a posteriori rank-1 certificate that converts relaxed solutions into explicit geometric guarantees at each timestep. On challenging benchmarks, e.g., cul-de-sac and dynamic obstacle avoidance scenarios that induce failures in local methods, TinySDP achieves collision-free navigation with up to 73% shorter paths than state-of-the-art baselines. We validate our approach on a Crazyflie quadrotor, demonstrating that semidefinite constraints can be enforced at real-time rates for agile embedded robotics.

</details>


### [210] [Learning Responsibility-Attributed Adversarial Scenarios for Testing Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13751)
*Yizhuo Xiao,Haotian Yan,Ying Wang,Zhongpan Zhu,Yuxin Zhang,Xintao Yan,Mustafa Suphi Erden,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARS是一个将责任归因直接集成到对抗性场景生成中的框架，能够生成既物理可行又具有诊断可归因性的碰撞场景，从而为自动驾驶系统提供可解释、符合法规的安全证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对抗性仿真方法虽能高效暴露碰撞，但缺乏区分碰撞是由可避免的系统缺陷还是不可避免的交通冲突导致的机制，难以建立可信的安全保证。

Method: CARS结合上下文感知的对手选择与生成式对抗策略，在闭环仿真中优化，生成既物理可行又具有诊断可归因性的碰撞场景，并通过多种法规规定的谨慎和称职驾驶员模型进行验证。

Result: 在涵盖异质国家交通环境的基准数据集上，CARS在多类法规规定的驾驶模型下，一致地发现了高归因率的可行碰撞场景。

Conclusion: CARS将对抗性生成与规范性责任评估相结合，将仿真测试从碰撞发现推进到可解释、符合法规的安全证据构建，为可扩展的ADS验证提供了新范式。

Abstract: Establishing trustworthy safety assurance for autonomous driving systems (ADSs) requires evidence that failures arise from avoidable system deficiencies rather than unavoidable traffic conflicts. Current adversarial simulation methods can efficiently expose collisions, but generally lack mechanisms to distinguish these fundamentally different failure modes. Here we present CARS (Context-Aware, Responsibility-attributed Scenario generation), a framework that integrates responsibility attribution directly into adversarial scenario generation. CARS combines context-aware adversary selection with a generative adversarial policy optimized in closed-loop simulation to construct collision scenarios that are both physically feasible and diagnostically attributable. Across benchmark datasets spanning heterogeneous national traffic environments, CARS consistently discovers feasible collision scenarios with high attribution rates under multiple regulation-prescribed careful and competent driver models. By coupling adversarial generation with normative responsibility assessment, CARS moves simulation testing beyond collision discovery toward the construction of interpretable, regulation-aligned safety evidence for scalable ADS validation.

</details>


### [211] [Belief-Space Residual Risk for Automated Driving under Localization Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12710)
*Nijinshan Karunainayagam,Nils Gehrke,Frank Diermeyer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将空间残差风险公式扩展到置信空间，通过将自车姿态不确定性建模为高斯分布，在基于粒子的风险估计框架中融合自车与目标的不确定性来计算碰撞概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有残差风险指标假设确定性自车姿态，仅关注感知误差和延迟效应，忽略了自动驾驶车辆在实际复杂城市环境及恶劣天气下存在的显著定位不确定性。

Method: 将自车姿态不确定性显式建模为高斯分布，在置信空间内重新定义残差风险为期望退化诱导风险；通过协方差融合自车与目标不确定性，在粒子风险估计框架中计算碰撞概率。

Result: 扩展了残差风险公式以纳入定位不确定性，实现了基于粒子的风险估计中自车与目标不确定性的协方差融合。

Conclusion: 通过建模自车定位不确定性，提升了自动驾驶系统安全评估的完整性，尤其适用于城市环境及恶劣天气条件。

Abstract: Residual risk metrics have recently been introduced to assess the safety implications of automated driving systems. Existing approaches typically assume a deterministic ego pose and concentrate mainly on perception errors related to surrounding objects and latency effects. In practice, however, automated vehicles operate under considerable localization uncertainty, especially in complex urban settings and in adverse weather conditions. This work extends the spatial residual risk formulation to the belief space by explicitly modeling ego pose uncertainty as a Gaussian distribution. Residual risk is reformulated as the expected degradation-induced risk over the ego pose belief distribution. Within a particle-based risk estimation framework, localization uncertainty is incorporated into the computation of collision probabilities through covariance fusion of ego and object uncertainties.

</details>


### [212] [Loiter UAV Reinsertion Guidance for Fixed-wing UAV Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13822)
*Pradeep J,Kedarisetty Siddhardha,Ashwini Ratnoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了固定翼无人机走廊的交通管理问题，重点解决无人机从盘旋等待车道安全重新插入主车道的冲突避免方法，并开发了引导算法计算过渡车道中的所需速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机走廊中，当主车道交通减少或盘旋无人机急需到达目的地时，需要将无人机从等待车道安全重新插入主车道，但现有研究缺乏针对这一场景的冲突避免引导策略。

Method: 提出了一种基于固定数量等距虚拟时隙的盘旋车道模型，考虑无人机速度限制和最小安全距离，开发了引导算法以计算过渡车道中无人机的所需速度，确保安全重新插入。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证了所提出的引导与自动化策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的引导算法能够在满足安全约束的条件下，实现无人机从盘旋车道到主车道的无冲突重新插入。

Abstract: This paper considers fixed-wing unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) corridors comprising a main lane, a circular loiter lane for managing traffic congestion, and transit lanes connecting the two. In particular, we address the problem of conflict-free reinsertion of UAVs from the loiter lane back into the main lane. The loiter lane contains a fixed number of equidistant virtual slots that UAVs can occupy. Reinsertion of loiter UAVs into the main lane becomes essential either due to reduced traffic in the main lane or due to a loiter UAV needing to reach its destination urgently. Given the total number of loiter slots, UAV speed limits, and the minimum safety distance, a guidance algorithm is developed to compute the required speed of a loiter UAV in the transit lane to ensure safe reinsertion. The proposed guidance and automation strategies are validated through numerical simulations.

</details>


### [213] [A Five-Layer MLOps Architecture for Connected Automated Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12719)
*Bastian Lampe,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向自动驾驶系统的五层集体学习MLOps架构，通过车队间数据共享与联合学习，支持持续的安全与性能保障，并能够检测和减少包括黑天鹅事件在内的边缘案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统运行在复杂、动态的开放世界环境中，面临大量罕见或开发阶段未预见的场景。尽管AI/ML技术使车辆能从运行数据中学习并持续适应，但如何在多车队、多实体间有效利用集体学习潜力以实现持续的安全与性能保障，仍缺乏系统化的架构蓝图。

Method: 基于成熟的MLOps原则和网联自动驾驶领域现有工作，本文设计了一个五层架构，明确了各层的主要职责及其交互方式，并通过多层级的自我评估机制来支持边缘案例和黑天鹅事件的检测与缓解。

Result: 提出了一个概念性蓝图架构，支持车队运营商等相关利益方设计和实施集体学习驱动的MLOps流程，从而利用跨车队、跨实体的数据汇集与联合学习能力。

Conclusion: 该五层架构为自动驾驶系统的持续安全与性能保障提供了系统化的集体学习路径，尤其有助于处理开发阶段难以预见的极端场景。

Abstract: The continual assurance of safety and performance of automated driving systems (ADSs) poses significant challenges. ADSs operate in complex, dynamic, open-world environments allowing a wide range of scenarios, including ones that are rare or not foreseen during initial development. While the incorporation of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) technology allows ADSs to learn from data gathered during operation and thus enables them to adapt over time, these approaches come with their own challenges. A key advantage of ADSs compared to human drivers is their greater ability to gather data collectively across a fleet of vehicles, or even across multiple fleets operated by different entities, and to learn from this data collectively. Vehicles can share and combine their data to identify additional learning opportunities otherwise missed by individual vehicles. This creates new opportunities to tackle the challenges of continual assurance of safety and performance, but requires the implementation of architectures that leverage the collective learning potential. Based on established MLOps principles and existing work in the field of connected automated driving, this paper presents a five-layer architecture for collective learning-enabled MLOps processes for ADSs. The goal of this architecture is to provide a conceptual blueprint for the design and implementation of MLOps processes by fleet operators and other relevant stakeholders. The paper describes the main responsibilities of each layer, their interactions, and how multi-level self-assessments enabled by the architecture can support the detection and reduction of edge cases including black swan events.

</details>


### [214] [The Unified Autonomy Stack: Toward a Blueprint for Generalizable Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12735)
*Mihir Dharmadhikari,Nikhil Khedekar,Mihir Kulkarni,Morten Nissov,Martin Jacquet,Angelos Zacharia,Marvin Harms,Albert Gassol Puigjaner,Philipp Weiss,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并开源了统一自主栈（Unified Autonomy Stack），一个实现空中与地面机器人跨形态韧性自主的系统级解决方案，融合多模态感知、多行为规划与多层安全导航三个协同模块，经过各类严苛环境实地验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人自主系统往往针对单一形态或特定环境设计，缺乏跨形态、跨场景的通用性与韧性，亟需一个统一的系统级框架以支持多种机器人平台在复杂、退化环境中的可靠自主运行。

Method: 架构包含三个核心协同模块：(a)多模态感知：融合LiDAR、雷达、视觉与惯性传感器，基于因子图实现鲁棒定位与建图，支持语义场景理解；(b)多行为规划：采用跨空间尺度的基于采样的运动规划与信息性路径规划；(c)多层安全导航：结合在线重建地图上的规划、深度学习驱动的外部感知策略以及基于控制屏障函数的最后防线安全滤波器。支持GNSS拒止环境下的未知区域导航、复杂环境探索、目标发现与高效巡检规划。

Result: 在旋翼飞行器和四足机器人平台上，于自相似环境、烟尘环境、复杂几何结构与高障碍物杂波等严苛条件下完成实地测试，验证了系统在挑战性环境中的韧性表现。

Conclusion: 提出的统一自主栈实现了跨形态机器人的全面任务自主能力，并通过开源推动社区采用与进一步开发。

Abstract: We introduce and open-source the Unified Autonomy Stack, a system-level solution that enables resilient autonomy across diverse aerial and ground robot morphologies. The architecture centers on three synergistic modules -- multi-modal perception, multi-behavior planning, and multi-layered safe navigation -- that together deliver comprehensive mission autonomy. The stack fuses data from LiDAR, radar, vision, and inertial sensing, enabling (a) robust localization and mapping through factor graph-based fusion, (b) semantic scene understanding, (c) motion and informative path planning through sampling-based techniques adaptive across spatial scales, as well as (d) multi-layered safe navigation both through planning on the online reconstructed map and deep learning-driven exteroceptive policies alongside last-resort safety filters using control barrier functions. The resulting behaviors include safe GNSS-denied navigation into unknown and perceptually-degraded regions, exploration of complex environments, object discovery, and efficient inspection planning. The stack has been field-tested and validated on both aerial (rotorcraft) and ground (legged) robots operating in a host of demanding environments, including self-similar and smoke-filled settings, with complex geometries and high obstacle clutter. These tests demonstrate resilient performance in challenging conditions. To facilitate ease of adoption, we open-source the implementation alongside supporting documentation, validation, and evaluation datasets https://github.com/ntnu-arl/unified_autonomy_stack. A video giving the overview of the paper and the field experiments is available at https://youtu.be/l8Su8OXsM-E.

</details>


### [215] [Emotional Expression in Low-Degrees-of-Freedom Robots: Assessing Perception with Reachy Mini](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12786)
*Amit Rogel,Elmira Yadollahi,Guy Laban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过在线实验（N=100）考察了人们对低自由度、非人形机器人Reachy Mini情感表达的感知。结果显示，精确情绪识别率中等且因情绪而异（愤怒、悲伤、兴趣识别率较高），但参与者能较好地恢复情感维度（效价和唤醒度）信息，且积极表达会提升对社会温暖性和社交性的感知。这表明即使是受限的机器人表达也能传达情感意义并影响社会印象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互中情感表达至关重要，但人们对具有稀疏、非拟人化表达能力的机器人如何解读情感知之甚少。

Method: 在线被试内实验，100名参与者观看了10段Reachy Mini表达不同情绪的短视频，并对每段视频识别情绪、评效价和唤醒度、评估社会感知特质。

Result: 精确情绪识别率中等且差异大（愤怒、悲伤、兴趣更可靠），但参与者能更好地恢复效价和唤醒度等情感维度信息；积极表达使机器人被认为更温暖、更善于社交，而生命感在不同条件下差异较小。

Conclusion: 即使表达受限的机器人也能传达情感意义并影响社会印象，Reachy Mini可作为研究低自由度机器人情感沟通的有用基准。

Abstract: Emotion expression is central to human--robot interaction, yet little is known about how people interpret affect on robots with sparse, non-anthropomorphic expressive capabilities. This study examined how people perceive emotional expressions displayed by Reachy Mini (Pollen Robotics and Hugging Face), a low-degree-of-freedom (low-DoF) robot with a constrained and distinctly non-human expressive repertoire.
