In this paper, we build a floater simulation inspired by the physics of the eye, use it to quantitatively assess text readability at varying levels of motion, and build a novel web extension that minimizes eye movement, maximizing the signal-to-noise ratio of performing browser tasks. Importantly, our tool works not only for text, but for all UI elements, requiring no modifications to existing websites.

</details>


### [253] [What Do You Think I Think? Accounting for Human Beliefs Using Second-Order Theory of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12745)
*Patrick Callaghan,Reid Simmons,Henny Admoni*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于二阶心理理论（ToM-2）的智能体，能够建模用户对智能体知识的错误信念及其认知偏差来源，在交互中检测偏差并生成自适应反馈。用户研究表明，该方法能显著提升教师行为的信息性，且反馈被认为更有用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体的实际知识与用户认为智能体所知道的知识之间存在差异，这会阻碍人机交互。如果能检测到这种差异，智能体可以提供反馈来弥补差距并改善交互。

Method: 以交互式部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（I-POMDP）为框架，实现二阶心理理论（ToM-2），使智能体能够建模用户对智能体的错误信念及其背后的认知偏差与启发式（CBH），从而在交互中检测CBH并自适应生成反馈。

Result: 真人用户实验表明，ToM-2学习者能够解释教师的认知偏差影响，显著提升教师行为的信息性；主观结果也显示用户认为ToM-2学习者的反馈更有用。

Conclusion: 通过建模用户的错误信念和认知偏差，智能体可以检测交互中的认知偏差并生成自适应反馈，从而改善人机交互效果。

Abstract: Discrepancies between an agent's actual knowledge and what a person thinks the agent knows can hinder interactions. If an agent could detect such discrepancies, it could provide feedback to account for them and improve current and future interactions. Using the I-POMDP as a framework for a second-order Theory of Mind (ToM-2), this work endows an agent with the ability to model the evolution of a person's erroneous beliefs about an agent and the cognitive biases and heuristics (CBH) from which they arise. In doing so, the agent can detect when CBH might be at play during an interaction and adaptively generate feedback that accounts for them. An in-person user study shows how a ToM-2 learner can account for the effects of a teacher's CBH to significantly improve the informativeness of teacher actions, and subjective results suggest people find the ToM-2 learner's feedback more useful.

</details>


### [254] [Seed Bank, Co-op, Stoop Swap: Metaphors for Governing Language Model Data for Creative Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12888)
*Alicia Guo,Carly Schnitzler,Katy Gero*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 超过100名创意作家通过工作坊提出了200多个隐喻（如社区花园、种子银行等），探讨如何以作家同意、参与和控制为核心，构建由创意作家群体治理的语言模型，最终指向小型、开放、编码群体价值的社区语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI语言模型的所有权和使用方式限制了创意作家的同意、参与和控制权，亟需探索由创意作家群体为自己治理的语言模型模式。

Method: 组织了四场工作坊，邀请超过100名创意作家提出并分析关于语言模型治理的隐喻，收集了200多个隐喻（包括对象、地点、过程、群体和基础设施），并从中提炼主题。

Result: 识别出四个核心主题：(1) 同意的重要性，(2) 社区边界的定义，(3) 贡献者认可的方式，(4) 语言模型规模与治理的权衡。隐喻指向小型、开放、编码群体价值的模型。

Conclusion: 隐喻驱动的设计方法揭示了创意作家对语言模型治理的核心诉求，论文讨论了实现社区语言模型的具体途径。

Abstract: How might we govern a language model run for and by creative writers? While generative AI use is on the rise, many language models are created and owned in ways that limit writers' consent, participation, and control. We report on four workshops where over one hundred creative writers came up with and analyzed metaphors for language model governance, resulting in over two hundred metaphors: objects, places, processes, groups, and infrastructure that support reasoning about language model governance. What if a language model was like a community garden? Or a seed bank? Or the bathroom in a dive bar? We report on four themes: (1) the importance of consent, (2) how to define community boundaries, (3) ways to give contributor recognition, and (4) trade-offs in scale of language models. These metaphors point towards smaller, open models that encode group values. We discuss concrete ways to make community language models a reality.

</details>


### [255] [Magical Touch: Transforming Raw Capacitive Streams into Expressive Hand-Touchscreen Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12902)
*Yuanlei Guo,Xizi Gong,Yizhong Zhang,Xiaoyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Magical Touch 利用原始电容感应数据，允许用户在现有触摸屏上使用任意手部手势进行自然、高效的交互，并通过物理游戏展示了该方法在手部几何形状和接触压力响应方面的表现力，扩展了触摸屏交互的设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触摸屏技术仅支持多点触控，但人类手部更丰富的表达潜力未被充分利用，因为大多数现有方法直接滤除了大面积手部接触信号。

Method: 将原始电容传感数据直接集成到交互回路中，利用完整的传感器数据而非过滤后的接触点信息，支持任意手部手势的自然交互。

Result: 实现了一个基于物理的交互游戏，包含单人、多人协作和压力敏感模式，展示了数字对象对手部几何形状和接触强度的实时响应能力。

Conclusion: 利用原始电容数据可以扩展触摸屏交互的设计空间，提供超越现有基于指尖方法的具身化、连续交互范式。

Abstract: Modern touchscreens utilize capacitive sensing technology to enable precise and robust multi-touch interaction. However, the broader expressive potential of the human hand remains underutilized, since most existing methods directly filter out larger-area hand-screen contact. This paper introduces Magical Touch, an interaction method based on raw capacitive sensing data. By directly integrating raw touchscreen sensor data into the interaction loop, our method allows users to interact with the screen naturally and efficiently using arbitrary hand gestures on existing touchscreen devices. To demonstrate the feasibility and expressive capacity of this approach, we implement a physics-based interactive game featuring single-player, multiplayer collaborative, and pressure-sensitive modes. These scenarios showcase how digital objects can respond in real-time to both the geometry and contact intensity of the user's hand. Our results indicate that leveraging raw capacitive data can expand the design space of touchscreen interaction, offering an embodied and continuous interaction paradigm beyond existing fingertip-based approaches.

</details>


### [256] [Discovery-Oriented Faceting: From Coverage to Blind-Spot Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12956)
*Youdi Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出盲点发现（Blind-Spot Discovery）替代传统的覆盖优化方法，通过DOF系统（Discovery-Oriented Faceting）对文档集合进行差异化排序和类别划分，帮助用户注意到被主流方法隐藏的异常、边缘和小众内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI工具（如摘要、主题建模）优化覆盖度，倾向于展示核心内容而隐藏少数视角和边缘案例，导致用户可能错过能够改变理解的异常发现。

Method: 提出三个设计目标：1）按明确边界对文档分类；2）按独特性而非规模对类别排序；3）支持迭代精化。实现为DOF系统，并在四个领域与覆盖导向排序进行对比。

Result: 两种方法呈现出根本不同的内容：DOF突出了被覆盖方法埋没的专业化类别，覆盖方法则侧重主流主题。

Conclusion: 从覆盖导向转向发现导向可能为探索大规模文本集合的用户提供一种互补式支持模式。

Abstract: When people explore large document collections to build understanding, they face a challenge: existing AI tools help them see what is central but tend to hide what is unusual. Summarization and topic modeling optimize for coverage, representing main themes while pushing minority viewpoints and edge cases out of view. This matters because discovery often depends on noticing what does not fit, such as unexpected findings, minority positions, or gaps in the literature. When tools hide this content, users may miss insights that could change their understanding. In this paper, we explore an alternative objective: blind-spot discovery, where the goal is to surface content that coverage methods suppress so that people can judge its significance for themselves. We propose three design goals and illustrate them through DOF (Discovery-Oriented Faceting), a system that organizes documents into categories with explicit boundaries, ranks categories by distinctiveness rather than size, and supports iterative refinement. Comparing DOF against coverage-based ranking across four domains, we find that the two approaches surface fundamentally different content, with DOF promoting specialized categories that coverage methods bury. We discuss how shifting from coverage to discovery may offer a complementary mode of support for people exploring large text collections.

</details>


### [257] [Doppler Prompting for Stable mmWave-based Human Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13233)
*Shuntian Zheng,Jiaqi Li,Xiaoman Lu,Shuai He,Yu Guan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 针对毫米波人体姿态估计中多普勒信号与空间幅值物理语义不一致的问题，提出PULSE方法，将多普勒特征转化为置信度感知的运动提示，通过约束交互注入空间推理，抑制虚假运动线索，提升姿态精度和时间稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有毫米波人体姿态估计方法要么未充分利用多普勒特征，要么简单融合多普勒信号与空间幅值，忽略了二者不同的物理语义，导致非人体多普勒信号被误判为人体运动线索，产生抖动轨迹。

Method: 提出PULSE框架，将多普勒特征转化为置信度感知的运动提示（Doppler prompts），通过受约束的交互方式注入空间幅值推理过程，先筛选多普勒提示以抑制虚假频谱运动线索，再用筛选后的提示稳定预测。

Result: 在三个覆盖单人及多人场景的数据集上，PULSE一致地提升了姿态精度和时间稳定性。

Conclusion: 受控的多普勒提示（controlled Doppler prompting）是实现稳定毫米波人体姿态估计的实用方向。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) enables privacy-preserving, illumination-robust human pose estimation (HPE), with each mmWave frame represented as a range-angle-Doppler tensor, providing spatial magnitude for localization and Doppler signatures for motion-related cues. However, existing mmWave-based HPE methods either underutilize or naïvely fuse Doppler signatures with spatial magnitude, disregarding their distinct physical semantics. As a result, non-human Doppler signatures can be misinterpreted as human motion cues, leading to jittery trajectories. We propose PULSE, which converts Doppler signatures into confidence-aware motion prompts and injects them into spatial magnitude reasoning through constrained interactions. By screening Doppler prompts before they influence prediction, PULSE first suppresses spurious spectral motion cues and then uses the screened prompts to stabilize prediction. Across three datasets spanning single- and multi-person settings, PULSE consistently improves pose accuracy and temporal stability, indicating that controlled Doppler prompting is a practical direction for stable mmWave HPE.

</details>


### [258] ["It became a self-fulfilling prophecy": How Lived Experiences are Entangled with AI Predictions in Menstrual Cycle Tracking Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13261)
*Wendy Zhou,Pelin Karaturhan,Alexandra Weilenmann,Jichen Zhu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过14次半结构化用户访谈和群体自我民族志，研究月经周期追踪应用中AI预测和解释如何影响用户的生活体验，发现用户根据AI预测理解自身经历但预测可能出错、界面设计不支持用户批判性参与、非规范用户感到孤立，并提出了设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏关于MCTAs中预测性AI特征和解释如何影响用户生活体验的研究，作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 14次半结构化用户访谈和群体自我民族志。

Result: (1)用户根据AI预测理解自身生活体验，但预测可能因不完美的记录实践而出错；(2)UI特征和AI解释不支持用户对这种纠缠和意义建构的觉察与批判性参与；(3)非规范MCTA用户在这种纠缠互动中感到孤立。

Conclusion: 基于发现提出针对预测性AI特征和解释的设计启示。

Abstract: In menstrual cycle tracking apps (MCTAs), AI-based predictions and insights have become increasingly popular. These features enable users to receive personalized information about their bodies and mental states. However, there is currently little research on how these predictive AI features and explanations affect users' lived experiences. This paper examines human-AI entanglement in MCTAs through 14 semi-structured user interviews and a group autoethnography. These methods uncover the processes leading to this phenomenon. Our results reveal that: (1) users understand their lived experiences in light of AI predictions, although these predictions can be faulty due to imperfect logging practices, (2) the user interface features and AI explanations do not support awareness or critical engagement with this entanglement and meaning-making, and (3) non-normative MCTA users report a sense of isolation in this entangled interaction. Based on our findings, we propose design implications for predictive AI features and explanations.

</details>


### [259] [Beyond VMAF: Towards Application-Specific Metrics for Teleoperation Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13525)
*Ines Trautmannsheimer,Richard Grauberger,Frank Diermeyer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 针对自动驾驶远程操作场景，研究者通过在线实验收集主观视频质量评分，对VMAF模型进行领域重训练，使RMSE降低15%、MAD降低27%，但发现某些关键区域存在客观评分与主观感知不一致的异常情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程操作中视频质量直接影响安全性与任务表现，而现有视频质量指标（如VMAF）并非针对自动驾驶远程操作场景训练，缺乏领域特异性，需要适配该特殊应用场景。

Method: 在Zenseact数据集上收集压缩视频序列的主观质量评分，利用这些评分对Video Multi-Method Assessment Fusion (VMAF)模型进行重训练，得到适配远程操作的变体模型。

Result: 重训练后的模型与人类评分的一致性优于原始4K VMAF：RMSE从10.36降至8.83，MAD从8.71降至6.38，分别提升15%和27%。但部分视频在驾驶关键区域存在明显退化时仍获得较高客观分数。

Conclusion: 融入领域特定数据能增强现有质量指标在安全关键应用中的预测能力；但在驾驶关键区域的退化检测方面仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Automated driving has made remarkable progress, yet situations still arise where human intervention is necessary. Teleoperation provides a scalable solution to address such cases, enabling remote operators to support vehicles without being physically present. In this context, video transmission forms the operator's primary source of situational awareness, making video quality a decisive factor for both safety and task performance. In an online study, participants rated compressed video sequences from the Zenseact Dataset and provided subjective quality ratings. These ratings were then used to retrain the Video Multi-Method Assessment Fusion (VMAF) model, yielding an adapted variant tailored to teleoperation. The retrained model demonstrated improved alignment with human ratings compared to the original 4K VMAF. In particular, RMSE decreased from 10.36 to 8.83, and MAD from 8.71 to 6.38, corresponding to improvements of 15% and 27%, respectively. These results highlight that incorporating domain-specific data can enhance the predictive power of established quality metrics in safety-critical applications. At the same time, Outlier cases emerged in which videos received high objective scores despite noticeable degradations in regions critical for the driving task.

</details>


### [260] [Beyond Anthropomorphism: Exploring the Roles of Perceived Non-humanity and Structural Similarity in Deep Self-Disclosure Toward Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13574)
*Satoru Shibuya*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探究了在向生成式AI进行深度自我表露时，感知非人性和结构相似性（而非拟人化）作为心理因素的作用。基于2025年收集的2400名参与者的横截面调查数据，发现高感知非人性和高结构相似性的群体自我表露发生率显著更高（OR=11.35），且表露深度也存在显著组间差异。结果表明，深度自我表露中的信任相关行为可能涉及拟人化感知之外的因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注拟人化在人类与AI交互中的作用，但作者认为向AI进行深度自我表露可能涉及拟人化之外的其他心理机制，因此提出感知非人性和结构相似性两个新构念。

Method: 采用横截面调查设计，收集2025年2400名参与者数据；使用逻辑回归分析各组间的自我表露发生率差异，使用ANOVA分析表露深度差异。

Result: 高感知非人性和高结构相似性的群体（Segment D）自我表露发生率显著高于基线组（Segment A），OR值为11.35；各组在自我表露深度上也存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 深度自我表露中的信任相关行为可能包含拟人化感知之外的因素，但鉴于研究为探索性且基于自我报告数据，结果应视为关联性而非因果性，未来需纵向或实验研究加以验证。

Abstract: This study investigates deep self-disclosure toward generative AI by examining perceived non-humanity and structural similarity as psychological factors beyond anthropomorphism. Perceived non-humanity may reduce evaluation apprehension, whereas structural similarity refers to the perceived logical alignment between a user's thinking and AI responses. Using cross-sectional survey data from 2,400 participants collected in 2025, this study analyzed associations with both the occurrence and depth of self-disclosure. Logistic regression indicated that the group high in both perceptions (Segment D) showed a significantly higher likelihood of disclosure than the baseline group (Segment A; OR = 11.35). ANOVA further showed significant between-group differences in disclosure depth. The findings suggest that trust-related behavior in deep self-disclosure may involve factors other than anthropomorphic perception. Because the study is exploratory and based on self-reported survey data, the results should be interpreted as associative rather than causal, and future longitudinal or experimental research is needed.

</details>


### [261] [Humanwashing -- It Should Leave You Feeling Dirty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13723)
*Ben Wilson,Matimba Swana,Peter Winter,Matt Roach*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文批判了“人在回路中”这一隐喻在AI决策系统中的滥用，指出其被用来“洗白”（humanwashing）AI系统，掩盖了实际过程和结果，而非真正提供安全保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “人在回路中”被广泛用于暗示AI决策系统的安全性，但对其实际含义缺乏充分审视，导致该术语被用来美化系统而非解决问题。

Method: 通过概念分析和隐喻批判，探讨“回路”这一隐喻是否有助于理解特定决策情境中的需求与成效，并揭示其模糊过程和结果、助长“人类洗白”的问题。

Result: “人在回路中”的 indiscriminate 使用模糊了决策过程和结果，使系统被美化为安全可靠，实则为“humanwashing”（人类洗白）。

Conclusion: “人在回路中”不应被无条件视为AI决策系统的安全保障；其隐喻的滥用掩盖了实际风险，需对其适用语境进行更严格的界定。

Abstract: The phrase 'human in the loop' is increasingly used to imply a sense of safety in relation to AI decision systems. It shouldn't. There are contexts where it can be applied appropriately, but these are not in the deployed decision systems we see dominating today. Human oversight of AI decision processes is one of the most popular proposals for addressing concerns, especially about bias, discrimination, misinformation, manipulation, accountability, and transparency. But there is insufficient examination of what human oversight actually means. The question raised in this paper is whether using the metaphor of a loop does anything to assist understanding of what is required and what is achieved in a particular decision context. Indiscriminate use of the loop metaphor obscures both processes and outcomes. It enables 'humanwashing', an activity analogous to 'greenwashing', where writers and commentators use language primarily aimed at putting systems in the best possible light.

</details>


### [262] ["Like Taking the Path of Least Resistance": Exploring the Impact of LLM Interaction on the Creative Process of Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13776)
*Zeinabsadat Saghi,Run Huang,Souti Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过20名程序员的被试内实验和回溯访谈，研究发现LLM辅助编程虽然能生成更正确、功能更完整的代码，但会显著缩短用户的创意生成时间（p=0.0004）并减少创意时刻（p=0.002），揭示了四种人机协作模式，并讨论了支持用户创造力的设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当GenAI承担了越来越多曾经需要人类想象力的生成性工作时，一个关键问题浮现：GenAI如何影响用户的创造力？

Method: 采用被试内设计（LLM辅助vs.非辅助），对20名程序员进行实验和回溯访谈，定量分析创意生成时间和创意时刻，定性分析交互行为并比较生成解决方案。

Result: LLM辅助下创意生成时间显著缩短（p=0.0004），创意时刻显著减少（p=0.002）；发现四种人机协作模式；LLM能生成更正确/功能完整的代码，但解决方案中的创意数量与被试自主生成相当。

Conclusion: LLM虽能提升代码质量和效率，但可能抑制用户的创造性思维过程；需优化LLM的设计以更好地支持而非替代用户创造力。

Abstract: Creativity is fundamentally human. As AI takes on more of the generative work that once required human imagination, despite documented limitations in creative ability, a critical question emerges: How does GenAI affect users' creativity? Through a within-subject study followed by retrospective interviews with (N=20) programmers, we investigated the impact of LLMs on participants' process of creative thinking in programming and the creativity of generated solutions. Across two conditions (LLM-assisted vs. unassisted), participants using LLMs had significantly shorter idea-generation periods (p=0.0004), leading to fewer creative moments (p=0.002). Qualitative analysis of participants' interactions and interviews revealed four different human-LLM collaboration modes supporting various problem-solving strategies. However, a comparative analysis of the generated solutions shows that while LLMs can help generate more correct and functional code, their solutions contain roughly the same number of ideas as participant-generated ones. Based on our findings, we discuss design implications and considerations for effectively using LLMs to support user creativity.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [Stress-Testing the Reasoning Competence of LLMs With Proofs Under Minimal Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12524)
*Konstantine Arkoudas,Serafim Batzoglou*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: ProofGrid是一个用于评估LLM推理能力的新型基准套件，专注于机器可验证的证明而非仅最终答案。包含15个任务，涵盖证明书写、检查、掩码和补全，采用紧凑的自然演绎语言NDL实现精确可审计验证。实验表明前沿模型在基础任务上表现良好，但在全局组合推理和低层证明合成等困难任务上仍远未解决，并发现了认知不稳定性现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理评估多依赖最终答案或人类/LLM评判，缺乏可机械复现、细粒度和可审计的验证方式。研究者希望通过机器可检查的证明来评估模型推理能力，克服现有基准在验证保证、推理深度和评估精度方面的局限性。

Method: 构建包含15个任务的ProofGrid基准套件，使用NDL（自然演绎语言）表达；开发了可容忍表面偏差的仪器化证明检查流水线，能定位首个实质性推理失败点；引入Epistemic Stability Index量化认知不稳定性；采用2PL IRT分析、Wright图和基于Fisher信息的归一化任务区分度测量进行补充评估。

Result: 前沿模型在多个基础任务上表现良好，但困难任务（尤其是全局组合推理和低层证明合成）远未解决；发现模型存在认知不稳定性——生成有缺陷的证明却能正确拒绝局部推理错误；通过Epistemic Stability Index形式化了这一现象。

Conclusion: LLM在形式化推理方面取得了快速进展但仍存在显著局限；ProofGrid提供了更精细、可复现的评估框架；认知不稳定性揭示了模型推理的深层次问题，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: We introduce ProofGrid, a benchmark suite for evaluating LLM reasoning through machine-checkable proofs rather than final answers alone. ProofGrid contains 15 tasks spanning proof writing, proof checking, proof masking, and proof gap-filling. Tasks are expressed in minimal formal notation, especially NDL, a compact natural-deduction language that fits in short prompts and supports precise, auditable verification. This yields mechanical, reproducible, and fine-grained evaluation rather than judgments by humans or LLMs. ProofGrid covers a calibrated difficulty spectrum, from foundational reasoning tests to structurally rich challenge tasks that no current model solves, while minimizing reliance on domain knowledge, solver delegation, and long-context artifacts. We also develop a comparative framework for reasoning benchmarks and use it to situate ProofGrid relative to existing work in terms of representation, verification guarantees, and reasoning depth.
  Methodologically, we introduce an instrumented proof-checking pipeline that tolerates minor surface deviations while locating the first substantive reasoning failure, improving measurement resolution and separating proof planning from low-level execution noise. Using this pipeline, we evaluate a broad range of open and proprietary models. Results show rapid progress but substantial remaining limits: frontier models perform well on several foundational tasks, yet difficult tasks, especially those requiring global combinatorial reasoning or low-level proof synthesis, remain far from solved. We also identify epistemic instability, where models generate flawed proofs yet correctly reject those local inferences in isolation, and formalize this with an Epistemic Stability Index. Finally, we complement accuracy with 2PL IRT analyses, Wright maps, and a normalized task-discrimination measure based on Fisher information.

</details>


### [264] [Biprofile Deviation Logic: Report-Replacement Frames and Audit Witnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12537)
*Faruk Alpay,Baris Basaran*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了双轮廓偏差逻辑（Biprofile deviation logic），将社会选择状态建模为真实现状与提交报告的双元组，通过联盟模态和固定律 $E_C \circ E_D = E_{C \cup D}$ 刻画策略性偏差。证明了 $H_{\mathrm{bp}}$ 对抽象框架类 $\mathrm{Dev}(N)$ 的可靠性与完备性，进行了坐标分离，并在社会选择层面贡献了审计层：类型化操纵见证、边界行定理、因子闭包准则。附带了可执行验证器、Lean/Alloy 形式化验证及运行日志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为社会选择中的策略操纵（strategic manipulation）提供一种形式逻辑框架，将真实现状与提交报告之间的偏差显式建模，实现对操纵行为的可审计表述和形式化验证。

Method: 提出双轮廓偏差逻辑 $H_{\mathrm{bp}}$，引入联盟模态 $E_C$ 满足固定律 $E_C \circ E_D = E_{C \cup D}$，通过反向组合中点（reverse-composition midpoint）构造典范证明，采用坐标分离技术区分抽象 $\mathrm{Dev}(N)$ 成分与真实报告坐标乘积。

Result: 证明了 $H_{\mathrm{bp}}$ 对 $\mathrm{Dev}(N)$ 的可靠性与完备性；提出了类型化操纵见证、边界行定理和因子闭包准则三个审计层核心结果；提供了可执行证书检查器及 Lean/Alloy 形式化验证工具。

Conclusion: 双轮廓偏差逻辑为策略性社会选择提供了一种可审计的形式化框架，且附带完整的形式化验证工具链，支撑理论结果的可信执行。

Abstract: Biprofile deviation logic models strategic social choice states as pairs $(R,P)$, where $R$ is the true profile used for welfare comparisons and $P$ is the submitted report profile used by the rule. Coalition modalities replace only the reports of the coalition, and their relations satisfy the fixed law $E_C \circ E_D = E_{C \cup D}$. The paper proves soundness and completeness of $H_{\mathrm{bp}}$ for the abstract frame class $\mathrm{Dev}(N)$, with the reverse-composition midpoint displayed inside the canonical proof. It then separates abstract $\mathrm{Dev}(N)$-components from genuine report-coordinate products by coordinate separation. On the social-choice side, the classical facts supply the source notions; the paper-specific contribution is the audit layer for representation changes: typed manipulation witnesses, a boundary-row theorem for off-domain extensions, and a factor-closure criterion for public deletions. The ancillary material contains the input formats, an executable certificate checker, Lean and Alloy companions for the finite relational lemmas and update patterns, recorded run logs, and checksums.

</details>


### [265] [ocLTL: LTL Realizability and Synthesis Modulo ω-Categorical Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12539)
*Ohad Asor*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ocLTL——一种在ω-范畴理论下的LTL+P变体，通过将每个数据子公式替换为完备类型的有限析取，将其可实现性和综合问题归约为命题LTL+P中的对应问题，复杂度保持在2-EXPTIME，额外爆炸仅依赖于理论而非公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展LTL+P（线性时态逻辑加谓词）以处理无穷域上的数据值，提出一种在ω-范畴理论框架下可判定的逻辑变体ocLTL，并解决其可实现性和综合问题。

Method: 将ocLTL的可实现性与综合问题归约为命题LTL+P中的对应问题。核心归约步骤是将每个数据子公式替换为完备类型的有限析取，利用ω-范畴理论的特性保证类型空间的有限性。

Result: 归约后复杂度保持在2-EXPTIME，额外复杂度爆炸仅依赖于所选的理论而非具体公式的长度。

Conclusion: ocLTL在保持命题LTL+P复杂度的前提下，成功处理了ω-范畴理论下的数据逻辑，为带数据约束的时态逻辑推理提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We introduce ocLTL, a version of LTL+P modulo ω-categorical theories. We reduce its realizability and synthesis problems into the corresponding problems in propositional LTL+P. The core of the reduction replaces each data subformula with a finite disjunction over complete types. The complexity remains 2-EXPTIME with additional blowup that depends only on the theory but not the formula.

</details>


### [266] [Cubical Type Theoretic Navya-Nyāya](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12548)
*Mrityunjoy Panday,Sudipta Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文在CCHM De Morgan立方体类型论（CTT）中形式化了印度新逻辑学派Navya-Nyaya的技术语言，解决了此前一阶逻辑、高阶逻辑和Martin-Lof类型论形式化中丢失的依赖界定、类型化缺位、非外延同一性和无界关系深度等核心结构，证明了四个内嵌定理和六个元理论结果，并在Cubical Agda中实现了15个《真理宝石分析》段落的编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前对Navya-Nyaya的形式化尝试（一阶逻辑、高阶逻辑、Martin-Lof类型论）均丢失了依赖界定、类型化缺位、非外延同一性、无界关系深度等负载结构，需要一个能够原生保留这些结构的形式化框架。

Method: 在CCHM De Morgan立方体类型论中编码Navya-Nyaya七个核心构造（关系、依赖界定、缺位、遍充、同一性、高阶关系、遍满）及限定词-限定结构；开发分层宇宙基础用于padartha体系；证明四个内嵌定理和六个元理论结果；在Cubical Agda中实现15个段落编码。

Result: 成功在CTT中形式化了Navya-Nyaya技术语言，证明了缺位对合律、kevalanvayi不可约性、共延无同一性、无h-set坍缩四个内嵌定理，以及可靠性、保守性、忠实性、区分保持性、可判定性、注释保守性六个元理论结果；给出了15个《真理宝石分析》段落的编码。

Conclusion: CCHM De Morgan立方体类型论能够原生支持Navya-Nyaya的所有负载结构，且Navya-Nyaya的对合否定学说要求使用De Morgan立方体而非笛卡尔立方体基础，这为从非西方哲学中获取类型论设计灵感提供了有力案例。

Abstract: We present a formalization of the technical language of Navya-Nyaya - the "New Logic" school of late-classical Indian philosophy - in CCHM De Morgan cubical type theory (CTT). Previous formalization attempts in first-order logic (Matilal), higher-order logic (Ganeri), and Martin-Lof type theory (Bhattacharyya) each lose load-bearing structure: dependent delimitation (avacchedaka), typed absence (abhava), non-extensional identity (tadatmya), or unbounded relational depth (parampara-sambandha). We argue that CTT closes this gap natively. We give CTT encodings for seven core constructs (sambandha, avacchedaka, abhava, vyapti, tadatmya, higher relations, paryapti) plus the qualifier-qualificand structure; develop a stratified-universe foundation for the padartha system; and prove four signature theorems internal to the encoding (involution of abhava, kevalanvayi irreducibility, coextension without identity, no h-set collapse) and six metatheoretic results (soundness, conservativity, faithfulness, distinction preservation, decidability, commentarial conservativity). We close with worked encodings of fifteen Tattvacintamani passages, comparison with prior formalizations, an implementation sketch in Cubical Agda, and five distinguishing predictions - including a novel argument from Navya-Nyaya's involutive-negation doctrine for the necessity of De Morgan over Cartesian cubical foundations.

</details>


### [267] [Ensuring Logic in the Fog: Sound POMDP Synthesis with LTL Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12581)
*Can Zhou,Yulong Gao,Pian Yu*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于信念的动态奖励塑形机制，将线性时序逻辑(LTL)规范与部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)相结合，通过改进的蒙特卡洛规划框架实现可验证的时序任务规划，在部分可观测环境中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测的不确定环境中，让自主智能体同时满足复杂时序约束（由LTL描述）非常困难。LTL在POMDP中的定性验证具有固有的不可判定性，导致定量合成困难，且现有近似求解器难以设计可靠的奖励信号。

Method: 提出一种新颖的、可证明正确的奖励塑形机制，该机制基于信念状态动态生成奖励，奖励信号来源于经过认证的LTL满足程度。将该机制集成到增强的蒙特卡洛规划框架中，使搜索过程专注于最大化可验证的成功概率。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在现有求解器失败的场景中仍能有效工作，并且在多个基准领域保持了有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 本文成功弥合了LTL规范与POMDP近似求解器之间的差距，为部分可观测环境下的复杂时序任务规划提供了一种可验证且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Synthesising autonomous agents that can navigate uncertain environments while adhering to complex temporal constraints remains a fundamental challenge. While Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) provides a rigorous language for specifying such tasks, the inherent undecidability of qualitatively verifying LTL satisfaction in partially observable Markov decision processes renders quantitative synthesis difficult, especially when designing reliable reward signals for approximate solvers. In this paper, we bridge this gap with a novel, sound reward-shaping mechanism that dynamically generates belief-dependent rewards grounded in certified LTL satisfaction. By integrating this mechanism into an enhanced Monte Carlo Planning framework, we empower agents to navigate the `fog' of partial observability with a search process focused on maximising verifiable success. Our experiments demonstrate that this approach not only thrives in scenarios where existing solvers fail but also maintains effectiveness and scalability across diverse benchmark domains.

</details>


### [268] [Quantitative Linear Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13348)
*Matteo Capucci,Robert Atkey,Charles Grellois,Ekaterina Komendantskaya*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出定量sequent演算(pQLL)，通过结合实数上加法与乘法的性质分析并修正sequent演算，为模糊逻辑中的加法连接词赋予可微分的"软"语义，解决了传统上加法连接词只能是"硬"格运算的问题。pQLL由硬度参数p索引，当p→∞时退化到MALL，并证明了截断消除定理和软格完备性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实值逻辑在概率和定量系统（尤其是机器学习模型）的验证中重获关注，可用于将规范直接整合到训练目标中。但要有效实现这一点，需要在连接词的逻辑性质与其语义之间取得平衡。主要障碍在于为加法连接词赋予"软"（即可微）语义——在线性逻辑和模糊逻辑中，加法连接词必然是"硬"的格运算。

Method: 将实数上加法与乘法的性质分析与sequent演算的显著修订相结合。引入"定量sequent演算"，同时推广了模糊逻辑的超sequent演算和深度推理，其中证明的有效性和序列的可证明性都是实值量。提出由硬度参数p索引的pQLL演算族，证明截断消除定理，并展示其在丰富的剩余"软"格上的完备性。

Result: 当p=∞时，pQLL退化为MALL（ multiplicative-additive linear logic），且pQLL中的可证明性在p→∞时收敛到MALL中的可证明性。证明了截断消除定理，并完成了软格上的完备性证明。

Conclusion: 成功为加法连接词赋予了可微分的软语义，为概率和定量系统验证中实值逻辑的应用提供了理论基础，特别是在机器学习模型中将规范直接整合到训练目标中具有潜力。

Abstract: Real-valued logics have seen a renewed interest in verification for probabilistic and quantitative systems, in particular machine learning models, where they can be used to directly integrate specifications in the training objective. To do so effectively one has to strike a balance between the logical properties of the connectives and their semantics. A major hurdle in this sense is to give ``soft'' (i.e. differentiable) semantics to additive connectives -- in linear and fuzzy logics, additives are necessarily ``hard'' lattice operations.
  In this paper, we solve this problem by combining an accurate analysis of the properties of sum and product on the reals with a significant revision of sequent calculus. We introduce `quantitative sequent calculi', which simultaneously generalize hypersequent calculi of fuzzy logics and deep inference, and in which validity of a proof and provability of a sequent are real-valued quantities. We present a family of calculi, pQLL, indexed by a hardness degree $p$, prove cut-elimination theorem for them, and show completeness for enriched residuated `soft' lattices. For $p = \infty$, pQLL reduces to MALL, with provability in pQLL converging to provability in MALL when $p \to \infty$.

</details>


### [269] [A Horn extension of DL-Lite with NL data complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13367)
*Janos Arpasi,Bartosz Jan Bednarczyk,Magdalena Ortiz*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文针对OMQA中AC0与PTime复杂度的二分法限制，提出了一种新的描述逻辑ELI⊴，通过引入分层机制控制合取与递归的交互，实现了对DL-Lite的严格扩展，支持可达性公理和受限合取，并证明其推理复杂度为NL，通过重写为嵌套二正则路径查询（GQL的子片段）实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OMQA领域存在AC0 (DL-Lite) vs PTime (其余DL)的二分法，限制了查询重写方案的选择，而图数据库查询语言（如GQL, SQL/PGQ）通常是NL完全的，因此需要找到一种既能表达丰富本体又能重写为图查询语言的Horn DL。

Method: 提出一种针对ELI的分层机制（stratification mechanism），控制合取与递归的交互，定义新的描述逻辑ELI⊴，并证明其可重写为嵌套二正则路径查询（nested two-way regular path queries），这是GQL的一个片段。

Result: ELI⊴严格扩展了核心DL-Lite，支持可达性公理和受限合取，推理复杂度为NL（与图查询语言匹配），并通过重写为GQL子片段提供了将OMQA扩展到图查询语言的初步证据。

Conclusion: ELI⊴是OMQA扩展到图查询语言的一个有前景的候选本体语言，突破了传统的AC0 vs PTime二分法限制。

Abstract: The literature on ontology-mediated query answering (OMQA) has been shaped by two key results: first-order rewritability for DL-Lite, and PTime-hardness of data complexity for essentially every description logic beyond it. This has effectively positioned DL-Lite as the only practical choice for query rewriting, restricting OMQA solutions to first-order queries and ontologies that can be rewritten into them. This AC0 vs. PTime dichotomy is especially limiting if we consider that OMQA targets graph-structured data, and that standard graph query languages (including the recent ISO standards GQL and SQL/PGQ) are typically NL-complete. Towards identifying a rich Horn DL that can be rewritten into graph query languages and that can still express many ELI and DL-Lite ontologies, we introduce a stratification mechanism for ELI that controls the interaction between conjunction and recursion. In this way, we obtain ELbotpreceq, a description logic that strictly extends the core DL-Lite, supports reachability axioms and restricted conjunction, and allows for reasoning in NL. We establish the NL upper bound via a rewriting into nested two-way regular path queries, a fragment of GQL, providing initial evidence that our ontology language is a promising candidate for extending OMQA to graph query languages.

</details>


### [270] [Verifying Exact Samplers for Continuous Distributions with a Discrete Program Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13526)
*Markus de Medeiros,Puming Liu,Kwing Hei Li,Alejandro Aguirre,Lars Birkedal,Joseph Tassarotti*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Continuous-Eris，一个用于验证可计算分布精确采样算法正确性的高阶分离逻辑，并在Rocq证明助手中验证了均匀分布、高斯分布和拉普拉斯分布的采样器以及精确实数算术库的正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续分布采样算法通常使用浮点数实现，引入舍入误差和近似，难以分析且可能引发差分隐私等安全算法的安全问题。精确采样算法基于可计算实数，可惰性生成任意精度的连续样本，但实现复杂且涉及概率选择、高阶函数和动态可变状态等语义挑战性的语言特性，缺乏形式化验证手段。

Method: 提出了Continuous-Eris，一个高阶分离逻辑，用于形式化验证基于可计算实数的精确采样算法的正确性。

Result: 在Rocq证明助手中成功验证了均匀分布、高斯分布和拉普拉斯分布的可计算采样器的正确性，以及一个用于处理生成样本的精确实数算术库的正确性。

Conclusion: Continuous-Eris能够有效验证可计算分布精确采样算法的正确性，为连续分布采样算法的安全性保障提供了形式化方法。

Abstract: Most implementations of sampling algorithms for continuous distributions use floating-point numbers, which introduce round-off errors and approximations. These errors can be difficult to analyze, and can cause security issues when used in algorithms for differential privacy. An alternative is to use exact sampling algorithms based on computable reals, which can lazily generate the digits of a continuous sample to arbitrary precision. However, these algorithms are intricate, and implementing and using them involves a combination of semantically challenging language features, such as probabilistic choice, higher-order functions, and dynamically-allocated mutable state.
  In this paper we present Continuous-Eris, a higher-order separation logic for verifying the correctness of exact sampling algorithms for computable distributions. To demonstrate Continuous-Eris, we verify the correctness of computable samplers for the uniform, Gaussian, and Laplace distributions, as well as a library for exact real arithmetic for working with generated samples. All of the results in this paper have been verified in the Rocq proof assistant.

</details>


### [271] [Monads and Distributive Laws in Substructural Contexts (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13533)
*Soichiro Fujii,Yun Chen Tsai,Yoàv Montacute,Ichiro Hasuo*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出子结构语境下 monad 与分配律的范畴理论。通过 Tronin 的词语范畴 W，统一形式化了子结构情形。引入 W-操作子 monad（由 W 中结构规则定义）和 W-交换 monad（在 W 的结构规则下不变）。给出 Set 上 monad 分配律 ST→TS 的规范构造，适用于 S 为 W-操作子、T 为 W-交换子的情形。当 S 不满足 W-操作子时，可通过精化强制实现 W-操作子性，这捕获了 Varacca 和 Winskel 的索引估值构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分配律研究中，变量上下文的结构规则（如交换、弱化、收缩）的有无起着关键作用。需要一种统一且与表示无关的形式化方法来处理这些子结构情形。

Method: 使用 Tronin 的词语范畴 W 来形式化子结构语境。定义 W-操作子 monad 和 W-交换 monad 两类。提供分配律的规范构造方法，以及对非 W-操作子 monad 进行精化强制 W-操作子性的方法。

Result: 给出了 Set 上 monad 间分配律 ST→TS 的规范构造，在 S 为 W-操作子、T 为 W-交换子（温和条件下）时适用。当 S 不满足 W-操作子性时，可通过精化强制实现，这统一解释了包括索引估值在内的多种已知和新的分配律。

Conclusion: 该理论以统一、与表示无关的方式形式化了子结构语境下的分配律，为 monad 组合提供了系统化的构造方法。

Abstract: We present a categorical theory of monads and distributive laws in substructural contexts. In the study of distributive laws, the roles of (the absence of) structural rules for variable contexts have been recognized; our theory formalizes these substructural situations using Tronin's verbal categories $\mathbf W$, in a uniform and presentation-independent manner. We introduce the classes of $\mathbf W$-operadic monads (those defined via the structural rules in $\mathbf W$) and of $\mathbf W$-commutative monads (those invariant under the structural rules in $\mathbf W$). We give a canonical construction of a distributive law $ST\to TS$ of monads on $\mathbf{Set}$; it is applicable when $S$ is $\mathbf W$-operadic and $T$ is $\mathbf W$-commutative (under mild conditions). This accounts for many known and new distributive laws. Even when $S$ fails to be $\mathbf W$-operadic, we can refine $S$ and force $\mathbf W$-operadicity; this captures Varacca and Winskel's construction of indexed valuations.

</details>


### [272] [Subsumption in $\mathcal{FL}_{\bot \mathit{reg}}$ with TBoxes Is in ExpTime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13553)
*Michał Henne,Barbara Morawska,Paweł Parys*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文研究了描述逻辑$\mathcal{FL}_{\bot \mathit{reg}}$及其相关片段中的概念包含问题，通过归约到奇性下推博弈，证明无TBox时PSpace完全，有TBox时ExpTime完全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 描述逻辑中概念包含是核心推理问题，但某些逻辑仍存在重要的开放问题，特别是包含正则表达式和空概念但缺乏概念否定的受限逻辑。

Method: 通过将概念包含问题归约到奇性下推博弈（parity pushdown games）来获得复杂性结果。

Result: $\mathcal{FL}_{\bot \mathit{reg}}$和$\mathcal{FL}_\mathit{reg}$中概念包含判定为PSpace完全；考虑TBox时复杂性提升至ExpTime完全。

Conclusion: 填补了包含正则表达式和空概念但无概念否定的描述逻辑中概念包含问题的复杂性理论空白，展示了奇性下推博弈在该类问题分析中的有效性。

Abstract: Description logics (DL) are a family of formal languages for representing and reasoning about structured knowledge in terms of concepts and their relationships. A central reasoning problem in DL is concept subsumption. Although this problem has been widely studied, important open problems remain for certain logics. The expressive power of DLs depends on the constructors available for building complex concepts. In this work, we investigate subsumption in the restricted logic $\mathcal{FL}_{\bot \mathit{reg}}$ and its related fragments $\mathcal{FL}_\mathit{reg}$, $\mathcal{FL}_\bot$, and $\mathcal{FL}_0$. These logics support value restrictions over role names, where the subscript $\bot$ denotes the presence of the empty concept and ${reg}$ denotes the use of regular expressions over roles. None of these logics includes concept negation. We show that deciding subsumption between two concept descriptions in $\mathcal{FL}_{\bot \mathit{reg}}$ and $\mathcal{FL}_\mathit{reg}$ is PSpace-complete. When subsumption is considered with respect to a TBox (i.e., a set of axioms), the complexity increases to ExpTime-complete. Our results are obtained via a novel reduction to parity pushdown games.

</details>


### [273] [Multi-Property Temporal Logic Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13668)
*Arınç Demir,Dogan Ulus*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在线多属性监控框架，通过将过去时间LTL和MTL规范编译为共享的有向无环图，并采用基于竞技场分配的双缓冲内存布局，实现子公式结果的复用与高效增量更新。实验表明，在多属性场景下每个属性的吞吐量相比传统单属性监控提升2至12倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时验证方法对每个时序属性独立构造监控器，导致大量冗余计算，难以扩展到同时监控数十到数百个属性的实际系统需求。

Method: 将多个属性编译为共享的有向无环图（每个子公式节点被多个属性复用），并采用基于竞技场分配的双缓冲内存布局存储中间结果，实现数据局部性优化和低开销增量更新。

Result: 在多属性配置下，每个属性的吞吐量相比传统单属性监控提升2至4.5倍（较小规模）和6至12倍（较大规模）。

Conclusion: 该框架可扩展到大规模规范集，适用于高性能和资源受限系统，为多属性运行时验证提供了一种高效可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Runtime verification enables checking temporal logic specifications over individual execution traces and offers a scalable alternative to exhaustive formal verification. In practice, systems must satisfy dozens to hundreds of temporal properties simultaneously; however, existing approaches monitor each property in isolation, resulting in redundant computation and limited scalability. In this work, we present an online multi-property monitoring framework that compiles past-time LTL and MTL specifications into a shared directed acyclic graph of subformulas with one output per property. Unlike prior approaches that construct monitors independently, our method extends compositional sequential network-based temporal logic monitor construction to a shared setting, enabling reuse of intermediate results across properties while preserving their individual structure. Central to our approach is a data-oriented execution model based on an arena-allocated, double-buffered layout that stores intermediate results for each subformula in compact, contiguous memory. This design favors spatial locality and enables incremental updates with minimal overhead. Experimental results demonstrate per-property throughput improvements of 2x to 4.5x and 6x to 12x in multi-property configurations compared to conventional single-property monitoring, enabling scalability to large specification sets and deployment in high-performance and resource-constrained systems.

</details>


### [274] [First Steps Towards Probabilistic Iris: Harmonizing Independence, Conditioning, and Dynamic Heap Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13765)
*Janine Lohse,Tim Rohde,Jimmy Xin,Niklas Mück,Iona Kuhn,Derek Dreyer,Deepak Garg,Emanuele D'Osualdo*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Amaryllis，这是首个支持概率独立性与条件推理，同时能处理动态内存分配的概率分离逻辑。通过提出新的索引化估值模型，将Iris风格的资源所有权提升到分布层面，并适配了Iris的核心概念（帧保持更新、权威资源代数、最弱前置条件模态）以支持概率推理，最终在Rocq证明助手中实现了机械化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用概率逻辑（GPLs）如PSL、Lilac、Bluebell和pcOL虽然支持概率分布上的Hoare式断言以及独立性和条件化等模块化原则，但均不支持动态分配的内存（堆指针），更无法处理Iris等现代分离逻辑中基于资源代数的ghost state。作者认为这是由于一个根本性障碍：内存的形状和位置的标识在不同随机结果下可能不同，指针所有权与概率独立性和条件化难以协调。此外，现有GPLs均未在证明助手中实现机械化。

Method: 提出一种新的"索引化估值"（indexed valuation）风格的概率断言模型，将标准Iris风格资源（如堆）的所有权以通用方式提升到分布层面；然后适配Iris的核心概念——帧保持更新（frame-preserving update）、权威资源代数（authoritative resource algebras）和最弱前置条件模态（weakest precondition modality）——使其对概率推理可靠并验证动态分配的正确性；最后在Rocq证明助手中机械化所有结果并开发了基于Iris的证明模式。

Result: 成功构建了Amaryllis，这是首个同时支持概率独立性/条件推理和动态内存分配的通用概率逻辑，并在Rocq中完成了机械化验证。

Conclusion: Amaryllis标志着通用概率逻辑与现代分离逻辑（如Iris）融合的重要第一步，为概率程序推理中处理动态分配和ghost state提供了新的理论基础和工具支持。

Abstract: There has recently been exciting progress in the realm of *probabilistic separation logics*. An important subclass of these -- including PSL, Lilac, Bluebell, and pcOL -- are *general-purpose probabilistic logics* (or GPLs, for short), meaning that they provide primitive Hoare-style assertions about probability distributions on the program state, along with powerful modularity principles like *independence* and *conditioning*. However, none of these logics support reasoning about dynamically allocated memory (i.e., pointers into a heap), let alone the more sophisticated resource algebra-based ghost state of modern separation logics like Iris. We argue that this is due to a fundamental obstacle: since the shape of memory (and identity of memory locations) may differ under different random outcomes, it is unclear how pointer ownership can be harmonized with probabilistic independence and conditioning. Furthermore, none of the existing GPLs have been mechanized in a proof assistant.
  In this paper, we take a substantial first step towards a marriage of GPLs and modern separation logics like Iris, in the form of **Amaryllis**. Amaryllis is the first GPL to support independence and conditional reasoning while also handling dynamic memory allocation. To overcome the aforementioned obstacle, we propose a new *indexed valuation*-style model of probabilistic assertions, whereby ownership of a standard Iris-style resource (e.g., heaps) can be promoted to ownership at the level of distributions in a generic fashion. We then show how to adapt the central Iris notions of *frame-preserving update*, *authoritative resource algebras*, and the *weakest precondition modality* to be sound for probabilistic reasoning and validate dynamic allocation. Finally, we have mechanized all our results in the Rocq proof assistant and developed an Iris-based proof mode for conducting proofs within Amaryllis.

</details>


### [275] [Quantitative Linear Logic for Neuro-Symbolic Learning and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13845)
*Thomas Flinkow,Ekaterina Komendantskaya,Matteo Capucci,Rosemary Monahan*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出定量线性逻辑（QLL），一种新的可微逻辑，通过将逻辑连接词解释为机器学习中常用的运算（如求和与log-sum-exp），在逻辑完备性与分析实用性之间取得平衡，在逻辑定律满足和对抗攻击验证方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可微逻辑在逻辑性质（如模糊逻辑的代数基础）与分析性质（如DL2的深度学习适配性）之间存在根本性张力，缺乏令人满意的理论基础。

Method: 提出定量线性逻辑（QLL），核心思想是"自然性"：逻辑约束翻译为损失时，连接词语义应使用ML实践中相关的运算（求和与log-sum-exp）作用于加性量（如logits），并受线性逻辑框架约束。

Result: QLL满足线性逻辑中大部分标准逻辑定律，在测试时性能（对抗攻击）与逻辑约束的实际验证（现成验证器）高度相关，在SoTA方法中表现突出。

Conclusion: QLL为可微逻辑领域提供了兼顾逻辑完备性与实证有效性的理论基础，解决了长期存在的模糊逻辑与特设可微逻辑之间的张力。

Abstract: Differentiable Logics are deployed in neuro-symbolic learning tasks as a way of embedding logical constraints in the training objective of neural networks. A differentiable logic consists of a syntax to write logical properties and a semantics to interpret them as real-valued functions to be folded in the loss function. A defining trade-off of the field is that between logical properties of the connectives, and analytic concerns for the semantics, with both aspects being relevant in applications. At one extreme we find fuzzy logics, that have well-established algebraic and proof-theoretic foundations, and at the other ad-hoc differentiable logics like Fischer's DL2, conceived for deep learning applications. However, no satisfactory foundation has emerged yet. We propose a resolution to this long-standing tension via a novel logic, Quantitative Linear Logic (QLL), with foundational ambitions. Our design is driven by naturality -- the idea that, since logical constraints are translated to losses, the semantics of the connectives should be pertinent operations used in ML practice (that is, sum and log-sum-exp) on additive quantities (like logits). We then judge the result on two aspects: logical adequacy -- that they satisfy most of the standard logical laws of Linear Logic; and empirical effectiveness -- test-time performance (as measured by adversarial attacks) is well-correlated to the actual verification of the logical constraints (as measured by off-the-shelf neural network verifiers), which makes QLL stand out among SoTA techniques.

</details>
